Saya akan susun sebagai peta praktis untuk microservice Jersey/JAX-RS, bukan teori testing generik: fokus ke boundary, test pyramid, database/MyBatis/PostgreSQL, containerized integration test, dan benchmark yang aman dibaca.

Berikut **Decomposition Map of practical categories/domain/skills** untuk **Unit Test, Integration Test, dan Benchmark Test** pada **Java Jersey/JAX-RS based microservice application**.

## Introduction

Dalam microservice Java berbasis **Jersey/JAX-RS**, testing sebaiknya tidak dipahami sebagai “menulis test sebanyak mungkin”, tetapi sebagai **strategi validasi berlapis**:

- **Unit test**: memastikan logic kecil benar, cepat, deterministik, tanpa dependency eksternal.
- **Integration test**: memastikan boundary antar-komponen benar, misalnya REST resource, MyBatis mapper, PostgreSQL, transaksi, serialization, Docker container, dan external API stub.
- **Benchmark test**: mengukur performa secara terkontrol, terutama untuk parsing, mapping, SQL access pattern, serialization, concurrency, dan hot-path logic.

Untuk Java modern, basis praktis yang umum dipakai adalah **JUnit Jupiter/JUnit Platform**, Mockito, Maven Surefire/Failsafe, Jersey Test Framework, Testcontainers, JaCoCo, dan JMH. JUnit terbaru saat ini adalah generasi JUnit 6 dan membutuhkan Java 17+, tetapi banyak enterprise masih menggunakan JUnit 5/Jupiter karena kompatibilitas ekosistemnya luas. ([JUnit](https://junit.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jersey menyediakan **Jersey Test Framework** untuk menguji aplikasi JAX-RS/Jersey dengan beberapa opsi execution environment. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/test-framework.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk integration test dengan PostgreSQL, Testcontainers menyediakan integrasi JUnit Jupiter dan model container per test method atau shared per test class. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/test_framework_integration/junit_5/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk benchmark JVM, JMH dari OpenJDK adalah harness standar untuk membuat dan menganalisis benchmark Java/JVM, dan direkomendasikan dijalankan sebagai standalone Maven benchmark project agar hasil lebih reliable. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openjdk/jmh/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Tabular Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill/domain | Fokus praktis di Jersey/JAX-RS microservice | Tools/konsep utama | Output yang diharapkan |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Testing strategy | Test pyramid | Memisahkan test cepat, test boundary, dan test runtime agar CI tidak lambat dan flaky | Unit, component, integration, contract, smoke, benchmark | Test suite punya struktur jelas |
| Test scope design | Menentukan “apa yang dites” | Tidak semua test perlu naik ke HTTP/DB; logic murni cukup unit test | Boundary thinking, dependency isolation | Test tidak over-engineered |
| Unit test | Service/domain logic | Menguji business rule, branching, state transition, validation decision | JUnit Jupiter/JUnit 5/6, AssertJ/Hamcrest | Test cepat dan deterministik |
| Unit test | Error-path testing | Menguji negative case, invalid state, forbidden transition, null/empty/boundary value | `assertThrows`, parameterized test | Failure behavior terdokumentasi |
| Unit test | Mapper test | Menguji DTO ↔ domain ↔ persistence model, terutama field wajib, enum, date/time, nested object | MapStruct/manual mapper test | Mapping aman dari regression |
| Unit test | Validation test | Menguji Bean Validation annotation dan custom validator sebelum masuk DB/API | Jakarta Validation, Hibernate Validator | Payload invalid tertangkap awal |
| Unit test | Mocking discipline | Mock hanya dependency luar; jangan mock object yang merupakan value/domain model | Mockito, fake repository, stub client | Test tetap readable dan tidak rapuh |
| Unit test | Parameterized/table-driven test | Menguji banyak kombinasi rule tanpa copy-paste | `@ParameterizedTest`, `@CsvSource`, `@MethodSource` | Coverage rule meningkat |
| Unit test | Time/randomness control | Menghindari flaky test karena `now()`, timezone, UUID/random | `Clock`, supplier, deterministic ID | Test stabil di lokal dan CI |
| JAX-RS resource test | Resource method behavior | Menguji endpoint behavior tanpa full deployment | Jersey Test Framework | Status code, body, headers benar |
| JAX-RS resource test | Serialization/deserialization | Menguji JSON-B/Jackson provider, date format, enum format, unknown field behavior | Jersey provider config | Contract JSON stabil |
| JAX-RS resource test | Exception mapping | Menguji `ExceptionMapper` untuk domain exception, validation error, DB error | JAX-RS `ExceptionMapper` | Error response konsisten |
| JAX-RS resource test | Filter/interceptor behavior | Menguji correlation ID, auth context, request logging, headers | ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter | Cross-cutting behavior valid |
| Integration test | MyBatis mapper test | Menguji SQL XML/annotation benar terhadap PostgreSQL real, bukan H2 palsu | MyBatis, PostgreSQL, Testcontainers | Query valid secara nyata |
| Integration test | Transaction behavior | Menguji commit/rollback, nested operation, partial failure | JDBC transaction, service transaction boundary | Data consistency terbukti |
| Integration test | PL/pgSQL function test | Menguji function/procedure DB yang berisi business logic atau batch operation | PostgreSQL, migration scripts | DB-side logic aman |
| Integration test | Migration compatibility | Menguji schema migration bisa naik dari baseline ke latest | Flyway/Liquibase bila dipakai, SQL script | CI menangkap migration rusak |
| Integration test | Repository/service + DB | Menguji service dengan DB real untuk path kritikal | Testcontainers PostgreSQL | Confidence terhadap persistence path |
| Integration test | REST + DB slice | Menguji endpoint sampai DB untuk case utama | JerseyTest + Testcontainers | API dan data layer sinkron |
| Integration test | External service stub | Menguji call ke sistem eksternal tanpa benar-benar call sistem luar | WireMock/MockServer, fake server | Retry/timeout/error mapping valid |
| Integration test | Idempotency | Menguji duplicate request, retry, same idempotency key, race condition | DB unique constraint, transaction, concurrent test | Tidak double insert/double process |
| Integration test | Concurrency behavior | Menguji lock, optimistic update, duplicate approval, race update | PostgreSQL lock, isolation level, concurrent executor | Race condition terdeteksi |
| Integration test | Camunda workflow integration | Menguji service task, delegate, process variable, incident path | Camunda test extension, embedded/runtime test | Workflow tidak hanya benar di diagram |
| CI execution | Maven Surefire | Menjalankan unit test di fase `test`; Surefire mendukung JUnit Platform dengan TestEngine. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-surefire-plugin/examples/junit-platform.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | `maven-surefire-plugin` | Fast feedback di PR |
| CI execution | Maven Failsafe | Menjalankan integration test di fase `integration-test` dan `verify`; dokumentasi Maven menyarankan `mvn verify` untuk integration test. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-failsafe-plugin/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | `maven-failsafe-plugin` | Integration test tidak mengganggu cleanup |
| CI execution | Test naming convention | Memisahkan `*Test`, `*IT`, `*Benchmark` agar pipeline bisa selektif | Surefire/Failsafe includes/excludes | Build stage jelas |
| CI execution | Test profile | Memisahkan local fast test, docker integration test, nightly benchmark | Maven profiles | Developer tidak tersiksa test lambat |
| CI execution | Coverage | Mengukur coverage sebagai indikator, bukan tujuan mutlak; JaCoCo Maven plugin menyediakan runtime agent dan report creation. ([EclEmma](https://www.eclemma.org/jacoco/trunk/doc/maven.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | JaCoCo | Blind spot terlihat |
| Benchmark test | JMH microbenchmark | Mengukur method hot path: mapper, parser, serializer, validator, algorithm | JMH | Data performa lebih kredibel |
| Benchmark test | Warm-up awareness | JVM butuh warm-up karena JIT; hasil tanpa warm-up bisa misleading | JMH warmup/measurement/fork | Angka tidak asal cepat/lambat |
| Benchmark test | Allocation profiling | Mengukur object allocation berlebih di hot path | JMH profilers, JFR, async-profiler | GC pressure terlihat |
| Benchmark test | Serialization benchmark | Membandingkan JSON-B/Jackson config, payload size, date format, nested DTO | JMH, representative payload | Pilihan serializer berbasis evidence |
| Benchmark test | SQL benchmark | Mengukur query pattern, index usage, batch insert/update | PostgreSQL EXPLAIN, pg_stat_statements, JMH cautiously | Bottleneck DB terlihat |
| Benchmark test | End-to-end performance smoke | Mengukur endpoint latency/throughput secara sistemik | k6/Gatling/JMeter, container env | Performance regression terlihat |
| Benchmark test | Regression threshold | Menentukan batas perubahan performa yang dianggap gagal | baseline, percentile, confidence interval | CI punya sinyal performa |
| Reliability | Fixture management | Data test harus eksplisit, kecil, dan mudah dipahami | builders, object mother, SQL seed | Test mudah dirawat |
| Reliability | Test isolation | Setiap test tidak bergantung pada urutan atau data test lain | container reset, transaction rollback, schema cleanup | Tidak flaky |
| Reliability | Observability in tests | Failure harus memberi alasan jelas: request, response, SQL, state | assertion message, logs, captured response | Debug cepat |
| Reliability | Flaky test prevention | Hindari sleep, timing magic, dependency network real | Awaitility, deterministic async hooks | CI stabil |
| Maintainability | Test architecture | Struktur test mengikuti layer aplikasi | `unit/`, `integration/`, `benchmark/` | Onboarding test mudah |
| Maintainability | Test data builders | Mengurangi noise object setup besar | builder pattern, fixture factory | Test fokus ke intent |
| Maintainability | Golden files | Untuk payload JSON/XML kompleks, gunakan sample file terkontrol | resource files, snapshot carefully | Contract payload mudah dicek |
| Governance | Quality gate | Menentukan minimal test untuk PR: unit pass, selected IT pass, no coverage drop | GitHub Actions, Maven, JaCoCo | Engineering discipline konsisten |

## Practical Categories yang Paling Penting untuk Senior Engineer

### 1. Unit Test bukan “test semua method”

Yang penting adalah menguji **decision point**:

```text
Good unit test target:
- business rule
- validation decision
- state transition
- mapper correctness
- exception behavior
- edge case
- idempotency decision
- retry decision

Bad unit test target:
- getter/setter
- framework wiring
- private method directly
- mocking everything until test tidak punya makna
```

Untuk aplikasi regulatory/case-management/workflow, unit test sebaiknya fokus pada:

```text
case status transition
approval/rejection rule
SLA calculation
escalation rule
role-based decision
document completeness check
payment/state reconciliation
retry/idempotency decision
```

---

### 2. Integration Test harus menguji boundary nyata

Untuk stack Anda, integration test paling bernilai biasanya di area ini:

```text
JAX-RS endpoint
   ↓
Resource class
   ↓
Service
   ↓
MyBatis mapper
   ↓
PostgreSQL / PL/pgSQL
```

Kenapa penting? Karena banyak bug enterprise bukan terjadi di pure Java logic, tetapi di boundary:

```text
JSON field mismatch
wrong enum mapping
wrong HTTP status
wrong SQL parameter
timezone issue
transaction rollback tidak sesuai
constraint violation tidak dimapping dengan benar
query lambat karena index tidak dipakai
```

---

### 3. Benchmark Test jangan dicampur dengan Unit Test

Benchmark bukan test correctness biasa. Benchmark menjawab:

```text
Apakah implementation A lebih cepat/stabil dari implementation B?
Apakah mapper ini allocation-heavy?
Apakah JSON serialization ini bottleneck?
Apakah batch insert lebih baik daripada row-by-row insert?
Apakah cache benar-benar membantu?
```

JMH penting karena benchmark manual di JVM sering misleading akibat JIT, warm-up, dead-code elimination, GC, dan runtime profile. Bahkan riset terbaru masih menekankan bahwa microbenchmark dapat misleading bila konteks produksinya tidak representatif, meskipun memakai JMH. ([arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23570?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jadi angka benchmark harus dibaca sebagai **evidence terkontrol**, bukan kebenaran absolut produksi.

---

```text
src/test/java

│   ├── service
│   ├── domain
│   ├── mapper
│   ├── validation
│   └── util

│
├── integration
│   ├── repository
│   ├── mybatis

│   ├── external
│   └── workflow
│
└── support
    ├── fixture
    ├── builders
    ├── containers
    └── assertions

src/jmh/java
└── benchmark
    ├── JsonSerializationBenchmark
    ├── MapperBenchmark
    ├── RuleEvaluationBenchmark
    └── QueryMappingBenchmark
```

## Recommended Maven Naming Convention

```text
Unit test:
- *Test.java
- run by Maven Surefire
- command: mvn test

Integration test:
- *IT.java
- run by Maven Failsafe
- command: mvn verify

Benchmark:
- *Benchmark.java
- run separately
- command: mvn -Pbenchmark clean verify
```

Pemisahan ini penting karena Maven Failsafe memang dirancang untuk integration test di lifecycle `integration-test` dan `verify`, sedangkan Surefire umum dipakai untuk unit test pada fase `test`. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-surefire-plugin/examples/junit-platform.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Kita mulai **Seri 1/14** dengan fondasi: bagaimana menentukan boundary test supaya unit, integration, dan benchmark tidak saling tumpang tindih. Saya akan buat praktis dengan contoh struktur project, naming convention, dan keputusan “test ini masuk layer mana”.

# Seri 1/14 - Test Pyramid & Test Boundary Design  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini menjawab pertanyaan inti:

> “Sebuah behavior di aplikasi microservice harus dites di level mana: unit test, resource test, integration test, contract test, end-to-end test, atau benchmark?”

Ini penting karena test yang salah layer biasanya menghasilkan dua masalah besar:

```text
1. Terlalu banyak mock
   → test hijau, tetapi aplikasi gagal saat jalan nyata.

2. Terlalu banyak integration/end-to-end test
   → CI lambat, flaky, developer malas menjalankan test.
```

Untuk stack Anda - **Java 17+, Jersey/JAX-RS, MyBatis, PostgreSQL, PL/pgSQL, Camunda, Docker, Kubernetes, Maven, GitHub Actions** - strategi testing yang sehat harus memisahkan:

```text
logic correctness
HTTP/API behavior
serialization/deserialization
database correctness
transaction correctness
workflow/process correctness
external integration behavior
performance characteristic
```

JUnit Platform adalah fondasi eksekusi test di JVM modern dan menyediakan mekanisme untuk menjalankan testing framework melalui TestEngine. ([JUnit](https://docs.junit.org/6.1.0/overview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk eksekusi Maven, unit test umumnya masuk Surefire, sedangkan integration test masuk Failsafe pada fase `integration-test` dan `verify`. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-failsafe-plugin/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model: Jangan Mulai dari Tool, Mulai dari Boundary

Kesalahan umum engineer saat mulai testing adalah langsung bertanya:

```text
Pakai JUnit?
Pakai Mockito?
Pakai Testcontainers?
Pakai JerseyTest?
Pakai JMH?
```

Pertanyaan yang lebih benar:

```text
Behavior apa yang ingin dibuktikan?
Dependency apa yang harus nyata?
Dependency apa yang boleh dipalsukan?
Failure mode apa yang penting?
Berapa cepat test ini harus berjalan?
Apakah test ini harus jalan di setiap PR?
```

## Boundary utama di Jersey/JAX-RS microservice

```text
Client
  ↓ HTTP
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓ DTO / validation / exception mapping
Service Layer
  ↓ business rules / orchestration
Repository / Mapper
  ↓ SQL / transaction
PostgreSQL / PL/pgSQL
  ↓
External system / workflow / message / storage
```

Setiap boundary punya jenis test yang berbeda.

---

# 3. Diagram Test Pyramid untuk Stack Ini

```text
                    ┌────────────────────────────┐
                    │ End-to-End / Smoke Test     │
                    │ Few, slow, critical paths   │
                    └─────────────▲──────────────┘
                                  │
                    ┌─────────────┴──────────────┐
                    │ Contract / API Test         │
                    │ API compatibility           │
                    └─────────────▲──────────────┘
                                  │
                    ┌─────────────┴──────────────┐
                    │ Integration Test            │
                    │ Jersey + MyBatis + DB       │
                    │ External stub / Camunda     │
                    └─────────────▲──────────────┘
                                  │
                    ┌─────────────┴──────────────┐
                    │ Resource / Component Test   │
                    │ JAX-RS resource, mapper,    │
                    │ filter, exception mapper    │
                    └─────────────▲──────────────┘
                                  │
                    ┌─────────────┴──────────────┐
                    │ Unit Test                   │
                    │ Domain, service, rule,      │
                    │ mapper, validator           │
                    └────────────────────────────┘

Separate lane:

                    ┌────────────────────────────┐
                    │ Benchmark Test              │
                    │ JMH / perf regression       │
                    │ Not correctness test        │
                    └────────────────────────────┘
```

Benchmark tidak masuk pyramid correctness. Benchmark adalah **evidence performa**, bukan pengganti unit/integration test.

---

# 4. Practical Boundary Decision Table

| Behavior yang ingin dibuktikan | Test layer yang tepat | Dependency nyata? | Dependency dipalsukan? | Contoh |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| Rule status case berubah dari `DRAFT` ke `SUBMITTED` | Unit test | Tidak perlu | Repository, clock, ID generator | `CaseSubmissionServiceTest` |
| Payload invalid menghasilkan HTTP 400 | Resource test | JAX-RS validation config | Service boleh fake | `CaseResourceValidationTest` |
| Domain exception menjadi error response standar | Resource/component test | `ExceptionMapper` nyata | Service fake | `ExceptionMapperTest` |
| MyBatis SQL benar terhadap PostgreSQL | Integration test | PostgreSQL nyata via container | External service dipalsukan | `CaseMapperIT` |
| Insert case dan audit log rollback bersama | Integration test | DB + transaction nyata | External service fake | `CaseTransactionIT` |
| Endpoint POST `/cases` benar sampai DB | Integration test | Jersey + service + DB nyata | External service stub | `CaseResourceIT` |
| Duplicate request tidak membuat double submission | Integration/concurrency test | DB constraint nyata | External service stub | `IdempotencyIT` |
| External API timeout dimapping ke retryable failure | Integration test dengan stub | HTTP client nyata ke stub | External system asli tidak dipakai | `PaymentGatewayClientIT` |
| Camunda process jalan ke activity berikutnya | Workflow integration test | Process engine/test runtime nyata | External task/service fake | `CaseWorkflowIT` |
| JSON serialization lebih cepat setelah ubah mapper | Benchmark test | Payload representatif | Tidak pakai mock | `JsonMapperBenchmark` |
| Query lambat setelah schema berubah | Integration/performance test | PostgreSQL nyata dengan data representatif | Tidak pakai H2 | `CaseSearchQueryIT` |
| Full happy path antar banyak service | E2E/smoke test | Environment deployed | Minimal fake | smoke pipeline |

---

# 5. Decomposition Map

## 5.1 Testing Strategy Layer

| Skill | Penjelasan praktis | Output |
|---|---|---|
| Test boundary design | Menentukan level test berdasarkan behavior, bukan berdasarkan class | Test tidak salah tempat |
| Test pyramid discipline | Mayoritas unit/component, sebagian integration, sangat sedikit E2E | CI cepat dan stabil |
| Risk-based testing | Test diprioritaskan pada area risiko tinggi: money, state, transaction, auth, workflow | Coverage bernilai, bukan sekadar angka |
| Test classification | Memisahkan test cepat, lambat, containerized, benchmark | Pipeline bisa dikontrol |
| Naming convention | `*Test`, `*IT`, `*Benchmark` | Maven lifecycle jelas |
| Build lifecycle mapping | Surefire untuk unit test, Failsafe untuk integration test | `mvn test` cepat, `mvn verify` lengkap |
| Failure-mode thinking | Setiap test penting harus punya happy path dan failure path | Bug kritikal lebih mudah tertangkap |

---

## 5.2 Unit Test Layer

| Skill | Fokus | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Domain rule testing | Business rule murni | Case tidak boleh approve jika dokumen belum lengkap |
| Service orchestration testing | Logic service tanpa DB nyata | Service memanggil repository dan event publisher sesuai kondisi |
| Mapper testing | DTO/domain/entity mapping | `CaseRequest` ke `CaseCommand` |
| Validator testing | Custom validation rule | `submittedAt` tidak boleh sebelum `createdAt` |
| Exception testing | Error path | Invalid transition throw `InvalidCaseStateException` |
| Time-sensitive test | SLA, due date, expiry | Pakai `Clock.fixed()` |
| Parameterized test | Banyak kombinasi input | Status transition matrix |

---

## 5.3 Jersey/JAX-RS Resource Test Layer

Jersey menyediakan **Jersey Test Framework** untuk menguji komponen server-side JAX-RS/Jersey dan mendukung beberapa execution environment. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/test-framework.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Skill | Fokus | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Resource endpoint behavior | HTTP method, path, status | `POST /cases` return 201 |
| Request body parsing | JSON payload ke DTO | Invalid JSON → 400 |
| Response serialization | DTO ke JSON | Field date format sesuai contract |
| ExceptionMapper testing | Exception ke response | `CaseNotFoundException` → 404 |
| Filter testing | Auth, correlation ID, tracing | Missing correlation ID auto-generated |
| Bean Validation testing | Annotation validation | Missing mandatory field → 400 |
| Header testing | Content-Type, Location, Cache-Control | POST create returns `Location` |

---

## 5.4 Integration Test Layer

Testcontainers mendukung JUnit Jupiter melalui annotation seperti `@Testcontainers` dan `@Container`; static container dapat dishare antar test method dalam satu class, sedangkan instance container dibuat per method. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/test_framework_integration/junit_5/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Testcontainers sendiri didesain untuk menyediakan instance ringan dan disposable dari database atau sistem lain yang berjalan di Docker container. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Skill | Fokus | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| MyBatis mapper integration | SQL XML/annotation benar | `selectCaseById()` return row yang benar |
| PostgreSQL compatibility | Pakai DB nyata, bukan H2 | JSONB, array, timestamp, function |
| Transaction testing | Commit/rollback | Insert case + audit log atomic |
| Migration testing | Schema valid | Migration latest bisa jalan dari baseline |
| Constraint testing | Unique/check/foreign key | Duplicate idempotency key gagal terkendali |
| PL/pgSQL testing | Function/procedure behavior | `calculate_sla_deadline()` benar |
| Repository concurrency test | Locking/race condition | Two approvals bersamaan hanya satu sukses |

---

## 5.5 Contract/API Test Layer

| Skill | Fokus | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Request/response schema stability | API tidak breaking consumer | Field wajib tidak hilang |
| Error contract | Format error konsisten | `code`, `message`, `traceId`, `details` |
| Backward compatibility | Consumer lama tetap jalan | Optional field baru tidak merusak |
| OpenAPI-based validation | Spec dan implementasi sinkron | Endpoint sesuai spec |
| Consumer-driven contract | Producer tidak merusak consumer | Pact-style contract |

---

## 5.6 End-to-End / Smoke Test Layer

| Skill | Fokus | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Critical path validation | Hanya flow paling penting | Create case → submit → approve |
| Deployment verification | App benar di environment | Health check + DB reachable |
| Cross-service flow | Validasi antar microservice | Order → payment → provisioning |
| Runtime config check | Config env benar | Secret, URL, timeout, feature flag |
| Observability check | Log/metric/tracing muncul | Correlation ID terlihat |

E2E test harus sedikit. Kalau terlalu banyak, biasanya menjadi lambat, flaky, dan mahal dirawat.

---

## 5.7 Benchmark Test Layer

| Skill | Fokus | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| JMH microbenchmark | Hot-path Java code | JSON mapping, validation, rule evaluation |
| Allocation benchmark | Object allocation | Mapper membuat terlalu banyak object |
| Serialization benchmark | JSON/XML payload | Jackson vs JSON-B config |
| Query benchmark | SQL latency | Search case dengan filter kompleks |
| Regression benchmark | Membandingkan baseline | PR menyebabkan latency naik 30% |
| Load/perf smoke | Endpoint-level performance | k6/Gatling/JMeter smoke |

Benchmark harus dipisah dari correctness test. Jangan jalankan semua benchmark di setiap PR kecuali benchmark-nya sangat kecil dan stabil.

---

# 6. Practical Decision Tree

Gunakan decision tree ini saat menentukan jenis test.

```text
Apakah behavior bisa diuji tanpa framework, DB, HTTP, Docker?
│
├── Ya
│   └── Unit Test
│
└── Tidak
    │
    ├── Apakah yang ingin diuji adalah HTTP/JAX-RS behavior?
    │   └── Resource Test / Jersey Test
    │
    ├── Apakah yang ingin diuji adalah SQL, transaction, DB constraint?
    │   └── Integration Test with PostgreSQL/Testcontainers
    │
    ├── Apakah yang ingin diuji adalah external API behavior?
    │   └── Integration Test with Stub Server
    │
    ├── Apakah yang ingin diuji adalah workflow/process?
    │   └── Camunda Workflow Integration Test
    │
    ├── Apakah yang ingin diuji adalah compatibility dengan consumer?
    │   └── Contract Test
    │
    ├── Apakah yang ingin diuji adalah deployed critical flow?
    │   └── E2E / Smoke Test
    │
    └── Apakah yang ingin diuji adalah speed/throughput/allocation?
        └── Benchmark Test
```

---

# 7. Practical Example: Case Submission Feature

Misalnya ada endpoint:

```http
POST /cases/{caseId}/submit
```

Behavior:

```text
- Case harus dalam status DRAFT
- Dokumen wajib harus lengkap
- User harus punya permission submit
- Status berubah menjadi SUBMITTED
- Audit log ditulis
- Workflow Camunda dilanjutkan
- Jika request dikirim dua kali, tidak boleh double submit
```

## Test breakdown yang sehat

| Behavior | Test layer | Kenapa |
|---|---|---|
| DRAFT boleh submit | Unit test | Pure business rule |
| APPROVED tidak boleh submit | Unit test | State transition rule |
| Missing document gagal | Unit test | Validation/rule |
| Unauthorized user return 403 | Resource test | HTTP/auth mapping |
| Invalid case ID return 404 | Resource/resource+service test | API behavior |
| Status update tersimpan ke DB | Integration test | DB/MyBatis nyata |
| Audit log rollback jika update gagal | Integration test | Transaction nyata |
| Duplicate submit tidak double process | Integration/concurrency test | Race/constraint nyata |
| Camunda signal/message dikirim | Workflow integration test | Process behavior |
| Endpoint response body benar | Resource/integration test | Contract |
| Submit throughput cukup cepat | Benchmark/performance test | Performance evidence |

---

# 9. Maven Execution Model

## 9.1 Unit Test

```bash
mvn test
```

Target:

```text
Cepat.
Tidak butuh Docker.
Tidak butuh PostgreSQL.
Tidak butuh external service.
Cocok jalan setiap save/commit/PR.
```

Naming:

```text
*Test.java
```

Contoh:

```text
CaseSubmissionServiceTest
CaseStateTransitionTest
CaseMapperTest
CaseValidatorTest
```

---

## 9.2 Integration Test

```bash
mvn verify
```

Target:

```text
Boleh butuh Docker.
Boleh start PostgreSQL container.
Boleh start stub external API.
Lebih lambat dari unit test.
Wajib untuk PR penting.
```

Naming:

```text
*IT.java
```

Contoh:

```text
CaseMapperIT
CaseResourceIT
CaseTransactionIT
PaymentGatewayClientIT
CaseWorkflowIT
```

Maven Failsafe memang digunakan untuk integration test di fase `integration-test` dan `verify`; dokumentasinya juga menekankan menjalankan integration test dengan `mvn verify`. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-failsafe-plugin/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9.3 Benchmark Test

```bash
mvn -Pbenchmark clean verify
```

Target:

```text
Tidak otomatis selalu jalan di setiap PR.
Dipakai saat ada perubahan hot path.
Dipakai untuk regression/performance investigation.
Hasil dibandingkan dengan baseline.
```

Naming:

```text
*Benchmark.java
```

Contoh:

```text
CaseMapperBenchmark
JsonSerializationBenchmark
CaseSearchBenchmark
```

---

# 10. Practical Test Tagging Strategy

Gunakan tagging agar CI bisa selektif.

```java
@Tag("unit")
class CaseStateTransitionTest {
}
```

```java
@Tag("integration")
class CaseMapperIT {
}
```

```java
@Tag("db")
class CaseRepositoryIT {
}
```

```java
@Tag("workflow")
class CaseWorkflowIT {
}
```

```java
@Tag("benchmark")
class JsonSerializationBenchmark {
}
```

Rekomendasi tag:

| Tag | Makna |
|---|---|
| `unit` | Test cepat tanpa dependency eksternal |
| `resource` | Jersey/JAX-RS resource/component test |
| `integration` | Test dengan dependency nyata/stub |
| `db` | Test yang butuh PostgreSQL |
| `workflow` | Test terkait Camunda |
| `external` | Test HTTP client/external system stub |
| `slow` | Test yang relatif lambat |
| `benchmark` | Benchmark/performance test |

---

# 11. Practical Rule: Apa yang Jangan Dilakukan

## 11.1 Jangan mock database untuk mengetes SQL

Buruk:

```text
Mock MyBatis mapper
Lalu assert mapper dipanggil dengan parameter tertentu
```

Itu tidak membuktikan SQL benar.

Lebih baik:

```text
Run MyBatis mapper terhadap PostgreSQL container
Seed data kecil
Call mapper
Assert result
```

---

## 11.2 Jangan pakai H2 untuk PostgreSQL behavior kompleks

Untuk aplikasi dengan PostgreSQL, PL/pgSQL, JSONB, locking, indexing, timestamp, function, dan constraint, H2 sering memberi rasa aman palsu.

Lebih baik:

```text
Testcontainers PostgreSQL
Schema migration yang sama dengan production
Data fixture kecil dan eksplisit
```

---

## 11.3 Jangan jadikan semua test sebagai integration test

Kalau business rule bisa diuji tanpa HTTP dan DB, jangan naikkan ke integration test.

Buruk:

```text
Untuk mengetes status transition, start Jersey + DB + container.
```

Lebih baik:

```text
CaseStateTransitionTest sebagai unit test.
Satu atau dua integration test untuk membuktikan wiring end-to-end.
```

---

## 11.4 Jangan benchmark dengan `System.currentTimeMillis()`

Buruk:

```java
long start = System.currentTimeMillis();
service.doSomething();
long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - start;
```

Masalah:

```text
JIT warm-up tidak dikontrol.
GC tidak dikontrol.
Dead-code elimination bisa terjadi.
Noise tinggi.
Hasil sering misleading.
```

Gunakan JMH untuk microbenchmark.

---

# 12. Practical Checklist untuk PR

Sebelum merge PR, tanya:

```text
[ ] Apakah business rule baru punya unit test?
[ ] Apakah error path penting sudah dites?
[ ] Apakah API behavior berubah? Jika ya, resource/contract test diperbarui?
[ ] Apakah SQL/MyBatis berubah? Jika ya, integration test dengan PostgreSQL ada?
[ ] Apakah transaction boundary berubah? Jika ya, rollback/commit test ada?
[ ] Apakah workflow Camunda berubah? Jika ya, process integration test ada?
[ ] Apakah external API behavior berubah? Jika ya, stub integration test ada?
[ ] Apakah hot path performa berubah? Jika ya, benchmark/perf test dipertimbangkan?
[ ] Apakah test bisa jalan lokal dengan command jelas?
[ ] Apakah test tidak bergantung pada urutan eksekusi?
```

---

# 13. Practical Boundary Examples

## Example A - Rule only

Requirement:

```text
Case tidak boleh disubmit jika mandatory document belum lengkap.
```

Test layer:

```text
Unit test
```

Kenapa:

```text
Tidak butuh HTTP.
Tidak butuh DB.
Tidak butuh Camunda.
Ini business rule.
```

---

## Example B - HTTP error response

Requirement:

```text
Jika document belum lengkap, endpoint return 409 dengan error code CASE_DOCUMENT_INCOMPLETE.
```

Test layer:

```text
Resource test
```

Kenapa:

```text
Yang diuji adalah mapping exception/domain failure ke HTTP response.
```

---

## Example C - SQL query

Requirement:

```text
Search case by status, assignee, date range, and tenant.
```

Test layer:

```text
Integration test with PostgreSQL
```

Kenapa:

```text
Yang diuji adalah SQL correctness, index usage, timezone, dan result mapping.
```

---

## Example D - Double submit

Requirement:

```text
Dua request submit bersamaan tidak boleh membuat dua audit log atau dua workflow signal.
```

Test layer:

```text
Integration/concurrency test
```

Kenapa:

```text
Unit test tidak cukup karena race condition muncul di DB transaction, lock, atau unique constraint.
```

---

## Example E - JSON mapping performance

Requirement:

```text
Endpoint menerima payload besar dan mapping mulai terasa lambat.
```

Test layer:

```text
JMH benchmark atau endpoint-level performance test
```

Kenapa:

```text
Yang diuji bukan correctness saja, tetapi latency, throughput, dan allocation.
```

---

# 14. Suggested Baseline Policy

Untuk microservice enterprise, baseline yang masuk akal:

```text
Every PR:
- Unit tests
- Resource/component tests
- Selected integration tests
- Static checks
- Coverage report

Every merge to main:
- Full integration tests
- DB migration tests
- Workflow tests

Nightly / scheduled:
- Full regression
- Contract tests
- Longer performance smoke
- Selected benchmark comparison

Before release:
- E2E smoke
- Backward compatibility check
- Migration dry-run
- Performance sanity check
```

---

# 15. Minimal Standard per Change Type

| Change type | Minimal test yang wajib |
|---|---|
| Business rule berubah | Unit test + negative case |
| DTO/API contract berubah | Resource test + contract/schema test |
| Exception behavior berubah | ExceptionMapper/resource test |
| MyBatis SQL berubah | PostgreSQL integration test |
| DB schema berubah | Migration + repository integration test |
| Transaction flow berubah | Commit/rollback integration test |
| Camunda BPMN berubah | Workflow integration test |
| External API client berubah | Stubbed integration test |
| Retry/idempotency berubah | Integration + failure path test |
| Performance-sensitive code berubah | Benchmark/performance smoke |

---

# 16. Ringkasan Seri 1

Inti dari seri ini:

```text
Testing strategy yang baik bukan berarti semua hal dites di semua layer.
Testing strategy yang baik berarti setiap behavior dites di boundary termurah yang masih membuktikan risiko nyata.
```

Prinsip praktis:

```text
Unit test untuk logic.
Resource test untuk HTTP/JAX-RS behavior.
Integration test untuk DB, transaction, workflow, external boundary.
Contract test untuk compatibility.
E2E test untuk critical deployed path.
Benchmark test untuk performance evidence.
```

Untuk stack Anda, kombinasi paling penting adalah:

```text
JUnit Platform/Jupiter
Mockito/Fake/Stub
Jersey Test Framework
Maven Surefire
Maven Failsafe
Testcontainers PostgreSQL
MyBatis integration test
Camunda workflow test
JMH benchmark
GitHub Actions pipeline
```

# Seri 2/14 - JUnit Jupiter Practical Unit Testing  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus ke **unit test praktis** untuk aplikasi Java microservice berbasis **Jersey/JAX-RS**.

Targetnya bukan sekadar tahu annotation JUnit, tetapi bisa membuat unit test yang:

```text
- cepat dijalankan
- mudah dibaca
- tidak bergantung ke DB/HTTP/Docker
- tidak over-mocking
- kuat untuk business rule, state transition, validation, mapper, dan error path
- cocok untuk service layer di Java 17+
```

JUnit Jupiter adalah programming model dan extension model untuk menulis test modern di JUnit Platform. JUnit 6 saat ini merupakan generasi terbaru dan membutuhkan Java 17+, tetapi konsep Jupiter tetap menjadi fondasi utama untuk style test modern di JVM. ([JUnit](https://docs.junit.org/6.1.0/overview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Posisi Unit Test dalam Microservice

Unit test menjawab pertanyaan:

```text
Apakah logic internal benar ketika dependency luar dikontrol?
```

Unit test **tidak** bertugas membuktikan:

```text
- apakah endpoint HTTP terdaftar dengan benar
- apakah JSON provider Jersey berjalan benar
- apakah SQL MyBatis valid di PostgreSQL
- apakah transaksi commit/rollback benar
- apakah Docker/Testcontainers berjalan
- apakah Camunda engine benar-benar mengeksekusi BPMN
```

Itu semua masuk ke resource/integration/workflow test.

## Unit test cocok untuk

```text
Business rule
State transition
Validation decision
Calculation
Mapper
Policy object
Retry decision
Idempotency decision
Error classification
Permission decision
SLA calculation
```

---

# 3. Struktur Minimal Unit Test

Contoh sederhana:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class CaseStatePolicyTest {

    @Test
    void shouldAllowSubmitWhenCaseIsDraftAndDocumentsAreComplete() {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = CaseRecord.builder()
                .status(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
                .mandatoryDocumentsComplete(true)
                .build();

        boolean allowed = CaseStatePolicy.canSubmit(caseRecord);

        assertThat(allowed).isTrue();
    }
}
```

Format yang disarankan:

```text
Arrange → Act → Assert
```

Atau dalam bahasa praktis:

```text
Given data awal
When behavior dijalankan
Then hasil diverifikasi
```

---

# 4. Naming Convention yang Direkomendasikan

## Nama class

```text
<ClassUnderTest>Test
```

Contoh:

```text
CaseSubmissionServiceTest
CaseStatePolicyTest
CaseMapperTest
SlaCalculatorTest
PaymentRetryPolicyTest
```

## Nama method

Gunakan nama yang menjelaskan behavior, bukan implementasi internal.

Baik:

```java
@Test
void shouldRejectSubmitWhenMandatoryDocumentsAreMissing() {
}
```

Kurang baik:

```java
@Test
void testSubmit1() {
}
```

Baik:

```java
@Test
void shouldCalculateDueDateUsingBusinessDaysOnly() {
}
```

Kurang baik:

```java
@Test
void testCalculateDueDate() {
}
```

Prinsipnya:

```text
Nama test = mini specification
```

---

# 5. Practical Example Domain

Misalnya kita punya domain sederhana:

```java
public enum CaseStatus {
    DRAFT,
    SUBMITTED,
    UNDER_REVIEW,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED,
    CANCELLED
}
```

```java
public record CaseRecord(
        String id,
        CaseStatus status,
        boolean mandatoryDocumentsComplete,
        String submittedBy
) {
}
```

Policy:

```java
public final class CaseStatePolicy {

    private CaseStatePolicy() {
    }

    public static boolean canSubmit(CaseRecord caseRecord) {
        return caseRecord.status() == CaseStatus.DRAFT
                && caseRecord.mandatoryDocumentsComplete();
    }
}
```

Unit test:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class CaseStatePolicyTest {

    @Test
    void shouldAllowSubmitWhenCaseIsDraftAndDocumentsAreComplete() {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.DRAFT,
                true,
                null
        );

        boolean result = CaseStatePolicy.canSubmit(caseRecord);

        assertThat(result).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectSubmitWhenCaseIsNotDraft() {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.APPROVED,
                true,
                null
        );

        boolean result = CaseStatePolicy.canSubmit(caseRecord);

        assertThat(result).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectSubmitWhenMandatoryDocumentsAreMissing() {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.DRAFT,
                false,
                null
        );

        boolean result = CaseStatePolicy.canSubmit(caseRecord);

        assertThat(result).isFalse();
    }
}
```

AssertJ banyak dipakai karena menyediakan fluent assertions yang readable dan error message yang lebih informatif dibanding assertion sederhana. ([AssertJ](https://assertj.github.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 6. Parameterized Test untuk Rule Matrix

Kalau rule punya banyak kombinasi, jangan copy-paste banyak test.

Contoh buruk:

```java
@Test
void shouldRejectApprovedCase() {
}

@Test
void shouldRejectRejectedCase() {
}

@Test
void shouldRejectCancelledCase() {
}
```

Lebih baik gunakan parameterized test.

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.CsvSource;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class CaseStatePolicyParameterizedTest {

    @ParameterizedTest
    @CsvSource({
            "DRAFT, true, true",
            "DRAFT, false, false",
            "SUBMITTED, true, false",
            "UNDER_REVIEW, true, false",
            "APPROVED, true, false",
            "REJECTED, true, false",
            "CANCELLED, true, false"
    })
    void shouldEvaluateSubmitEligibility(
            CaseStatus status,
            boolean mandatoryDocumentsComplete,
            boolean expected
    ) {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                status,
                mandatoryDocumentsComplete,
                null
        );

        boolean result = CaseStatePolicy.canSubmit(caseRecord);

        assertThat(result).isEqualTo(expected);
    }
}
```

Gunakan parameterized test untuk:

```text
- state transition matrix
- validation rule matrix
- permission matrix
- retryable/non-retryable error matrix
- enum mapping
- boundary value
- date/time scenarios
```

---

# 7. Testing Exception Path

Service/domain logic enterprise sering lebih penting di error path daripada happy path.

Contoh exception:

```java
public class InvalidCaseStateException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String caseId;
    private final CaseStatus currentStatus;

    public InvalidCaseStateException(String caseId, CaseStatus currentStatus) {
        super("Case " + caseId + " cannot be submitted from status " + currentStatus);
        this.caseId = caseId;
        this.currentStatus = currentStatus;
    }

    public String caseId() {
        return caseId;
    }

    public CaseStatus currentStatus() {
        return currentStatus;
    }
}
```

Service:

```java
public class CaseSubmissionValidator {

    public void validateCanSubmit(CaseRecord caseRecord) {
        if (!CaseStatePolicy.canSubmit(caseRecord)) {
            throw new InvalidCaseStateException(
                    caseRecord.id(),
                    caseRecord.status()
            );
        }
    }
}
```

Test:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

class CaseSubmissionValidatorTest {

    private final CaseSubmissionValidator validator = new CaseSubmissionValidator();

    @Test
    void shouldThrowInvalidCaseStateExceptionWhenCaseCannotBeSubmitted() {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.APPROVED,
                true,
                null
        );

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> validator.validateCanSubmit(caseRecord))
                .isInstanceOf(InvalidCaseStateException.class)
                .hasMessageContaining("CASE-001")
                .hasMessageContaining("APPROVED");
    }
}
```

Praktik penting:

```text
Jangan hanya assert exception type.
Assert juga informasi error yang penting:
- error code
- case id
- current status
- reason
```

---

# 8. Fixture Builder

Masalah umum unit test enterprise: setup object terlalu panjang.

Buruk:

```java
CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
        "CASE-001",
        CaseStatus.DRAFT,
        true,
        "user-123"
);
```

Kalau object makin besar, test makin noise.

Lebih baik buat fixture builder khusus test.

```java
public final class CaseRecordFixture {

    private String id = "CASE-001";
    private CaseStatus status = CaseStatus.DRAFT;
    private boolean mandatoryDocumentsComplete = true;
    private String submittedBy = null;

    private CaseRecordFixture() {
    }

    public static CaseRecordFixture aCase() {
        return new CaseRecordFixture();
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withId(String id) {
        this.id = id;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withStatus(CaseStatus status) {
        this.status = status;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withMissingMandatoryDocuments() {
        this.mandatoryDocumentsComplete = false;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withCompleteMandatoryDocuments() {
        this.mandatoryDocumentsComplete = true;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecord build() {
        return new CaseRecord(
                id,
                status,
                mandatoryDocumentsComplete,
                submittedBy
        );
    }
}
```

Pemakaian:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static com.company.caseapp.testfixture.CaseRecordFixture.aCase;

class CaseStatePolicyFixtureTest {

    @Test
    void shouldRejectSubmitWhenMandatoryDocumentsAreMissing() {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
                .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
                .withMissingMandatoryDocuments()
                .build();

        boolean result = CaseStatePolicy.canSubmit(caseRecord);

        assertThat(result).isFalse();
    }
}
```

Keuntungan:

```text
- test lebih fokus ke behavior
- default valid object bisa dipakai ulang
- perubahan constructor tidak merusak semua test
- test data lebih expressive
```

---

# 9. Unit Test untuk Service Layer dengan Mock

Sekarang contoh service yang punya dependency repository dan workflow gateway.

```java
public interface CaseRepository {
    Optional<CaseRecord> findById(String caseId);

    void markSubmitted(String caseId, String submittedBy);
}
```

```java
public interface WorkflowGateway {
    void signalCaseSubmitted(String caseId);
}
```

```java
public class CaseSubmissionService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final WorkflowGateway workflowGateway;

    public CaseSubmissionService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            WorkflowGateway workflowGateway
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.workflowGateway = workflowGateway;
    }

    public void submit(String caseId, String submittedBy) {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findById(caseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new CaseNotFoundException(caseId));

        if (!CaseStatePolicy.canSubmit(caseRecord)) {
            throw new InvalidCaseStateException(caseId, caseRecord.status());
        }

        caseRepository.markSubmitted(caseId, submittedBy);
        workflowGateway.signalCaseSubmitted(caseId);
    }
}
```

Test dengan Mockito Jupiter extension:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import java.util.Optional;

import static com.company.caseapp.testfixture.CaseRecordFixture.aCase;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class CaseSubmissionServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private CaseRepository caseRepository;

    @Mock
    private WorkflowGateway workflowGateway;

    @InjectMocks
    private CaseSubmissionService service;

    @Test
    void shouldSubmitCaseAndSignalWorkflow() {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
                .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
                .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
                .build();

        when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
                .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

        service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

        verify(caseRepository).markSubmitted("CASE-001", "user-123");
        verify(workflowGateway).signalCaseSubmitted("CASE-001");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldNotUpdateCaseOrSignalWorkflowWhenCaseIsNotFound() {
        when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-404"))
                .thenReturn(Optional.empty());

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit("CASE-404", "user-123"))
                .isInstanceOf(CaseNotFoundException.class);

        verify(caseRepository, never()).markSubmitted(anyString(), anyString());
        verifyNoInteractions(workflowGateway);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldNotSignalWorkflowWhenCaseCannotBeSubmitted() {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
                .withStatus(CaseStatus.APPROVED)
                .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
                .build();

        when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
                .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123"))
                .isInstanceOf(InvalidCaseStateException.class);

        verify(caseRepository, never()).markSubmitted(anyString(), anyString());
        verifyNoInteractions(workflowGateway);
    }
}
```

Mockito menyediakan integrasi JUnit Jupiter melalui `mockito-junit-jupiter`, dan `MockitoExtension` adalah extension untuk menginisialisasi mock di JUnit Jupiter. ([JavaDoc](https://javadoc.io/doc/org.mockito/mockito-junit-jupiter/3.3.3/org/mockito/junit/jupiter/MockitoExtension.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 10. Mocking Discipline

## Kapan pakai mock?

Gunakan mock untuk dependency yang:

```text
- lambat
- eksternal
- nondeterministik
- memiliki side effect
- sulit dikontrol
```

Contoh dependency yang boleh dimock di unit test:

```text
Repository
HTTP client
Workflow gateway
Message publisher
Clock wrapper
ID generator
File storage gateway
Email/SMS gateway
```

## Jangan mock ini

```text
DTO
Record
Entity sederhana
Value object
Enum
Business rule class
Mapper sederhana
Collection
String/date primitive wrapper
```

Contoh buruk:

```java
CaseRecord caseRecord = mock(CaseRecord.class);
when(caseRecord.status()).thenReturn(CaseStatus.DRAFT);
```

Lebih baik:

```java
CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
        .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
        .build();
```

Mock value object membuat test rapuh dan tidak natural.

---

# 11. Testing dengan Fake daripada Mock

Kadang fake lebih baik daripada mock.

Contoh fake repository:

```java
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

public class FakeCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final Map<String, CaseRecord> cases = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, String> submittedBy = new HashMap<>();

    public void save(CaseRecord caseRecord) {
        cases.put(caseRecord.id(), caseRecord);
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CaseRecord> findById(String caseId) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(cases.get(caseId));
    }

    @Override
    public void markSubmitted(String caseId, String submittedByUser) {
        submittedBy.put(caseId, submittedByUser);
    }

    public boolean isSubmittedBy(String caseId, String userId) {
        return userId.equals(submittedBy.get(caseId));
    }
}
```

Test:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static com.company.caseapp.testfixture.CaseRecordFixture.aCase;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class CaseSubmissionServiceWithFakeTest {

    private final FakeCaseRepository caseRepository = new FakeCaseRepository();
    private final RecordingWorkflowGateway workflowGateway = new RecordingWorkflowGateway();

    private final CaseSubmissionService service =
            new CaseSubmissionService(caseRepository, workflowGateway);

    @Test
    void shouldSubmitCase() {
        caseRepository.save(aCase()
                .withId("CASE-001")
                .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
                .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
                .build());

        service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

        assertThat(caseRepository.isSubmittedBy("CASE-001", "user-123"))
                .isTrue();

        assertThat(workflowGateway.signalledCaseIds())
                .containsExactly("CASE-001");
    }
}
```

Recording fake gateway:

```java
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class RecordingWorkflowGateway implements WorkflowGateway {

    private final List<String> signalledCaseIds = new ArrayList<>();

    @Override
    public void signalCaseSubmitted(String caseId) {
        signalledCaseIds.add(caseId);
    }

    public List<String> signalledCaseIds() {
        return List.copyOf(signalledCaseIds);
    }
}
```

Fake cocok ketika:

```text
- banyak interaction membuat Mockito test noisy
- ingin test behavior, bukan sekadar verify method dipanggil
- dependency punya state sederhana
```

---

# 12. Testing Date/Time dengan Clock

Jangan panggil `LocalDate.now()` langsung di business logic.

Buruk:

```java
public LocalDate calculateDueDate(int slaDays) {
    return LocalDate.now().plusDays(slaDays);
}
```

Lebih baik:

```java
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.ZoneId;

public class SlaCalculator {

    private final Clock clock;

    public SlaCalculator(Clock clock) {
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public LocalDate calculateDueDate(int slaDays) {
        return LocalDate.now(clock).plusDays(slaDays);
    }
}
```

Test:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.ZoneId;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class SlaCalculatorTest {

    @Test
    void shouldCalculateDueDateFromFixedCurrentDate() {
        Clock fixedClock = Clock.fixed(
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T00:00:00Z"),
                ZoneId.of("UTC")
        );

        SlaCalculator calculator = new SlaCalculator(fixedClock);

        LocalDate dueDate = calculator.calculateDueDate(5);

        assertThat(dueDate).isEqualTo(LocalDate.of(2026, 7, 20));
    }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text
Waktu adalah dependency.
Kalau tidak dikontrol, test mudah flaky.
```

---

# 13. Testing ID Generator

Jangan generate UUID random langsung kalau hasilnya perlu diverifikasi.

Buruk:

```java
String caseId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
```

Lebih baik buat interface:

```java
public interface CaseIdGenerator {
    String nextId();
}
```

Production implementation:

```java
import java.util.UUID;

public class UuidCaseIdGenerator implements CaseIdGenerator {

    @Override
    public String nextId() {
        return UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    }
}
```

Test implementation:

```java
public class FixedCaseIdGenerator implements CaseIdGenerator {

    private final String fixedId;

    public FixedCaseIdGenerator(String fixedId) {
        this.fixedId = fixedId;
    }

    @Override
    public String nextId() {
        return fixedId;
    }
}
```

Test menjadi deterministic:

```java
@Test
void shouldCreateCaseWithGeneratedId() {
    CaseIdGenerator idGenerator = new FixedCaseIdGenerator("CASE-001");

    CaseCreator creator = new CaseCreator(idGenerator);

    CaseRecord result = creator.createDraftCase();

    assertThat(result.id()).isEqualTo("CASE-001");
}
```

---

# 14. Unit Test untuk Mapper

Misalnya request DTO:

```java
public record SubmitCaseRequest(
        String submittedBy,
        String comment
) {
}
```

Command:

```java
public record SubmitCaseCommand(
        String caseId,
        String submittedBy,
        String comment
) {
}
```

Mapper:

```java
public class SubmitCaseCommandMapper {

    public SubmitCaseCommand toCommand(
            String caseId,
            SubmitCaseRequest request
    ) {
        return new SubmitCaseCommand(
                caseId,
                request.submittedBy(),
                request.comment()
        );
    }
}
```

Test:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class SubmitCaseCommandMapperTest {

    private final SubmitCaseCommandMapper mapper =
            new SubmitCaseCommandMapper();

    @Test
    void shouldMapRequestToCommand() {
        SubmitCaseRequest request = new SubmitCaseRequest(
                "user-123",
                "Ready for review"
        );

        SubmitCaseCommand command =
                mapper.toCommand("CASE-001", request);

        assertThat(command.caseId()).isEqualTo("CASE-001");
        assertThat(command.submittedBy()).isEqualTo("user-123");
        assertThat(command.comment()).isEqualTo("Ready for review");
    }
}
```

Untuk mapper, test yang penting:

```text
- mandatory field tidak hilang
- enum mapping benar
- date/time format/zone benar
- nested object benar
- null handling jelas
- default value benar
```

---

# 15. Unit Test untuk Validation Policy

Contoh policy:

```java
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class SubmitCasePolicy {

    public List<String> validate(CaseRecord caseRecord) {
        List<String> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        if (caseRecord.status() != CaseStatus.DRAFT) {
            errors.add("CASE_NOT_IN_DRAFT");
        }

        if (!caseRecord.mandatoryDocumentsComplete()) {
            errors.add("MANDATORY_DOCUMENTS_MISSING");
        }

        return errors;
    }
}
```

Test:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.List;

import static com.company.caseapp.testfixture.CaseRecordFixture.aCase;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class SubmitCasePolicyTest {

    private final SubmitCasePolicy policy = new SubmitCasePolicy();

    @Test
    void shouldReturnNoErrorsWhenCaseCanBeSubmitted() {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
                .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
                .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
                .build();

        List<String> errors = policy.validate(caseRecord);

        assertThat(errors).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void shouldReturnAllValidationErrors() {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
                .withStatus(CaseStatus.APPROVED)
                .withMissingMandatoryDocuments()
                .build();

        List<String> errors = policy.validate(caseRecord);

        assertThat(errors)
                .containsExactly(
                        "CASE_NOT_IN_DRAFT",
                        "MANDATORY_DOCUMENTS_MISSING"
                );
    }
}
```

Prinsip penting:

```text
Kalau policy bisa menghasilkan banyak error, test harus memastikan semua error penting muncul,
bukan hanya error pertama.
```

---

# 16. Unit Test untuk State Transition Matrix

Contoh transition policy:

```java
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

public class CaseTransitionPolicy {

    private static final Map<CaseStatus, Set<CaseStatus>> ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS =
            Map.of(
                    CaseStatus.DRAFT, Set.of(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED, CaseStatus.CANCELLED),
                    CaseStatus.SUBMITTED, Set.of(CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW, CaseStatus.CANCELLED),
                    CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW, Set.of(CaseStatus.APPROVED, CaseStatus.REJECTED),
                    CaseStatus.APPROVED, Set.of(),
                    CaseStatus.REJECTED, Set.of(),
                    CaseStatus.CANCELLED, Set.of()
            );

    public boolean canTransition(CaseStatus from, CaseStatus to) {
        return ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS
                .getOrDefault(from, Set.of())
                .contains(to);
    }
}
```

Parameterized test:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.CsvSource;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class CaseTransitionPolicyTest {

    private final CaseTransitionPolicy policy = new CaseTransitionPolicy();

    @ParameterizedTest
    @CsvSource({
            "DRAFT, SUBMITTED, true",
            "DRAFT, CANCELLED, true",
            "DRAFT, APPROVED, false",
            "SUBMITTED, UNDER_REVIEW, true",
            "SUBMITTED, APPROVED, false",
            "UNDER_REVIEW, APPROVED, true",
            "UNDER_REVIEW, REJECTED, true",
            "APPROVED, REJECTED, false",
            "REJECTED, APPROVED, false",
            "CANCELLED, SUBMITTED, false"
    })
    void shouldEvaluateCaseStatusTransition(
            CaseStatus from,
            CaseStatus to,
            boolean expected
    ) {
        boolean result = policy.canTransition(from, to);

        assertThat(result).isEqualTo(expected);
    }
}
```

Ini sangat relevan untuk sistem:

```text
- enforcement lifecycle
- case management
- order lifecycle
- payment lifecycle
- approval workflow
- regulatory workflow
```

---

# 17. Anti-Pattern dalam Unit Test

## 17.1 Test terlalu tahu implementasi

Buruk:

```java
verify(repository).findById("CASE-001");
verify(repository).markSubmitted("CASE-001", "user-123");
verify(workflowGateway).signalCaseSubmitted("CASE-001");
```

Tidak selalu buruk, tapi hati-hati: kalau semua test hanya verify call sequence, test mudah pecah saat refactor.

Lebih baik jika memungkinkan:

```text
Assert behavior/output/state.
Gunakan verify hanya untuk side effect penting.
```

---

## 17.2 Mock semua hal

Buruk:

```text
Mock DTO
Mock entity
Mock List
Mock mapper sederhana
Mock value object
```

Efeknya:

```text
Test tidak lagi merepresentasikan cara object dipakai nyata.
```

---

## 17.3 Satu test menguji terlalu banyak hal

Buruk:

```java
@Test
void shouldCreateSubmitApproveAndRejectCase() {
}
```

Lebih baik pisahkan:

```text
shouldCreateDraftCase
shouldSubmitDraftCase
shouldApproveCaseUnderReview
shouldRejectCaseUnderReview
```

---

## 17.4 Assertion terlalu lemah

Buruk:

```java
assertThat(result).isNotNull();
```

Lebih baik:

```java
assertThat(result.status()).isEqualTo(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED);
assertThat(result.submittedBy()).isEqualTo("user-123");
```

---

## 17.5 Test tanpa negative case

Happy path saja tidak cukup.

Minimal untuk rule penting:

```text
1 happy path
1 invalid input
1 invalid state
1 boundary case
1 permission/error case jika relevan
```

---

# 18. Unit Test Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini saat membuat unit test baru:

```text
[ ] Nama test menjelaskan behavior.
[ ] Test mengikuti Arrange-Act-Assert.
[ ] Tidak membutuhkan DB, HTTP, Docker, filesystem nyata, atau network.
[ ] Dependency eksternal dimock/fake.
[ ] Value object/DTO/entity sederhana tidak dimock.
[ ] Ada happy path.
[ ] Ada negative/error path.
[ ] Boundary value diuji.
[ ] Time/random/ID dikontrol.
[ ] Assertion spesifik, bukan sekadar not null.
[ ] Test failure message mudah dipahami.
[ ] Fixture tidak terlalu noisy.
[ ] Tidak bergantung pada urutan test lain.
```

---

# 19. Practical Maven Dependencies

Contoh dependency umum untuk unit test:

```xml
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.junit</groupId>
        <artifactId>junit-bom</artifactId>
        <version>${junit.version}</version>
        <type>pom</type>
        <scope>import</scope>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.junit.jupiter</groupId>
        <artifactId>junit-jupiter</artifactId>
        <scope>test</scope>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.mockito</groupId>
        <artifactId>mockito-junit-jupiter</artifactId>
        <scope>test</scope>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.assertj</groupId>
        <artifactId>assertj-core</artifactId>
        <scope>test</scope>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

Catatan praktis:

```text
Gunakan version management dari BOM atau parent POM internal perusahaan.
Jangan campur banyak assertion library tanpa alasan.
```

---

# 20. Minimal Maven Surefire Config

```xml
<build>
    <plugins>
        <plugin>
            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
            <artifactId>maven-surefire-plugin</artifactId>
            <version>${maven-surefire-plugin.version}</version>
            <configuration>
                <includes>
                    <include>**/*Test.java</include>
                </includes>
            </configuration>
        </plugin>
    </plugins>
</build>
```

Surefire mendukung JUnit Platform dengan TestEngine yang sesuai tersedia di test classpath. ([JUnit](https://docs.junit.org/6.1.0/overview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 21. Ringkasan Praktis

Unit test yang baik di Java microservice adalah test yang membuktikan behavior kecil dengan cepat dan stabil.

Pegangan utama:

```text
Test behavior, bukan private implementation.
Mock dependency eksternal, bukan value object.
Gunakan fixture builder untuk mengurangi noise.
Gunakan parameterized test untuk matrix rule.
Kontrol time, random, dan ID.
Assert hasil spesifik.
Selalu test error path.
```

Untuk stack Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL:

```text
Unit test:
- service decision
- domain policy
- mapper
- validator
- state transition
- retry/idempotency decision

Bukan unit test:
- SQL correctness
- HTTP routing
- JSON provider behavior
- transaction rollback
- PostgreSQL constraint
- Camunda process execution
```

---

```text
Seri 2/14 selesai:
JUnit Jupiter Practical Unit Testing
```

## Rekomendasi berikutnya
