# Seri 3/14 - Mockito, Fake, Stub, Spy  
## Kapan Dipakai dan Kapan Dihindari

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada **test double strategy** dalam unit test Java microservice.

Pertanyaan utamanya:

> “Saat unit test service/domain logic, dependency mana yang harus dimock, distub, dibuat fake, dibuat spy, atau justru tidak perlu diganti sama sekali?”

Ini penting karena banyak unit test menjadi rapuh bukan karena JUnit-nya salah, tetapi karena penggunaan mock yang berlebihan.

```text id="vk692l"
Over-mocking symptoms:
- test banyak verify method call
- test pecah saat refactor internal
- test hijau tapi behavior nyata salah
- setup when(...) lebih panjang daripada assertion
- value object/DTO/entity ikut dimock
- test sulit dibaca oleh engineer baru
```

Mockito adalah mocking framework populer untuk Java yang menyediakan mock creation, stubbing, dan verification API. Mockito juga menyediakan `MockitoExtension` untuk JUnit Jupiter, yang menginisialisasi mock dan menangani strict stubbing. ([javadoc.io](https://www.javadoc.io/doc/org.mockito/mockito-junit-jupiter/5.14.0/org/mockito/junit/jupiter/MockitoExtension.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model: Test Double

**Test double** adalah object pengganti dependency nyata di test.

```text id="cak3ei"
Class under test
   ↓ depends on
Dependency

Dalam unit test:
Dependency bisa diganti dengan:
- dummy
- stub
- mock
- fake
- spy
- captor
```

## Diagram

```text id="meifcy"
Test Double Strategy
│
├── Dummy
│   └── Object hanya untuk memenuhi parameter
│
├── Stub
│   └── Memberikan jawaban tetap untuk dependency
│
├── Mock
│   └── Memverifikasi interaksi / side effect
│
├── Fake
│   └── Implementasi sederhana tapi bekerja
│
├── Spy
│   └── Object nyata sebagian dimonitor/diubah behavior-nya
│
└── ArgumentCaptor
    └── Menangkap argument yang dikirim ke dependency
```

---

# 3. Perbedaan Praktis

| Jenis | Fungsi | Cocok untuk | Hindari untuk |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dummy | Object pengisi parameter, tidak dipakai | Constructor arg yang tidak relevan | Behavior penting |
| Stub | Mengembalikan data terkontrol | Repository find, config, clock, external response | Verifikasi side effect |
| Mock | Verifikasi interaksi | Message publisher, workflow gateway, notification sender | DTO, entity, value object |
| Fake | Implementasi ringan yang punya state | In-memory repository, recording gateway | Sistem kompleks seperti PostgreSQL penuh |
| Spy | Object nyata yang sebagian dimock | Legacy code, partial override sulit | Desain baru yang bisa dibuat testable |
| ArgumentCaptor | Menangkap argument untuk assertion | Event object, command object, audit object | Stubbing; Mockito sendiri memperingatkan captor lebih cocok untuk verification daripada stubbing. ([javadoc.io](https://www.javadoc.io/doc/org.mockito/mockito-core/5.13.0/org/mockito/ArgumentCaptor.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |

---

# 4. Rule of Thumb

```text id="uwtakl"
Mock behavior eksternal.
Jangan mock data.
```

## Boleh dimock

```text id="qbnf2o"
Repository
External API client
Message publisher
Workflow gateway
Email/SMS gateway
Storage gateway
Clock wrapper
ID generator
Feature flag provider
Authorization gateway
```

## Jangan dimock

```text id="u6ku0l"
DTO
Record
Entity sederhana
Value object
Enum
Collection
String
LocalDate
BigDecimal
Mapper sederhana
Business policy object yang pure
```

Contoh buruk:

```java id="0n7npf"
CaseRecord caseRecord = mock(CaseRecord.class);
when(caseRecord.status()).thenReturn(CaseStatus.DRAFT);
```

Lebih baik:

```java id="w0o06o"
CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
        "CASE-001",
        CaseStatus.DRAFT,
        true,
        null
);
```

Atau:

```java id="67lnk0"
CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
        .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
        .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
        .build();
```

---

# 5. Running Example

Misalnya service berikut:

```java id="8w7l1b"
public class CaseSubmissionService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final WorkflowGateway workflowGateway;
    private final AuditPublisher auditPublisher;
    private final Clock clock;

    public CaseSubmissionService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            WorkflowGateway workflowGateway,
            AuditPublisher auditPublisher,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.workflowGateway = workflowGateway;
        this.auditPublisher = auditPublisher;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public void submit(String caseId, String submittedBy) {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findById(caseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new CaseNotFoundException(caseId));

        if (!CaseStatePolicy.canSubmit(caseRecord)) {
            throw new InvalidCaseStateException(caseId, caseRecord.status());
        }

        Instant submittedAt = Instant.now(clock);

        caseRepository.markSubmitted(caseId, submittedBy, submittedAt);
        workflowGateway.signalCaseSubmitted(caseId);
        auditPublisher.publish(new AuditEvent(
                caseId,
                "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                submittedBy,
                submittedAt
        ));
    }
}
```

Dependency:

```java id="m1w31b"
public interface CaseRepository {
    Optional<CaseRecord> findById(String caseId);

    void markSubmitted(String caseId, String submittedBy, Instant submittedAt);
}
```

```java id="qboujb"
public interface WorkflowGateway {
    void signalCaseSubmitted(String caseId);
}
```

```java id="wfq060"
public interface AuditPublisher {
    void publish(AuditEvent event);
}
```

```java id="l0b4hw"
public record AuditEvent(
        String caseId,
        String action,
        String actor,
        Instant occurredAt
) {
}
```

---

# 6. Mockito Setup dengan JUnit Jupiter

Dependency test yang umum:

```xml id="k1q1wo"
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.mockito</groupId>
    <artifactId>mockito-junit-jupiter</artifactId>
    <scope>test</scope>
</dependency>
```

Setup test:

```java id="0n4dyh"
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.Optional;

import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class CaseSubmissionServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private CaseRepository caseRepository;

    @Mock
    private WorkflowGateway workflowGateway;

    @Mock
    private AuditPublisher auditPublisher;

    private final Clock fixedClock = Clock.fixed(
            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
            ZoneOffset.UTC
    );

    private CaseSubmissionService service;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        service = new CaseSubmissionService(
                caseRepository,
                workflowGateway,
                auditPublisher,
                fixedClock
        );
    }
}
```

Catatan: `@InjectMocks` boleh dipakai, tetapi untuk service dengan constructor eksplisit, manual construction sering lebih jelas dan mudah dikontrol.

---

# 7. Stub: Mengatur Jawaban Dependency

Stub digunakan ketika dependency harus memberi data ke class under test.

Contoh:

```java id="rib7ch"
when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
        .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));
```

Test lengkap:

```java id="459lxp"
@Test
void shouldSubmitDraftCase() {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
            .withId("CASE-001")
            .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
            .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
            .build();

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

    service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    verify(caseRepository).markSubmitted(
            eq("CASE-001"),
            eq("user-123"),
            eq(Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"))
    );
}
```

## Kapan stub tepat?

```text id="yvbr2g"
- repository mengembalikan entity
- config provider mengembalikan feature flag
- external client mengembalikan response
- authorization gateway mengembalikan permission
- clock/id generator mengembalikan nilai tetap
```

## Hindari unnecessary stubbing

Buruk:

```java id="yeaxl6"
when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
        .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

when(featureFlagProvider.isEnabled("unused-flag"))
        .thenReturn(true);
```

Kalau stub tidak dipakai, test menjadi misleading. Mockito memiliki mekanisme strict stubbing dan `UnnecessaryStubbingException` untuk mendeteksi unused stubs; dokumentasi Mockito menyatakan unused stubbings sebaiknya dihapus agar test tetap bersih. ([javadoc.io](https://www.javadoc.io/doc/org.mockito/mockito-core/2.8.9/org/mockito/exceptions/misusing/UnnecessaryStubbingException.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 8. Mock: Memverifikasi Side Effect

Mock cocok ketika output utama method adalah side effect.

Contoh side effect:

```text id="5p5e5r"
- publish event
- send workflow signal
- send notification
- write audit
- call external gateway
```

Test:

```java id="4jq3aa"
@Test
void shouldSignalWorkflowAndPublishAuditEventAfterSubmit() {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
            .withId("CASE-001")
            .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
            .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
            .build();

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

    service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    verify(workflowGateway).signalCaseSubmitted("CASE-001");
    verify(auditPublisher).publish(any(AuditEvent.class));
}
```

Masalahnya: `any(AuditEvent.class)` terlalu lemah. Test ini tidak membuktikan isi event benar.

Lebih baik gunakan `ArgumentCaptor`.

---

# 9. ArgumentCaptor: Menangkap Object yang Dikirim

```java id="hk5vqh"
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;

class CaseSubmissionServiceCaptorTest {

    @Test
    void shouldPublishAuditEventWithCorrectPayload() {
        CaseRepository caseRepository = mock(CaseRepository.class);
        WorkflowGateway workflowGateway = mock(WorkflowGateway.class);
        AuditPublisher auditPublisher = mock(AuditPublisher.class);

        Clock fixedClock = Clock.fixed(
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                ZoneOffset.UTC
        );

        CaseSubmissionService service = new CaseSubmissionService(
                caseRepository,
                workflowGateway,
                auditPublisher,
                fixedClock
        );

        CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
                .withId("CASE-001")
                .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
                .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
                .build();

        when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
                .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

        service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

        ArgumentCaptor<AuditEvent> captor =
                ArgumentCaptor.forClass(AuditEvent.class);

        verify(auditPublisher).publish(captor.capture());

        AuditEvent event = captor.getValue();

        assertThat(event.caseId()).isEqualTo("CASE-001");
        assertThat(event.action()).isEqualTo("CASE_SUBMITTED");
        assertThat(event.actor()).isEqualTo("user-123");
        assertThat(event.occurredAt())
                .isEqualTo(Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"));
    }
}
```

Mockito documentation menjelaskan `ArgumentCaptor` digunakan untuk menangkap argument agar bisa diasersi lebih lanjut, dan verification biasanya lebih cocok daripada stubbing untuk penggunaan captor. ([javadoc.io](https://www.javadoc.io/doc/org.mockito/mockito-core/5.13.0/org/mockito/ArgumentCaptor.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Kapan captor cocok?

```text id="82wmvh"
- event object dibuat internal oleh service
- command object dikirim ke gateway
- audit payload perlu diverifikasi
- notification payload perlu dicek
- request object ke external client perlu dicek
```

## Kapan captor tidak perlu?

Kalau object sudah tersedia di test, pakai `eq()` atau assertion langsung.

---

# 10. Fake: Implementasi Ringan yang Bekerja

Fake sering lebih readable daripada mock ketika dependency punya state sederhana.

## Fake repository

```java id="puvpm2"
public class FakeCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final Map<String, CaseRecord> records = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, SubmissionRecord> submissions = new HashMap<>();

    public void save(CaseRecord caseRecord) {
        records.put(caseRecord.id(), caseRecord);
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CaseRecord> findById(String caseId) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(records.get(caseId));
    }

    @Override
    public void markSubmitted(
            String caseId,
            String submittedBy,
            Instant submittedAt
    ) {
        submissions.put(
                caseId,
                new SubmissionRecord(submittedBy, submittedAt)
        );
    }

    public Optional<SubmissionRecord> submissionOf(String caseId) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(submissions.get(caseId));
    }
}
```

```java id="z34m41"
public record SubmissionRecord(
        String submittedBy,
        Instant submittedAt
) {
}
```

## Recording fake publisher

```java id="6epkt3"
public class RecordingAuditPublisher implements AuditPublisher {

    private final List<AuditEvent> events = new ArrayList<>();

    @Override
    public void publish(AuditEvent event) {
        events.add(event);
    }

    public List<AuditEvent> events() {
        return List.copyOf(events);
    }
}
```

## Test dengan fake

```java id="hbsac9"
@Test
void shouldSubmitCaseUsingFakes() {
    FakeCaseRepository caseRepository = new FakeCaseRepository();
    RecordingWorkflowGateway workflowGateway = new RecordingWorkflowGateway();
    RecordingAuditPublisher auditPublisher = new RecordingAuditPublisher();

    Clock fixedClock = Clock.fixed(
            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
            ZoneOffset.UTC
    );

    CaseSubmissionService service = new CaseSubmissionService(
            caseRepository,
            workflowGateway,
            auditPublisher,
            fixedClock
    );

    caseRepository.save(aCase()
            .withId("CASE-001")
            .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
            .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
            .build());

    service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    assertThat(caseRepository.submissionOf("CASE-001"))
            .hasValue(new SubmissionRecord(
                    "user-123",
                    Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
            ));

    assertThat(workflowGateway.signalledCaseIds())
            .containsExactly("CASE-001");

    assertThat(auditPublisher.events())
            .containsExactly(new AuditEvent(
                    "CASE-001",
                    "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                    "user-123",
                    Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
            ));
}
```

## Fake lebih cocok daripada mock jika

```text id="28g3kl"
- test lebih mudah dibaca sebagai behavior
- dependency punya state sederhana
- banyak verify membuat test noisy
- interaction detail bukan hal utama
- Anda ingin test mendekati cara pemakaian nyata
```

## Fake tidak cocok jika

```text id="hpblen"
- fake menjadi terlalu kompleks
- fake mulai meniru PostgreSQL, Camunda, atau HTTP server
- behavior dependency terlalu banyak cabang
- fake sendiri butuh banyak test
```

Kalau fake mulai kompleks, kemungkinan test tersebut sudah harus naik ke integration test.

---

# 11. Spy: Gunakan Jarang

Spy adalah object nyata yang sebagian method-nya bisa diverifikasi atau distub.

Contoh:

```java id="qptfbl"
CaseNumberFormatter formatter = spy(new CaseNumberFormatter());

when(formatter.prefix()).thenReturn("TEST");

String result = formatter.format(123);
```

Spy biasanya muncul pada:

```text id="am6pgw"
- legacy code
- class sulit dipecah
- method internal sulit dikontrol
- partial override sementara
```

## Masalah spy

```text id="m3x2p5"
- mudah menyembunyikan desain yang buruk
- bisa menjalankan real method tanpa disadari
- test makin tahu implementasi internal
- refactor kecil bisa merusak test
```

## Rekomendasi

```text id="2czk1i"
Untuk code baru:
- prefer dependency injection
- prefer interface kecil
- prefer fake/stub/mock biasa
- hindari spy kecuali ada alasan kuat
```

---

# 12. Dummy Object

Dummy hanya object pengisi yang tidak dipakai.

Contoh:

```java id="g90i0p"
SubmitCaseCommand command = new SubmitCaseCommand(
        "CASE-001",
        "user-123",
        "unused comment"
);
```

Kalau parameter benar-benar tidak relevan terhadap test, dummy acceptable.

Tetapi jangan sampai dummy menyembunyikan behavior penting.

Buruk:

```java id="mc2cm8"
service.submit(null, null);
```

Kalau `caseId` dan `submittedBy` penting, jangan dummy-kan.

---

# 13. Stub vs Mock vs Fake Decision Tree

```text id="bbq69u"
Apakah dependency hanya perlu mengembalikan nilai?
│
├── Ya
│   └── Stub
│
└── Tidak
    │
    ├── Apakah dependency punya side effect yang harus diverifikasi?
    │   └── Mock + verify / ArgumentCaptor
    │
    ├── Apakah dependency punya state sederhana dan test lebih readable dengan state?
    │   └── Fake
    │
    ├── Apakah object nyata perlu dipakai tapi sebagian behavior diganti?
    │   └── Spy, tapi hati-hati
    │
    └── Apakah object hanya pengisi parameter?
        └── Dummy
```

---

# 14. Interaction Testing: Kapan Perlu `verify()`

`verify()` bagus untuk side effect penting.

Contoh yang layak diverifikasi:

```text id="j4a3ul"
- event published
- workflow signalled
- external client called
- repository update called
- notification sent
- audit log written
```

Contoh yang biasanya tidak perlu diverifikasi:

```text id="fmnavc"
- getter dipanggil
- mapper method internal dipanggil
- private helper indirectly dipanggil
- repository find dipanggil kalau output sudah membuktikan behavior
```

## Contoh verify yang terlalu internal

Buruk:

```java id="n4n69j"
verify(caseRepository).findById("CASE-001");
verify(caseRepository).markSubmitted("CASE-001", "user-123", submittedAt);
verify(workflowGateway).signalCaseSubmitted("CASE-001");
verify(auditPublisher).publish(any());
verifyNoMoreInteractions(caseRepository, workflowGateway, auditPublisher);
```

Masalah:

```text id="ec5bxm"
- refactor internal mudah memecahkan test
- test terlalu mengunci implementation
- behavior utama bisa tenggelam oleh detail call
```

Lebih baik:

```java id="dzksko"
verify(workflowGateway).signalCaseSubmitted("CASE-001");

ArgumentCaptor<AuditEvent> captor =
        ArgumentCaptor.forClass(AuditEvent.class);

verify(auditPublisher).publish(captor.capture());

assertThat(captor.getValue().action())
        .isEqualTo("CASE_SUBMITTED");
```

---

# 15. `verifyNoMoreInteractions`: Gunakan Sangat Selektif

`verifyNoMoreInteractions()` sering membuat test terlalu rapuh.

Boleh dipakai untuk:

```text id="aak7pn"
- memastikan external side effect tidak terjadi
- memastikan workflow tidak disignal pada error path
- memastikan notification tidak terkirim
- memastikan payment capture tidak dipanggil
```

Contoh bagus:

```java id="kws00f"
@Test
void shouldNotSignalWorkflowWhenCaseCannotBeSubmitted() {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
            .withStatus(CaseStatus.APPROVED)
            .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
            .build();

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123"))
            .isInstanceOf(InvalidCaseStateException.class);

    verifyNoInteractions(workflowGateway);
    verifyNoInteractions(auditPublisher);
}
```

Jangan pakai hanya untuk mengunci semua detail internal.

---

# 16. `any()` vs `eq()` vs Captor

## `any()`

Gunakan jika nilai argument tidak penting.

```java id="xvfqpn"
verify(auditPublisher).publish(any(AuditEvent.class));
```

Risiko:

```text id="m4zlf1"
Test bisa hijau walau payload salah.
```

## `eq()`

Gunakan jika nilai sederhana dan diketahui.

```java id="mlwc7e"
verify(workflowGateway).signalCaseSubmitted(eq("CASE-001"));
```

## Captor

Gunakan jika object dibuat internal dan perlu dicek detailnya.

```java id="4t4jc4"
ArgumentCaptor<AuditEvent> captor =
        ArgumentCaptor.forClass(AuditEvent.class);

verify(auditPublisher).publish(captor.capture());

assertThat(captor.getValue().caseId()).isEqualTo("CASE-001");
```

Practical rule:

```text id="apkrpe"
any() untuk tidak penting.
eq() untuk value sederhana.
captor untuk object penting yang dibuat internal.
```

---

# 17. Testing Error Path dengan Mock

Error path sering paling penting dalam service enterprise.

```java id="b0705s"
@Test
void shouldNotMarkSubmittedOrPublishEventWhenCaseIsNotFound() {
    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-404"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.empty());

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit("CASE-404", "user-123"))
            .isInstanceOf(CaseNotFoundException.class);

    verify(caseRepository, never()).markSubmitted(
            anyString(),
            anyString(),
            any()
    );

    verifyNoInteractions(workflowGateway);
    verifyNoInteractions(auditPublisher);
}
```

Yang dibuktikan:

```text id="c85cyb"
- exception benar
- DB update tidak dilakukan
- workflow tidak disignal
- audit event tidak dipublish
```

---

# 18. Testing Call Order: Jarang, Tapi Kadang Perlu

Gunakan `InOrder` hanya jika order memang business-critical.

Contoh:

```text id="8dkkki"
Audit harus ditulis setelah status update.
Workflow signal harus setelah DB update.
Payment capture harus sebelum order confirmed.
```

Test:

```java id="lggkj8"
import org.mockito.InOrder;

import static org.mockito.Mockito.inOrder;

@Test
void shouldUpdateCaseBeforeSignallingWorkflow() {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
            .withId("CASE-001")
            .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
            .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
            .build();

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

    service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    InOrder inOrder = inOrder(caseRepository, workflowGateway);

    inOrder.verify(caseRepository).markSubmitted(
            eq("CASE-001"),
            eq("user-123"),
            any()
    );

    inOrder.verify(workflowGateway).signalCaseSubmitted("CASE-001");
}
```

Hindari order verification kalau urutan tidak relevan.

---

# 19. Strict Stubbing

Strict stubbing membantu mendeteksi test yang punya stub tidak dipakai atau argument mismatch.

Contoh masalah:

```java id="rqm6pg"
when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
        .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

service.submit("CASE-999", "user-123");
```

Stub untuk `CASE-001` tidak dipakai karena service dipanggil dengan `CASE-999`.

Ini bisa berarti:

```text id="y8xmsn"
- test setup salah
- input test salah
- production code berubah
- stub sudah tidak dibutuhkan
```

MockitoExtension menangani strict stubbing dalam integrasi JUnit Jupiter. ([javadoc.io](https://www.javadoc.io/doc/org.mockito/mockito-junit-jupiter/5.14.0/org/mockito/junit/jupiter/MockitoExtension.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Hindari `lenient()` kecuali perlu

Buruk jika terlalu sering:

```java id="tokpuy"
lenient().when(configProvider.isEnabled("feature-x"))
        .thenReturn(true);
```

`lenient()` boleh untuk setup bersama yang memang tidak semua test memakai, tetapi jika terlalu banyak, biasanya fixture test perlu dipecah.

---

# 20. Static Mocking: Jangan Jadi Default

Mockito modern bisa melakukan static mocking, tetapi ini sebaiknya bukan strategi utama.

Lebih baik refactor:

```text id="bo6u9s"
LocalDate.now()       → inject Clock
UUID.randomUUID()     → inject IdGenerator
System.getenv()       → inject ConfigProvider
Files.readString()    → inject FileReader/Gateway
```

Static mocking boleh untuk:

```text id="0zmzbd"
- legacy code yang belum bisa direfactor
- third-party static utility yang sulit dikontrol
- transitional test saat refactor bertahap
```

Untuk code baru, gunakan dependency injection.

---

# 21. Practical Example: Retry Policy

Retry policy cocok untuk unit test tanpa mock sama sekali.

```java id="dad8g4"
public enum ExternalErrorType {
    TIMEOUT,
    CONNECTION_RESET,
    BAD_REQUEST,
    UNAUTHORIZED,
    INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR
}
```

```java id="03ub9q"
public class RetryPolicy {

    public boolean shouldRetry(ExternalErrorType errorType) {
        return switch (errorType) {
            case TIMEOUT, CONNECTION_RESET, INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR -> true;
            case BAD_REQUEST, UNAUTHORIZED -> false;
        };
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="lyv8zv"
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.CsvSource;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class RetryPolicyTest {

    private final RetryPolicy retryPolicy = new RetryPolicy();

    @ParameterizedTest
    @CsvSource({
            "TIMEOUT, true",
            "CONNECTION_RESET, true",
            "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, true",
            "BAD_REQUEST, false",
            "UNAUTHORIZED, false"
    })
    void shouldDetermineRetryableErrors(
            ExternalErrorType errorType,
            boolean expected
    ) {
        boolean result = retryPolicy.shouldRetry(errorType);

        assertThat(result).isEqualTo(expected);
    }
}
```

Tidak perlu Mockito di sini.

Prinsip:

```text id="ynbbji"
Kalau bisa diuji tanpa mock, jangan pakai mock.
```

---

# 22. Practical Example: Idempotency Decision

```java id="l5pnmg"
public enum IdempotencyDecision {
    PROCESS_NEW_REQUEST,
    RETURN_PREVIOUS_RESPONSE,
    REJECT_CONFLICT
}
```

```java id="wzwxgi"
public record ExistingRequest(
        String key,
        String requestHash,
        String responseBody
) {
}
```

```java id="e92iq1"
public class IdempotencyPolicy {

    public IdempotencyDecision decide(
            Optional<ExistingRequest> existingRequest,
            String incomingHash
    ) {
        if (existingRequest.isEmpty()) {
            return IdempotencyDecision.PROCESS_NEW_REQUEST;
        }

        if (existingRequest.get().requestHash().equals(incomingHash)) {
            return IdempotencyDecision.RETURN_PREVIOUS_RESPONSE;
        }

        return IdempotencyDecision.REJECT_CONFLICT;
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="pcrm8i"
class IdempotencyPolicyTest {

    private final IdempotencyPolicy policy = new IdempotencyPolicy();

    @Test
    void shouldProcessNewRequestWhenKeyDoesNotExist() {
        IdempotencyDecision decision =
                policy.decide(Optional.empty(), "hash-1");

        assertThat(decision)
                .isEqualTo(IdempotencyDecision.PROCESS_NEW_REQUEST);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldReturnPreviousResponseWhenHashMatches() {
        ExistingRequest existing = new ExistingRequest(
                "idem-001",
                "hash-1",
                "{}"
        );

        IdempotencyDecision decision =
                policy.decide(Optional.of(existing), "hash-1");

        assertThat(decision)
                .isEqualTo(IdempotencyDecision.RETURN_PREVIOUS_RESPONSE);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectConflictWhenHashDiffers() {
        ExistingRequest existing = new ExistingRequest(
                "idem-001",
                "hash-1",
                "{}"
        );

        IdempotencyDecision decision =
                policy.decide(Optional.of(existing), "hash-2");

        assertThat(decision)
                .isEqualTo(IdempotencyDecision.REJECT_CONFLICT);
    }
}
```

Lagi-lagi: tidak butuh Mockito. DB uniqueness dan race condition baru dites di integration test.

---

# 23. Practical Example: Authorization Gateway Stub

Kadang dependency eksternal cukup distub.

```java id="7wssgw"
public interface AuthorizationGateway {
    boolean canSubmitCase(String userId, String caseId);
}
```

Service:

```java id="g0o1dc"
public class CasePermissionService {

    private final AuthorizationGateway authorizationGateway;

    public CasePermissionService(AuthorizationGateway authorizationGateway) {
        this.authorizationGateway = authorizationGateway;
    }

    public void assertCanSubmit(String userId, String caseId) {
        boolean allowed = authorizationGateway.canSubmitCase(userId, caseId);

        if (!allowed) {
            throw new ForbiddenCaseActionException(userId, caseId);
        }
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="mxexua"
@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class CasePermissionServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private AuthorizationGateway authorizationGateway;

    private CasePermissionService service;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        service = new CasePermissionService(authorizationGateway);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldAllowSubmitWhenGatewayAllowsAction() {
        when(authorizationGateway.canSubmitCase("user-123", "CASE-001"))
                .thenReturn(true);

        service.assertCanSubmit("user-123", "CASE-001");
    }

    @Test
    void shouldRejectSubmitWhenGatewayDeniesAction() {
        when(authorizationGateway.canSubmitCase("user-123", "CASE-001"))
                .thenReturn(false);

        assertThatThrownBy(() ->
                service.assertCanSubmit("user-123", "CASE-001")
        ).isInstanceOf(ForbiddenCaseActionException.class);
    }
}
```

Ini stub, bukan interaction-heavy mock. Kita tidak perlu verify `canSubmitCase()` jika behavior sudah terbukti dari outcome.

---

# 24. Practical Example: Event Publisher dengan Captor

```java id="fky4bj"
@Test
void shouldPublishCaseSubmittedEvent() {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
            .withId("CASE-001")
            .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
            .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
            .build();

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

    service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    ArgumentCaptor<AuditEvent> auditCaptor =
            ArgumentCaptor.forClass(AuditEvent.class);

    verify(auditPublisher).publish(auditCaptor.capture());

    assertThat(auditCaptor.getValue())
            .usingRecursiveComparison()
            .isEqualTo(new AuditEvent(
                    "CASE-001",
                    "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                    "user-123",
                    Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
            ));
}
```

Kalau `AuditEvent` adalah record, assertion bisa langsung equality.

```java id="szz6bl"
assertThat(auditCaptor.getValue())
        .isEqualTo(expectedEvent);
```

---

# 25. Practical Example: Jangan Verify Getter/Mapper Internal

Buruk:

```java id="xzcil6"
verify(caseRecord).status();
verify(caseRecord).mandatoryDocumentsComplete();
verify(mapper).toCommand(request);
```

Yang lebih penting adalah outcome:

```java id="3g31wa"
assertThat(result.status()).isEqualTo(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED);
assertThat(errors).containsExactly("MANDATORY_DOCUMENTS_MISSING");
```

Kalau mapper adalah dependency nyata yang kompleks, test mapper secara terpisah. Jangan unit test service terlalu terikat ke cara mapper dipanggil.

---

# 26. Mockito Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Contoh | Risiko | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mocking data | Mock DTO/entity/record | Test tidak natural | Buat object nyata |
| Over-verification | Verify semua method call | Rapuh saat refactor | Verify side effect penting saja |
| Unused stubbing | `when()` tidak dipakai | Test misleading | Hapus stub / split fixture |
| Weak assertion | `any()` semua argument | Payload salah tetap hijau | `eq()` / captor |
| Spy by default | Partial mock class baru | Desain buruk tersembunyi | Dependency injection |
| Static mocking by default | Mock `LocalDate.now()` | Sulit dirawat | Inject `Clock` |
| One huge mock setup | 20 lines `when()` | Test susah dibaca | Fake atau split use case |
| Mocking SQL behavior | Stub mapper result | SQL tidak terbukti | Integration test PostgreSQL |
| Verify private behavior indirectly | Verify helper dependency internal | Refactor pecah | Assert business outcome |
| Lenient everywhere | Banyak `lenient()` | Strict stubbing kehilangan manfaat | Fixture per test |

---

# 27. Practical Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini sebelum menulis mock:

```text id="jx2jzu"
[ ] Apakah dependency ini benar-benar eksternal terhadap behavior yang diuji?
[ ] Apakah dependency perlu mengembalikan data? Jika ya, stub cukup.
[ ] Apakah dependency menghasilkan side effect penting? Jika ya, mock + verify.
[ ] Apakah dependency punya state sederhana? Jika ya, fake mungkin lebih baik.
[ ] Apakah object ini hanya data? Jika ya, jangan mock.
[ ] Apakah verify ini menguji behavior atau implementation detail?
[ ] Apakah argument penting sudah diasersi dengan eq/captor?
[ ] Apakah ada unused stubbing?
[ ] Apakah test masih readable tanpa tahu internal service?
[ ] Apakah integration test lebih tepat untuk behavior ini?
```

---

# 28. Recommended Pattern untuk Service Test

## Pattern 1 - Pure decision

```text id="5pem44"
No Mockito
Use real object
Assert return value
```

Contoh:

```text id="7mb0qb"
RetryPolicyTest
CaseTransitionPolicyTest
IdempotencyPolicyTest
SlaCalculatorTest
```

## Pattern 2 - Dependency returns data

```text id="tq087z"
Stub dependency
Assert outcome
Minimal verify
```

Contoh:

```text id="wax53d"
Repository find
Authorization check
Feature flag check
```

## Pattern 3 - Dependency receives side effect

```text id="r6hj9z"
Mock dependency
Verify side effect
Use captor if payload matters
```

Contoh:

```text id="uu6utb"
AuditPublisher
WorkflowGateway
NotificationSender
ExternalCommandGateway
```

## Pattern 4 - Stateful collaboration

```text id="gqnze7"
Use fake
Assert state/output
Avoid excessive verify
```

Contoh:

```text id="6zgb2n"
Fake repository
Recording event publisher
In-memory token store
```

## Pattern 5 - Hard external behavior

```text id="5y0fmn"
Do not unit test with mock only
Move to integration test with stub server/container
```

Contoh:

```text id="fx6ujx"
HTTP client timeout
PostgreSQL lock
Camunda process
MyBatis SQL
Kubernetes config
```

---

# 29. Quick Reference

| Situation | Recommended double |
|---|---|
| Repository returns existing case | Stub |
| Repository stores state and test checks result | Fake |
| Audit event must be published | Mock + captor |
| Workflow signal must not happen on failure | Mock + `verifyNoInteractions` |
| External API response controls branch | Stub |
| External API timeout behavior | Integration test with stub server |
| DTO passed through layers | Real object |
| Time must be fixed | `Clock.fixed()` |
| UUID must be deterministic | Fake ID generator |
| Legacy class hard to isolate | Spy, temporarily |
| SQL correctness | No mock; PostgreSQL integration test |
| JAX-RS response mapping | Jersey resource test |
| Performance characteristic | JMH benchmark |

---

# 30. Ringkasan Seri 3

Inti dari seri ini:

```text id="7kgxx3"
Mockito bukan tujuan.
Mockito hanya alat untuk mengontrol dependency dan memverifikasi side effect.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text id="xsfga1"
Gunakan object nyata untuk data.
Gunakan stub untuk dependency yang memberi jawaban.
Gunakan mock untuk side effect penting.
Gunakan captor untuk payload penting.
Gunakan fake jika state sederhana membuat test lebih readable.
Gunakan spy hanya untuk legacy/partial override.
Jangan mock SQL, HTTP runtime, transaction, atau workflow engine.
```

Untuk microservice Jersey/JAX-RS dengan MyBatis/PostgreSQL/Camunda:

```text id="5peh28"
Unit test:
- service decision
- policy
- retry/idempotency logic
- mapper
- validation
- side effect intent

Integration/resource test:
- SQL correctness
- transaction behavior
- HTTP serialization
- ExceptionMapper
- external timeout
- Camunda process
```

# Seri 4/14 - Testing Service Layer, Business Rules, and State Transitions  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada bagian yang biasanya paling penting di enterprise microservice:

```text id="7fh71n"
Service layer
Business rules
State transition
Approval/rejection lifecycle
Escalation decision
Idempotency decision
Side effect intent
```

Di aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL + Camunda**, service layer biasanya menjadi tempat orkestrasi antara:

```text id="3bdudh"
REST Resource
  ↓
Service Layer
  ↓
Domain Policy / Validator
  ↓
Repository / MyBatis Mapper
  ↓
PostgreSQL
  ↓
Camunda / Event / External Integration
```

Unit test service layer tidak membuktikan SQL, HTTP routing, atau Camunda BPMN benar. Unit test service layer membuktikan:

```text id="497fry"
Jika input dan dependency response tertentu diberikan,
maka service membuat decision, state change intent, dan side effect intent yang benar.
```

Untuk test seperti ini, JUnit Jupiter cocok untuk test biasa dan parameterized test; JUnit 6 saat ini menyediakan dokumentasi user guide terbaru untuk model testing modern di JVM. ([JUnit](https://docs.junit.org/6.1.0/overview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Mockito menyediakan mock creation, stubbing, dan verification API untuk dependency collaboration. ([Mockito](https://site.mockito.org/javadoc/current/org/mockito/Mockito.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) AssertJ cocok dipakai untuk assertion yang readable dan strongly typed di JVM. ([AssertJ](https://assertj.github.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Posisi Service Layer Test

Service layer test berada di antara:

```text id="5zev8n"
Pure unit test:
- policy
- calculator
- validator
- mapper

Integration test:
- DB
- transaction
- HTTP
- Camunda
- external system
```

Service layer test biasanya masih **unit test**, tetapi dependency-nya dikontrol dengan mock, stub, atau fake.

## Yang diuji di service layer

```text id="evdehr"
Business decision
State transition intent
Validation orchestration
Repository call intent
Workflow signal intent
Audit/event publish intent
Retry/idempotency decision
Permission decision
Error classification
```

## Yang tidak diuji di service layer unit test

```text id="00o0zj"
SQL query correctness
PostgreSQL constraint
JAX-RS serialization
ExceptionMapper HTTP response
Transaction rollback nyata
Camunda BPMN execution
External API timeout nyata
Kubernetes/Docker runtime config
```

---

# 3. Running Example: Case Lifecycle

Misalnya sistem punya lifecycle:

```text id="rrj84h"
DRAFT
  → SUBMITTED
  → UNDER_REVIEW
  → APPROVED
  → CLOSED

Alternative:
DRAFT
  → CANCELLED

UNDER_REVIEW
  → REJECTED
```

## Domain enum

```java id="gsuxgx"
public enum CaseStatus {
    DRAFT,
    SUBMITTED,
    UNDER_REVIEW,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED,
    CANCELLED,
    CLOSED
}
```

## Domain record

```java id="k942f6"
import java.time.Instant;

public record CaseRecord(
        String id,
        CaseStatus status,
        boolean mandatoryDocumentsComplete,
        String assigneeUserId,
        Instant submittedAt,
        Instant approvedAt,
        int version
) {
}
```

## Service responsibility

Service tidak sekadar update status. Service biasanya harus:

```text id="i3b8qd"
1. Load current case.
2. Validate current state.
3. Validate actor permission.
4. Validate business precondition.
5. Decide next state.
6. Persist state transition intent.
7. Publish audit/event.
8. Signal workflow.
9. Return result.
```

---

# 4. Diagram Service Layer Test Boundary

```text id="9feynu"
                ┌────────────────────────────┐
                │        Unit Test            │
                └─────────────┬──────────────┘
                              │
                              ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              CaseSubmissionService                │
│                                                  │
│  - validates state                               │
│  - checks permission                             │
│  - checks business rule                          │
│  - updates repository intent                     │
│  - publishes audit intent                        │
│  - signals workflow intent                       │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
      ▲              ▲                ▲
      │              │                │
      │ stub/mock    │ stub/mock      │ mock/captor
      │              │                │
┌────────────┐ ┌───────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐
│ Repository │ │ Auth Gateway   │ │ Event/Workflow  │
│ stub/fake  │ │ stub           │ │ mock/captor     │
└────────────┘ └───────────────┘ └─────────────────┘

Not here:
- Real PostgreSQL
- Real Camunda engine
- Real HTTP/JAX-RS runtime
```

---

# 5. Decomposition Map

| Area | Skill | Fokus praktis | Test style |
|---|---|---|---|
| Service orchestration | Load-validate-act-publish | Memastikan urutan decision masuk akal | Unit test with mock/fake |
| Business rule | Preconditions | Mandatory document, active user, allowed state | Pure unit/parameterized |
| State transition | Status lifecycle | `DRAFT → SUBMITTED`, invalid transition rejected | Parameterized test |
| Permission decision | Actor authorization | Submitter/approver/reviewer role | Stub gateway |
| Error path | Exception behavior | Not found, forbidden, invalid state | `assertThatThrownBy` |
| Side effect intent | Workflow/event/audit | Signal/publish hanya saat sukses | Mock + captor |
| Idempotency | Duplicate command | Same key returns previous result or no-op | Policy + service test |
| Versioning | Optimistic concurrency intent | Version passed to repository update | Captor/verify |
| Time control | `submittedAt`, `approvedAt` | Fixed `Clock` | Deterministic unit test |
| Transaction intent | Service call grouping | Unit test hanya intent, rollback di IT | Unit + integration split |
| Escalation logic | SLA breach/action | Calculate escalation decision | Pure unit |
| Maintainability | Fixture/data builders | Test readable | Builder/object mother |

---

# 6. Service Layer Design agar Testable

Service yang mudah dites biasanya punya pola:

```text id="q8i1cz"
Constructor injection
Small dependency interfaces
Clock injected
ID generator injected
Policy extracted
Repository abstraction
External side effect behind gateway
No static call for business dependency
```

Contoh dependency:

```java id="dh7mq6"
public interface CaseRepository {
    Optional<CaseRecord> findById(String caseId);

    void markSubmitted(
            String caseId,
            String submittedBy,
            Instant submittedAt,
            int expectedVersion
    );
}
```

```java id="l3rqs0"
public interface AuthorizationGateway {
    boolean canSubmitCase(String userId, String caseId);
}
```

```java id="cm2u23"
public interface WorkflowGateway {
    void signalCaseSubmitted(String caseId);
}
```

```java id="stpc8u"
public interface AuditPublisher {
    void publish(AuditEvent event);
}
```

```java id="chc4ng"
public record AuditEvent(
        String caseId,
        String action,
        String actorUserId,
        Instant occurredAt
) {
}
```

---

# 7. Service Implementation Example

```java id="fzcp2l"
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;

public class CaseSubmissionService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final AuthorizationGateway authorizationGateway;
    private final WorkflowGateway workflowGateway;
    private final AuditPublisher auditPublisher;
    private final Clock clock;

    public CaseSubmissionService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            AuthorizationGateway authorizationGateway,
            WorkflowGateway workflowGateway,
            AuditPublisher auditPublisher,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.authorizationGateway = authorizationGateway;
        this.workflowGateway = workflowGateway;
        this.auditPublisher = auditPublisher;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public void submit(String caseId, String submittedBy) {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findById(caseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new CaseNotFoundException(caseId));

        if (!authorizationGateway.canSubmitCase(submittedBy, caseId)) {
            throw new ForbiddenCaseActionException(submittedBy, caseId);
        }

        if (caseRecord.status() != CaseStatus.DRAFT) {
            throw new InvalidCaseStateException(caseId, caseRecord.status());
        }

        if (!caseRecord.mandatoryDocumentsComplete()) {
            throw new CaseDocumentIncompleteException(caseId);
        }

        Instant submittedAt = Instant.now(clock);

        caseRepository.markSubmitted(
                caseId,
                submittedBy,
                submittedAt,
                caseRecord.version()
        );

        workflowGateway.signalCaseSubmitted(caseId);

        auditPublisher.publish(new AuditEvent(
                caseId,
                "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                submittedBy,
                submittedAt
        ));
    }
}
```

Catatan desain:

```text id="ykw9zr"
Repository masih abstraction.
Workflow masih gateway.
Audit masih publisher.
Clock injected.
Business precondition eksplisit.
```

---

# 8. Test Setup

```java id="xgqdi6"
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.Optional;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class CaseSubmissionServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private CaseRepository caseRepository;

    @Mock
    private AuthorizationGateway authorizationGateway;

    @Mock
    private WorkflowGateway workflowGateway;

    @Mock
    private AuditPublisher auditPublisher;

    private final Clock fixedClock = Clock.fixed(
            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
            ZoneOffset.UTC
    );

    private CaseSubmissionService service;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        service = new CaseSubmissionService(
                caseRepository,
                authorizationGateway,
                workflowGateway,
                auditPublisher,
                fixedClock
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 9. Happy Path Test

```java id="4ezjyp"
@Test
void shouldSubmitDraftCaseWhenUserIsAuthorizedAndDocumentsAreComplete() {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
            "CASE-001",
            CaseStatus.DRAFT,
            true,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            7
    );

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

    when(authorizationGateway.canSubmitCase("user-123", "CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(true);

    service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    verify(caseRepository).markSubmitted(
            "CASE-001",
            "user-123",
            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
            7
    );

    verify(workflowGateway).signalCaseSubmitted("CASE-001");
}
```

Apa yang dibuktikan:

```text id="tony61"
- case berhasil diload
- authorization sukses
- valid state diterima
- document complete diterima
- repository update intent benar
- workflow signal terjadi
```

Tapi audit payload belum dicek detail. Untuk itu gunakan captor.

---

# 10. Captor untuk Audit Event

```java id="c1ylar"
@Test
void shouldPublishAuditEventWhenCaseIsSubmitted() {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
            "CASE-001",
            CaseStatus.DRAFT,
            true,
            null,
            null,
            null,
            7
    );

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

    when(authorizationGateway.canSubmitCase("user-123", "CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(true);

    service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    ArgumentCaptor<AuditEvent> auditCaptor =
            ArgumentCaptor.forClass(AuditEvent.class);

    verify(auditPublisher).publish(auditCaptor.capture());

    assertThat(auditCaptor.getValue())
            .isEqualTo(new AuditEvent(
                    "CASE-001",
                    "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                    "user-123",
                    Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
            ));
}
```

`ArgumentCaptor` cocok saat object dibuat di dalam service dan perlu diverifikasi detailnya. Mockito javadoc menjelaskan captor digunakan untuk menangkap argument agar bisa diasersi setelah verification. ([Mockito](https://site.mockito.org/javadoc/current/org/mockito/ArgumentCaptor.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 15. Mengurangi Duplikasi dengan Fixture Builder

Banyak test di atas verbose. Gunakan fixture builder.

```java id="j87h94"
public final class CaseRecordFixture {

    private String id = "CASE-001";
    private CaseStatus status = CaseStatus.DRAFT;
    private boolean mandatoryDocumentsComplete = true;
    private String assigneeUserId = null;
    private Instant submittedAt = null;
    private Instant approvedAt = null;
    private int version = 7;

    private CaseRecordFixture() {
    }

    public static CaseRecordFixture aCase() {
        return new CaseRecordFixture();
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withId(String id) {
        this.id = id;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withStatus(CaseStatus status) {
        this.status = status;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withCompleteMandatoryDocuments() {
        this.mandatoryDocumentsComplete = true;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withMissingMandatoryDocuments() {
        this.mandatoryDocumentsComplete = false;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withVersion(int version) {
        this.version = version;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecord build() {
        return new CaseRecord(
                id,
                status,
                mandatoryDocumentsComplete,
                assigneeUserId,
                submittedAt,
                approvedAt,
                version
        );
    }
}
```

# 16. Extract Policy untuk State Transition

Kalau rule state mulai banyak, jangan semua ditaruh di service.

```java id="53m6yl"
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

public class CaseTransitionPolicy {

    private static final Map<CaseStatus, Set<CaseStatus>> ALLOWED =
            Map.of(
                    CaseStatus.DRAFT, Set.of(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED, CaseStatus.CANCELLED),
                    CaseStatus.SUBMITTED, Set.of(CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW, CaseStatus.CANCELLED),
                    CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW, Set.of(CaseStatus.APPROVED, CaseStatus.REJECTED),
                    CaseStatus.APPROVED, Set.of(CaseStatus.CLOSED),
                    CaseStatus.REJECTED, Set.of(),
                    CaseStatus.CANCELLED, Set.of(),
                    CaseStatus.CLOSED, Set.of()
            );

    public boolean canTransition(CaseStatus from, CaseStatus to) {
        return ALLOWED.getOrDefault(from, Set.of()).contains(to);
    }
}
```

Parameterized test:

```java id="zeyc4v"
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.CsvSource;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class CaseTransitionPolicyTest {

    private final CaseTransitionPolicy policy = new CaseTransitionPolicy();

    @ParameterizedTest
    @CsvSource({
            "DRAFT, SUBMITTED, true",
            "DRAFT, CANCELLED, true",
            "DRAFT, APPROVED, false",
            "SUBMITTED, UNDER_REVIEW, true",
            "SUBMITTED, APPROVED, false",
            "UNDER_REVIEW, APPROVED, true",
            "UNDER_REVIEW, REJECTED, true",
            "APPROVED, CLOSED, true",
            "APPROVED, REJECTED, false",
            "REJECTED, CLOSED, false",
            "CANCELLED, SUBMITTED, false",
            "CLOSED, DRAFT, false"
    })
    void shouldEvaluateAllowedTransitions(
            CaseStatus from,
            CaseStatus to,
            boolean expected
    ) {
        assertThat(policy.canTransition(from, to))
                .isEqualTo(expected);
    }
}
```

JUnit mendukung parameterized test untuk menjalankan test yang sama dengan argument berbeda; ini cocok untuk matrix rule/state transition. ([JUnit](https://docs.junit.org/6.0.3/writing-tests/parameterized-classes-and-tests.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 17. Service Menggunakan Policy

```java id="r7sjh9"
public class CaseReviewService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CaseTransitionPolicy transitionPolicy;
    private final AuditPublisher auditPublisher;
    private final Clock clock;

    public CaseReviewService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            CaseTransitionPolicy transitionPolicy,
            AuditPublisher auditPublisher,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.transitionPolicy = transitionPolicy;
        this.auditPublisher = auditPublisher;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public void startReview(String caseId, String reviewerUserId) {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findById(caseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new CaseNotFoundException(caseId));

        if (!transitionPolicy.canTransition(
                caseRecord.status(),
                CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW
        )) {
            throw new InvalidCaseStateException(caseId, caseRecord.status());
        }

        Instant now = Instant.now(clock);

        caseRepository.markUnderReview(
                caseId,
                reviewerUserId,
                now,
                caseRecord.version()
        );

        auditPublisher.publish(new AuditEvent(
                caseId,
                "CASE_REVIEW_STARTED",
                reviewerUserId,
                now
        ));
    }
}
```

Practical design:

```text id="10nm7k"
Policy dites sendiri secara pure unit.
Service dites untuk orkestrasi penggunaan policy dan side effect intent.
```

# 18. Testing Approval Service

Approval biasanya lebih sensitif karena irreversible.

```java id="9doybe"
public class CaseApprovalService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CaseTransitionPolicy transitionPolicy;
    private final AuthorizationGateway authorizationGateway;
    private final WorkflowGateway workflowGateway;
    private final AuditPublisher auditPublisher;
    private final Clock clock;

    public CaseApprovalService(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            CaseTransitionPolicy transitionPolicy,
            AuthorizationGateway authorizationGateway,
            WorkflowGateway workflowGateway,
            AuditPublisher auditPublisher,
            Clock clock
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.transitionPolicy = transitionPolicy;
        this.authorizationGateway = authorizationGateway;
        this.workflowGateway = workflowGateway;
        this.auditPublisher = auditPublisher;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public void approve(String caseId, String approverUserId) {
        CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findById(caseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new CaseNotFoundException(caseId));

        if (!authorizationGateway.canApproveCase(approverUserId, caseId)) {
            throw new ForbiddenCaseActionException(approverUserId, caseId);
        }

        if (!transitionPolicy.canTransition(caseRecord.status(), CaseStatus.APPROVED)) {
            throw new InvalidCaseStateException(caseId, caseRecord.status());
        }

        Instant approvedAt = Instant.now(clock);

        caseRepository.markApproved(
                caseId,
                approverUserId,
                approvedAt,
                caseRecord.version()
        );

        workflowGateway.signalCaseApproved(caseId);

        auditPublisher.publish(new AuditEvent(
                caseId,
                "CASE_APPROVED",
                approverUserId,
                approvedAt
        ));
    }
}
```

Happy path test:

```java id="w05fdp"

            workflowGateway,

            fixedClock

            "CASE-001",
            "manager-1",
            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
            12
    );

    verify(workflowGateway).signalCaseApproved("CASE-001");
}
```

# 20. Testing Escalation Decision

Escalation cocok dipisah ke policy/calculator.

```java id="nazqgm"
public enum EscalationDecision {
    NO_ACTION,
    ESCALATE_TO_SUPERVISOR,
    ESCALATE_TO_COMPLIANCE
}
```

```java id="j5l9tk"
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;

public class CaseEscalationPolicy {

    public EscalationDecision decide(
            CaseStatus status,
            Instant submittedAt,
            Instant now
    ) {
        if (status != CaseStatus.SUBMITTED && status != CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW) {
            return EscalationDecision.NO_ACTION;
        }

        long ageHours = Duration.between(submittedAt, now).toHours();

        if (ageHours >= 72) {
            return EscalationDecision.ESCALATE_TO_COMPLIANCE;
        }

        if (ageHours >= 24) {
            return EscalationDecision.ESCALATE_TO_SUPERVISOR;
        }

        return EscalationDecision.NO_ACTION;
    }
}
```

Parameterized test:

```java id="zyhb5n"
@ParameterizedTest
@CsvSource({
        "SUBMITTED, 23, NO_ACTION",
        "SUBMITTED, 24, ESCALATE_TO_SUPERVISOR",
        "SUBMITTED, 71, ESCALATE_TO_SUPERVISOR",
        "SUBMITTED, 72, ESCALATE_TO_COMPLIANCE",
        "UNDER_REVIEW, 72, ESCALATE_TO_COMPLIANCE",
        "APPROVED, 100, NO_ACTION",
        "REJECTED, 100, NO_ACTION"
})
void shouldDecideEscalation(
        CaseStatus status,
        long ageHours,
        EscalationDecision expected
) {
    Instant submittedAt = Instant.parse("2026-07-10T10:00:00Z");
    Instant now = submittedAt.plus(Duration.ofHours(ageHours));

    CaseEscalationPolicy policy = new CaseEscalationPolicy();

    assertThat(policy.decide(status, submittedAt, now))
            .isEqualTo(expected);
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="we7myx"
Escalation rule lebih baik dites sebagai pure policy.
Service scheduler/escalation job cukup dites orchestration-nya.
```

---

# 21. Testing Idempotency Decision di Service Layer

Idempotency biasanya punya dua level:

```text id="csyw19"
1. Decision policy:
   same key + same request hash → return previous result
   same key + different hash → conflict
   no key → process new request

2. Integration safety:
   unique constraint / transaction / race handling di PostgreSQL
```

Unit test service hanya membuktikan decision flow.

```java id="8vagyf"
public enum IdempotencyDecision {
    PROCESS_NEW,
    RETURN_PREVIOUS,
    REJECT_CONFLICT
}
```

```java id="9u87y4"
public record IdempotencyRecord(
        String key,
        String requestHash,
        String responseBody
) {
}
```

```java id="1m2lpi"
public class IdempotencyPolicy {

    public IdempotencyDecision decide(
            Optional<IdempotencyRecord> existing,
            String incomingHash
    ) {
        if (existing.isEmpty()) {
            return IdempotencyDecision.PROCESS_NEW;
        }

        if (existing.get().requestHash().equals(incomingHash)) {
            return IdempotencyDecision.RETURN_PREVIOUS;
        }

        return IdempotencyDecision.REJECT_CONFLICT;
    }
}
```

Policy test:

```java id="julmcd"
@Test
void shouldReturnPreviousResultWhenRequestHashMatches() {
    IdempotencyPolicy policy = new IdempotencyPolicy();

    IdempotencyRecord existing = new IdempotencyRecord(
            "idem-001",
            "hash-abc",
            "{\"caseId\":\"CASE-001\"}"
    );

    IdempotencyDecision decision =
            policy.decide(Optional.of(existing), "hash-abc");

    assertThat(decision)
            .isEqualTo(IdempotencyDecision.RETURN_PREVIOUS);
}
```

Service unit test:

```java id="k87zd2"
@Test
void shouldRejectConflictWhenIdempotencyKeyExistsWithDifferentHash() {
    when(idempotencyRepository.findByKey("idem-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(new IdempotencyRecord(
                    "idem-001",
                    "hash-old",
                    "{\"caseId\":\"CASE-001\"}"
            )));

    SubmitCaseRequest request = new SubmitCaseRequest(
            "idem-001",
            "hash-new",
            "user-123"
    );

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit(request))
            .isInstanceOf(IdempotencyConflictException.class);

    verifyNoInteractions(workflowGateway);
    verifyNoInteractions(auditPublisher);
}
```

Race condition idempotency tidak cukup dengan unit test. Itu masuk integration/concurrency test dengan PostgreSQL unique constraint.

---

# 22. Testing Transaction Boundary Intent

Di unit test, jangan pura-pura membuktikan rollback. Unit test hanya membuktikan **intent dan ordering** jika memang penting.

Misalnya:

```text id="n7keat"
1. mark case submitted
2. publish event after DB update
```

Jika event publish harus setelah update:

```java id="wulz8e"
@Test
void shouldMarkSubmittedBeforePublishingAuditEvent() {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
            .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
            .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
            .build();

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

    when(authorizationGateway.canSubmitCase("user-123", "CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(true);

    service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    InOrder inOrder = inOrder(caseRepository, auditPublisher);

    inOrder.verify(caseRepository).markSubmitted(
            eq("CASE-001"),
            eq("user-123"),
            any(),
            anyInt()
    );

    inOrder.verify(auditPublisher).publish(any(AuditEvent.class));
}
```

Tetapi rollback behavior harus di integration test:

```text id="52sg0l"
Service unit test:
- update intent dipanggil
- event intent dipanggil/tidak dipanggil

Integration test:
- jika event/outbox insert gagal, DB rollback benar
- jika update gagal, audit tidak committed
```

---

# 23. Avoid Fat Service Test

Kalau satu service test setup-nya sudah terlalu besar:

```text id="79bqvr"
10 mocks
20 stubs
5 captors
banyak verify order
banyak branch
```

Itu sinyal desain mungkin perlu dipecah:

```text id="gwddel"
CaseSubmissionPolicy
CasePermissionChecker
CaseTransitionPolicy
CaseAuditFactory
CaseWorkflowNotifier
CaseSubmissionService
```

Contoh audit factory:

```java id="ncp7c3"
public class CaseAuditFactory {

    public AuditEvent submitted(
            String caseId,
            String actorUserId,
            Instant occurredAt
    ) {
        return new AuditEvent(
                caseId,
                "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                actorUserId,
                occurredAt
        );
    }
}
```

Audit factory bisa dites pure unit:

```java id="hfs7nr"
@Test

    AuditEvent event = factory.submitted(
            "CASE-001",
            "user-123",
            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")

    assertThat(event).isEqualTo(new AuditEvent(
            "CASE-001",
            "CASE_SUBMITTED",
            "user-123",
            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
    ));
}
```

Service test menjadi lebih kecil.

# 24. Testing with Result Object

Kadang service lebih mudah dites jika mengembalikan result object.

```java id="fofq1b"
public record SubmitCaseResult(
        String caseId,
        CaseStatus newStatus,
        Instant submittedAt
) {
}
```

Service:

```java id="hm2q3s"
public SubmitCaseResult submit(String caseId, String submittedBy) {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findById(caseId)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new CaseNotFoundException(caseId));

    // validations...

    Instant submittedAt = Instant.now(clock);

    caseRepository.markSubmitted(
            caseId,
            submittedBy,
            submittedAt,
            caseRecord.version()
    );

    workflowGateway.signalCaseSubmitted(caseId);

    return new SubmitCaseResult(
            caseId,
            CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
            submittedAt
    );
}
```

Test:

```java id="u2fpyb"
@Test
void shouldReturnSubmitCaseResult() {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
            .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
            .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
            .build();

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

    when(authorizationGateway.canSubmitCase("user-123", "CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(true);

    SubmitCaseResult result = service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    assertThat(result).isEqualTo(new SubmitCaseResult(
            "CASE-001",
            CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
    ));
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="uhk8o4"
- lebih mudah assert outcome
- tidak semua harus verify interaction
- resource layer bisa langsung map result ke response
```

# 25. Testing State Transition sebagai Matrix

Untuk lifecycle kompleks, buat matrix eksplisit.

```java id="ysh0rd"
static Stream<Arguments> invalidSubmitStates() {
    return Stream.of(
            Arguments.of(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED),
            Arguments.of(CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW),
            Arguments.of(CaseStatus.APPROVED),
            Arguments.of(CaseStatus.REJECTED),
            Arguments.of(CaseStatus.CANCELLED),
            Arguments.of(CaseStatus.CLOSED)
    );
}
```

```java id="fmwctu"
@ParameterizedTest
@MethodSource("invalidSubmitStates")
void shouldRejectSubmitFromInvalidStates(CaseStatus invalidStatus) {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
            .withStatus(invalidStatus)
            .withCompleteMandatoryDocuments()
            .build();

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));

    when(authorizationGateway.canSubmitCase("user-123", "CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(true);

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123"))
            .isInstanceOf(InvalidCaseStateException.class);

    verify(caseRepository, never()).markSubmitted(
            anyString(),
            anyString(),
            any(),
            anyInt()
    );
}
```

Kenapa `@MethodSource` lebih baik daripada `@CsvSource` kadang-kadang?

```text id="th2z8x"
- input kompleks
- enum/object lebih aman
- expected object bisa dibuat langsung
- lebih readable untuk scenario besar
```

---

# 26. Testing Permission Matrix

Contoh role:

```java id="rd8x53"
public enum CaseRole {
    SUBMITTER,
    REVIEWER,
    APPROVER,
    SUPERVISOR,
    READ_ONLY
}
```

Policy:

```java id="jt1ulw"
public class CasePermissionPolicy {

    public boolean canSubmit(CaseRole role) {
        return role == CaseRole.SUBMITTER
                || role == CaseRole.SUPERVISOR;
    }

    public boolean canApprove(CaseRole role) {
        return role == CaseRole.APPROVER
                || role == CaseRole.SUPERVISOR;
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="euzfwm"
@ParameterizedTest
@CsvSource({
        "SUBMITTER, true",
        "SUPERVISOR, true",
        "REVIEWER, false",
        "APPROVER, false",
        "READ_ONLY, false"
})
void shouldEvaluateSubmitPermission(
        CaseRole role,
        boolean expected
) {
    CasePermissionPolicy policy = new CasePermissionPolicy();

    assertThat(policy.canSubmit(role)).isEqualTo(expected);
}
```

Service layer tidak perlu mengetes semua role lagi jika policy sudah dites lengkap. Service cukup test:

```text id="pjgxy3"
- authorized path
- unauthorized path
```

---

# 27. Testing Domain Event Creation

Service sering membuat event.

```java id="g6l2dq"
public record CaseSubmittedEvent(
        String caseId,
        String submittedBy,
        Instant submittedAt,
        int version
) {
}
```

Factory:

```java id="gh3wqo"
public class CaseEventFactory {

    public CaseSubmittedEvent submitted(
            CaseRecord caseRecord,
            String submittedBy,
            Instant submittedAt
    ) {
        return new CaseSubmittedEvent(
                caseRecord.id(),
                submittedBy,
                submittedAt,
                caseRecord.version() + 1
        );
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="v7lsyu"
@Test
void shouldCreateCaseSubmittedEvent() {
    CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
            .withId("CASE-001")
            .withVersion(7)
            .build();

    CaseEventFactory factory = new CaseEventFactory();

    CaseSubmittedEvent event = factory.submitted(
            caseRecord,
            "user-123",
            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
    );

    assertThat(event).isEqualTo(new CaseSubmittedEvent(
            "CASE-001",
            "user-123",
            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
            8
    ));
}
```

Service test cukup verify event published.

---

# 28. Practical Service Test Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini untuk setiap service method penting:

```text id="s8mfd3"
[ ] Happy path diuji.
[ ] Not found path diuji.
[ ] Unauthorized path diuji.
[ ] Invalid state path diuji.
[ ] Missing precondition path diuji.
[ ] Side effect tidak terjadi saat failure.
[ ] Repository update intent benar.
[ ] Event/audit/workflow payload penting dicek.
[ ] Time dikontrol dengan Clock.
[ ] Version/optimistic locking parameter diteruskan.
[ ] Rule matrix dipisah ke policy test jika kompleks.
[ ] Tidak mengetes SQL/transaction nyata di unit test.
[ ] Test tidak terlalu terikat implementation detail.
```

---

# 29. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Semua rule di service | Service test membengkak | Extract policy |
| Semua test pakai integration test | CI lambat | Unit test rule dulu |
| Mock DTO/entity | Test tidak natural | Pakai real object/fixture |
| Hanya happy path | Error behavior tidak aman | Tambah negative path |
| Tidak kontrol waktu | Test flaky | Inject `Clock` |
| Verify semua method | Test rapuh | Verify side effect penting |
| Tidak cek payload event | Event salah tetap hijau | ArgumentCaptor |
| Unit test klaim rollback | Salah boundary | Rollback di integration test |
| Rule matrix copy-paste | Test susah dirawat | Parameterized test |
| Tidak test invalid transition | Lifecycle rentan bug | State transition matrix |

---

# 30. Recommended Structure

```text id="v89etq"
src/test/java/com/company/caseapp
├── service
│   ├── CaseSubmissionServiceTest.java
│   ├── CaseApprovalServiceTest.java
│   ├── CaseRejectionServiceTest.java
│   └── CaseEscalationServiceTest.java
│
├── policy
│   ├── CaseTransitionPolicyTest.java
│   ├── CasePermissionPolicyTest.java
│   ├── CaseEscalationPolicyTest.java
│   └── IdempotencyPolicyTest.java
│
├── factory
│   ├── CaseAuditFactoryTest.java
│   └── CaseEventFactoryTest.java
│
└── support
    ├── CaseRecordFixture.java
    ├── UserFixture.java
    └── CommandFixture.java
```

# 31. Minimal Standard untuk Service Method Kritis

Untuk method seperti:

```text id="f2vuj8"
submit
approve
reject
cancel
reopen
escalate
capture payment
retry integration
trigger workflow
```

Minimal test:

```text id="9envgh"
1 happy path
1 not found
1 unauthorized
1 invalid state
1 invalid/missing precondition
1 side effect suppression on failure
1 event/audit/workflow payload assertion
```

Kalau method mengubah lifecycle:

```text id="0g3o3b"
Tambahkan state transition matrix.
```

Kalau method punya idempotency:

```text id="pmfdnf"
Tambahkan:
- new key
- same key same hash
- same key different hash
```

Kalau method punya SLA/escalation:

```text id="dngq6w"
Tambahkan boundary:
- just before threshold
- exactly at threshold
- after threshold
```

# 32. Ringkasan Seri 4

Inti dari seri ini:

```text id="sr362h"
Service layer test membuktikan decision dan orchestration,
bukan membuktikan framework, database, atau runtime eksternal.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text id="l3w1t9"
Extract complex rule ke policy.
Test lifecycle sebagai matrix.
Test service untuk orchestration.
Gunakan Clock untuk time.
Gunakan captor untuk event/audit payload.
Pastikan side effect tidak terjadi saat failure.
Jangan klaim rollback di unit test.
Jangan mock data object.
```

Boundary yang benar:

```text id="lpijev"
Unit/service test:
- business decision
- state transition intent
- side effect intent

Integration test:
- SQL
- PostgreSQL constraint
- transaction rollback
- concurrency
- Camunda execution
- real HTTP boundary
```
