# Seri 5/14 - Jersey/JAX-RS Resource Testing  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada **testing resource layer** di aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS**.

Resource layer adalah boundary HTTP aplikasi:

```text id="bz5bch"
HTTP request
  ↓
JAX-RS Resource
  ↓
DTO / validation / mapper
  ↓
Service
  ↓
HTTP response
```

Resource test bertujuan membuktikan:

```text id="ce8zdc"
- endpoint path benar
- HTTP method benar
- request JSON bisa dibaca
- response JSON benar
- status code benar
- headers benar
- validation error benar
- exception mapping benar
- filter/interceptor behavior benar
```

Resource test **bukan** tempat utama untuk membuktikan SQL, transaction rollback, PostgreSQL constraint, atau Camunda BPMN execution. Itu masuk integration/workflow test.

Jersey menyediakan **Jersey Test Framework** untuk menguji server-side JAX-RS/Jersey components; dokumentasinya menyebut framework ini berbasis JUnit dan mudah diintegrasikan dengan Maven-based project. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/test-framework.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jakarta RESTful Web Services juga mendefinisikan konsep provider seperti filter, entity interceptor, request/response processing, dan client API objects seperti `Invocation` dan `WebTarget`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Posisi Resource Test dalam Test Pyramid

```text id="t9qzok"
Unit Test
  ↓
Resource / Component Test      ← fokus seri ini
  ↓
Integration Test
  ↓
Contract Test
  ↓
E2E / Smoke Test
```

Resource test berada di tengah:

```text id="1rj3wc"
Lebih tinggi dari unit test:
- karena menjalankan JAX-RS resource runtime
- request/response lewat HTTP-like client
- serialization/deserialization aktif

Lebih rendah dari integration test:
- biasanya service/repository dimock atau difake
- tidak perlu PostgreSQL nyata
- tidak perlu Camunda nyata
- tidak perlu external system nyata
```

---

# 3. Apa yang Harus Diuji di Resource Layer

| Concern | Resource test? | Kenapa |
|---|---:|---|
| HTTP path | Ya | Salah path membuat API tidak bisa dipakai |
| HTTP method | Ya | `POST`, `GET`, `PUT`, `PATCH`, `DELETE` punya semantics berbeda |
| Status code | Ya | Consumer bergantung pada status code |
| Request JSON parsing | Ya | Boundary API |
| Response JSON shape | Ya | API contract |
| Header | Ya | `Location`, `Content-Type`, correlation ID |
| Bean Validation | Ya | Request invalid harus gagal dengan benar |
| ExceptionMapper | Ya | Domain exception harus jadi response standar |
| Auth/correlation filter | Ya, sebagian | Cross-cutting HTTP behavior |
| Service business rule detail | Tidak utama | Sudah di service unit test |
| MyBatis SQL | Tidak | Integration test dengan PostgreSQL |
| Transaction rollback | Tidak | Integration test |
| Camunda process execution | Tidak | Workflow integration test |
| External timeout nyata | Tidak | Integration/stub server test |

---

# 4. Diagram Boundary

```text id="ew0ovj"
Resource Test Boundary

Test client
   │
   │ HTTP-like request
   ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ JerseyTest / ResourceConfig               │
│                                          │
│  ┌────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │ CaseResource                       │  │
│  │ - path                             │  │
│  │ - method                           │  │
│  │ - consumes/produces JSON           │  │
│  │ - validation                       │  │
│  │ - maps request to command          │  │
│  │ - calls service                    │  │
│  └────────────────────────────────────┘  │
│                                          │
│  Providers:                              │
│  - JSON provider                         │
│  - ExceptionMapper                       │
│  - Validation support                    │
│  - Filters                               │
└──────────────────────────────────────────┘
   │
   │ service call
   ▼
Mock/Fake service

Not included:
- real PostgreSQL
- real MyBatis SQL
- real Camunda
- real external API
```

---

# 5. Running Example: CaseResource

Misalnya endpoint:

```http id="ue00b1"
POST /cases/{caseId}/submit
Content-Type: application/json
```

Request:

```json id="3aopnp"
{
  "submittedBy": "user-123",
  "comment": "Ready for review"
}
```

Expected response:

```http id="mbu6aq"
HTTP/1.1 202 Accepted
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json id="abtspr"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-001",
  "status": "SUBMITTED"
}
```

---

# 6. Resource Class Example

```java id="tqn7yy"
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Consumes;
import jakarta.ws.rs.POST;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.PathParam;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Produces;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

@Path("/cases")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseSubmissionService caseSubmissionService;

    public CaseResource(CaseSubmissionService caseSubmissionService) {
        this.caseSubmissionService = caseSubmissionService;
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{caseId}/submit")
    public Response submitCase(
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
            @Valid SubmitCaseRequest request
    ) {
        SubmitCaseResult result = caseSubmissionService.submit(
                caseId,
                request.submittedBy(),
                request.comment()
        );

        return Response.accepted(new SubmitCaseResponse(
                result.caseId(),
                result.status().name()
        )).build();
    }

    public record SubmitCaseRequest(
            @NotBlank String submittedBy,
            String comment
    ) {
    }

    public record SubmitCaseResponse(
            String caseId,
            String status
    ) {
    }
}
```

Service contract:

```java id="qvnr5z"
public interface CaseSubmissionService {
    SubmitCaseResult submit(
            String caseId,
            String submittedBy,
            String comment
    );
}
```

```java id="x2o7ng"
public record SubmitCaseResult(
        String caseId,
        CaseStatus status
) {
}
```

---

# 7. Basic JerseyTest Setup

Jersey Test Framework biasanya menggunakan `JerseyTest` dan `ResourceConfig`.

```java id="xkucxk"
import jakarta.ws.rs.client.Entity;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Application;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;
import org.glassfish.jersey.test.JerseyTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;

class CaseResourceTest extends JerseyTest {

    private CaseSubmissionService caseSubmissionService;

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        caseSubmissionService = mock(CaseSubmissionService.class);

        return new ResourceConfig()
                .register(new CaseResource(caseSubmissionService));
    }

    @Test
    void shouldSubmitCase() {
        when(caseSubmissionService.submit(
                "CASE-001",
                "user-123",
                "Ready for review"
        )).thenReturn(new SubmitCaseResult(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED
        ));

        Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
                .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .post(Entity.json("""
                        {
                          "submittedBy": "user-123",
                          "comment": "Ready for review"
                        }
                        """));

        assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(202);

        CaseResource.SubmitCaseResponse body =
                response.readEntity(CaseResource.SubmitCaseResponse.class);

        assertThat(body.caseId()).isEqualTo("CASE-001");
        assertThat(body.status()).isEqualTo("SUBMITTED");

        verify(caseSubmissionService).submit(
                "CASE-001",
                "user-123",
                "Ready for review"
        );
    }
}
```

Yang diuji:

```text id="bh4qli"
- POST path benar
- JSON request bisa dibaca
- path param diteruskan
- request body diteruskan
- response status 202
- response body benar
```

---

# 8. ResourceConfig sebagai Test Composition Root

`ResourceConfig` di resource test berperan seperti mini application bootstrap.

```java id="wq9c4c"
@Override
protected Application configure() {
    return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(new CaseResource(caseSubmissionService))
            .register(new CaseExceptionMapper())
            .register(new ValidationExceptionMapper())
            .register(new CorrelationIdFilter());
}
```

Di sini Anda memilih dependency yang memang dibutuhkan untuk resource boundary.

Prinsip praktis:

```text id="90ij01"
Register hanya komponen yang relevan dengan resource behavior yang sedang diuji.
Jangan otomatis bootstrap seluruh aplikasi kalau tujuannya resource test.
```

Kalau semua komponen production di-start, test ini berubah menjadi integration test.

---

# 9. Testing Status Code

Status code adalah contract penting.

| Scenario | Status yang umum | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Create success | 201 | Biasanya dengan `Location` header |
| Async accepted | 202 | Cocok jika workflow diproses async |
| Get success | 200 | Return representation |
| Update success | 200/204 | 200 jika return body, 204 jika no body |
| Validation error | 400 | Request tidak valid |
| Unauthorized | 401 | Belum authenticated |
| Forbidden | 403 | Authenticated tapi tidak punya akses |
| Not found | 404 | Resource tidak ada |
| Invalid state/conflict | 409 | State conflict, duplicate, idempotency conflict |
| Unsupported media type | 415 | Content-Type salah |
| Server error | 500 | Unexpected failure |

Contoh invalid state:

```java id="tpbrtj"
@Test
void shouldReturnConflictWhenCaseCannotBeSubmittedFromCurrentState() {
    when(caseSubmissionService.submit(
            "CASE-001",
            "user-123",
            "Ready for review"
    )).thenThrow(new InvalidCaseStateException(
            "CASE-001",
            CaseStatus.APPROVED
    ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "user-123",
                      "comment": "Ready for review"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(409);
}
```

Test ini butuh `ExceptionMapper`, yang akan kita detailkan di bawah.

---

# 10. Error Response Model

Sebaiknya error response konsisten.

```java id="jkjb4z"
import java.util.List;

public record ApiErrorResponse(
        String code,
        String message,
        List<ApiErrorDetail> details,
        String traceId
) {
}
```

```java id="9aq5ej"
public record ApiErrorDetail(
        String field,
        String issue
) {
}
```

Contoh response:

```json id="my0eoh"
{
  "code": "CASE_INVALID_STATE",
  "message": "Case CASE-001 cannot be submitted from status APPROVED",
  "details": [],
  "traceId": "trace-123"
}
```

---

# 11. ExceptionMapper Test

JAX-RS/Jakarta REST supports providers for extending runtime behavior, including filters and other provider types. Exception mappers are part of the provider model used to map Java exceptions to HTTP responses. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Exception mapper:

```java id="jqp9w5"
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.util.List;

@Provider
public class InvalidCaseStateExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<InvalidCaseStateException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(InvalidCaseStateException exception) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.CONFLICT)
                .entity(new ApiErrorResponse(
                        "CASE_INVALID_STATE",
                        exception.getMessage(),
                        List.of(),
                        null
                ))
                .build();
    }
}
```

Register mapper di test:

```java id="kmpxjn"
@Override
protected Application configure() {
    caseSubmissionService = mock(CaseSubmissionService.class);

    return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(new CaseResource(caseSubmissionService))
            .register(new InvalidCaseStateExceptionMapper());
}
```

Test:

```java id="hwhuhg"
@Test
void shouldMapInvalidCaseStateToConflictResponse() {
    when(caseSubmissionService.submit(
            "CASE-001",
            "user-123",
            "Ready for review"
    )).thenThrow(new InvalidCaseStateException(
            "CASE-001",
            CaseStatus.APPROVED
    ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "user-123",
                      "comment": "Ready for review"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(409);

    ApiErrorResponse error = response.readEntity(ApiErrorResponse.class);

    assertThat(error.code()).isEqualTo("CASE_INVALID_STATE");
    assertThat(error.message()).contains("CASE-001");
    assertThat(error.message()).contains("APPROVED");
}
```

Yang diuji:

```text id="1thqrn"
Domain exception → HTTP 409
Error code benar
Message membawa context penting
Body JSON bisa diserialize
```

---

# 12. Validation Testing

Jersey memiliki dokumentasi khusus untuk Bean Validation support, termasuk dependency, configuration, registration, dan usage. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/bean-validation.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jakarta Validation bertujuan menyediakan fasilitas constraint declaration dan validation untuk Java object, termasuk method dan constructor validation. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/bean-validation/4.0/jakarta-validation-spec-4.0.0-m1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Request DTO:

```java id="p5t00l"
public record SubmitCaseRequest(
        @NotBlank String submittedBy,
        String comment
) {
}
```

Test invalid request:

```java id="n9ni0w"
@Test
void shouldReturnBadRequestWhenSubmittedByIsBlank() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "",
                      "comment": "Ready for review"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);

    verifyNoInteractions(caseSubmissionService);
}
```

Prinsip penting:

```text id="d27v3r"
Jika request invalid di boundary,
service tidak boleh dipanggil.
```

Kalau ingin error response validation konsisten, buat mapper untuk validation exception sesuai stack Jersey yang dipakai.

---

# 13. Validation Error Response Test

Contoh target response:

```json id="rqx354"
{
  "code": "VALIDATION_ERROR",
  "message": "Request validation failed",
  "details": [
    {
      "field": "submittedBy",
      "issue": "must not be blank"
    }
  ],
  "traceId": "trace-123"
}
```

Test praktis:

```java id="39kg4t"
@Test
void shouldReturnValidationErrorResponseWhenSubmittedByIsBlank() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "",
                      "comment": "Ready for review"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);

    ApiErrorResponse error = response.readEntity(ApiErrorResponse.class);

    assertThat(error.code()).isEqualTo("VALIDATION_ERROR");
    assertThat(error.details())
            .anySatisfy(detail -> {
                assertThat(detail.field()).contains("submittedBy");
                assertThat(detail.issue()).isNotBlank();
            });

    verifyNoInteractions(caseSubmissionService);
}
```

Catatan praktis:

```text id="du3lyf"
Field path validation bisa berbeda tergantung provider/config.
Jangan assert terlalu spesifik jika format field path tidak distandarkan internal.
Standarkan di mapper internal jika ingin contract stabil.
```

---

# 14. Testing JSON Serialization Shape

Resource test harus memastikan JSON shape yang dilihat consumer benar.

Misalnya response:

```json id="kkd9yq"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-001",
  "status": "SUBMITTED"
}
```

Test dengan `readEntity(String.class)`:

```java id="2ezaze"
@Test
void shouldReturnExpectedJsonShape() {
    when(caseSubmissionService.submit(
            "CASE-001",
            "user-123",
            "Ready for review"
    )).thenReturn(new SubmitCaseResult(
            "CASE-001",
            CaseStatus.SUBMITTED
    ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "user-123",
                      "comment": "Ready for review"
                    }
                    """));

    String json = response.readEntity(String.class);

    assertThat(json).contains("\"caseId\"");
    assertThat(json).contains("\"CASE-001\"");
    assertThat(json).contains("\"status\"");
    assertThat(json).contains("\"SUBMITTED\"");
}
```

Lebih robust jika pakai JSON assertion library, tetapi untuk cheatsheet ini cukup prinsipnya:

```text id="lwut81"
Assert contract field yang penting.
Jangan terlalu bergantung ke urutan field JSON.
```

---

# 15. Testing Request Mapping ke Service Command

Sering resource mengubah request menjadi command.

Command:

```java id="vx3dxq"
public record SubmitCaseCommand(
        String caseId,
        String submittedBy,
        String comment,
        String correlationId
) {
}
```

Service:

```java id="ymyjpp"
public interface CaseSubmissionApplicationService {
    SubmitCaseResult submit(SubmitCaseCommand command);
}
```

Resource:

```java id="wylnvt"
@POST
@Path("/{caseId}/submit")
public Response submitCase(
        @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
        @HeaderParam("X-Correlation-ID") String correlationId,
        @Valid SubmitCaseRequest request
) {
    SubmitCaseResult result = service.submit(new SubmitCaseCommand(
            caseId,
            request.submittedBy(),
            request.comment(),
            correlationId
    ));

    return Response.accepted(new SubmitCaseResponse(
            result.caseId(),
            result.status().name()
    )).build();
}
```

Test dengan captor:

```java id="m0294s"
@Test
void shouldMapHttpRequestToSubmitCaseCommand() {
    when(service.submit(any(SubmitCaseCommand.class)))
            .thenReturn(new SubmitCaseResult(
                    "CASE-001",
                    CaseStatus.SUBMITTED
            ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .header("X-Correlation-ID", "corr-123")
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "user-123",
                      "comment": "Ready for review"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(202);

    ArgumentCaptor<SubmitCaseCommand> captor =
            ArgumentCaptor.forClass(SubmitCaseCommand.class);

    verify(service).submit(captor.capture());

    assertThat(captor.getValue()).isEqualTo(new SubmitCaseCommand(
            "CASE-001",
            "user-123",
            "Ready for review",
            "corr-123"
    ));
}
```

Ini sangat berguna untuk resource layer karena resource sering hanya melakukan mapping HTTP → command.

# 16. Header Testing

## Location header untuk create

Resource:

```java id="78xrh6"
@POST
public Response createCase(@Valid CreateCaseRequest request) {
    CreateCaseResult result = service.create(request);

    URI location = URI.create("/cases/" + result.caseId());

    return Response.created(location)
            .entity(new CreateCaseResponse(result.caseId()))
            .build();
}
```

Test:

```java id="eng9hl"
@Test
void shouldReturnCreatedWithLocationHeader() {
    when(service.create(any()))
            .thenReturn(new CreateCaseResult("CASE-001"));

    Response response = target("/cases")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "title": "New case"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(201);
    assertThat(response.getHeaderString("Location"))
            .endsWith("/cases/CASE-001");
}
```

Headers yang sering penting:

```text id="ew6k1s"
Location
Content-Type
ETag
Cache-Control
X-Correlation-ID
Retry-After
Deprecation/Sunset headers jika API lifecycle dipakai
```

---

# 17. Filter Testing: Correlation ID

Filter:

```java id="5j3rba"
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.UUID;

@Provider
public class CorrelationIdFilter
        implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    public static final String HEADER = "X-Correlation-ID";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext)
            throws IOException {
        String correlationId = requestContext.getHeaderString(HEADER);

        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        requestContext.setProperty(HEADER, correlationId);
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) throws IOException {
        Object correlationId = requestContext.getProperty(HEADER);

        if (correlationId != null) {
            responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle(HEADER, correlationId);
        }
    }
}
```

Register:

```java id="a3jzwh"
@Override
protected Application configure() {
    return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(new HealthResource())
            .register(new CorrelationIdFilter());
}
```

Test existing correlation ID:

```java id="k3o8qw"
@Test
void shouldEchoCorrelationIdHeader() {
    Response response = target("/health")
            .request()
            .header("X-Correlation-ID", "corr-123")
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);
    assertThat(response.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-ID"))
            .isEqualTo("corr-123");
}
```

Test generated correlation ID:

```java id="e015tt"
@Test
void shouldGenerateCorrelationIdWhenMissing() {
    Response response = target("/health")
            .request()
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);
    assertThat(response.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-ID"))
            .isNotBlank();
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="txqwfw"
Untuk production, UUID generation sebaiknya bisa dikontrol jika butuh deterministik.
Namun untuk test header generated, cukup assert not blank jika format tidak distandarkan.
```

---

# 18. Testing Unsupported Media Type

Kalau endpoint hanya menerima JSON:

```java id="03cl75"
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
```

Test:

```java id="vh3p9e"
@Test
void shouldReturnUnsupportedMediaTypeWhenContentTypeIsTextPlain() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.entity(
                    "submittedBy=user-123",
                    MediaType.TEXT_PLAIN
            ));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(415);

    verifyNoInteractions(caseSubmissionService);
}
```

Ini penting karena consumer kadang salah kirim `Content-Type`.

# 19. Testing Malformed JSON

```java id="amcikq"
@Test
void shouldReturnBadRequestWhenJsonIsMalformed() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "user-123",
                      "comment": 
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);

    verifyNoInteractions(caseSubmissionService);
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="sxjv54"
Malformed JSON harus berhenti di boundary.
Service tidak boleh dipanggil.
```

# 20. Testing Path Param

Resource:

```java id="1u0072"
@GET
@Path("/{caseId}")
public Response getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
    CaseDto result = service.getById(caseId);
    return Response.ok(result).build();
}
```

Test:

```java id="viq2z4"
@Test
void shouldPassPathParamToService() {
    when(service.getById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(new CaseDto("CASE-001", "SUBMITTED"));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);

    verify(service).getById("CASE-001");
}
```

Jika path param punya format tertentu, misalnya hanya uppercase alphanumeric, pertimbangkan:

```text id="nqz9ys"
- regex path constraint di resource
- validation di command validator
- resource test untuk invalid format
```

# 21. Testing Query Param

Endpoint:

```http id="7g7skl"
GET /cases?status=SUBMITTED&assignee=user-123&page=0&size=20
```

Command:

```java id="srpwzr"
public record SearchCaseQuery(
        CaseStatus status,
        String assignee,
        int page,
        int size
) {
}
```

Resource:

```java id="onjbq8"
@GET
public Response searchCases(
        @QueryParam("status") CaseStatus status,
        @QueryParam("assignee") String assignee,
        @DefaultValue("0") @QueryParam("page") int page,
        @DefaultValue("20") @QueryParam("size") int size
) {
    SearchCaseResult result = service.search(new SearchCaseQuery(
            status,
            assignee,
            page,
            size
    ));

    return Response.ok(result).build();
}
```

Test:

```java id="5l2uwx"
@Test
void shouldMapQueryParamsToSearchQuery() {
    when(service.search(any(SearchCaseQuery.class)))
            .thenReturn(new SearchCaseResult(List.of(), 0));

    Response response = target("/cases")
            .queryParam("status", "SUBMITTED")
            .queryParam("assignee", "user-123")
            .queryParam("page", "2")
            .queryParam("size", "50")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);

    ArgumentCaptor<SearchCaseQuery> captor =
            ArgumentCaptor.forClass(SearchCaseQuery.class);

    verify(service).search(captor.capture());

    assertThat(captor.getValue()).isEqualTo(new SearchCaseQuery(
            CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
            "user-123",
            2,
            50
    ));
}
```

Test default value:

```java id="qg1zog"
@Test
void shouldUseDefaultPaginationWhenPageAndSizeAreMissing() {
    when(service.search(any(SearchCaseQuery.class)))
            .thenReturn(new SearchCaseResult(List.of(), 0));

    Response response = target("/cases")
            .queryParam("status", "SUBMITTED")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);

    ArgumentCaptor<SearchCaseQuery> captor =
            ArgumentCaptor.forClass(SearchCaseQuery.class);

    verify(service).search(captor.capture());

    assertThat(captor.getValue().page()).isEqualTo(0);
    assertThat(captor.getValue().size()).isEqualTo(20);
}
```

# 24. Resource Test vs Service Test

Contoh rule:

```text id="yw7o38"
Case APPROVED tidak boleh disubmit ulang.
```

Jangan test semua state matrix di resource test.

Buruk:

```text id="dwruna"
Resource test untuk:
DRAFT
SUBMITTED
UNDER_REVIEW
APPROVED
REJECTED
CANCELLED
CLOSED
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="nzojis"
Service/policy test:
- semua state transition matrix

Resource test:
- satu contoh invalid state menghasilkan HTTP 409
```

Resource test cukup memastikan exception dari service dimapping ke HTTP response yang benar.

---

# 25. Resource Test vs Integration Test

## Resource test

```text id="czxev4"
Service mocked/faked.
DB tidak nyata.
Cepat.
Fokus HTTP boundary.
```

## Integration test

```text id="f9m1ve"
Service nyata.
Repository nyata.
MyBatis nyata.
PostgreSQL nyata.
Lebih lambat.
Fokus wiring + persistence behavior.
```

Contoh pembagian:

| Scenario | Resource test | Integration test |
|---|---:|---:|
| POST `/cases/{id}/submit` return 202 | Ya | Mungkin satu happy path |
| Invalid JSON return 400 | Ya | Tidak perlu |
| Missing field return 400 | Ya | Tidak perlu |
| Service throws invalid state → 409 | Ya | Tidak perlu semua variant |
| MyBatis update status benar | Tidak | Ya |
| Audit log persisted | Tidak | Ya |
| Transaction rollback | Tidak | Ya |
| DB unique idempotency key | Tidak | Ya |

---

# 26. Practical Test Data Strategy

Untuk resource test, gunakan JSON literal atau fixture file.

## Inline JSON cocok untuk payload kecil

```java id="bwjhl1"
Entity.json("""
        {
          "submittedBy": "user-123",
          "comment": "Ready for review"
        }
        """)
```

## Fixture file cocok untuk payload besar

```text id="aecui9"
src/test/resources/payloads/submit-case-request.json
src/test/resources/payloads/create-case-request-invalid.json
```

Helper:

```java id="ercbys"
public final class TestPayloads {

    private TestPayloads() {
    }

    public static String readPayload(String path) {
        try (InputStream inputStream =
                     TestPayloads.class.getResourceAsStream(path)) {
            if (inputStream == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Payload not found: " + path);
            }

            return new String(inputStream.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(exception);
        }
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java id="97h36d"
String payload = TestPayloads.readPayload(
        "/payloads/submit-case-request.json"
);
```

Prinsip:

```text id="f8axpy"
Payload kecil inline lebih readable.
Payload besar pakai fixture file.
Jangan sembunyikan payload penting tanpa alasan.
```

# 27. Suggested Package Layout

```text id="2xr8xc"
src/test/java/com/company/caseapp/resource
├── CaseResourceTest.java
├── CaseSearchResourceTest.java
├── CaseExceptionMapperTest.java
├── ValidationResourceTest.java
├── CorrelationIdFilterTest.java
└── support
    ├── ResourceTestSupport.java
    ├── TestPayloads.java
    └── JsonAssertions.java
```

Kalau resource banyak:

```text id="8f7q6r"
resource
├── case
│   ├── CaseCommandResourceTest.java
│   ├── CaseQueryResourceTest.java
│   └── CaseExceptionMapperTest.java
├── payment
│   ├── PaymentResourceTest.java
│   └── PaymentExceptionMapperTest.java
└── support
```

---

# 28. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Resource test start full app | Lambat, mirip integration test | Register komponen relevan saja |
| Semua business matrix dites via HTTP | Duplikasi dan lambat | Matrix di service/policy test |
| Service mock return terlalu detail | Test jadi tidak natural | Mock hanya boundary result |
| Tidak test validation | Invalid payload bocor ke service | Test 400 + no service interaction |
| Tidak test error mapper | Error contract tidak stabil | Register mapper dan assert body |
| Assert JSON raw urutan field | Rapuh | Assert parsed body atau JSON semantic |
| Tidak test headers | Contract kurang lengkap | Assert `Location`, correlation, content type |
| Pakai DB di resource test | Boundary kabur | Pindahkan ke integration test |
| Test hanya status code | Body contract bisa rusak | Assert status + key fields |
| Validation field path terlalu spesifik | Rapuh antar provider | Standarkan mapper atau assert longgar |

---

# 29. Practical Resource Test Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini untuk endpoint penting:

```text id="e0e9yo"
[ ] Path dan HTTP method benar.
[ ] Request JSON valid bisa diparse.
[ ] Request DTO diteruskan ke service/command dengan benar.
[ ] Response status code benar.
[ ] Response body field penting benar.
[ ] Content-Type benar.
[ ] Header penting diuji.
[ ] Validation error return 400.
[ ] Service tidak dipanggil saat request invalid.
[ ] Domain exception dimapping ke status code benar.
[ ] Error response punya code/message/details sesuai contract.
[ ] Malformed JSON return 400.
[ ] Unsupported media type return 415 jika relevan.
[ ] Query/path/header param mapping diuji.
[ ] Tidak memakai DB nyata kecuali memang integration test.
```

---

# 30. Minimal Test Set per Endpoint

Untuk endpoint command seperti:

```http id="qm9x72"
POST /cases/{caseId}/submit
```

Minimal resource test:

```text id="lim5k6"
1. valid request → expected success status + response body
2. invalid request body → 400 + service not called
3. service throws not found → 404
4. service throws forbidden → 403
5. service throws invalid state/conflict → 409
6. malformed JSON → 400
7. unsupported media type → 415
8. required header missing → 400/401 depending design
```

Untuk endpoint query seperti:

```http id="yzvh0y"
GET /cases?status=SUBMITTED&page=0&size=20
```

Minimal resource test:

```text id="tm5tvs"
1. query params mapped correctly
2. default pagination works
3. invalid enum/query value → 400
4. successful response body shape
5. service throws not found/forbidden if applicable
```

---

```text id="5noa50"
Sesuaikan artifact JSON provider dengan stack perusahaan:
- Jackson
- JSON-B
- MOXy
```

Jersey documentation menyediakan bagian khusus untuk Bean Validation support dan dependency/configuration terkait. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/bean-validation.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 32. Maven Surefire Reminder

Resource test biasanya tetap bernama:

```text id="3p59rh"
*Test.java
```

Dan dijalankan oleh Surefire:

```bash id="gv9gcu"
mvn test
```

Maven Surefire documentation menyebut sejak Surefire 3.6.0, JUnit Platform menjadi mekanisme eksekusi terpadu untuk test frameworks yang didukung. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-surefire-plugin/examples/junit-platform.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Jika resource test mulai lambat karena container/DB, berarti kemungkinan sudah berubah menjadi integration test dan harus dinamai:

```text id="4o5m1l"
*IT.java
```

Lalu dijalankan via:

```bash id="4n3l6j"
mvn verify
```

# 33. Ringkasan Seri 5

Inti dari resource test:

```text id="eix18c"
Resource test membuktikan HTTP boundary,
bukan membuktikan database, workflow engine, atau external system.
```

Yang harus dikuasai:

```text id="wo9acn"
JerseyTest
ResourceConfig
request JSON
response JSON
status code
headers
path/query/header param
Bean Validation
ExceptionMapper
filters/interceptors
mock/fake service
```

Boundary yang benar:

```text id="q91ix5"
Resource test:
- HTTP mapping
- validation
- serialization
- exception mapping
- headers

Service unit test:
- business rule
- state transition
- permission decision

Integration test:
- MyBatis
- PostgreSQL
- transaction
- Camunda
- external systems
```

# Seri 6/14 - ExceptionMapper, Validation, Filter, and Serialization Testing  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini memperdalam **HTTP boundary testing** pada aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS**.

Kalau Seri 5 membahas resource test secara umum, Seri 6 fokus pada cross-cutting behavior yang sering menjadi sumber bug API enterprise:

```text id="sv5mim"
ExceptionMapper
Validation response
Correlation ID filter
Auth/request context filter
Response headers
JSON serialization/deserialization
Enum/date/time format
Error contract consistency
```

Di aplikasi production, consumer tidak hanya peduli endpoint berhasil. Consumer juga sangat bergantung pada:

```text id="cbrp3z"
- error code stabil
- HTTP status konsisten
- validation detail jelas
- trace/correlation ID selalu ada
- enum/date JSON tidak berubah mendadak
- unauthorized vs forbidden tidak tertukar
- internal exception tidak bocor ke response
```

Jakarta REST mendefinisikan provider model seperti `ExceptionMapper`, filter, dan response/request processing; `ExceptionMapper` harus diregister secara programmatic atau diberi `@Provider` agar ditemukan runtime. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jersey juga menyediakan dukungan Bean Validation, termasuk dependency, configuration, registration, dan usage. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/bean-validation.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model: HTTP Boundary Contract

```text id="0zucpw"
HTTP Boundary Contract
│
├── Success contract
│   ├── status code
│   ├── response body
│   ├── headers
│   └── media type
│
├── Error contract
│   ├── status code
│   ├── error code
│   ├── message
│   ├── details
│   └── trace/correlation id
│
├── Validation contract
│   ├── field errors
│   ├── object-level errors
│   ├── query/path/header errors
│   └── service not called when invalid
│
├── Filter contract
│   ├── correlation id
│   ├── auth principal
│   ├── tenant context
│   ├── request metadata
│   └── response enrichment
│
└── Serialization contract
    ├── enum format
    ├── date/time format
    ├── null handling
    ├── unknown fields
    └── naming strategy
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | Fokus testing | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|---|
| ExceptionMapper | Domain exception → HTTP response | `404`, `403`, `409`, `422`, `500` | Consumer butuh error yang stabil |
| Validation | Bean Validation response | Missing field, blank, invalid enum, invalid query | Invalid request berhenti di boundary |
| Error contract | Standard API error model | `code`, `message`, `details`, `traceId` | Debugging dan API compatibility |
| Filter | Correlation ID | Request tanpa ID dibuatkan, request dengan ID diecho | Observability |
| Filter | Auth context | Header/token diparse ke principal/context | Authorization boundary |
| Filter | Tenant context | Tenant header wajib/valid | Multi-tenant isolation |
| Serialization | JSON provider config | Jackson/JSON-B behavior | Contract JSON tidak berubah |
| Serialization | Enum | `SUBMITTED` vs `submitted` vs object | Consumer compatibility |
| Serialization | Date/time | ISO-8601, timezone, offset | Bug timezone sangat umum |
| Deserialization | Unknown/missing fields | Strict vs tolerant API | Backward compatibility |
| Security | Error leakage prevention | Stacktrace/internal message tidak bocor | Security/compliance |
| Test architecture | Provider-focused tests | Mapper/filter/serialization punya test sendiri | Test tidak membengkak |

---

# 4. Recommended Error Response Model

Gunakan satu model error standar.

```java id="8bisep"
import java.util.List;

public record ApiErrorResponse(
        String code,
        String message,
        List<ApiErrorDetail> details,
        String traceId
) {
}
```

```java id="m5irsc"
public record ApiErrorDetail(
        String field,
        String issue,
        String rejectedValue
) {
}
```

Contoh error response:

```json id="tdbyhh"
{
  "code": "CASE_INVALID_STATE",
  "message": "Case cannot be submitted from current status",
  "details": [
    {
      "field": "status",
      "issue": "must be DRAFT",
      "rejectedValue": "APPROVED"
    }
  ],
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

Prinsip praktis:

```text id="z6s4ko"
HTTP status menjelaskan kategori error.
error code menjelaskan error bisnis/aplikasi secara stabil.
message boleh human-readable.
details menjelaskan field/object error.
traceId membantu debugging lintas service.
```

---

# 5. Error Code Taxonomy

Gunakan taxonomy yang konsisten.

| Error category | HTTP status | Example code | Contoh |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Validation | 400 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | Field wajib kosong |
| Authentication | 401 | `AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED` | Token/header hilang |
| Authorization | 403 | `FORBIDDEN_ACTION` | User tidak boleh approve |
| Not found | 404 | `CASE_NOT_FOUND` | Case ID tidak ada |
| Conflict/state | 409 | `CASE_INVALID_STATE` | Submit case yang sudah approved |
| Idempotency conflict | 409 | `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT` | Key sama, payload beda |
| Unsupported media | 415 | `UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE` | Kirim text/plain ke endpoint JSON |
| Unprocessable domain | 422 | `BUSINESS_RULE_VIOLATION` | Request syntactically valid, rule gagal |
| Unexpected | 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Bug/exception tidak terduga |
| Dependency unavailable | 503 | `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE` | External system down |

Catatan: `400`, `409`, dan `422` sering diperdebatkan. Yang terpenting adalah **konsisten dan terdokumentasi**.

---

# 6. ExceptionMapper untuk Domain Exception

## Exception

```java id="b930ob"
public class CaseNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String caseId;

    public CaseNotFoundException(String caseId) {
        super("Case not found: " + caseId);
        this.caseId = caseId;
    }

    public String caseId() {
        return caseId;
    }
}
```

## Mapper

```java id="4o42mj"
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Context;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;

import java.util.List;

@Provider
public class CaseNotFoundExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<CaseNotFoundException> {

    @Context
    private ContainerRequestContext requestContext;

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(CaseNotFoundException exception) {
        String traceId = (String) requestContext.getProperty("X-Correlation-ID");

        return Response.status(Response.Status.NOT_FOUND)
                .entity(new ApiErrorResponse(
                        "CASE_NOT_FOUND",
                        "Case was not found",
                        List.of(new ApiErrorDetail(
                                "caseId",
                                "does not exist",
                                exception.caseId()
                        )),
                        traceId
                ))
                .build();
    }
}
```

## Test

```java id="pc5jln"
class CaseNotFoundExceptionMapperTest extends JerseyTest {

    private CaseQueryService service;

    @Override
    protected Application configure() {
        service = mock(CaseQueryService.class);

        return new ResourceConfig()
                .register(new CaseResource(service))
                .register(new CaseNotFoundExceptionMapper())
                .register(new CorrelationIdFilter());
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMapCaseNotFoundTo404ErrorResponse() {
        when(service.getCase("CASE-404"))
                .thenThrow(new CaseNotFoundException("CASE-404"));

        Response response = target("/cases/CASE-404")
                .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .header("X-Correlation-ID", "corr-123")
                .get();

        assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(404);

        ApiErrorResponse error = response.readEntity(ApiErrorResponse.class);

        assertThat(error.code()).isEqualTo("CASE_NOT_FOUND");
        assertThat(error.message()).isEqualTo("Case was not found");
        assertThat(error.traceId()).isEqualTo("corr-123");
        assertThat(error.details())
                .containsExactly(new ApiErrorDetail(
                        "caseId",
                        "does not exist",
                        "CASE-404"
                ));
    }
}
```

# 7. ExceptionMapper untuk Invalid State / Conflict

```java id="0q0p79"
public class InvalidCaseStateException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String caseId;
    private final CaseStatus currentStatus;
    private final CaseStatus expectedStatus;

    public InvalidCaseStateException(
            String caseId,
            CaseStatus currentStatus,
            CaseStatus expectedStatus
    ) {
        super("Case " + caseId + " is in " + currentStatus
                + ", expected " + expectedStatus);
        this.caseId = caseId;
        this.currentStatus = currentStatus;
        this.expectedStatus = expectedStatus;
    }

    public String caseId() {
        return caseId;
    }

    public CaseStatus currentStatus() {
        return currentStatus;
    }

    public CaseStatus expectedStatus() {
        return expectedStatus;
    }
}
```

Mapper:

```java id="b0ml29"
@Provider
public class InvalidCaseStateExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<InvalidCaseStateException> {

    @Context
    private ContainerRequestContext requestContext;

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(InvalidCaseStateException exception) {
        String traceId = (String) requestContext.getProperty("X-Correlation-ID");

        return Response.status(Response.Status.CONFLICT)
                .entity(new ApiErrorResponse(
                        "CASE_INVALID_STATE",
                        "Case is not in a valid state for this operation",
                        List.of(
                                new ApiErrorDetail(
                                        "caseId",
                                        "operation is not allowed for this case",
                                        exception.caseId()
                                ),
                                new ApiErrorDetail(
                                        "status",
                                        "expected " + exception.expectedStatus(),
                                        exception.currentStatus().name()
                                )
                        ),
                        traceId
                ))
                .build();
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="whadfj"
@Test
void shouldMapInvalidCaseStateTo409() {
    when(service.submit(any()))
            .thenThrow(new InvalidCaseStateException(
                    "CASE-001",
                    CaseStatus.APPROVED,
                    CaseStatus.DRAFT
            ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .header("X-Correlation-ID", "corr-123")
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "user-123"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(409);

    ApiErrorResponse error = response.readEntity(ApiErrorResponse.class);

    assertThat(error.code()).isEqualTo("CASE_INVALID_STATE");
    assertThat(error.traceId()).isEqualTo("corr-123");
    assertThat(error.details())
            .anySatisfy(detail -> {
                assertThat(detail.field()).isEqualTo("status");
                assertThat(detail.rejectedValue()).isEqualTo("APPROVED");
            });
}
```

# 8. Catch-All ExceptionMapper

Catch-all mapper penting, tetapi harus hati-hati agar tidak menelan exception yang harus dimapping spesifik.

```java id="1k7xhv"
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.util.List;

@Provider
public class UnhandledExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .entity(new ApiErrorResponse(
                        "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                        "An unexpected error occurred",
                        List.of(),
                        null
                ))
                .build();
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="7iq7q1"
@Test
void shouldNotExposeInternalExceptionDetails() {
    when(service.getCase("CASE-001"))
            .thenThrow(new IllegalStateException(
                    "SQL password=secret internal stack detail"
            ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(500);

    String body = response.readEntity(String.class);

    assertThat(body).contains("INTERNAL_ERROR");
    assertThat(body).contains("An unexpected error occurred");
    assertThat(body).doesNotContain("password");
    assertThat(body).doesNotContain("SQL");
    assertThat(body).doesNotContain("stack");
}
```

Prinsip security:

```text id="g48nhn"
Internal exception boleh masuk log.
Internal exception tidak boleh bocor ke API response.
```

# 9. Validation Testing: Request Body

Request DTO:

```java id="j52g0e"
public record SubmitCaseRequest(
        @NotBlank String submittedBy,
        @Size(max = 500) String comment
) {
}
```

Resource:

```java id="4ttzg1"
@POST
@Path("/{caseId}/submit")
public Response submit(
        @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
        @Valid SubmitCaseRequest request
) {
    SubmitCaseResult result = service.submit(new SubmitCaseCommand(
            caseId,
            request.submittedBy(),
            request.comment()
    ));

    return Response.accepted(new SubmitCaseResponse(
            result.caseId(),
            result.status().name()
    )).build();
}
```

Test blank field:

```java id="bztnp7"
@Test
void shouldReturn400WhenSubmittedByIsBlank() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "",
                      "comment": "Ready"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);

    verifyNoInteractions(service);
}
```

Test max length:

```java id="iq4bbj"
@Test
void shouldReturn400WhenCommentIsTooLong() {
    String longComment = "x".repeat(501);

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "user-123",
                      "comment": "%s"
                    }
                    """.formatted(longComment)));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);

    verifyNoInteractions(service);
}
```

Jakarta Validation mendefinisikan constraint annotations untuk memvalidasi objek, method, dan constructor; Jersey menyediakan integrasi khusus agar validation bekerja di resource layer. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/bean-validation/3.1/jakarta-validation-spec-3.1.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 10. Custom Validation Error Mapper

Tipe exception validation bisa berbeda tergantung versi Jersey/Jakarta Validation dan konfigurasi. Prinsip desainnya: normalisasi exception provider menjadi `ApiErrorResponse`.

Pseudo mapper:

```java id="n49n0a"
@Provider
public class ValidationExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<ConstraintViolationException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(ConstraintViolationException exception) {
        List<ApiErrorDetail> details = exception.getConstraintViolations()
                .stream()
                .map(violation -> new ApiErrorDetail(
                        normalizeFieldPath(violation.getPropertyPath().toString()),
                        violation.getMessage(),
                        String.valueOf(violation.getInvalidValue())
                ))
                .toList();

        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                .entity(new ApiErrorResponse(
                        "VALIDATION_ERROR",
                        "Request validation failed",
                        details,
                        null
                ))
                .build();
    }

    private String normalizeFieldPath(String rawPath) {
        int lastDot = rawPath.lastIndexOf('.');
        if (lastDot >= 0) {
            return rawPath.substring(lastDot + 1);
        }
        return rawPath;
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="og3dn5"
@Test
void shouldReturnStandardValidationErrorBody() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "",
                      "comment": "Ready"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);

    ApiErrorResponse error = response.readEntity(ApiErrorResponse.class);

    assertThat(error.code()).isEqualTo("VALIDATION_ERROR");
    assertThat(error.message()).isEqualTo("Request validation failed");
    assertThat(error.details())
            .anySatisfy(detail -> {
                assertThat(detail.field()).contains("submittedBy");
                assertThat(detail.issue()).isNotBlank();
            });

    verifyNoInteractions(service);
}
```

Practical note:

```text id="p1cim6"
Jangan biarkan raw provider validation error menjadi API contract.
Normalisasi dulu supaya consumer tidak bergantung pada detail Jersey/Hibernate Validator.
```

Jangan hanya validasi body. Query/header/path param juga bagian dari contract.

```java id="fhwj4u"
@GET
public Response search(
        @Pattern(regexp = "DRAFT|SUBMITTED|APPROVED")
        @QueryParam("status") String status,

        @Min(0)
        @DefaultValue("0")
        @QueryParam("page") int page,

        @Min(1)
        @Max(100)
        @DefaultValue("20")
        @QueryParam("size") int size
) {
    SearchCaseResult result = service.search(status, page, size);
    return Response.ok(result).build();
}
```

# 12. Correlation ID Filter

## Filter

```java id="2qv1u4"
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.UUID;

@Provider
public class CorrelationIdFilter
        implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    public static final String HEADER = "X-Correlation-ID";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext)
            throws IOException {
        String correlationId = requestContext.getHeaderString(HEADER);

        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        requestContext.setProperty(HEADER, correlationId);
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) throws IOException {
        Object correlationId = requestContext.getProperty(HEADER);

        if (correlationId != null) {
            responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle(HEADER, correlationId);
        }
    }
}
```

Jakarta REST defines request and response filters as provider-based extension points around request/response processing. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Test echo existing ID

```java id="f60rjw"

void shouldEchoExistingCorrelationId() {
    Response response = target("/health")
            .request()
            .header("X-Correlation-ID", "corr-123")
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);
    assertThat(response.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-ID"))
            .isEqualTo("corr-123");
}
```

## Test generated ID

```java id="taydvf"
@Test
void shouldGenerateCorrelationIdWhenMissing() {
    Response response = target("/health")
            .request()
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);
    assertThat(response.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-ID"))
            .isNotBlank();
}
```

# 13. Auth Filter Testing

Contoh sederhana: header `X-User-ID` wajib.

## Auth context

```java id="thkzbw"
public record AuthenticatedUser(
        String userId,
        Set<String> roles
) {
}
```

## Filter

```java id="36onlr"
@Provider
public class AuthContextFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    public static final String USER_CONTEXT = "authenticatedUser";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext)
            throws IOException {
        String userId = requestContext.getHeaderString("X-User-ID");

        if (userId == null || userId.isBlank()) {
            requestContext.abortWith(Response.status(Response.Status.UNAUTHORIZED)
                    .entity(new ApiErrorResponse(
                            "AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED",
                            "Authentication is required",
                            List.of(),
                            null
                    ))
                    .build());
            return;
        }

        requestContext.setProperty(
                USER_CONTEXT,
                new AuthenticatedUser(userId, Set.of())
        );
    }
}
```

## Resource reads context

```java id="dt5rts"
@POST
@Path("/{caseId}/submit")
public Response submit(
        @Context ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
        @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
        @Valid SubmitCaseRequest request
) {
    AuthenticatedUser user =
            (AuthenticatedUser) requestContext.getProperty(
                    AuthContextFilter.USER_CONTEXT
            );

    SubmitCaseResult result = service.submit(new SubmitCaseCommand(
            caseId,
            user.userId(),
            request.comment()
    ));

    return Response.accepted(new SubmitCaseResponse(
            result.caseId(),
            result.status().name()
    )).build();
}
```

```java id="2dvu6c"
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)

                    {
                      "comment": "Ready"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(401);

    ApiErrorResponse error = response.readEntity(ApiErrorResponse.class);

    assertThat(error.code()).isEqualTo("AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED");

}
```

## Test context passed to service

```java id="8a045o"
@Test
void shouldPassAuthenticatedUserToCommand() {
    when(service.submit(any(SubmitCaseCommand.class)))
            .thenReturn(new SubmitCaseResult(
                    "CASE-001",
                    CaseStatus.SUBMITTED
            ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .header("X-User-ID", "user-123")
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "comment": "Ready"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(202);

    ArgumentCaptor<SubmitCaseCommand> captor =
            ArgumentCaptor.forClass(SubmitCaseCommand.class);

    verify(service).submit(captor.capture());

    assertThat(captor.getValue().submittedBy())
            .isEqualTo("user-123");
}
```

# 14. Tenant Context Filter Testing

Untuk multi-tenant system, tenant header sering critical.

```java id="n5zkuw"
@Provider
public class TenantContextFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    public static final String TENANT_CONTEXT = "tenantId";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext)
            throws IOException {
        String tenantId = requestContext.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-ID");

        if (tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank()) {
            requestContext.abortWith(Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                    .entity(new ApiErrorResponse(
                            "TENANT_REQUIRED",
                            "Tenant id is required",
                            List.of(new ApiErrorDetail(
                                    "X-Tenant-ID",
                                    "header is required",
                                    null
                            )),
                            null
                    ))
                    .build());
            return;
        }

        requestContext.setProperty(TENANT_CONTEXT, tenantId);
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="ferfse"
@Test
void shouldReturn400WhenTenantHeaderIsMissing() {
    Response response = target("/cases")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .header("X-User-ID", "user-123")
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);

    ApiErrorResponse error = response.readEntity(ApiErrorResponse.class);

    assertThat(error.code()).isEqualTo("TENANT_REQUIRED");

    verifyNoInteractions(service);
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="pbrc99"
Tenant context failure harus berhenti sebelum service layer agar tidak ada risiko cross-tenant access.
```

# 15. Serialization Testing: Enum Format

Misalnya public API ingin status lowercase:

```json id="01w0lh"
{
  "status": "submitted"
}
```

Jangan biarkan enum `name()` bocor jika contract butuh lowercase.

DTO:

```java id="qshl92"
public record CaseResponse(
        String caseId,
        String status
) {
    public static CaseResponse from(CaseDto dto) {
        return new CaseResponse(
                dto.caseId(),
                dto.status().name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)
        );
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="ivf6uz"
@Test
void shouldSerializeCaseStatusAsLowercase() {
    when(service.getCase("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(new CaseDto(
                    "CASE-001",
                    CaseStatus.SUBMITTED
            ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);

    String json = response.readEntity(String.class);

    assertThat(json).contains("\"status\":\"submitted\"");
    assertThat(json).doesNotContain("\"SUBMITTED\"");
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="z0vxlx"
Enum internal boleh berubah.
Enum API adalah contract.
Pisahkan jika perlu.
```

# 16. Serialization Testing: Date/Time Format

Gunakan format eksplisit, misalnya ISO-8601 instant:

```json id="a8q2x3"
{
  "submittedAt": "2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"
}
```

DTO:

```java id="amrn81"
public record CaseResponse(
        String caseId,
        String status,
        Instant submittedAt
) {
}
```

Test:

```java id="r1trd7"
@Test
void shouldSerializeInstantAsIso8601Utc() {
    when(service.getCase("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(new CaseResponse(
                    "CASE-001",
                    "submitted",
                    Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
            ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(200);

    String json = response.readEntity(String.class);

    assertThat(json)
            .contains("\"submittedAt\":\"2026-07-15T10:00:00Z\"");
}
```

Common bug:

```text id="6owlh5"
- LocalDateTime tanpa timezone
- server timezone mempengaruhi response
- timestamp serialized sebagai epoch padahal consumer expect string
- offset hilang
```

# 17. Jackson Provider Configuration Test

Jersey supports common media type representations and includes JSON/Jackson examples for consume/produce JSON. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/media.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jika stack Anda menggunakan Jackson, buat provider/config yang eksplisit.

```java id="lu4mc3"
@Provider
public class ObjectMapperProvider
        implements ContextResolver<ObjectMapper> {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public ObjectMapperProvider() {
        this.objectMapper = JsonMapper.builder()
                .addModule(new JavaTimeModule())
                .disable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_AS_TIMESTAMPS)
                .disable(DeserializationFeature.FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES)
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    public ObjectMapper getContext(Class<?> type) {
        return objectMapper;
    }
}
```

Register di test:

```java id="kaxv3c"
@Override
protected Application configure() {
    return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(new CaseResource(service))
            .register(ObjectMapperProvider.class)
            .register(JacksonFeature.class);
}
```

Test date format:

```java id="82kmmc"
@Test
void shouldUseConfiguredObjectMapperForJavaTime() {
    when(service.getCase("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(new CaseResponse(
                    "CASE-001",
                    "submitted",
                    Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
            ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .get();

    String json = response.readEntity(String.class);

    assertThat(json).contains("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z");
}
```

---

# 18. Deserialization Testing: Unknown Fields

Untuk backward/forward compatibility, API sering dibuat tolerant terhadap unknown fields.

Request:

```json id="6hicsd"
{
  "submittedBy": "user-123",
  "comment": "Ready",
  "futureField": "ignored"
}
```

Test:

```java id="bt4j3k"
@Test
void shouldIgnoreUnknownJsonFieldsWhenConfiguredAsTolerant() {
    when(service.submit(any()))
            .thenReturn(new SubmitCaseResult(
                    "CASE-001",
                    CaseStatus.SUBMITTED
            ));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "user-123",
                      "comment": "Ready",
                      "futureField": "ignored"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(202);

    verify(service).submit(any());
}
```

Jika perusahaan memilih strict API, expectation-nya berlawanan:

```text id="qml87s"
Unknown field → 400
```

Yang penting adalah jangan ambigu.

---

# 19. Deserialization Testing: Missing vs Null

Payload missing:

```json id="id2htt"
{
  "comment": "Ready"
}
```

Payload explicit null:

```json id="u9geqq"
{
  "submittedBy": null,
  "comment": "Ready"
}
```

Test keduanya:

```java id="qwm2m7"
@Test
void shouldReturn400WhenSubmittedByIsMissing() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "comment": "Ready"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);
    verifyNoInteractions(service);
}
```

```java id="8b3syz"
@Test
void shouldReturn400WhenSubmittedByIsNull() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "submittedBy": null,
                      "comment": "Ready"
                    }
                    """));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(400);
    verifyNoInteractions(service);
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="njnxzt"
Missing dan null sering punya behavior berbeda.
Untuk field wajib, test keduanya.
```

---

# 20. Media Type Testing

```java id="njnyq3"
@Test
void shouldReturn415WhenContentTypeIsNotJson() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001/submit")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .post(Entity.entity(
                    "submittedBy=user-123",
                    MediaType.TEXT_PLAIN
            ));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(415);

    verifyNoInteractions(service);
}
```

Test `Accept` header jika API mendukung content negotiation:

```java id="3pk1qk"
@Test
void shouldReturnNotAcceptableWhenAcceptHeaderIsUnsupported() {
    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-001")
            .request(MediaType.TEXT_HTML)
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(406);
}
```

---

# 21. Testing Error Response Content-Type

Error response juga harus JSON.

```java id="snktej"
@Test
void shouldReturnJsonContentTypeForErrorResponse() {
    when(service.getCase("CASE-404"))
            .thenThrow(new CaseNotFoundException("CASE-404"));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-404")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(404);
    assertThat(response.getHeaderString("Content-Type"))
            .contains("application/json");
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="0t30xt"
Success JSON tapi error HTML/plaintext adalah API contract problem.
```

---

# 22. Provider Registration Test

Masalah umum: mapper/filter ada di codebase, tetapi tidak ter-register di runtime.

Resource test harus register provider sama seperti production composition root.

```java id="wdrgeo"
@Override
protected Application configure() {
    return new ResourceConfig()
            .register(CaseResource.class)
            .register(CaseNotFoundExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(InvalidCaseStateExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(ValidationExceptionMapper.class)
            .register(CorrelationIdFilter.class)
            .register(AuthContextFilter.class)
            .register(ObjectMapperProvider.class)
            .register(JacksonFeature.class);
}
```

Test smoke provider:

```java id="tb4s47"
@Test
void shouldUseRegisteredExceptionMapperInsteadOfDefaultErrorResponse() {
    when(service.getCase("CASE-404"))
            .thenThrow(new CaseNotFoundException("CASE-404"));

    Response response = target("/cases/CASE-404")
            .request(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
            .get();

    String body = response.readEntity(String.class);

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(404);
    assertThat(body).contains("CASE_NOT_FOUND");
    assertThat(body).doesNotContain("<html");
}
```

---

# 23. Recommended Test Class Split

Jangan satu resource test class menampung semuanya.

```text id="9uadci"
resource
├── CaseResourceTest.java
├── CaseResourceValidationTest.java
├── CaseExceptionMapperTest.java
├── CorrelationIdFilterTest.java
├── AuthContextFilterTest.java
├── TenantContextFilterTest.java
└── JsonSerializationTest.java
```

Atau berdasarkan concern:

```text id="pz7ri9"
http
├── error
│   ├── CaseNotFoundMapperTest.java
│   ├── InvalidCaseStateMapperTest.java
│   └── UnhandledExceptionMapperTest.java
├── validation
│   └── SubmitCaseValidationTest.java
├── filter
│   ├── CorrelationIdFilterTest.java
│   └── AuthContextFilterTest.java
└── serialization
    ├── CaseResponseSerializationTest.java
    └── SubmitCaseDeserializationTest.java
```

---

# 24. Practical Checklist: ExceptionMapper

```text id="f03bq8"
[ ] Setiap domain exception penting punya mapper.
[ ] Mapper menghasilkan HTTP status yang konsisten.
[ ] Error code stabil dan tidak bergantung message.
[ ] Message tidak membocorkan detail internal.
[ ] Details berisi field/context penting.
[ ] traceId/correlationId ikut response.
[ ] Error response Content-Type JSON.
[ ] Catch-all mapper tidak membocorkan stacktrace.
[ ] Test memastikan mapper ter-register.
```

---

# 25. Practical Checklist: Validation

```text id="d2jtw3"
[ ] Missing required field → 400.
[ ] Null required field → 400.
[ ] Blank string → 400.
[ ] Too long string → 400.
[ ] Invalid enum/query/path/header → 400.
[ ] Validation response memakai ApiErrorResponse standar.
[ ] Field error dinormalisasi.
[ ] Service tidak dipanggil saat validation gagal.
[ ] Object-level validation dites jika ada.
```

---

# 26. Practical Checklist: Filter

```text id="ljyuok"
[ ] Correlation ID existing diecho.
[ ] Correlation ID missing dibuatkan.
[ ] Auth header missing → 401.
[ ] Invalid auth context → 401/403.
[ ] Tenant header missing → 400/401/403 sesuai policy.
[ ] Context diteruskan ke resource/service command.
[ ] Filter tidak menelan response error.
[ ] Response tetap punya correlation ID saat error.
```

---

# 27. Practical Checklist: Serialization

```text id="r0wls6"
[ ] Date/time serialized dengan format stabil.
[ ] Enum serialized sesuai public API contract.
[ ] Unknown field behavior jelas: ignored atau rejected.
[ ] Missing vs null behavior jelas.
[ ] Error response juga JSON.
[ ] Content-Type dan Accept behavior dites.
[ ] JSON provider production sama dengan test.
[ ] ObjectMapper/JSON-B config tidak implicit.
```

---

# 28. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Raw exception message jadi API message | Internal detail bocor | Map ke safe message |
| Tidak punya error code | Consumer parsing message | Stable error code |
| Validation error provider-native | Contract berubah saat upgrade provider | Normalisasi response |
| Stacktrace keluar di response | Security issue | Catch-all safe mapper |
| Correlation ID hanya di success | Error sulit ditrace | Add in error too |
| Service dipanggil saat request invalid | Boundary bocor | Assert `verifyNoInteractions` |
| Enum internal langsung jadi API | Refactor enum breaking API | API DTO mapping |
| `LocalDateTime` tanpa timezone | Timezone ambiguity | `Instant`/`OffsetDateTime` |
| JSON config berbeda di test/production | Test palsu aman | Shared provider config |
| Semua test di satu class besar | Sulit maintain | Split by concern |

---

# 29. Minimal Test Set untuk HTTP Boundary

Untuk endpoint command penting:

```text id="8go4mu"
Success:
[ ] valid request → 2xx
[ ] response body field penting benar
[ ] response header penting benar

Validation:
[ ] missing required field → 400
[ ] null/blank field → 400
[ ] too long/invalid format → 400
[ ] service not called

Errors:
[ ] not found → 404 + code
[ ] forbidden → 403 + code
[ ] invalid state/conflict → 409 + code
[ ] unexpected → 500 safe message

Filters:
[ ] correlation id existing/missing
[ ] auth/tenant context

Serialization:
[ ] enum/date format
[ ] unknown field behavior
```

---

# 30. Ringkasan Seri 6

Inti dari seri ini:

```text id="3a75yi"
HTTP boundary bukan hanya resource method.
HTTP boundary juga mencakup exception mapping, validation, filter, dan serialization.
```

Yang harus dijaga stabil:

```text id="nnxwjj"
status code
error code
error body
validation details
correlation id
auth/tenant context
enum format
date/time format
media type
```

Boundary yang benar:

```text id="kk5z27"
Resource/provider test:
- ExceptionMapper
- Bean Validation response
- Filter/interceptor
- JSON provider behavior

Service unit test:
- business rule
- state transition
- permission decision

Integration test:
- DB transaction
- MyBatis SQL
- PostgreSQL constraint
- Camunda workflow
```
