# Seri 7/14 - MyBatis + PostgreSQL Integration Test with Testcontainers  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada **integration test untuk persistence layer**:

```text id="5d3tdq"
Java service/repository
  ↓
MyBatis mapper
  ↓
SQL / Mapper XML / Annotation
  ↓
PostgreSQL nyata
  ↓
Result mapping / constraint / transaction behavior
```

Tujuan utamanya:

> Membuktikan bahwa SQL MyBatis benar-benar valid terhadap PostgreSQL nyata, schema nyata, constraint nyata, type nyata, dan result mapping nyata.

Ini berbeda dari unit test service layer. Di unit test, repository biasanya dimock. Di integration test persistence, repository/mapper **tidak boleh dimock**, karena yang ingin dibuktikan justru SQL dan mapping-nya.

Testcontainers menyediakan integrasi JUnit Jupiter untuk menjalankan container yang bisa dibuat per test method atau dishare dalam satu test class. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/test_framework_integration/junit_5/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Testcontainers juga punya PostgreSQL module untuk menjalankan PostgreSQL container dari Java test. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/modules/databases/postgres/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk MyBatis, dokumentasi resmi menekankan bahwa kekuatan utama MyBatis ada pada mapped statements, baik di XML maupun annotation, sehingga integration test harus benar-benar mengeksekusi mapped statement tersebut. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Kenapa Tidak Cukup Unit Test?

Contoh unit test service:

```java id="5ye20c"
when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
        .thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));
```

Test ini hanya membuktikan service bereaksi benar terhadap return value repository.

Tapi tidak membuktikan:

```text id="j0722q"
- SQL valid secara syntax PostgreSQL
- nama column benar
- resultMap benar
- enum mapping benar
- timestamp/timezone benar
- JSONB/array/UUID mapping benar
- constraint berjalan
- index/query plan masuk akal
- PL/pgSQL function bisa dieksekusi
- transaction behavior sesuai
```

Itulah domain integration test.

---

# 3. Diagram Boundary

```text id="n31giq"
Persistence Integration Test Boundary

JUnit / Failsafe
   │
   ▼
Repository / Mapper Test
   │
   ▼
MyBatis SqlSession / Mapper Proxy
   │
   ▼
Mapped Statement
   │
   ├── XML mapper
   └── Annotation mapper
   │
   ▼
PostgreSQL Testcontainer
   │
   ├── schema
   ├── constraints
   ├── indexes
   ├── functions
   ├── transaction behavior
   └── real PostgreSQL types

Not mocked:
- MyBatis mapper
- SQL
- PostgreSQL

Usually mocked/not included:
- Jersey resource
- external API
- Camunda engine
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | Fokus praktis | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| Testcontainers setup | PostgreSQL container | Start DB real untuk test | Test bisa jalan lokal/CI tanpa install DB |
| Schema setup | DDL/migration | Apply schema sebelum test | DB test sama dengan production-like schema |
| MyBatis config | Mapper registration | XML/annotation mapper terbaca | Mapper proxy bisa dipakai |
| Mapper select test | Query correctness | `SELECT` by ID/filter/paging | Result benar |
| Mapper insert test | Insert + generated value | UUID/sequence/timestamp | Row tersimpan |
| Mapper update test | Optimistic locking/status update | `WHERE id=? AND version=?` | Affected row benar |
| Mapper delete/archive test | Soft delete/hard delete | Status/deleted flag | Data lifecycle benar |
| ResultMap test | Column → object mapping | enum, timestamp, nested field | Mapping tidak silent wrong |
| Constraint test | Unique/FK/check/not null | DB menjaga invariant | Invalid data gagal |
| Transaction test | Commit/rollback dasar | Multi-step atomicity | Partial write tidak committed |
| PL/pgSQL test | Function/procedure behavior | Call DB function | DB-side logic valid |
| Query test | Dynamic filter | MyBatis dynamic SQL/XML branches | Semua kombinasi penting valid |
| Test fixture | Seed data | Data kecil, eksplisit | Test readable |
| Cleanup strategy | Isolation | Test tidak saling ganggu | Tidak flaky |
| Maven lifecycle | Failsafe `*IT` | Integration test di `mvn verify` | Pipeline rapi |

---

# 5. Naming Convention

Gunakan pemisahan tegas:

```text id="n9g3fy"
Unit test:
- *Test.java
- mvn test
- Maven Surefire

Integration test:
- *IT.java
- mvn verify
- Maven Failsafe
```

Maven Failsafe memang digunakan pada fase `integration-test` dan `verify` untuk menjalankan integration tests. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-failsafe-plugin/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh nama:

```text id="5rzdak"
CaseMapperIT
CaseRepositoryIT
CaseSearchMapperIT
CaseTransactionIT
CaseFunctionIT
IdempotencyRepositoryIT
```

# 7. Maven Failsafe Configuration

```xml id="mz74lv"
<build>
    <plugins>
        <plugin>
            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
            <artifactId>maven-failsafe-plugin</artifactId>
            <version>${maven-failsafe-plugin.version}</version>
            <configuration>
                <includes>
                    <include>**/*IT.java</include>
                </includes>
            </configuration>
            <executions>
                <execution>
                    <goals>
                        <goal>integration-test</goal>
                        <goal>verify</goal>
                    </goals>
                </execution>
            </executions>
        </plugin>
    </plugins>
</build>
```

Command:

```bash id="jydlx0"
mvn verify
```

---

# 8. Base PostgreSQL Container Test

```java id="nvef0x"
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.testcontainers.containers.PostgreSQLContainer;
import org.testcontainers.junit.jupiter.Container;
import org.testcontainers.junit.jupiter.Testcontainers;

@Testcontainers
abstract class PostgreSqlIntegrationTestSupport {

    @Container
    static final PostgreSQLContainer<?> POSTGRES =
            new PostgreSQLContainer<>("postgres:16-alpine")
                    .withDatabaseName("caseapp")
                    .withUsername("test")
                    .withPassword("test");

    protected String jdbcUrl() {
        return POSTGRES.getJdbcUrl();
    }

    protected String username() {
        return POSTGRES.getUsername();
    }

    protected String password() {
        return POSTGRES.getPassword();
    }

    @BeforeEach
    void resetDatabase() throws Exception {
        DatabaseTestSupport.cleanDatabase(
                jdbcUrl(),
                username(),
                password()
        );

        DatabaseTestSupport.applySchema(
                jdbcUrl(),
                username(),
                password()
        );
    }
}
```

Practical note:

```text id="317vel"
Static container:
- lebih cepat
- dishare per class
- perlu cleanup data antar test

Instance container:
- lebih isolated
- lebih lambat
```

Testcontainers JUnit Jupiter extension mendukung container restart per method maupun shared antar method dalam satu class. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/test_framework_integration/junit_5/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. Schema Setup Strategy

Ada beberapa opsi:

| Strategy | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| Raw SQL schema file | Project sederhana | Mudah dimengerti | Bisa drift dari migration production |
| Flyway/Liquibase migration | Enterprise app | Sama dengan production path | Setup lebih berat |
| MyBatis schema script | Mapper-focused test | Cepat | Bisa beda dari deployment |
| Reusable template DB | Test suite besar | Cepat | Lebih kompleks |

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text id="x0dmfp"
Jika production memakai migration tool:
gunakan migration yang sama di integration test.

Jika belum ada:
gunakan schema.sql yang eksplisit dan reviewable.
```

---

# 10. Example Schema

```sql id="i0yhvn"
CREATE TABLE cases (
    id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    mandatory_documents_complete BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    assignee_user_id VARCHAR(64),
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE case_audit_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES cases(id),
    action VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    actor_user_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE idempotency_records (
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) PRIMARY KEY,
    request_hash VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    response_body TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

---

# 11. Database Test Support

```java id="u375p3"
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.DriverManager;
import java.sql.Statement;

public final class DatabaseTestSupport {

    private DatabaseTestSupport() {
    }

    public static void applySchema(
            String jdbcUrl,
            String username,
            String password
    ) throws Exception {
        executeSqlResource(
                jdbcUrl,
                username,
                password,
                "/db/schema.sql"
        );
    }

    public static void cleanDatabase(
            String jdbcUrl,
            String username,
            String password
    ) throws Exception {
        try (Connection connection = DriverManager.getConnection(
                jdbcUrl,
                username,
                password
        );
             Statement statement = connection.createStatement()) {

            statement.execute("""
                    DROP TABLE IF EXISTS case_audit_log CASCADE;
                    DROP TABLE IF EXISTS idempotency_records CASCADE;
                    DROP TABLE IF EXISTS cases CASCADE;
                    """);
        }
    }

    public static void executeSqlResource(
            String jdbcUrl,
            String username,
            String password,
            String resourcePath
    ) throws Exception {
        String sql = new String(
                DatabaseTestSupport.class
                        .getResourceAsStream(resourcePath)
                        .readAllBytes(),
                StandardCharsets.UTF_8
        );

        try (Connection connection = DriverManager.getConnection(
                jdbcUrl,
                username,
                password
        );
             Statement statement = connection.createStatement()) {
            statement.execute(sql);
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="ox53o7"
Untuk production-grade test suite, gunakan migration tool dan cleanup strategy yang lebih aman.
Contoh di atas dibuat sederhana agar konsepnya jelas.
```

---

```java id="06n9fe"
import java.time.Instant;

public record CaseRecord(
        String id,
        CaseStatus status,
        boolean mandatoryDocumentsComplete,
        String assigneeUserId,
        Instant submittedAt,
        Instant approvedAt,
        int version
) {
}
```

```java id="1rtw5j"
public enum CaseStatus {
    DRAFT,
    SUBMITTED,
    UNDER_REVIEW,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED,
    CANCELLED,
    CLOSED
}
```

# 13. MyBatis Mapper Interface

```java id="le7hb7"
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

public interface CaseMapper {

    Optional<CaseRecord> findById(@Param("id") String id);

    void insert(CaseRecord caseRecord);

    int markSubmitted(
            @Param("id") String id,
            @Param("submittedBy") String submittedBy,
            @Param("submittedAt") Instant submittedAt,
            @Param("expectedVersion") int expectedVersion
    );

    List<CaseRecord> search(
            @Param("status") CaseStatus status,
            @Param("assigneeUserId") String assigneeUserId,
            @Param("limit") int limit,
            @Param("offset") int offset
    );
}
```

---

# 14. MyBatis XML Mapper

```xml id="6q4kp0"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper
        PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
        "https://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="com.company.caseapp.persistence.CaseMapper">

    <resultMap id="CaseRecordResultMap"
               type="com.company.caseapp.domain.CaseRecord">
        <constructor>
            <arg column="id" javaType="string"/>
            <arg column="status"
                 javaType="com.company.caseapp.domain.CaseStatus"/>
            <arg column="mandatory_documents_complete"
                 javaType="boolean"/>
            <arg column="assignee_user_id" javaType="string"/>
            <arg column="submitted_at" javaType="java.time.Instant"/>
            <arg column="approved_at" javaType="java.time.Instant"/>
            <arg column="version" javaType="int"/>
        </constructor>
    </resultMap>

    <select id="findById" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
        SELECT
            id,
            status,
            mandatory_documents_complete,
            assignee_user_id,
            submitted_at,
            approved_at,
            version
        FROM cases
        WHERE id = #{id}
    </select>

    <insert id="insert">
        INSERT INTO cases (
            id,
            status,
            mandatory_documents_complete,
            assignee_user_id,
            submitted_at,
            approved_at,
            version
        ) VALUES (
            #{id},
            #{status},
            #{mandatoryDocumentsComplete},
            #{assigneeUserId},
            #{submittedAt},
            #{approvedAt},
            #{version}
        )
    </insert>

    <update id="markSubmitted">
        UPDATE cases
        SET
            status = 'SUBMITTED',
            submitted_at = #{submittedAt},
            version = version + 1,
            updated_at = now()
        WHERE id = #{id}
          AND version = #{expectedVersion}
    </update>

    <select id="search" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
        SELECT
            id,
            status,
            mandatory_documents_complete,
            assignee_user_id,
            submitted_at,
            approved_at,
            version
        FROM cases
        WHERE 1 = 1
        <if test="status != null">
            AND status = #{status}
        </if>
        <if test="assigneeUserId != null">
            AND assignee_user_id = #{assigneeUserId}
        </if>
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
        LIMIT #{limit}
        OFFSET #{offset}
    </select>

</mapper>
```

MyBatis mapper XML files define mapped statements such as `select`, `insert`, `update`, and result mapping, and MyBatis intentionally keeps developers close to SQL rather than hiding it behind ORM abstraction. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. MyBatis Test Configuration

```java id="i5dece"
import org.apache.ibatis.datasource.pooled.PooledDataSource;
import org.apache.ibatis.io.Resources;
import org.apache.ibatis.mapping.Environment;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.Configuration;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactory;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactoryBuilder;
import org.apache.ibatis.transaction.jdbc.JdbcTransactionFactory;

public final class MyBatisTestFactory {

    private MyBatisTestFactory() {
    }

    public static SqlSessionFactory create(
            String jdbcUrl,
            String username,
            String password
    ) throws Exception {
        PooledDataSource dataSource = new PooledDataSource(
                "org.postgresql.Driver",
                jdbcUrl,
                username,
                password
        );

        Environment environment = new Environment(
                "test",
                new JdbcTransactionFactory(),
                dataSource
        );

        Configuration configuration = new Configuration(environment);
        configuration.addMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        try (Reader mapperXml = Resources.getResourceAsReader(
                "mappers/CaseMapper.xml"
        )) {
            new XMLMapperBuilder(
                    mapperXml,
                    configuration,
                    "mappers/CaseMapper.xml",
                    configuration.getSqlFragments()
            ).parse();
        }

        return new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(configuration);
    }
}
```

Catatan praktis:

```text id="l1e2s7"
Di project nyata, Anda mungkin sudah punya MyBatis module/factory internal.
Yang penting: integration test memakai mapper XML/annotation yang sama dengan production.
```

# 16. Mapper Insert + Select Test

```java id="hme0qp"
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSession;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactory;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.Optional;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class CaseMapperIT extends PostgreSqlIntegrationTestSupport {

    @Test
    void shouldInsertAndFindCaseById() throws Exception {
        SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
                MyBatisTestFactory.create(
                        jdbcUrl(),
                        username(),
                        password()
                );

        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

            CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
                    "CASE-001",
                    CaseStatus.DRAFT,
                    true,
                    "user-123",
                    null,
                    null,
                    0
            );

            mapper.insert(caseRecord);

            Optional<CaseRecord> result =
                    mapper.findById("CASE-001");

            assertThat(result).hasValue(caseRecord);
        }
    }
}
```

Yang diuji:

```text id="4xwj8z"
- insert statement valid
- select statement valid
- resultMap constructor mapping benar
- enum status bisa round-trip
- boolean field benar
```

# 17. Select Not Found Test

```java id="jvgdt8"
@Test
void shouldReturnEmptyWhenCaseDoesNotExist() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
```

Kenapa penting:

```text id="au071h"
Repository/mapper contract harus jelas:
- tidak ada row → Optional.empty()
- bukan null
- bukan exception kecuali memang policy-nya begitu
```

# 18. Update with Optimistic Locking Test

SQL:

```sql id="rmt8my"
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'SUBMITTED',
    submitted_at = #{submittedAt},
    version = version + 1
WHERE id = #{id}
  AND version = #{expectedVersion}
```

Test success:

```java id="2aof33"
@Test
void shouldMarkCaseSubmittedWhenVersionMatches() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    Instant submittedAt = Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z");

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.DRAFT,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                0
        ));

        int affectedRows = mapper.markSubmitted(
                "CASE-001",
                "user-123",
                submittedAt,
                0
        );

        assertThat(affectedRows).isEqualTo(1);

        CaseRecord updated = mapper.findById("CASE-001")
                .orElseThrow();

        assertThat(updated.status()).isEqualTo(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED);
        assertThat(updated.submittedAt()).isEqualTo(submittedAt);
        assertThat(updated.version()).isEqualTo(1);
    }
}
```

Test version mismatch:

```java id="yct3u2"
@Test
void shouldNotMarkSubmittedWhenVersionDoesNotMatch() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.DRAFT,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                3
        ));

        int affectedRows = mapper.markSubmitted(
                "CASE-001",
                "user-123",
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                2
        );

        assertThat(affectedRows).isEqualTo(0);

        CaseRecord unchanged = mapper.findById("CASE-001")
                .orElseThrow();

        assertThat(unchanged.status()).isEqualTo(CaseStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(unchanged.version()).isEqualTo(3);
    }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="6xbi64"
Optimistic locking harus dites di DB nyata,
karena affected row count adalah bagian dari contract.
```

# 19. Dynamic Search Test

```java id="j66pxi"
@Test
void shouldSearchCasesByStatusAndAssignee() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                true,
                "user-123",
                null,
                null,
                0
        ));

        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-002",
                CaseStatus.DRAFT,
                true,
                "user-123",
                null,
                null,
                0
        ));

        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-003",
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                true,
                "user-999",
                null,
                null,
                0
        ));

        List<CaseRecord> result = mapper.search(
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                "user-123",
                20,
                0
        );

        assertThat(result)
                .extracting(CaseRecord::id)
                .containsExactly("CASE-001");
    }
}
```

Dynamic SQL branches yang perlu dites:

```text id="f9xsna"
- no filter
- status only
- assignee only
- status + assignee
- pagination
- empty result
```

Jangan semua kombinasi ekstrem jika tidak bernilai, tetapi cabang SQL penting harus tersentuh.

# 20. Constraint Test: Unique Idempotency Key

Mapper:

```java id="2mfdbv"
public interface IdempotencyMapper {

    void insert(IdempotencyRecord record);

    Optional<IdempotencyRecord> findByKey(
            @Param("key") String key
    );
}
```

Record:

```java id="3371qa"
public record IdempotencyRecord(
        String key,
        String requestHash,
        String responseBody
) {
}
```

Test duplicate key:

```java id="yc4uf3"
@Test
void shouldRejectDuplicateIdempotencyKey() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        IdempotencyMapper mapper =
                session.getMapper(IdempotencyMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new IdempotencyRecord(
                "idem-001",
                "hash-1",
                "{\"caseId\":\"CASE-001\"}"
        ));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> mapper.insert(new IdempotencyRecord(
                "idem-001",
                "hash-1",
                "{\"caseId\":\"CASE-001\"}"
        ))).isInstanceOf(PersistenceException.class);
    }
}
```

Kenapa ini harus integration test:

```text id="0sf2vh"
Unit test policy hanya membuktikan decision.
DB integration test membuktikan unique constraint benar-benar menjaga invariant.
```

---

# 21. Foreign Key Constraint Test

```java id="glo4yo"
@Test
void shouldRejectAuditLogForUnknownCaseId() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseAuditMapper auditMapper =
                session.getMapper(CaseAuditMapper.class);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> auditMapper.insert(new CaseAuditRecord(
                null,
                "CASE-404",
                "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                "user-123",
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
        ))).isInstanceOf(PersistenceException.class);
    }
}
```

Yang dibuktikan:

```text id="uur12p"
Audit log tidak bisa menunjuk case yang tidak ada.
Invariant dijaga DB, bukan hanya Java service.
```

# 22. Transaction Test: Commit

```java id="m50wqa"
@Test
void shouldCommitCaseAndAuditLogTogether() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
        CaseMapper caseMapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
        CaseAuditMapper auditMapper = session.getMapper(CaseAuditMapper.class);

        caseMapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                true,
                null,
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                null,
                0
        ));

        auditMapper.insert(new CaseAuditRecord(
                null,
                "CASE-001",
                "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                "user-123",
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
        ));

        session.commit();
    }

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper caseMapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
        CaseAuditMapper auditMapper = session.getMapper(CaseAuditMapper.class);

        assertThat(caseMapper.findById("CASE-001")).isPresent();
        assertThat(auditMapper.findByCaseId("CASE-001")).hasSize(1);
    }
}
```

PostgreSQL documentation describes transactions as bundling multiple steps into a single all-or-nothing operation. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/tutorial-transactions.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 23. Transaction Test: Rollback

```java id="endnwi"
@Test
void shouldRollbackCaseWhenAuditInsertFails() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
        CaseMapper caseMapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
        CaseAuditMapper auditMapper = session.getMapper(CaseAuditMapper.class);

        caseMapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                true,
                null,
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                null,
                0
        ));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> auditMapper.insert(new CaseAuditRecord(
                null,
                "CASE-404",
                "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                "user-123",
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
        ))).isInstanceOf(PersistenceException.class);

        session.rollback();
    }

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper caseMapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        assertThat(caseMapper.findById("CASE-001")).isEmpty();
    }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="xgg1mc"
Rollback harus dibuktikan dengan membaca ulang dari session/transaction baru.
Jangan hanya percaya exception terjadi.
```

---

# 24. PostgreSQL Type Mapping Test

PostgreSQL punya type behavior yang sering tidak sama dengan DB lain:

```text id="g4jgfl"
TIMESTAMPTZ
JSONB
UUID
ARRAY
NUMERIC
TEXT
BOOLEAN
ENUM-like VARCHAR
```

Contoh timestamp test:

```java id="lmcyql"
@Test
void shouldRoundTripSubmittedAtAsInstant() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    Instant submittedAt = Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z");

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                true,
                null,
                submittedAt,
                null,
                0
        ));

        CaseRecord result = mapper.findById("CASE-001")
                .orElseThrow();

        assertThat(result.submittedAt()).isEqualTo(submittedAt);
    }
}
```

Kenapa penting:

```text id="ob0l83"
Timezone bug sering lolos kalau hanya unit test.
TIMESTAMPTZ harus dites dengan PostgreSQL nyata.
```

---

# 25. PL/pgSQL Function Test

Schema:

```sql id="8t1170"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION calculate_case_age_hours(
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    now_at TIMESTAMPTZ
)
RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (now_at - submitted_at)) / 3600;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

Mapper:

```java id="4isjgm"
public interface CaseFunctionMapper {

    int calculateCaseAgeHours(
            @Param("submittedAt") Instant submittedAt,
            @Param("nowAt") Instant nowAt
    );
}
```

XML:

```xml id="yqirf7"
<select id="calculateCaseAgeHours" resultType="int">
    SELECT calculate_case_age_hours(
        #{submittedAt},
        #{nowAt}
    )
</select>
```

Test:

```java id="zywpy8"
@Test
void shouldCalculateCaseAgeHoursUsingPostgresFunction() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseFunctionMapper mapper =
                session.getMapper(CaseFunctionMapper.class);

        int hours = mapper.calculateCaseAgeHours(
                Instant.parse("2026-07-14T10:00:00Z"),
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T16:00:00Z")
        );

        assertThat(hours).isEqualTo(30);
    }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="ho2rxe"
Jika business logic berada di PL/pgSQL,
maka harus ada integration test DB-side.
```

---

# 26. Cleanup Strategy

## Option A - Drop and recreate schema

```text id="x7yiyf"
+ sangat bersih
+ sederhana
- lebih lambat
```

## Option B - TRUNCATE tables

```sql id="aqweug"
TRUNCATE TABLE
    case_audit_log,
    idempotency_records,
    cases
RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE;
```

```text id="8h7oj5"
+ lebih cepat
+ cukup bersih
- urutan table/FK perlu diperhatikan
```

## Option C - Transaction rollback per test

```text id="6pty8p"
+ cepat
+ bersih
- sulit jika code membuka transaction sendiri
- sulit untuk concurrency test
```

Rekomendasi:

```text id="m39uvb"
Untuk mapper IT:
- static container
- schema once per class atau suite
- truncate before each test

Untuk transaction/concurrency test:
- pastikan cleanup eksplisit
- jangan bergantung pada rollback test framework saja
```

---

# 27. Fixture Data Strategy

Buat seed helper:

```java id="w9l4jk"
public final class CaseDbFixture {

    private final CaseMapper mapper;

    public CaseDbFixture(CaseMapper mapper) {
        this.mapper = mapper;
    }

    public void draftCase(String id) {
        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                id,
                CaseStatus.DRAFT,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                0
        ));
    }

    public void submittedCase(String id, String assigneeUserId) {
        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                id,
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                true,
                assigneeUserId,
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                null,
                0
        ));
    }
}
```

Test jadi lebih readable:

```java id="gcu91q"
@Test
void shouldFindSubmittedCaseByAssignee() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
        CaseDbFixture fixture = new CaseDbFixture(mapper);

        fixture.submittedCase("CASE-001", "user-123");
        fixture.submittedCase("CASE-002", "user-999");
        fixture.draftCase("CASE-003");

        List<CaseRecord> result = mapper.search(
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                "user-123",
                20,
                0
        );

        assertThat(result)
                .extracting(CaseRecord::id)
                .containsExactly("CASE-001");
    }
}
```

# 28. What Not to Do

## Jangan mock MyBatis mapper di integration test

Buruk:

```java id="y2sns6"
CaseMapper mapper = mock(CaseMapper.class);
when(mapper.findById("CASE-001")).thenReturn(Optional.of(caseRecord));
```

Ini bukan integration test. Ini unit test.

---

## Jangan pakai H2 untuk PostgreSQL-specific behavior

Jika production PostgreSQL, H2 tidak membuktikan:

```text id="6yc2mn"
JSONB
TIMESTAMPTZ
PostgreSQL locking
PL/pgSQL
constraint behavior persis
index behavior
isolation behavior
SQL dialect
```

Gunakan PostgreSQL container.

---

## Jangan seed data terlalu besar

Buruk:

```text id="q81cx8"
Load 10.000 rows untuk semua mapper test.
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="25663m"
Seed hanya data yang dibutuhkan test.
Untuk performance/query plan test, pisahkan suite khusus.
```

---

## Jangan assert terlalu longgar

Buruk:

```java id="z0q9s3"
assertThat(result).isNotEmpty();
```

Lebih baik:

```java id="ltl6rg"
assertThat(result)
        .extracting(CaseRecord::id)
        .containsExactly("CASE-001");
```

---

# 29. Integration Test Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini untuk mapper/repository integration test:

```text id="tqpgzr"
[ ] Test memakai PostgreSQL nyata via Testcontainers.
[ ] Mapper XML/annotation yang sama dengan production dipakai.
[ ] Schema sama dengan production migration atau schema test eksplisit.
[ ] Test class memakai suffix *IT.
[ ] Test dijalankan via Maven Failsafe / mvn verify.
[ ] Data test kecil dan eksplisit.
[ ] Cleanup antar test jelas.
[ ] SELECT by ID diuji.
[ ] INSERT round-trip diuji.
[ ] UPDATE affected row diuji.
[ ] Dynamic SQL branch penting diuji.
[ ] ResultMap enum/date/boolean/null diuji.
[ ] Constraint penting diuji.
[ ] Transaction commit/rollback diuji bila relevan.
[ ] PL/pgSQL function diuji bila ada business logic DB-side.
[ ] Tidak ada mock untuk mapper/SQL/DB.
```

---

# 30. Minimal Test Set per Mapper

Untuk mapper penting seperti `CaseMapper`:

```text id="0o80ek"
1. insert + findById round-trip
2. findById not found
3. update success affectedRows = 1
4. update version mismatch affectedRows = 0
5. search no filter
6. search with key filters
7. pagination
8. enum/status mapping
9. timestamp mapping
10. constraint violation if mapper inserts critical data
```

Untuk mapper idempotency:

```text id="zloasx"
1. insert new key
2. find existing key
3. find missing key
4. duplicate key rejected
5. same key different hash handled by service/repository policy
```

Untuk audit mapper:

```text id="n7ksfk"
1. insert audit for existing case
2. reject audit for missing case
3. find audit by case id
4. order by occurred_at/id
```

# 31. Ringkasan Seri 7

Inti dari seri ini:

```text id="v4deym"
Persistence integration test membuktikan SQL, MyBatis mapping,
PostgreSQL schema, constraint, dan transaction behavior nyata.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text id="jkd2vm"
Jangan mock mapper.
Jangan pakai DB pengganti yang beda dialect.
Gunakan PostgreSQL Testcontainer.
Gunakan schema/migration yang sama dengan production.
Gunakan data kecil dan eksplisit.
Assert affected rows, result mapping, dan constraint.
Pisahkan *IT dari *Test.
Jalankan integration test dengan mvn verify.
```

Boundary yang benar:

```text id="0w8q1b"
Unit test:
- service decision
- policy
- mapper object conversion

Integration test:
- MyBatis SQL
- PostgreSQL types
- constraints
- transaction
- PL/pgSQL
```

# Seri 8/14 - Transaction, Locking, Idempotency, and Concurrency Test  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada bug yang paling sering muncul di production tetapi jarang tertangkap unit test:

```text id="uvhjgz"
race condition
double submit
double approve
duplicate request
lost update
stale version update
idempotency conflict
deadlock
transaction isolation anomaly
pessimistic lock blocking
optimistic lock affected row = 0
```

Dalam stack **Java 17+ + Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, area ini harus dites dengan **PostgreSQL nyata**, bukan mock dan bukan H2. Alasannya sederhana: bug concurrency muncul dari interaksi nyata antara **transaction**, **lock**, **constraint**, **isolation level**, dan **query/update SQL**.

PostgreSQL mendokumentasikan transaction isolation sebagai mekanisme untuk mengontrol efek transaksi konkuren; dokumentasi juga menjelaskan bahwa aplikasi tidak boleh membiarkan koneksi “idle in transaction” terlalu lama karena bisa menahan resource/lock. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PostgreSQL explicit locking documentation juga menjelaskan bahwa lock yang sudah diperoleh biasanya ditahan sampai akhir transaksi. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Testcontainers mendukung JUnit Jupiter melalui `@Testcontainers` dan `@Container`, termasuk static container yang dishare antar test method. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/test_framework_integration/junit_5/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model

```text id="otv3vf"
Concurrency-sensitive behavior
│
├── Correctness rule
│   ├── submit hanya sekali
│   ├── approve hanya sekali
│   ├── idempotency key tidak konflik
│   └── balance/status tidak lost update
│
├── Database protection
│   ├── unique constraint
│   ├── foreign key
│   ├── check constraint
│   ├── optimistic version column
│   ├── SELECT ... FOR UPDATE
│   └── transaction isolation level
│
├── Application behavior
│   ├── affected row = 1 → success
│   ├── affected row = 0 → stale state/version
│   ├── duplicate key → return existing/reject conflict
│   ├── lock timeout → retryable failure
│   └── serialization failure → retryable transaction
│
└── Test behavior
    ├── run real concurrent transactions
    ├── coordinate start with CountDownLatch/CyclicBarrier
    ├── avoid Thread.sleep as synchronization
    ├── assert final database state
    └── assert exactly-one-success when required
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | Fokus test | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|---|
| Transaction boundary | Commit/rollback | Multi-write atomicity | Mencegah partial write |
| Optimistic locking | `version` column | `UPDATE ... WHERE id=? AND version=?` | Mencegah lost update |
| Pessimistic locking | `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` | Blocking/serialized update | Mencegah concurrent mutation |
| Unique constraint | Idempotency key | Duplicate request protection | Rule dijaga DB |
| Concurrent command | Double submit/approve | Exactly one succeeds | Bug produksi umum |
| Retry behavior | Serialization/deadlock/lock timeout | Retryable vs non-retryable | Resilience |
| Isolation level | Read committed/repeatable read/serializable | Visibility/anomaly | Data consistency |
| Lock timeout | Fail fast saat lock lama | Tidak menggantung CI/production | Stabilitas |
| Deadlock detection | Opposite lock order | Error ditangani | Avoid stuck transaction |
| Test coordination | Barrier/latch | Thread mulai bersamaan | Concurrency test tidak flaky |
| Final state assertion | DB state after concurrent operations | Assert outcome nyata | Bukan hanya assert exception |
| Idempotency behavior | Same key same hash/different hash | Return previous vs conflict | API safe untuk retry |

---

# 4. Running Example

Kita gunakan contoh `cases` dan `idempotency_records`.

```sql id="xvmyuv"
CREATE TABLE cases (
    id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    mandatory_documents_complete BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    approved_by VARCHAR(64),
    version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE case_audit_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES cases(id),
    action VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    actor_user_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE idempotency_records (
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) PRIMARY KEY,
    request_hash VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    response_body TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Critical invariants:

```text id="4cb6o6"
1. Case tidak boleh disubmit dua kali.
2. Case tidak boleh di-approve dua kali.
3. Idempotency key tidak boleh menghasilkan dua proses berbeda.
4. Update stale version harus gagal.
5. Audit log tidak boleh double-write untuk satu state transition.
```

---

# 5. Optimistic Locking Pattern

## SQL

```xml id="d6e621"
<update id="markSubmitted">
    UPDATE cases
    SET
        status = 'SUBMITTED',
        submitted_at = #{submittedAt},
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = #{id}
      AND status = 'DRAFT'
      AND version = #{expectedVersion}
</update>
```

## Mapper

```java id="s58nsj"
public interface CaseMapper {

    Optional<CaseRecord> findById(@Param("id") String id);

    int markSubmitted(
            @Param("id") String id,
            @Param("submittedAt") Instant submittedAt,
            @Param("expectedVersion") int expectedVersion
    );

    int markApproved(
            @Param("id") String id,
            @Param("approvedBy") String approvedBy,
            @Param("approvedAt") Instant approvedAt,
            @Param("expectedVersion") int expectedVersion
    );
}
```

## Service behavior

```java id="owum4k"
public void submit(String caseId) {
    CaseRecord current = caseMapper.findById(caseId)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new CaseNotFoundException(caseId));

    int affectedRows = caseMapper.markSubmitted(
            caseId,
            Instant.now(clock),
            current.version()
    );

    if (affectedRows == 0) {
        throw new ConcurrentCaseModificationException(caseId);
    }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="7nsttl"
affectedRows = 1 → update menang
affectedRows = 0 → state/version sudah berubah
```

---

# 6. Optimistic Locking Integration Test

```java id="2ssbxq"
@Test
void shouldRejectStaleVersionUpdate() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory factory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.DRAFT,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                0
        ));

        int firstUpdate = mapper.markSubmitted(
                "CASE-001",
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                0
        );

        int staleUpdate = mapper.markSubmitted(
                "CASE-001",
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:01:00Z"),
                0
        );

        assertThat(firstUpdate).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(staleUpdate).isEqualTo(0);

        CaseRecord result = mapper.findById("CASE-001")
                .orElseThrow();

        assertThat(result.status()).isEqualTo(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED);
        assertThat(result.version()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(result.submittedAt())
                .isEqualTo(Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"));
    }
}
```

Yang diuji:

```text id="9fhksq"
- stale version tidak overwrite data baru
- affected row count benar
- status dan timestamp tidak berubah oleh update kedua
```

---

# 7. Concurrent Double Submit Test

Ini test yang lebih mendekati production race.

## Test helper

```java id="xyeq2m"
public final class ConcurrentTestRunner {

    private ConcurrentTestRunner() {
    }

    public static List<Throwable> runConcurrently(
            int threadCount,
            ThrowingRunnable task
    ) throws InterruptedException {
        ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(threadCount);
        CountDownLatch ready = new CountDownLatch(threadCount);
        CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);
        CountDownLatch done = new CountDownLatch(threadCount);

        List<Throwable> errors = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();

        for (int i = 0; i < threadCount; i++) {
            executor.submit(() -> {
                try {
                    ready.countDown();
                    start.await();
                    task.run();
                } catch (Throwable throwable) {
                    errors.add(throwable);
                } finally {
                    done.countDown();
                }
            });
        }

        ready.await();
        start.countDown();
        done.await();

        executor.shutdownNow();

        return errors;
    }

    @FunctionalInterface
    public interface ThrowingRunnable {
        void run() throws Exception;
    }
}
```

## Test

```java id="mfluex"
@Test
void shouldAllowOnlyOneConcurrentSubmit() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory factory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.DRAFT,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                0
        ));
    }

    AtomicInteger successCount = new AtomicInteger();
    AtomicInteger staleCount = new AtomicInteger();

    List<Throwable> errors = ConcurrentTestRunner.runConcurrently(
            2,
            () -> {
                try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
                    CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

                    CaseRecord current = mapper.findById("CASE-001")
                            .orElseThrow();

                    int affectedRows = mapper.markSubmitted(
                            "CASE-001",
                            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                            current.version()
                    );

                    if (affectedRows == 1) {
                        successCount.incrementAndGet();
                        session.commit();
                    } else {
                        staleCount.incrementAndGet();
                        session.rollback();
                    }
                }
            }
    );

    assertThat(errors).isEmpty();
    assertThat(successCount.get()).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(staleCount.get()).isEqualTo(1);

    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        CaseRecord result = mapper.findById("CASE-001")
                .orElseThrow();

        assertThat(result.status()).isEqualTo(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED);
        assertThat(result.version()).isEqualTo(1);
    }
}
```

Prinsip penting:

```text id="gpqxpd"
Concurrency test harus assert final DB state.
Jangan hanya assert salah satu thread error/sukses.
```

---

# 8. Kenapa `Thread.sleep()` Tidak Ideal

Buruk:

```java id="9jpol1"
Thread.sleep(100);
```

Masalah:

```text id="tlyxj9"
- lambat
- flaky
- berbeda antara laptop dan CI
- tidak menjamin thread benar-benar sudah mencapai titik tertentu
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="9mc41a"
CountDownLatch
CyclicBarrier
Phaser
Semaphore
database lock_timeout
Awaitility untuk polling kondisi async
```

Gunakan sleep hanya sebagai fallback kecil, bukan mekanisme utama sinkronisasi.

---

# 9. Pessimistic Locking Pattern

Pessimistic lock cocok jika:

```text id="ifcq13"
- update butuh membaca state kompleks sebelum mutate
- collision sering
- invariant sangat kritikal
- operasi harus serial per row/entity
```

SQL:

```xml id="94x4mk"
<select id="findByIdForUpdate" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
    SELECT
        id,
        status,
        mandatory_documents_complete,
        submitted_at,
        approved_at,
        approved_by,
        version
    FROM cases
    WHERE id = #{id}
    FOR UPDATE
</select>
```

Mapper:

```java id="6zxzwp"
Optional<CaseRecord> findByIdForUpdate(@Param("id") String id);
```

PostgreSQL explicit locking documentation states that locks, once acquired, are normally held until the end of the transaction. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 10. Pessimistic Lock Blocking Test

Tujuan: transaksi kedua tidak boleh update row sebelum transaksi pertama selesai.

```java id="jp2jfq"
@Test
void shouldBlockSecondTransactionWhenFirstTransactionHoldsForUpdateLock()
        throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory factory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.DRAFT,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                0
        ));
    }

    CountDownLatch firstLockAcquired = new CountDownLatch(1);
    CountDownLatch releaseFirstTransaction = new CountDownLatch(1);
    AtomicReference<Throwable> secondError = new AtomicReference<>();

    ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);

    executor.submit(() -> {
        try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

            mapper.findByIdForUpdate("CASE-001").orElseThrow();
            firstLockAcquired.countDown();

            releaseFirstTransaction.await();

            session.commit();
        } catch (Throwable throwable) {
            throw new RuntimeException(throwable);
        }
    });

    firstLockAcquired.await();

    Future<?> second = executor.submit(() -> {
        try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
            setLocalLockTimeout(session, "500ms");

            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

            mapper.findByIdForUpdate("CASE-001").orElseThrow();

            session.commit();
        } catch (Throwable throwable) {
            secondError.set(throwable);
        }
    });

    second.get();

    releaseFirstTransaction.countDown();
    executor.shutdownNow();

    assertThat(secondError.get())
            .isNotNull();
}
```

Helper:

```java id="yfgtl1"
private void setLocalLockTimeout(SqlSession session, String timeout) {
    session.getConnection()
            .createStatement()
            .execute("SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '" + timeout + "'");
}
```

Catatan praktis:

```text id="ecpje9"
Test blocking jangan dibiarkan menggantung.
Gunakan lock_timeout kecil.
Assert error class/kode SQL sesuai abstraction internal Anda.
```

---

# 11. `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`

Untuk job worker / queue pattern, PostgreSQL sering dipakai dengan:

```sql id="i00g8u"
SELECT *
FROM jobs
WHERE status = 'READY'
ORDER BY created_at
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
LIMIT 1;
```

Use case:

```text id="q90s3h"
Beberapa worker mengambil pekerjaan.
Worker A lock job 1.
Worker B tidak menunggu job 1, tetapi skip dan ambil job 2.
```

Test target:

```text id="7pjs79"
Dua worker concurrent tidak mengambil job yang sama.
```

Mapper:

```xml id="7vnn5r"
<select id="claimNextReadyJob" resultMap="JobResultMap">
    SELECT
        id,
        status,
        payload,
        version
    FROM jobs
    WHERE status = 'READY'
    ORDER BY created_at
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    LIMIT 1
</select>
```

Test concept:

```java id="72wklt"
@Test
void shouldNotClaimSameJobConcurrentlyWithSkipLocked() throws Exception {
    // seed JOB-001 and JOB-002 as READY

    Set<String> claimedJobIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

    List<Throwable> errors = ConcurrentTestRunner.runConcurrently(
            2,
            () -> {
                try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
                    JobMapper mapper = session.getMapper(JobMapper.class);

                    JobRecord job = mapper.claimNextReadyJob()
                            .orElseThrow();

                    mapper.markJobRunning(job.id());

                    claimedJobIds.add(job.id());

                    session.commit();
                }
            }
    );

    assertThat(errors).isEmpty();
    assertThat(claimedJobIds).hasSize(2);
    assertThat(claimedJobIds)
            .containsExactlyInAnyOrder("JOB-001", "JOB-002");
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="2jt90v"
SKIP LOCKED cocok untuk worker queue.
Tidak cocok untuk user-facing operation yang harus menunggu row tertentu.
```

---

# 12. Idempotency Key Pattern

Idempotency dibutuhkan saat client retry request karena:

```text id="s6j1pc"
network timeout
gateway timeout
client tidak tahu request sukses atau gagal
duplicate submit dari UI
message redelivery
external webhook duplicate
```

## Table

```sql id="7yji1n"
CREATE TABLE idempotency_records (
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) PRIMARY KEY,
    request_hash VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    response_body TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

## Behavior

```text id="b66yxv"
No existing key:
  process request
  store key + request_hash + response

Existing key + same hash:
  return previous response

Existing key + different hash:
  reject conflict
```

---

# 13. Idempotency Mapper

```java id="c81z93"
public interface IdempotencyMapper {

    Optional<IdempotencyRecord> findByKey(
            @Param("key") String key
    );

    int insert(IdempotencyRecord record);
}
```

```java id="kk2kfj"
public record IdempotencyRecord(
        String key,
        String requestHash,
        String responseBody
) {
}
```

---

# 14. Idempotency Duplicate Insert Test

```java id="d4h2qu"
@Test
void shouldAllowOnlyOneInsertForSameIdempotencyKey() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory factory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    AtomicInteger successCount = new AtomicInteger();
    AtomicInteger duplicateCount = new AtomicInteger();

    List<Throwable> errors = ConcurrentTestRunner.runConcurrently(
            2,
            () -> {
                try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
                    IdempotencyMapper mapper =
                            session.getMapper(IdempotencyMapper.class);

                    try {
                        mapper.insert(new IdempotencyRecord(
                                "idem-001",
                                "hash-1",
                                "{\"caseId\":\"CASE-001\"}"
                        ));
                        session.commit();
                        successCount.incrementAndGet();
                    } catch (PersistenceException exception) {
                        session.rollback();
                        duplicateCount.incrementAndGet();
                    }
                }
            }
    );

    assertThat(errors).isEmpty();
    assertThat(successCount.get()).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(duplicateCount.get()).isEqualTo(1);

    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(true)) {
        IdempotencyMapper mapper =
                session.getMapper(IdempotencyMapper.class);

        Optional<IdempotencyRecord> record =
                mapper.findByKey("idem-001");

        assertThat(record).isPresent();
        assertThat(record.get().requestHash()).isEqualTo("hash-1");
    }
}
```

Yang dibuktikan:

```text id="o7z003"
DB primary key benar-benar melindungi idempotency key.
Dua concurrent insert tidak menghasilkan dua record.
```

---

# 15. Idempotency Same Hash vs Different Hash

Unit policy sudah dites di seri sebelumnya. Integration test memastikan record persistence benar.

```java id="iy277g"
@Test
void shouldReturnExistingRecordForSameIdempotencyKey() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory factory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(true)) {
        IdempotencyMapper mapper =
                session.getMapper(IdempotencyMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new IdempotencyRecord(
                "idem-001",
                "hash-1",
                "{\"caseId\":\"CASE-001\"}"
        ));

        Optional<IdempotencyRecord> existing =
                mapper.findByKey("idem-001");

        assertThat(existing).hasValue(new IdempotencyRecord(
                "idem-001",
                "hash-1",
                "{\"caseId\":\"CASE-001\"}"
        ));
    }
}
```

Service integration-level behavior:

```text id="e501qc"
same key + same hash:
  response = previous response

same key + different hash:
  HTTP 409 / service conflict
```

---

# 16. Transaction Isolation: What to Test

PostgreSQL supports standard isolation level concepts, but behavior depends on database implementation. PostgreSQL’s documentation describes isolation levels and explains how concurrent transactions interact under them. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk aplikasi microservice, Anda biasanya tidak perlu mengetes teori isolation level secara penuh. Yang perlu dites adalah **business anomaly**.

Contoh anomaly yang relevan:

```text id="he7tos"
Lost update:
  dua user update row yang sama, update pertama hilang

Double approval:
  dua approver approve case bersamaan

Write skew:
  dua transaksi membaca kondisi yang sama lalu masing-masing menulis row berbeda sehingga invariant global rusak

Duplicate command:
  dua request dengan idempotency key sama diproses dua kali

Stale decision:
  service membaca status DRAFT, lalu sebelum update status sudah berubah
```

---

# 17. Lost Update Test

Buruk jika SQL seperti ini:

```sql id="fv7e8l"
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'SUBMITTED',
    version = version + 1
WHERE id = #{id}
```

Tanpa `status` dan `version` condition, update kedua bisa overwrite.

Test target:

```text id="x919sa"
Dua update berbasis old version tidak boleh dua-duanya sukses.
```

Sudah tercakup oleh optimistic locking test:

```text id="cpp5ub"
WHERE id = ?
  AND status = 'DRAFT'
  AND version = ?
```

Minimal assertion:

```text id="zlyu3l"
successCount = 1
staleCount = 1
final version = 1
final status = expected winning status
```

---

# 18. Double Approval Test

```java id="gr59ax"
@Test
void shouldAllowOnlyOneConcurrentApproval() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory factory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW,
                true,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                5
        ));
    }

    AtomicInteger successCount = new AtomicInteger();
    AtomicInteger conflictCount = new AtomicInteger();

    List<Throwable> errors = ConcurrentTestRunner.runConcurrently(
            2,
            () -> {
                try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
                    CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

                    CaseRecord current = mapper.findById("CASE-001")
                            .orElseThrow();

                    int affectedRows = mapper.markApproved(
                            "CASE-001",
                            "approver-" + Thread.currentThread().threadId(),
                            Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                            current.version()
                    );

                    if (affectedRows == 1) {
                        successCount.incrementAndGet();
                        session.commit();
                    } else {
                        conflictCount.incrementAndGet();
                        session.rollback();
                    }
                }
            }
    );

    assertThat(errors).isEmpty();
    assertThat(successCount.get()).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(conflictCount.get()).isEqualTo(1);

    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        CaseRecord result = mapper.findById("CASE-001")
                .orElseThrow();

        assertThat(result.status()).isEqualTo(CaseStatus.APPROVED);
        assertThat(result.version()).isEqualTo(6);
    }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="e9jkn1"
Untuk lifecycle irreversible, exactly-one-success adalah invariant penting.
```

# 19. Audit Log Double-Write Protection

Jika audit tidak boleh double untuk action tertentu, DB bisa bantu dengan unique constraint.

```sql id="bxhssj"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_case_audit_once
ON case_audit_log(case_id, action)
WHERE action IN ('CASE_SUBMITTED', 'CASE_APPROVED');
```

Test:

```java id="kyuhhg"
@Test
void shouldRejectDuplicateSubmittedAuditLog() throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory factory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(true)) {
        CaseMapper caseMapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
        CaseAuditMapper auditMapper = session.getMapper(CaseAuditMapper.class);

        caseMapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                true,
                null,
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                null,
                1
        ));

        auditMapper.insert(new CaseAuditRecord(
                null,
                "CASE-001",
                "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                "user-123",
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z")
        ));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> auditMapper.insert(new CaseAuditRecord(
                null,
                "CASE-001",
                "CASE_SUBMITTED",
                "user-123",
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:01:00Z")
        ))).isInstanceOf(PersistenceException.class);
    }
}
```

---

# 20. Deadlock Test

Deadlock sering terjadi jika dua transaksi lock resource dalam urutan berbeda.

```text id="hnr9g2"
Transaction A:
  lock CASE-001
  lock CASE-002

Transaction B:
  lock CASE-002
  lock CASE-001
```

Test concept:

```java id="06ztr0"
@Test
void shouldSurfaceDeadlockWhenLocksAreAcquiredInOppositeOrder()
        throws Exception {
    SqlSessionFactory factory =
            MyBatisTestFactory.create(jdbcUrl(), username(), password());

    seedCase("CASE-001");
    seedCase("CASE-002");

    CountDownLatch bothLockedFirstRow = new CountDownLatch(2);
    CountDownLatch proceedToSecondLock = new CountDownLatch(1);

    AtomicInteger deadlockCount = new AtomicInteger();

    List<Throwable> errors = ConcurrentTestRunner.runConcurrently(
            2,
            () -> {
                boolean firstThread =
                        Thread.currentThread().getName().endsWith("1");

                String firstId = firstThread ? "CASE-001" : "CASE-002";
                String secondId = firstThread ? "CASE-002" : "CASE-001";

                try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
                    setLocalLockTimeout(session, "5s");

                    CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

                    mapper.findByIdForUpdate(firstId).orElseThrow();

                    bothLockedFirstRow.countDown();
                    bothLockedFirstRow.await();

                    proceedToSecondLock.countDown();

                    mapper.findByIdForUpdate(secondId).orElseThrow();

                    session.commit();
                } catch (PersistenceException exception) {
                    deadlockCount.incrementAndGet();
                }
            }
    );

    assertThat(deadlockCount.get()).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(1);
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="t4quj5"
Deadlock test bisa flaky jika terlalu bergantung timing.
Lebih sering cukup test lock ordering rule di code review + integration test untuk path kritikal.
```

Practical prevention:

```text id="4rh13r"
Selalu lock resources dengan urutan deterministik:
ORDER BY id
atau sort IDs sebelum lock.
```

---

# 21. Transaction Retry Classification

Tidak semua database error harus diperlakukan sama.

| Error type | Biasanya retry? | Contoh |
|---|---:|---|
| Duplicate key idempotency | Tidak selalu | Return previous atau conflict |
| Optimistic lock affected row 0 | Tidak otomatis | Return 409/stale state |
| Lock timeout | Mungkin retry | Tergantung command |
| Deadlock detected | Biasanya retryable | Retry transaction terbatas |
| Serialization failure | Biasanya retryable | Retry transaction terbatas |
| FK violation | Tidak | Data/input error |
| Check constraint violation | Tidak | Data/input error |

PostgreSQL transaction isolation documentation discusses serialization and concurrent transaction behavior; practical applications commonly classify serialization/deadlock-style failures as retryable, but retry policy must be bounded. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 22. Retry Policy Unit Test

```java id="59gp75"
public enum DatabaseFailureType {
    DUPLICATE_KEY,
    OPTIMISTIC_LOCK_CONFLICT,
    LOCK_TIMEOUT,
    DEADLOCK,
    SERIALIZATION_FAILURE,
    FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION,
    CHECK_VIOLATION
}
```

```java id="0lckl5"
public class DatabaseRetryPolicy {

    public boolean shouldRetry(DatabaseFailureType failureType) {
        return switch (failureType) {
            case LOCK_TIMEOUT, DEADLOCK, SERIALIZATION_FAILURE -> true;
            case DUPLICATE_KEY,
                 OPTIMISTIC_LOCK_CONFLICT,
                 FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION,
                 CHECK_VIOLATION -> false;
        };
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="jtsw7j"
@ParameterizedTest
@CsvSource({
        "LOCK_TIMEOUT, true",
        "DEADLOCK, true",
        "SERIALIZATION_FAILURE, true",
        "DUPLICATE_KEY, false",
        "OPTIMISTIC_LOCK_CONFLICT, false",
        "FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION, false",
        "CHECK_VIOLATION, false"
})
void shouldClassifyRetryableDatabaseFailures(
        DatabaseFailureType failureType,
        boolean expected
) {
    DatabaseRetryPolicy policy = new DatabaseRetryPolicy();

    assertThat(policy.shouldRetry(failureType))
            .isEqualTo(expected);
}
```

Integration test membuktikan error muncul. Unit test membuktikan klasifikasi.

---

# 23. MyBatis Transaction Notes

MyBatis `SqlSession` adalah interface utama untuk menjalankan mapped statements dan mengelola transaction; dokumentasi Java API MyBatis menyebut `SqlSession` dipakai untuk execute commands, get mappers, dan manage transactions. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jika memakai MyBatis-Spring, transaksi biasanya dikelola oleh Spring transaction manager, dan MyBatis-Spring akan mengelola commit/rollback sesuai transaksi. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/spring/transactions.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk aplikasi non-Spring/Jersey manual:

```java id="7llcaj"
try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
    CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

    mapper.doSomething();

    session.commit();
} catch (Exception exception) {
    session.rollback();
    throw exception;
}
```

Untuk aplikasi dengan transaction manager internal/perusahaan:

```text id="57lv8c"
Pastikan integration test memakai transaction path yang sama dengan production,
bukan manual transaction path yang berbeda.
```

---

# 24. Concurrency Test Stability Rules

Concurrency test rawan flaky. Pakai aturan ini:

```text id="hqdyiw"
[ ] Gunakan PostgreSQL Testcontainer nyata.
[ ] Gunakan barrier/latch untuk start bersamaan.
[ ] Hindari Thread.sleep sebagai sinkronisasi utama.
[ ] Gunakan lock_timeout kecil untuk blocking test.
[ ] Gunakan timeout JUnit agar test tidak menggantung.
[ ] Assert final DB state.
[ ] Assert jumlah success/failure.
[ ] Gunakan data unik per test.
[ ] Cleanup eksplisit antar test.
[ ] Jangan parallelize test class concurrency jika shared DB tidak aman.
```

Contoh timeout:

```java id="l6vlix"
@Test
@Timeout(value = 10, unit = TimeUnit.SECONDS)
void shouldAllowOnlyOneConcurrentSubmit() {
    // test
}
```

---

# 25. Integration Test Layout

```text id="hvz4ti"
src/test/java/com/company/caseapp/integration
├── CaseOptimisticLockingIT.java
├── CasePessimisticLockingIT.java
├── CaseDoubleSubmitIT.java
├── CaseDoubleApprovalIT.java
├── IdempotencyRepositoryIT.java
├── TransactionRollbackIT.java
├── LockTimeoutIT.java
└── support
    ├── PostgreSqlIntegrationTestSupport.java
    ├── ConcurrentTestRunner.java
    ├── DatabaseTestSupport.java
    └── CaseDbFixture.java
```

---

# 26. What Not to Do

## Jangan klaim concurrency aman hanya dari unit test

Buruk:

```java id="7q8stw"
when(repository.markSubmitted(...)).thenReturn(1);
```

Itu tidak membuktikan race condition.

## Jangan assert hanya exception

Buruk:

```java id="9s74n7"
assertThatThrownBy(...).isInstanceOf(Exception.class);
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="cgawkp"
- assert success count
- assert conflict count
- assert final DB row
- assert no duplicate audit
```

## Jangan biarkan test menunggu lock tanpa timeout

Selalu gunakan:

```sql id="lbqp35"
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '500ms';
```

atau timeout test level.

## Jangan rely pada urutan thread

Thread scheduling tidak deterministik. Test harus valid walau thread mana pun menang.

---

# 27. Minimal Test Set untuk Concurrency-Sensitive Feature

Untuk command `submitCase`:

```text id="v3l81r"
[ ] Single submit success.
[ ] Stale version submit affectedRows = 0.
[ ] Concurrent double submit → exactly one success.
[ ] Audit log tidak double.
[ ] Idempotency key duplicate protected.
[ ] Same idempotency key + same hash returns previous response.
[ ] Same idempotency key + different hash returns conflict.
[ ] Transaction rollback jika audit insert gagal.
```

Untuk command `approveCase`:

```text id="2yi9fy"
[ ] Approve from UNDER_REVIEW success.
[ ] Approve from APPROVED rejected.
[ ] Concurrent double approve → exactly one success.
[ ] Version increment once.
[ ] approved_by/approved_at milik winner.
[ ] Workflow/event tidak double-publish.
```

Untuk worker/job claim:

```text id="5e0wnl"
[ ] Two workers do not claim same job.
[ ] Locked job skipped or waited based on design.
[ ] Failed worker releases lock after rollback.
[ ] Retried job not lost.
```

---

# 28. Practical Design Recommendations

## Prefer DB-enforced invariants for critical rules

Application check saja tidak cukup.

```text id="lvozf0"
Good:
- unique idempotency key
- optimistic version condition
- unique audit action where applicable
- FK constraints
- check constraints for basic status domain if feasible
```

## Keep transaction short

```text id="7kwdv3"
Do not:
- hold DB transaction while calling external API
- hold lock while waiting on Camunda/external service
- do slow file/network operation inside transaction
```

PostgreSQL warns against leaving transactions idle because they hold resources and can interfere with vacuum/locking behavior. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Use outbox pattern for side effect consistency

If state update and event publish must be atomic:

```text id="g0z9e2"
Transaction:
- update case
- insert outbox event

After commit:
- outbox publisher sends event/workflow signal
```

Do not rely on:

```text id="vc6mkf"
update DB
then publish external event directly
```

because event publish can fail after DB commit.

---

# 29. Ringkasan Seri 8

Inti dari seri ini:

```text id="qk0hm1"
Concurrency correctness tidak bisa dibuktikan dengan mock.
Harus dites dengan PostgreSQL nyata, transaction nyata, constraint nyata, dan concurrent execution nyata.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text id="c5pg3i"
Optimistic locking:
- WHERE version = ?
- affectedRows = 0 berarti conflict

Pessimistic locking:
- SELECT FOR UPDATE
- lock ditahan sampai transaction selesai

Idempotency:
- unique key di DB
- same key same hash → previous response
- same key different hash → conflict

Concurrency test:
- coordinate dengan latch/barrier
- assert final DB state
- gunakan timeout
- hindari sleep sebagai sinkronisasi utama
```

Boundary yang benar:

```text id="wheh2j"
Unit test:
- retry policy
- idempotency decision
- error classification

Integration test:
- row lock
- unique constraint
- concurrent update
- transaction rollback
- affected row count
- final database state
```

# Seri 9/14 - External API Integration Test with Stub Server  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada testing integrasi **keluar** dari microservice Anda ke sistem eksternal:

```text id="af6wnr"
Java service
  ↓
HTTP client / gateway
  ↓
External API
```

Contoh external system:

```text id="mim1cx"
payment gateway
identity provider
document service
OCR/media service
notification service
fraud/risk service
order management platform
Camunda external worker endpoint
third-party regulatory API
internal platform API
```

Tujuan external API integration test bukan memanggil service asli. Tujuannya adalah membuktikan bahwa client/gateway Anda benar saat berhadapan dengan **HTTP behavior nyata**:

```text id="6qk7pf"
- request path benar
- method benar
- header benar
- JSON body benar
- query param benar
- timeout benar
- retry benar
- error response dimapping benar
- idempotency header dikirim
- correlation ID diteruskan
- auth/signature header benar
- malformed/slow/5xx response ditangani benar
```

Gunakan **stub server** seperti **WireMock** atau **OkHttp MockWebServer**, bukan external service production/staging. WireMock adalah tool untuk mensimulasikan HTTP APIs/dependencies dan cocok untuk API mocking, edge cases, serta environment development/test yang stabil. ([WireMock](https://wiremock.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) WireMock juga punya integrasi JUnit Jupiter, termasuk behavior default yang membuat test gagal bila ada unmatched request. ([WireMock](https://wiremock.org/docs/junit-jupiter/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) OkHttp MockWebServer juga menyediakan library untuk testing HTTP, HTTPS, dan HTTP/2 clients. ([Square Open Source](https://square.github.io/okhttp/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Posisi External API Integration Test

```text id="6j9j4l"
Unit test:
- retry policy
- error classification
- request mapper
- response mapper

External API integration test:
- real HTTP client
- stub HTTP server
- request/response over actual HTTP
- timeout/retry/error mapping

End-to-end test:
- real external system or full deployed environment
- very few tests only
```

External API integration test berada di tengah:

```text id="o2c01e"
Lebih nyata dari unit test:
- HTTP client benar-benar melakukan network call ke local stub server
- serialization/deserialization nyata
- timeout behavior bisa diuji
- headers/path/body diverifikasi

Lebih aman dari E2E:
- tidak bergantung external system asli
- deterministik
- bisa jalan di CI
- bisa mensimulasikan edge cases yang sulit dibuat di service asli
```

---

# 3. Diagram Boundary

```text id="qyg2g8"
External API Integration Test Boundary

JUnit / Failsafe
   │
   ▼
Service / Gateway under test
   │
   ▼
Real HTTP Client
   │
   ├── JDK HttpClient / Jersey Client / Apache HttpClient / OkHttp
   │
   ▼
Stub Server
   │
   ├── expected request matching
   ├── canned response
   ├── delayed response
   ├── 4xx / 5xx / malformed response
   └── request journal / verification

Not used:
- real external API
- production credentials
- production network dependency
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | Fokus test | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| Gateway design | External client wrapper | Isolasi HTTP details dari service | Testable boundary |
| Stub server setup | WireMock/MockWebServer | Local HTTP API simulation | Deterministic test |
| Request mapping | Method/path/query/body | Client mengirim contract benar | Consumer-side correctness |
| Header propagation | Correlation, tenant, auth | Metadata tidak hilang | Observability/security |
| Idempotency header | `Idempotency-Key` | Retry aman | Duplicate prevention |
| Response mapping | 2xx JSON → domain object | Deserialization benar | Client result benar |
| Error mapping | 400/401/403/404/409/429/500 | Domain exception/return type benar | Failure readable |
| Timeout handling | Slow server | Client tidak menggantung | Resilience |
| Retry behavior | 5xx/timeout/transient error | Retry terbatas dan aman | Stability |
| Circuit breaker | Open/half-open/closed behavior | Stop hammering dependency | Resilience |
| Malformed response | Broken JSON/wrong content-type | Defensive parsing | Robustness |
| Contract drift | Expected request/response fixture | Detect API change early | Safer integration |
| CI lifecycle | `*IT` + Failsafe | Integration test terpisah | Pipeline maintainable |

---

# 5. Running Example: Payment Gateway Client

Misalnya aplikasi Anda perlu capture payment ke external payment API.

External API contract:

```http id="thfbg4"
POST /v1/payments/{paymentId}/capture
Content-Type: application/json
Authorization: Bearer <token>
X-Correlation-ID: corr-123
Idempotency-Key: idem-001
```

Request:

```json id="k73gsd"
{
  "amount": 150000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "capturedBy": "user-123"
}
```

Response success:

```json id="2v0hp7"
{
  "paymentId": "PAY-001",
  "status": "CAPTURED",
  "providerReference": "PGW-999"
}
```
