# 6. Gateway Interface

Service layer sebaiknya tidak tahu detail HTTP.

```java id="aewpvk"
public interface PaymentGatewayClient {

    CapturePaymentResult capturePayment(
            CapturePaymentCommand command
    );
}
```

Command:

```java id="9dq9yj"
import java.math.BigDecimal;

public record CapturePaymentCommand(
        String paymentId,
        BigDecimal amount,
        String currency,
        String capturedBy,
        String idempotencyKey,
        String correlationId
) {
}
```

Result:

```java id="wp84qs"
public record CapturePaymentResult(
        String paymentId,
        PaymentCaptureStatus status,
        String providerReference
) {
}
```

Enum:

```java id="as8qmd"
public enum PaymentCaptureStatus {
    CAPTURED,
    PENDING,
    FAILED
}
```

---

# 7. HTTP Client Implementation Example

Contoh menggunakan Java `HttpClient` agar tidak bergantung framework tertentu.

```java id="h3itau"
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.time.Duration;

public class HttpPaymentGatewayClient implements PaymentGatewayClient {

    private final HttpClient httpClient;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final URI baseUri;
    private final String bearerToken;

    public HttpPaymentGatewayClient(
            HttpClient httpClient,
            ObjectMapper objectMapper,
            URI baseUri,
            String bearerToken
    ) {
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.baseUri = baseUri;
        this.bearerToken = bearerToken;
    }

    @Override
    public CapturePaymentResult capturePayment(
            CapturePaymentCommand command
    ) {
        try {
            CapturePaymentRequest requestBody =
                    new CapturePaymentRequest(
                            command.amount(),
                            command.currency(),
                            command.capturedBy()
                    );

            String json = objectMapper.writeValueAsString(requestBody);

            HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                    .uri(baseUri.resolve(
                            "/v1/payments/" + command.paymentId() + "/capture"
                    ))
                    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
                    .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .header("Accept", "application/json")
                    .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + bearerToken)
                    .header("X-Correlation-ID", command.correlationId())
                    .header("Idempotency-Key", command.idempotencyKey())
                    .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(json))
                    .build();

            HttpResponse<String> response = httpClient.send(
                    request,
                    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()
            );

            return mapResponse(response);

        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new ExternalDependencyUnavailableException(
                    "PAYMENT_GATEWAY",
                    exception
            );
        } catch (InterruptedException exception) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new ExternalDependencyUnavailableException(
                    "PAYMENT_GATEWAY",
                    exception
            );
        }
    }

    private CapturePaymentResult mapResponse(
            HttpResponse<String> response
    ) throws IOException {
        int statusCode = response.statusCode();

        if (statusCode >= 200 && statusCode < 300) {
            CapturePaymentResponse body = objectMapper.readValue(
                    response.body(),
                    CapturePaymentResponse.class
            );

            return new CapturePaymentResult(
                    body.paymentId(),
                    PaymentCaptureStatus.valueOf(body.status()),
                    body.providerReference()
            );
        }

        if (statusCode == 409) {
            throw new PaymentAlreadyCapturedException();
        }

        if (statusCode == 429 || statusCode >= 500) {
            throw new RetryableExternalDependencyException(
                    "PAYMENT_GATEWAY",
                    statusCode
            );
        }

        throw new NonRetryableExternalDependencyException(
                "PAYMENT_GATEWAY",
                statusCode,
                response.body()
        );
    }

    private record CapturePaymentRequest(
            BigDecimal amount,
            String currency,
            String capturedBy
    ) {
    }

    private record CapturePaymentResponse(
            String paymentId,
            String status,
            String providerReference
    ) {
    }
}
```

---

# 8. WireMock Setup with JUnit Jupiter

WireMock JUnit Jupiter extension bisa dipakai secara declarative atau programmatic, dan dokumentasinya menyebut unmatched requests default-nya akan membuat test gagal. Ini bagus karena request salah path/header/body tidak diam-diam lolos. ([WireMock](https://wiremock.org/docs/junit-jupiter/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Maven dependency:

```xml id="iv4q6i"
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.wiremock</groupId>
    <artifactId>wiremock-junit5</artifactId>
    <scope>test</scope>
</dependency>
```

Test skeleton:

```java id="lc2de9"
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.wiremock.junit5.WireMockExtension;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.time.Duration;

import static com.github.tomakehurst.wiremock.client.WireMock.*;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.wiremock.junit5.WireMockExtension.newInstance;

class PaymentGatewayClientIT {

    static WireMockExtension paymentApi = newInstance()
            .options(wireMockConfig().dynamicPort())
            .build();

    private HttpPaymentGatewayClient client() {
        return new HttpPaymentGatewayClient(
                HttpClient.newBuilder()
                        .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(1))
                        .build(),
                new ObjectMapper(),
                URI.create(paymentApi.baseUrl()),
                "test-token"
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 9. Success Response Test

```java id="c283mt"
@Test
void shouldCapturePaymentSuccessfully() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .withHeader("Authorization", equalTo("Bearer test-token"))
            .withHeader("X-Correlation-ID", equalTo("corr-123"))
            .withHeader("Idempotency-Key", equalTo("idem-001"))
            .withHeader("Content-Type", containing("application/json"))
            .withRequestBody(matchingJsonPath("$.amount", equalTo("150000")))
            .withRequestBody(matchingJsonPath("$.currency", equalTo("IDR")))
            .withRequestBody(matchingJsonPath("$.capturedBy", equalTo("user-123")))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(200)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                            {
                              "paymentId": "PAY-001",
                              "status": "CAPTURED",
                              "providerReference": "PGW-999"
                            }
                            """)));

    CapturePaymentResult result = client().capturePayment(
            new CapturePaymentCommand(
                    "PAY-001",
                    new BigDecimal("150000"),
                    "IDR",
                    "user-123",
                    "idem-001",
                    "corr-123"
            )
    );

    assertThat(result).isEqualTo(new CapturePaymentResult(
            "PAY-001",
            PaymentCaptureStatus.CAPTURED,
            "PGW-999"
    ));

    paymentApi.verify(postRequestedFor(
            urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture")
    ));
}
```

Yang dibuktikan:

```text id="4xuipl"
- URL path benar
- method POST benar
- auth header benar
- correlation ID benar
- idempotency key benar
- JSON request body benar
- success JSON response dimapping benar
```

# 10. Error Mapping Test: 409 Conflict

```java id="x14ac8"
@Test
void shouldMap409ConflictToPaymentAlreadyCapturedException() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(409)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                            {
                              "code": "PAYMENT_ALREADY_CAPTURED",
                              "message": "Payment already captured"
                            }
                            """)));

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> client().capturePayment(
            new CapturePaymentCommand(
                    "PAY-001",
                    new BigDecimal("150000"),
                    "IDR",
                    "user-123",
                    "idem-001",
                    "corr-123"
            )
    )).isInstanceOf(PaymentAlreadyCapturedException.class);
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="y1ou78"
HTTP status dari external API jangan bocor mentah ke service layer.
Map ke exception/result domain yang jelas.
```

---

# 11. Error Mapping Table

| External response | Client behavior | Retry? |
|---|---|---:|
| `200/201/202` | Return success/pending result | No |
| `400` | Non-retryable invalid request | No |
| `401` | Auth/config failure | Usually no |
| `403` | Forbidden/permission/config failure | Usually no |
| `404` | External resource not found | Depends |
| `409` | Conflict/idempotency/state conflict | Usually no |
| `422` | Business validation failure | No |
| `429` | Rate limited | Yes, bounded |
| `500` | Provider internal error | Yes, bounded |
| `502/503/504` | Dependency unavailable | Yes, bounded |
| timeout | Dependency unavailable | Yes, bounded |
| malformed JSON | Provider contract error | Usually no or limited retry |

---

# 12. Timeout Test

Timeout harus dites dengan response delay.

```java id="35jjuo"
@Test
void shouldThrowDependencyUnavailableWhenPaymentApiTimesOut() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withFixedDelay(3000)
                    .withStatus(200)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                            {
                              "paymentId": "PAY-001",
                              "status": "CAPTURED",
                              "providerReference": "PGW-999"
                            }
                            """)));

    HttpPaymentGatewayClient slowTimeoutClient =
            new HttpPaymentGatewayClient(
                    HttpClient.newBuilder()
                            .connectTimeout(Duration.ofMillis(200))
                            .build(),
                    new ObjectMapper(),
                    URI.create(paymentApi.baseUrl()),
                    "test-token"
            );

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> slowTimeoutClient.capturePayment(
            new CapturePaymentCommand(
                    "PAY-001",
                    new BigDecimal("150000"),
                    "IDR",
                    "user-123",
                    "idem-001",
                    "corr-123"
            )
    )).isInstanceOf(ExternalDependencyUnavailableException.class);
}
```

Catatan praktis:

```text id="h4r6my"
Test timeout harus menggunakan duration kecil.
Jangan membuat CI menunggu detik panjang untuk setiap test.
```

---

# 13. Retry Test

Retry sebaiknya dites di dua layer:

```text id="9td8x7"
Unit test:
- retry policy classification

Integration test:
- HTTP client benar-benar melakukan retry ke stub server
```

Dengan WireMock scenario:

```java id="y1ht2h"
@Test
void shouldRetryTransientServerErrorAndEventuallySucceed() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .inScenario("retry capture")
            .whenScenarioStateIs(STARTED)
            .willReturn(aResponse().withStatus(503))
            .willSetStateTo("second attempt"));

    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .inScenario("retry capture")
            .whenScenarioStateIs("second attempt")
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(200)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                            {
                              "paymentId": "PAY-001",
                              "status": "CAPTURED",
                              "providerReference": "PGW-999"
                            }
                            """)));

    CapturePaymentResult result = retryingClient().capturePayment(
            new CapturePaymentCommand(
                    "PAY-001",
                    new BigDecimal("150000"),
                    "IDR",
                    "user-123",
                    "idem-001",
                    "corr-123"
            )
    );

    assertThat(result.status()).isEqualTo(PaymentCaptureStatus.CAPTURED);

    paymentApi.verify(2, postRequestedFor(
            urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture")
    ));
}
```

Resilience4j menyediakan module retry dengan `RetryRegistry` untuk membuat dan mengambil Retry instance. ([resilience4j](https://resilience4j.readme.io/docs/retry?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jika Anda memakai Resilience4j, retry behavior bisa dikonfigurasi secara eksplisit dan dites dengan stub server.

---

# 14. Bounded Retry Test

Pastikan retry tidak infinite.

```java id="0kzfvh"
@Test
void shouldStopRetryingAfterMaximumAttempts() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(503)
                    .withBody("Service unavailable")));

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> retryingClient().capturePayment(
            new CapturePaymentCommand(
                    "PAY-001",
                    new BigDecimal("150000"),
                    "IDR",
                    "user-123",
                    "idem-001",
                    "corr-123"
            )
    )).isInstanceOf(RetryableExternalDependencyException.class);

    paymentApi.verify(3, postRequestedFor(
            urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture")
    ));
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="ikq3pp"
Retry harus bounded.
Tanpa limit, dependency outage bisa menjadi thread exhaustion.
```

---

# 15. Circuit Breaker Test

Circuit breaker cocok untuk dependency yang sering gagal. Resilience4j menyediakan `CircuitBreakerRegistry` in-memory dan thread-safety/atomicity guarantees untuk registry-nya. ([resilience4j](https://resilience4j.readme.io/docs/circuitbreaker?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Yang perlu dites:

```text id="6qxcnu"
- setelah failure threshold, circuit open
- saat open, request tidak dikirim ke external API
- fallback/exception benar
- setelah wait duration, half-open behavior sesuai
```

Conceptual test:

```java id="d0htq5"
@Test
void shouldOpenCircuitBreakerAfterRepeatedFailures() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .willReturn(aResponse().withStatus(503)));

    PaymentGatewayClient client = circuitBreakerClient();

    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.capturePayment(command()))
                .isInstanceOf(ExternalDependencyUnavailableException.class);
    }

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.capturePayment(command()))
            .isInstanceOf(CircuitBreakerOpenException.class);

    paymentApi.verify(5, postRequestedFor(
            urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture")
    ));
}
```

Assertion penting:

```text id="nzukyz"
Saat circuit open, request berikutnya tidak boleh sampai ke stub server.
```

---

Correlation ID wajib diteruskan agar tracing lintas service bisa dilakukan.

```java id="f4l5dw"
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .withHeader("X-Correlation-ID", equalTo("corr-123"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(200)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                            {
                              "paymentId": "PAY-001",
                              "status": "CAPTURED",
                              "providerReference": "PGW-999"
                            }
                            """)));

    client().capturePayment(new CapturePaymentCommand(
            "PAY-001",
            new BigDecimal("150000"),
            "IDR",
            "user-123",
            "idem-001",
            "corr-123"
    ));

    paymentApi.verify(postRequestedFor(
            urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture")
    ).withHeader("X-Correlation-ID", equalTo("corr-123")));
}
```

# 17. Idempotency Header Test

Untuk external payment/order API, idempotency key harus selalu dikirim pada command mutating.

```java id="mxroaz"
@Test
void shouldSendIdempotencyKeyForCaptureRequest() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .withHeader("Idempotency-Key", equalTo("idem-001"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(200)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                            {
                              "paymentId": "PAY-001",
                              "status": "CAPTURED",
                              "providerReference": "PGW-999"
                            }
                            """)));

    client().capturePayment(new CapturePaymentCommand(
            "PAY-001",
            new BigDecimal("150000"),
            "IDR",
            "user-123",
            "idem-001",
            "corr-123"
    ));

    paymentApi.verify(postRequestedFor(
            urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture")
    ).withHeader("Idempotency-Key", equalTo("idem-001")));
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="qcwulp"
Mutating external request harus idempotent jika client bisa retry.
```

# 18. Auth Header / Token Test

```java id="2jopav"
@Test
void shouldSendBearerTokenToPaymentApi() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .withHeader("Authorization", equalTo("Bearer test-token"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(200)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                            {
                              "paymentId": "PAY-001",
                              "status": "CAPTURED",
                              "providerReference": "PGW-999"
                            }
                            """)));

    client().capturePayment(command());

    paymentApi.verify(postRequestedFor(
            urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture")
    ).withHeader("Authorization", equalTo("Bearer test-token")));
}
```

Security note:

```text id="pcjjbd"
Jangan gunakan production token di test.
Gunakan dummy token dan assert tidak muncul di exception/log response jika ada error.
```

# 19. Request Signing Test

Beberapa external API butuh signature header.

Contoh header:

```text id="g239ei"
X-Signature: HMAC-SHA256(...)
X-Timestamp: 2026-07-15T10:00:00Z
```

Desain testable:

```java id="2srm3y"
public interface RequestSigner {
    SignatureHeaders sign(String method, String path, String body);
}
```

Unit test signer:

```java id="z62uqx"
@Test
void shouldGenerateExpectedSignature() {
    RequestSigner signer = new HmacRequestSigner(
            "test-secret",
            Clock.fixed(
                    Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                    ZoneOffset.UTC
            )
    );

    SignatureHeaders headers = signer.sign(
            "POST",
            "/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture",
            "{\"amount\":150000}"
    );

    assertThat(headers.timestamp())
            .isEqualTo("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z");

    assertThat(headers.signature())
            .isNotBlank();
}
```

Integration test client sends headers:

```java id="ckgpdh"
@Test
void shouldSendSignatureHeaders() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .withHeader("X-Timestamp", equalTo("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"))
            .withHeader("X-Signature", matching(".+"))
            .willReturn(okJson("""
                    {
                      "paymentId": "PAY-001",
                      "status": "CAPTURED",
                      "providerReference": "PGW-999"
                    }
                    """)));

    signedClient().capturePayment(command());

    paymentApi.verify(postRequestedFor(
            urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture")
    ).withHeader("X-Signature", matching(".+")));
}
```

# 20. Malformed JSON Response Test

External API bisa mengembalikan response tidak sesuai contract.

```java id="j9s4cs"
@Test
void shouldMapMalformedJsonToProviderContractException() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(200)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                            {
                              "paymentId": "PAY-001",
                              "status":
                            }
                            """)));

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> client().capturePayment(command()))
            .isInstanceOf(ExternalProviderContractException.class);
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="dyen68"
2xx response tidak otomatis berarti sukses.
Body tetap harus valid sesuai contract.
```

# 21. Wrong Content-Type Test

```java id="qjhkf2"
@Test
void shouldRejectUnexpectedContentType() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(200)
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "text/html")
                    .withBody("<html>ok</html>")));

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> client().capturePayment(command()))
            .isInstanceOf(ExternalProviderContractException.class);
}
```

Ini penting karena sebagian gateway/load balancer mengembalikan HTML error page.

# 22. Rate Limit Test: 429

```java id="8edcuf"
@Test
void shouldMap429ToRetryableRateLimitException() {
    paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
            .willReturn(aResponse()
                    .withStatus(429)
                    .withHeader("Retry-After", "10")
                    .withHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .withBody("""
                            {
                              "code": "RATE_LIMITED",
                              "message": "Too many requests"
                            }
                            """)));

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> client().capturePayment(command()))
            .isInstanceOf(RateLimitedExternalDependencyException.class);
}
```

Practical behavior:

```text id="t3slel"
429 biasanya retryable, tetapi harus bounded dan respect Retry-After jika design mendukung.
```

---

# 23. Using MockWebServer Instead of WireMock

OkHttp MockWebServer cocok jika Anda ingin server sederhana berbasis queued responses dan recorded requests. OkHttp docs menyebut MockWebServer sebagai scriptable web server: caller menyediakan canned responses dan server me-replay responses secara berurutan. ([Square Open Source](https://square.github.io/okhttp/3.x/mockwebserver/index.html?okhttp3%2Fmockwebserver%2FMockWebServer.html=&utm_source=chatgpt.com)) OkHttp juga menyediakan module `mockwebserver-junit5` untuk integrasi dengan JUnit 5. ([GitHub](https://github.com/square/okhttp/blob/master/mockwebserver-junit5/README.md?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Example concept:

```java id="u8wd2d"
@Test
void shouldSendExpectedRequestWithMockWebServer() throws Exception {
    MockWebServer server = new MockWebServer();

    server.enqueue(new MockResponse()
            .setResponseCode(200)
            .setHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .setBody("""
                    {
                      "paymentId": "PAY-001",
                      "status": "CAPTURED",
                      "providerReference": "PGW-999"
                    }
                    """));

    server.start();

    try {
        PaymentGatewayClient client = new HttpPaymentGatewayClient(
                HttpClient.newHttpClient(),
                new ObjectMapper(),
                server.url("/").uri(),
                "test-token"
        );

        CapturePaymentResult result = client.capturePayment(command());

        RecordedRequest request = server.takeRequest();

        assertThat(request.getMethod()).isEqualTo("POST");
        assertThat(request.getPath())
                .isEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture");
        assertThat(request.getHeader("Idempotency-Key"))
                .isEqualTo("idem-001");

        assertThat(result.status())
                .isEqualTo(PaymentCaptureStatus.CAPTURED);
    } finally {
        server.shutdown();
    }
}
```

Rule of thumb:

```text id="5g0mny"
WireMock:
- request matching kuat
- scenario/stateful stubbing
- body matchers
- verification richer

MockWebServer:
- simple queued responses
- recorded requests
- lightweight
- cocok untuk client-level tests
```

---

# 24. Contract Fixture Strategy

Simpan payload external API di file agar contract readable.

```text id="a23gjh"
src/test/resources/external/payment/capture-success-response.json
src/test/resources/external/payment/capture-conflict-response.json
src/test/resources/external/payment/capture-request.json
```

Helper:

```java id="a23al3"
public final class ExternalApiFixtures {

    private ExternalApiFixtures() {
    }

    public static String fixture(String path) {
        try (InputStream input =
                     ExternalApiFixtures.class.getResourceAsStream(path)) {
            if (input == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Fixture not found: " + path);
            }
            return new String(input.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(exception);
        }
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java id="u5k029"
paymentApi.stubFor(post(urlEqualTo("/v1/payments/PAY-001/capture"))
        .willReturn(okJson(fixture(
                "/external/payment/capture-success-response.json"
        ))));
```

Prinsip:

```text id="v4pgse"
Payload kecil boleh inline.
Payload besar/contract penting sebaiknya fixture file.
```

---

# 25. Test Class Layout

```text id="yuu3bq"
src/test/java/com/company/payment/integration
├── PaymentGatewayClientIT.java
├── PaymentGatewayRetryIT.java
├── PaymentGatewayTimeoutIT.java
├── PaymentGatewayErrorMappingIT.java
├── PaymentGatewayAuthHeaderIT.java
└── support
    ├── WireMockPaymentApiSupport.java
    ├── ExternalApiFixtures.java
    └── PaymentCommands.java
```

Atau per external system:

```text id="g82s3n"
integration/external
├── payment
│   ├── PaymentCaptureClientIT.java
│   ├── PaymentRefundClientIT.java
│   └── PaymentWebhookClientIT.java
├── identity
│   └── IdentityClientIT.java
└── document
    └── DocumentServiceClientIT.java
```

---

# 26. Maven Failsafe

External API integration test biasanya dinamai:

```text id="lwyiz1"
*IT.java
```

Run:

```bash id="wu8s7a"
mvn verify
```

Maven Failsafe Plugin digunakan pada fase `integration-test` dan `verify` untuk menjalankan integration tests. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-failsafe-plugin/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Jika test sangat cepat dan tidak butuh Docker/network eksternal, sebagian tim menjalankannya di `mvn test`. Tetapi untuk konsistensi, external stub-server tests biasanya lebih aman dipisah ke `*IT`.

---

# 27. CI/CD Practical Policy

```text id="m7ixbe"
Every PR:
- critical external client IT
- request mapping
- response mapping
- common error mapping

Main branch:
- full external client test suite
- retry/timeout tests
- contract fixtures

Nightly:
- optional provider sandbox smoke test
- provider contract drift check
```

Jangan memanggil external sandbox di setiap PR jika:

```text id="39dlb6"
- lambat
- rate limited
- unstable
- butuh credential sensitif
- stateful dan sulit cleanup
```

---

# 28. What Not to Do

## Jangan panggil external production/staging di integration test PR

Risiko:

```text id="uqjx1l"
- flaky
- lambat
- rate limit
- data kotor
- credential leak
- environment dependency
```

Gunakan stub server.

## Jangan hanya unit test request mapper

Unit test mapper penting, tetapi tidak membuktikan HTTP client benar-benar mengirim header/path/body.

## Jangan retry semua error

Buruk:

```text id="0zjv8h"
Retry 400/401/403/422
```

Itu biasanya memperparah beban dan tidak akan sukses.

## Jangan retry mutating request tanpa idempotency key

Retry POST/PUT/PATCH tanpa idempotency strategy bisa menyebabkan double charge/double submit.

## Jangan biarkan timeout default library

Default timeout sering terlalu lama atau tidak sesuai production SLA. Set eksplisit.

---

# 29. Practical Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini untuk setiap external API client penting:

```text id="sj3u00"
[ ] Base URL injectable untuk test.
[ ] HTTP client timeout eksplisit.
[ ] Request method/path/query benar.
[ ] Required headers dikirim.
[ ] Auth/signature header benar.
[ ] Correlation ID diteruskan.
[ ] Tenant/header context diteruskan jika relevan.
[ ] Idempotency key dikirim untuk mutating operation.
[ ] Request JSON body sesuai contract.
[ ] 2xx response dimapping ke domain result.
[ ] 4xx response dimapping ke non-retryable exception.
[ ] 409 conflict/idempotency conflict ditangani khusus.
[ ] 429/5xx/timeout dimapping ke retryable failure.
[ ] Retry bounded dan jumlah attempt dites.
[ ] Circuit breaker tidak mengirim request saat open.
[ ] Malformed JSON ditangani sebagai provider contract error.
[ ] Wrong content-type ditangani.
[ ] No production credential dalam test.
[ ] Test memakai stub server, bukan external system asli.
```

---

# 30. Minimal Test Set per External Client

Untuk mutating external command seperti payment capture:

```text id="tyum64"
1. success 200/201/202 → result benar
2. request body/header/path verified
3. idempotency key verified
4. correlation ID verified
5. 400/422 → non-retryable
6. 401/403 → auth/config/forbidden error
7. 409 → conflict-specific exception
8. 429 → rate limited/retryable
9. 500/503 → retryable
10. timeout → dependency unavailable
11. retry eventually succeeds
12. retry stops after max attempts
13. malformed JSON → provider contract exception
14. wrong content-type → provider contract exception
```

Untuk query external API:

```text id="8f2fnb"
1. success 200 → result mapping
2. query params verified
3. pagination token verified
4. 404 behavior clear
5. 429/5xx retry behavior
6. malformed response
```

---

# 31. Ringkasan Seri 9

Inti dari seri ini:

```text id="2k1khz"
External API integration test membuktikan HTTP client behavior nyata
tanpa bergantung pada external system asli.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text id="blyqqe"
Gunakan stub server.
Inject base URL.
Set timeout eksplisit.
Verify method/path/header/body.
Propagate correlation ID.
Kirim idempotency key untuk mutating request.
Map error response ke exception/domain result yang jelas.
Retry hanya transient failure dan harus bounded.
Test malformed/wrong content-type response.
Jangan pakai production credentials.
```

Boundary yang benar:

```text id="jwwc3e"
Unit test:
- request mapper
- response mapper
- retry policy
- error classifier

External API integration test:
- real HTTP client
- stub server
- timeout
- retry
- headers
- request/response mapping

E2E/sandbox smoke:
- sangat sedikit
- hanya untuk provider connectivity atau release validation
```

```text id="04qrsk"
External API Integration Test with Stub Server
```

Lanjut ke **Seri 10/14**. Fokusnya workflow boundary: bagaimana memastikan BPMN/process, delegate/worker, process variable, business error, incident, dan integrasi service tidak hanya “jalan di diagram”, tapi benar secara runtime test.

# Seri 10/14 - Camunda Workflow Integration Testing  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada testing workflow/process layer untuk aplikasi yang memakai **Camunda 7 / Camunda 8** di sekitar microservice Java Jersey/JAX-RS.

Targetnya adalah memastikan BPMN tidak hanya valid secara desain, tetapi benar secara runtime:

```text
- process bisa start
- process variable benar
- service task / delegate / job worker terpanggil
- gateway mengambil path yang benar
- timer/escalation behavior benar
- business error ditangani benar
- incident/error path terdeteksi
- message/signal correlation benar
- workflow tidak double-trigger
- integration boundary dengan service layer jelas
```

Untuk Camunda 7, testing biasanya memakai embedded process engine dan extension JUnit. Camunda 7 menyediakan package `org.camunda.bpm.engine.test.junit5` dengan `ProcessEngineExtension` untuk membuat dan menginjeksi `ProcessEngine` ke test class. ([Camunda Documentation](https://docs.camunda.org/javadoc/camunda-bpm-platform/7.21/org/camunda/bpm/engine/test/junit5/package-summary.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk Camunda 8, testing landscape sudah berubah: dokumentasi Camunda menyebut **Camunda Process Test** sebagai Java library untuk mengetes BPMN process dan process application, dengan runtime berbasis Testcontainers sebagai default atau remote runtime seperti Camunda 8 Run. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/testing/getting-started/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Catatan penting terbaru: **Zeebe Process Test deprecated sejak Camunda 8.8 dan direncanakan dihapus di Camunda 8.10**, sehingga untuk Camunda 8 baru sebaiknya arahkan ke **Camunda Process Test**. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/testing/zeebe-process-test/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model: Apa yang Dites?

Workflow test bukan pengganti unit test service. Workflow test menjawab pertanyaan berbeda.

```text
Service unit test:
Apakah business logic Java benar?

Resource test:
Apakah HTTP boundary benar?

DB integration test:
Apakah SQL/transaction benar?

Workflow integration test:
Apakah process orchestration benar?
```

## Workflow boundary

```text
JAX-RS Resource / Scheduler / Message Consumer
        ↓
Application Service
        ↓
Camunda Runtime
        ↓
BPMN Process Instance
        ↓
Service Task / Delegate / Worker
        ↓
Domain Service / Repository / External Gateway
```

Workflow test membuktikan hubungan antara:

```text
BPMN model
process variables
gateway conditions
service task binding
delegate/worker behavior
message/signal/timer
business error
incident/retry behavior
```

---

# 3. Diagram

```text
Camunda Workflow Test Boundary
│
├── 1. Process Definition Test
│   ├── Deploy BPMN
│   ├── Start process instance
│   ├── Assert active/waiting/completed state
│   └── Assert path through gateways
│
├── 2. Variable Mapping Test
│   ├── Start variables
│   ├── Task input mapping
│   ├── Task output mapping
│   ├── Business key/correlation key
│   └── Variable type/serialization
│
├── 3. Service Task / Delegate / Worker Test
│   ├── JavaDelegate / delegate expression
│   ├── Camunda 8 job worker
│   ├── Mock/fake domain service
│   ├── Assert worker receives variables
│   └── Assert variables updated
│
├── 4. Gateway / Rule Path Test
│   ├── Exclusive gateway
│   ├── Parallel gateway
│   ├── Event-based gateway
│   ├── Boundary event
│   └── Error path
│
├── 5. Event Test
│   ├── Message correlation
│   ├── Signal event
│   ├── Timer event
│   ├── Escalation event
│   └── Compensation event
│
├── 6. Failure / Incident Test
│   ├── Technical exception
│   ├── BPMN business error
│   ├── Retry exhausted
│   ├── Incident created
│   └── Dead-letter / manual intervention path
│
├── 7. Human Task Test
│   ├── Task created
│   ├── Candidate group/user
│   ├── Form variables
│   ├── Complete task
│   └── Next state
│
└── 8. Integration Boundary
    ├── Service layer mocked/faked
    ├── DB real only if necessary
    ├── External API stubbed
    └── End-to-end minimized
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | Fokus test | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| BPMN deployment | Deploy process model in test | BPMN bisa diload runtime | Model tidak rusak |
| Process start | Start by key/message | Business key dan variables benar | Instance berjalan |
| Process state assertion | Active/completed/waiting | Process sampai node yang benar | Path terbukti |
| Variable mapping | Input/output variables | DTO/service result menjadi variable benar | Tidak salah nama/type |
| Gateway path | Exclusive/parallel gateway | Branching rule benar | BPMN decision valid |
| Delegate/worker | Service task implementation | Worker/delegate menerima dan mengubah data benar | Binding benar |
| Business error | BPMN error boundary | Expected business failure ditangani | Tidak jadi incident |
| Technical error | Exception/retry/incident | Failure teknis menghasilkan retry/incident | Operability |
| Message correlation | Message event | Correlation key benar | Process resume benar |
| Timer | SLA/escalation/timer event | Time-based path benar | Escalation valid |
| Human task | User task lifecycle | Task created/assigned/completed | Manual step benar |
| Idempotency | Duplicate start/message | Tidak double process | Safe retry |
| Outbox/integration | Workflow side effect | Event/command after commit | Consistency |
| Test isolation | Clean runtime per test | Test tidak saling ganggu | CI stabil |

---

# 5. Test Scope yang Disarankan

Workflow test harus dibagi berdasarkan kedalaman.

| Scope | Isi | Kapan dipakai |
|---|---|---|
| Process model test | BPMN + variables + path assertion | Hampir semua process penting |
| Delegate/worker unit test | Java class worker/delegate tanpa engine | Logic worker kompleks |
| Workflow integration test | BPMN runtime + fake service | Validasi orchestration |
| Workflow + DB integration test | BPMN runtime + DB real | Jika process benar-benar bergantung persistence |
| E2E process test | Full app + Camunda runtime + DB + stub external | Sedikit, critical path saja |

Prinsip praktis:

```text
Jangan semua workflow test menjadi full E2E.
Sebagian besar cukup process runtime + fake service.
```

---

# 6. Running Example: Case Approval Process

Misalnya BPMN lifecycle:

```text
Start Case Review
  ↓
Validate Documents
  ↓
Exclusive Gateway:
  ├── documents complete → Start Review
  └── documents incomplete → Request More Documents
  ↓
User Task: Review Case
  ↓
Exclusive Gateway:
  ├── approved → Mark Case Approved
  └── rejected → Mark Case Rejected
  ↓
End
```

Business variables:

```text
caseId
tenantId
submittedBy
documentsComplete
reviewDecision
reviewerUserId
approvalReason
rejectionReason
```

Expected technical behavior:

```text
- process start dengan businessKey = caseId
- document validation task membaca caseId
- gateway mengikuti documentsComplete
- user task dibuat untuk reviewer group
- completion dengan reviewDecision=APPROVED menuju approve path
- service task mark approved dipanggil sekali
- audit/workflow variable terisi
```

---

# 7. Camunda 7: Process Test Shape

Camunda 7 test biasanya memakai embedded process engine. Konsep umum:

```java
@ExtendWith(ProcessEngineExtension.class)
class CaseApprovalProcessTest {

    @Deployment(resources = "bpmn/case-approval.bpmn")
    @Test
    void shouldStartCaseApprovalProcess() {
        ProcessInstance processInstance =
                runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                        "caseApprovalProcess",
                        "CASE-001",
                        Map.of(
                                "caseId", "CASE-001",
                                "documentsComplete", true
                        )
                );

        assertThat(processInstance).isNotNull();
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text
- @Deployment deploy BPMN untuk test.
- runtimeService start process.
- taskService dipakai untuk user task.
- managementService/job executor dipakai untuk job/timer/async continuation.
- historyService bisa dipakai untuk audit path jika history enabled.
```

Camunda 7 menyediakan JUnit 5 `ProcessEngineExtension` untuk membuat/inject `ProcessEngine` dalam test class. ([Camunda Documentation](https://docs.camunda.org/javadoc/camunda-bpm-platform/7.21/org/camunda/bpm/engine/test/junit5/package-summary.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 8. Camunda 8: Process Test Shape

Untuk Camunda 8, arah terbaru adalah **Camunda Process Test**. Dokumentasi Camunda menyebut CPT sebagai Java library untuk mengetes BPMN process dan process application, dengan runtime default berbasis Testcontainers/Docker atau remote runtime. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/testing/getting-started/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Konsep test Camunda 8 umumnya:

```text
1. Start managed Camunda runtime.
2. Deploy BPMN process.
3. Start process instance.
4. Register/execute job workers.
5. Complete jobs or throw errors.
6. Assert process state.
```

Peringatan penting:

```text
Jika Anda menemukan contoh lama dengan Zeebe Process Test:
- masih relevan untuk project lama,
- tetapi untuk Camunda 8.8+ perlu hati-hati,
- dokumentasi Camunda menyatakan Zeebe Process Test deprecated sejak 8.8 dan akan dihapus di 8.10.
```

([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/testing/zeebe-process-test/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. Workflow Test vs Delegate/Worker Unit Test

## Delegate/worker unit test

Cocok untuk logic Java di dalam task.

```text
Input variables:
caseId = CASE-001
documentsComplete = true

Expected:
service.validateDocuments("CASE-001") called
output variable documentsComplete = true
```

## Process integration test

Cocok untuk membuktikan BPMN path.

```text
Given documentsComplete = true
When process starts
Then process reaches Review Case user task
```

## Jangan campur semua

Buruk:

```text
Satu test:
- start BPMN
- hit REST endpoint
- call real DB
- call real external API
- complete user task
- assert SQL rows
```

Itu E2E berat dan flaky.

Lebih baik:

```text
Delegate unit test:
- validate Java worker logic

Process test:
- validate BPMN path

DB integration test:
- validate persistence

External API IT:
- validate HTTP client
```

---

# 10. Practical BPMN Path Matrix

Untuk process `caseApprovalProcess`, minimal path test:

| Scenario | Start variable | Expected path |
|---|---|---|
| Documents complete | `documentsComplete=true` | Review user task |
| Documents incomplete | `documentsComplete=false` | Request more documents |
| Approved | `reviewDecision=APPROVED` | Mark approved service task |
| Rejected | `reviewDecision=REJECTED` | Mark rejected service task |
| Invalid decision | `reviewDecision=null/UNKNOWN` | Error/default path |
| Service task business error | delegate throws BPMN error | Boundary error path |
| Technical exception | delegate throws runtime exception | Retry/incident |
| Timer SLA elapsed | timer advanced | Escalation task/event |
| Message received | message correlated | Waiting process resumes |

---

# 11. Testing Process Variables

Variable names are API contracts inside workflow.

Common bugs:

```text
case_id vs caseId
tenantId missing
reviewDecision enum casing mismatch
BigDecimal serialized as String
Instant serialized differently
Boolean null treated as false
```

Recommended pattern:

```java
public final class CaseWorkflowVariables {

    public static final String CASE_ID = "caseId";
    public static final String TENANT_ID = "tenantId";
    public static final String DOCUMENTS_COMPLETE = "documentsComplete";
    public static final String REVIEW_DECISION = "reviewDecision";
    public static final String REVIEWER_USER_ID = "reviewerUserId";

    private CaseWorkflowVariables() {
    }
}
```

Test should use constants:

```java
Map<String, Object> variables = Map.of(
        CASE_ID, "CASE-001",
        TENANT_ID, "TENANT-001",
        DOCUMENTS_COMPLETE, true
);
```

Practical rule:

```text
Variable name typo should fail test early.
Avoid raw string scattered across delegates, resources, and tests.
```

---

# 12. Testing Gateway Branch

Example gateway condition:

```text
${documentsComplete == true}
```

Test case:

```java
@Test
void shouldRouteToReviewWhenDocumentsAreComplete() {
    ProcessInstance processInstance =
            runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                    "caseApprovalProcess",
                    "CASE-001",
                    Map.of(
                            "caseId", "CASE-001",
                            "documentsComplete", true
                    )
            );

    Task reviewTask = taskService.createTaskQuery()
            .processInstanceId(processInstance.getId())
            .taskDefinitionKey("Review_Case")
            .singleResult();

    assertThat(reviewTask).isNotNull();
}
```

Companion test:

```java
@Test
void shouldRouteToRequestDocumentsWhenDocumentsAreIncomplete() {
    ProcessInstance processInstance =
            runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                    "caseApprovalProcess",
                    "CASE-002",
                    Map.of(
                            "caseId", "CASE-002",
                            "documentsComplete", false
                    )
            );

    Task requestDocsTask = taskService.createTaskQuery()
            .processInstanceId(processInstance.getId())
            .taskDefinitionKey("Request_More_Documents")
            .singleResult();

    assertThat(requestDocsTask).isNotNull();
}
```

Yang dibuktikan:

```text
- gateway condition benar
- process berhenti di user task yang tepat
- variable documentsComplete dibaca benar
```

---

# 13. Testing JavaDelegate / Worker Logic

Misalnya Camunda 7 JavaDelegate:

```java
public class ValidateDocumentsDelegate implements JavaDelegate {

    private final CaseDocumentService caseDocumentService;

    public ValidateDocumentsDelegate(
            CaseDocumentService caseDocumentService
    ) {
        this.caseDocumentService = caseDocumentService;
    }

    @Override
    public void execute(DelegateExecution execution) {
        String caseId = (String) execution.getVariable("caseId");

        boolean complete =
                caseDocumentService.hasCompleteMandatoryDocuments(caseId);

        execution.setVariable("documentsComplete", complete);
    }
}
```

Unit test delegate dengan fake execution/mock execution:

```java
@Test
void shouldSetDocumentsCompleteVariable() {
    CaseDocumentService documentService =
            mock(CaseDocumentService.class);

    when(documentService.hasCompleteMandatoryDocuments("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(true);

    DelegateExecution execution = mock(DelegateExecution.class);

    when(execution.getVariable("caseId"))
            .thenReturn("CASE-001");

    ValidateDocumentsDelegate delegate =
            new ValidateDocumentsDelegate(documentService);

    delegate.execute(execution);

    verify(execution).setVariable("documentsComplete", true);
}
```

Prinsip:

```text
Delegate unit test membuktikan Java logic.
Process test membuktikan delegate terhubung ke BPMN task dengan benar.
```

---

# 14. Testing Worker Boundary in Camunda 8

Untuk Camunda 8 job worker, desain worker harus kecil dan testable.

```java
public class ValidateDocumentsWorker {

    private final CaseDocumentService caseDocumentService;

    public ValidateDocumentsWorker(
            CaseDocumentService caseDocumentService
    ) {
        this.caseDocumentService = caseDocumentService;
    }

    public Map<String, Object> handle(Map<String, Object> variables) {
        String caseId = (String) variables.get("caseId");

        boolean complete =
                caseDocumentService.hasCompleteMandatoryDocuments(caseId);

        return Map.of("documentsComplete", complete);
    }
}
```

Unit test:

```java
@Test
void shouldReturnDocumentsCompleteOutputVariable() {
    CaseDocumentService documentService =
            mock(CaseDocumentService.class);

    when(documentService.hasCompleteMandatoryDocuments("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(true);

    ValidateDocumentsWorker worker =
            new ValidateDocumentsWorker(documentService);

    Map<String, Object> output = worker.handle(Map.of(
            "caseId", "CASE-001"
    ));

    assertThat(output)
            .containsEntry("documentsComplete", true);
}
```

Practical design:

```text
Keep worker adapter thin.
Put business logic in service/policy class.
Make worker test mostly variable mapping + service call.
```

---

# 15. Testing Business Error vs Technical Error

Workflow must distinguish:

```text
Business error:
- expected business failure
- BPMN boundary error handles it
- no incident expected

Technical error:
- unexpected failure
- retry/incident expected
```

## Business error example

```java
public class CaseDocumentIncompleteBpmnException
        extends RuntimeException {
}
```

Better in Camunda 7 delegate:

```java
throw new BpmnError("DOCUMENTS_INCOMPLETE");
```

Test target:

```text
Given document validation throws BPMN error
Then process moves to Request More Documents path
And no incident is created
```

## Technical error example

```java
throw new RuntimeException("database unavailable");
```

Test target:

```text
Given delegate throws technical exception
Then job retries decrease
And incident is created after retries exhausted
```

Practical rule:

```text
Business exception should be modeled in BPMN.
Technical exception should be observable and recoverable operationally.
```

---

# 16. Testing User Task Creation

Camunda 7 style:

```java
@Test
void shouldCreateReviewUserTaskForReviewerGroup() {
    ProcessInstance processInstance =
            runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                    "caseApprovalProcess",
                    "CASE-001",
                    Map.of(
                            "caseId", "CASE-001",
                            "documentsComplete", true
                    )
            );

    Task task = taskService.createTaskQuery()
            .processInstanceId(processInstance.getId())
            .taskDefinitionKey("Review_Case")
            .singleResult();

    assertThat(task).isNotNull();
    assertThat(task.getName()).isEqualTo("Review Case");
}
```

If candidate groups are configured:

```java
List<IdentityLink> identityLinks =
        taskService.getIdentityLinksForTask(task.getId());

assertThat(identityLinks)
        .anySatisfy(link ->
                assertThat(link.getGroupId()).isEqualTo("case-reviewers")
        );
```

What to test:

```text
- task exists
- task definition key correct
- candidate group/user correct
- assignee behavior if relevant
- form key/form variables if used
```

---

# 17. Testing User Task Completion

```java
@Test
void shouldApproveCaseAfterReviewTaskCompletedWithApprovedDecision() {
    ProcessInstance processInstance =
            runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                    "caseApprovalProcess",
                    "CASE-001",
                    Map.of(
                            "caseId", "CASE-001",
                            "documentsComplete", true
                    )
            );

    Task reviewTask = taskService.createTaskQuery()
            .processInstanceId(processInstance.getId())
            .taskDefinitionKey("Review_Case")
            .singleResult();

    taskService.complete(reviewTask.getId(), Map.of(
            "reviewDecision", "APPROVED",
            "reviewerUserId", "reviewer-123"
    ));

    verify(caseApprovalService).approve(
            "CASE-001",
            "reviewer-123"
    );
}
```

Jika service task async, job execution perlu dipicu sesuai engine/test runtime.

Practical assertion:

```text
Do not only assert task completed.
Assert next service task/effect/path.
```

---

# 18. Testing Message Correlation

Use case:

```text
Process waits for payment captured message.
External payment callback arrives.
Message is correlated by paymentId or caseId.
Process resumes.
```

Variables:

```text
caseId = CASE-001
paymentId = PAY-001
```

Test target:

```text
- process waits at message catch event
- message with matching correlation resumes process
- message with wrong correlation does not resume
```

Camunda 7 conceptual test:

```java
@Test
void shouldResumeProcessWhenPaymentCapturedMessageIsCorrelated() {
    ProcessInstance processInstance =
            runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                    "casePaymentProcess",
                    "CASE-001",
                    Map.of(
                            "caseId", "CASE-001",
                            "paymentId", "PAY-001"
                    )
            );

    runtimeService.createMessageCorrelation("PaymentCaptured")
            .processInstanceBusinessKey("CASE-001")
            .setVariable("paymentStatus", "CAPTURED")
            .correlate();

    // assert next activity or process completed
}
```

Practical concerns:

```text
- correlation key must be stable
- duplicate messages must be idempotent
- wrong message must not wake wrong process
```

---

# 19. Testing Timer / SLA Escalation

Timer tests can be tricky. Avoid waiting real time.

Camunda 7 embedded engine allows controlling job execution by querying timer jobs and executing them.

Conceptual test:

```java
@Test
void shouldEscalateWhenReviewTimerFires() {
    ProcessInstance processInstance =
            runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                    "caseApprovalProcess",
                    "CASE-001",
                    Map.of(
                            "caseId", "CASE-001",
                            "documentsComplete", true
                    )
            );

    Job timerJob = managementService.createJobQuery()
            .processInstanceId(processInstance.getId())
            .timers()
            .singleResult();

    managementService.executeJob(timerJob.getId());

    Task escalationTask = taskService.createTaskQuery()
            .processInstanceId(processInstance.getId())
            .taskDefinitionKey("Escalate_Review")
            .singleResult();

    assertThat(escalationTask).isNotNull();
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
Never wait 24 hours / 5 minutes in test.
Drive engine time/job execution explicitly.
```

---

# 20. Testing Incident Path

Incident/retry testing is important for operability.

Scenario:

```text
Service task calls caseApprovalService.
Service throws RuntimeException.
Retries exhausted.
Incident exists.
```

Conceptual assertion:

```java
@Test
void shouldCreateIncidentWhenTechnicalFailureRetriesAreExhausted() {
    when(caseApprovalService.approve(anyString(), anyString()))
            .thenThrow(new RuntimeException("DB unavailable"));

    ProcessInstance processInstance =
            runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                    "caseApprovalProcess",
                    "CASE-001",
                    variablesForApproval()
            );

    Job job = managementService.createJobQuery()
            .processInstanceId(processInstance.getId())
            .singleResult();

    // Execute/retry according to engine setup
    assertThatThrownBy(() ->
            managementService.executeJob(job.getId())
    ).isInstanceOf(Exception.class);

    // reduce retries or execute until incident, depending setup

    Incident incident = runtimeService.createIncidentQuery()
            .processInstanceId(processInstance.getId())
            .singleResult();

    assertThat(incident).isNotNull();
}
```

Test goal:

```text
- technical failure is visible
- not silently swallowed
- process does not wrongly complete
```

---

# 21. Testing No Double Workflow Trigger

Common bug:

```text
REST retry starts two process instances for same case.
Message redelivery triggers same transition twice.
Concurrent submit triggers duplicate workflow signal.
```

Workflow invariant:

```text
one caseId → one active process instance for given workflow type
```

Possible protections:

```text
- business key unique at application level
- DB idempotency table
- process instance lookup before start
- external task/job idempotency
```

Test target:

```java
@Test
void shouldNotStartDuplicateCaseApprovalProcessForSameCaseId() {
    workflowStartService.startCaseApproval("CASE-001");
    workflowStartService.startCaseApproval("CASE-001");

    long count = runtimeService.createProcessInstanceQuery()
            .processInstanceBusinessKey("CASE-001")
            .processDefinitionKey("caseApprovalProcess")
            .count();

    assertThat(count).isEqualTo(1);
}
```

Caution:

```text
Camunda engine alone may not enforce uniqueness of business key.
If uniqueness matters, enforce it in application/DB/idempotency layer.
```

---

# 22. Testing Process Variable Serialization

Variable serialization bugs are common.

Risky variable types:

```text
BigDecimal
Instant / OffsetDateTime
LocalDate
Enum
complex DTO
Map/List nested object
large JSON payload
```

Recommendation:

```text
Prefer simple explicit variables:
- String
- boolean
- long/int
- ISO-8601 string for time if cross-system
- small JSON object only when necessary
```

Test:

```java
@Test
void shouldPreserveReviewDecisionVariable() {
    ProcessInstance processInstance =
            runtimeService.startProcessInstanceByKey(
                    "caseApprovalProcess",
                    "CASE-001",
                    Map.of("reviewDecision", "APPROVED")
            );

    Object value = runtimeService.getVariable(
            processInstance.getId(),
            "reviewDecision"
    );

    assertThat(value).isEqualTo("APPROVED");
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
Do not put arbitrary Java object graphs into workflow variables unless your serialization/versioning strategy is clear.
```

---

# 23. Testing BPMN Model Coverage

For complex workflows, ask:

```text
Which BPMN paths are covered by tests?
Which gateways have untested branches?
Which boundary events are untested?
Which incidents are untested?
Which timers/messages are untested?
```

Coverage matrix example:

| BPMN element/path | Test exists? |
|---|---:|
| Start by case submission | Yes |
| Documents complete path | Yes |
| Documents incomplete path | Yes |
| Review approved path | Yes |
| Review rejected path | Yes |
| Timer escalation | Yes |
| BPMN business error | Yes |
| Technical incident | Yes |
| Cancel process | No |
| Duplicate message | No |

Community tools exist around process test coverage, but even a manual matrix in test docs is valuable.

---

# 24. Test Data / Fixture Strategy

Recommended BPMN test fixture:

```java
public final class CaseWorkflowFixtures {

    private CaseWorkflowFixtures() {
    }

    public static Map<String, Object> caseWithCompleteDocuments() {
        return Map.of(
                "caseId", "CASE-001",
                "tenantId", "TENANT-001",
                "documentsComplete", true
        );
    }

    public static Map<String, Object> caseWithMissingDocuments() {
        return Map.of(
                "caseId", "CASE-002",
                "tenantId", "TENANT-001",
                "documentsComplete", false
        );
    }

    public static Map<String, Object> approvalDecision() {
        return Map.of(
                "reviewDecision", "APPROVED",
                "reviewerUserId", "reviewer-123"
        );
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
Keep workflow variables explicit.
Do not hide important branch variables too deeply in generic fixture.
```

---

# 25. CI/CD Execution Strategy

Workflow tests can be heavier than unit tests.

Recommended split:

```text
Every PR:
- delegate/worker unit tests
- critical BPMN path tests
- error mapper/business error tests

Main branch:
- full process integration tests
- timer/message/error path tests

Nightly:
- heavier Camunda runtime tests
- process coverage matrix
- optional environment smoke
```

For Camunda 8 with CPT Testcontainers runtime:

```text
- ensure Docker available in CI
- cache dependencies where possible
- avoid starting runtime per tiny test if suite becomes slow
- group process tests carefully
```

---

# 26. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Only test delegates, not BPMN | Diagram path bisa salah | Add process path tests |
| Only manually test in Modeler/Operate | Regression tidak tertangkap | Automated BPMN tests |
| Put all business logic in BPMN expressions | Sulit unit test | Extract Java policy/service |
| Huge Java object as variable | Serialization/versioning fragile | Use simple variables |
| No test for error boundary | Business error jadi incident | Test BPMN error path |
| No incident test | Technical failure swallowed | Test retry/incident |
| Wait real time for timer | Slow/flaky | Execute timer job / manipulate time |
| Start duplicate process allowed | Double workflow | Idempotency/business key protection |
| BPMN variable names as raw strings | Typos | Constants |
| Full E2E for every workflow branch | Slow/flaky | Split process/unit/integration scopes |

---

# 27. Practical Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini untuk workflow penting:

```text
[ ] BPMN deploys successfully in test.
[ ] Process can start by key/message.
[ ] Business key/correlation key is correct.
[ ] Required start variables are validated.
[ ] Happy path reaches expected end/task.
[ ] Main gateway branches are covered.
[ ] User task creation is asserted.
[ ] User task completion moves process correctly.
[ ] Service task/delegate/worker binding is tested.
[ ] Input/output variable mapping is tested.
[ ] BPMN business error path is tested.
[ ] Technical exception/retry/incident path is tested.
[ ] Timer/message/signal path is tested if used.
[ ] Duplicate start/message behavior is tested.
[ ] Variable serialization strategy is tested.
[ ] External API/DB behavior is not unnecessarily included.
```

---

# 28. Minimal Test Set per Workflow

Untuk workflow `caseApprovalProcess`:

```text
1. Start process with valid variables.
2. documentsComplete=true → review task.
3. documentsComplete=false → request more documents.
4. Complete review task with APPROVED → approve service task.
5. Complete review task with REJECTED → reject service task.
6. Missing/invalid variable → validation/error path.
7. Delegate/worker business error → BPMN boundary path.
8. Delegate/worker technical exception → retry/incident.
9. Timer SLA → escalation path.
10. Duplicate start/message → no duplicate process/effect.
```

Untuk workflow payment/order:

```text
1. Start with order/payment ID.
2. Payment pending → wait message.
3. Payment captured message → continue.
4. Payment failed message → compensation/failure path.
5. Duplicate webhook/message → idempotent.
6. Timeout/timer → retry/escalation.
7. External call failure → retry/incident or BPMN error.
```

---

# 29. Design Recommendations

## Keep BPMN orchestration-focused

BPMN should express:

```text
sequence
branching
human task
message/timer waiting
error boundary
compensation
escalation
```

Java should handle:

```text
business rule
data validation
repository transaction
external API call
idempotency logic
complex calculations
```

## Use explicit gateway variables

Prefer:

```text
documentsComplete = true
reviewDecision = APPROVED
paymentStatus = CAPTURED
```

Avoid:

```text
complex nested DTO with expression-heavy gateway
```

## Make delegates/workers thin

Good delegate/worker:

```text
read variables
call service
write variables
map business error
```

Bad delegate/worker:

```text
contains 200 lines of business logic
does SQL directly
calls external API directly without gateway
handles transaction manually
```

---

# 30. Ringkasan Seri 10

Inti dari seri ini:

```text
Workflow integration test membuktikan BPMN orchestration behavior:
path, variables, tasks, messages, timers, errors, incidents, and worker/delegate binding.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text
Test BPMN path, not only Java delegate.
Test delegate/worker logic separately.
Use constants for variable names.
Separate business error from technical error.
Do not wait real time for timers.
Assert user task and message correlation.
Test duplicate workflow start/message.
Keep DB/external API out unless needed.
For Camunda 8 new work, prefer Camunda Process Test over deprecated Zeebe Process Test.
```

Boundary yang benar:

```text
Unit test:
- worker/delegate logic
- policy/service logic
- variable mapper

Workflow integration test:
- BPMN path
- process variable flow
- service task binding
- user task/message/timer/error behavior

Other integration tests:
- DB transaction
- external API HTTP client
- resource/API boundary
```
