# Seri 11/14 - Maven Surefire/Failsafe, GitHub Actions, and Test Profiles  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada **test execution strategy**:

```text id="nwt3zf"
Bagaimana unit test, resource test, integration test, Testcontainers test, coverage check,
dan benchmark test dijalankan secara rapi di Maven dan GitHub Actions.
```

Target praktisnya:

```text id="p2gyba"
- mvn test cepat untuk developer
- mvn verify lengkap untuk CI
- unit test tidak tercampur dengan integration test
- Testcontainers berjalan stabil di GitHub Actions
- coverage report dan quality gate jelas
- benchmark tidak memperlambat PR biasa
- pipeline cepat, deterministik, dan defensible
```

Maven Failsafe memang dipanggil melalui lifecycle `verify` untuk integration test, sedangkan Surefire dipakai untuk test fase `test`; dokumentasi Failsafe menyebut integration test dijalankan dengan `mvn verify`. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-failsafe-plugin/usage.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) GitHub `actions/setup-java` menyediakan setup Java, Maven/Gradle publishing support, problem matcher, dan dependency caching untuk Maven/Gradle. ([GitHub](https://github.com/actions/setup-java?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) JaCoCo Maven Plugin menyediakan runtime agent dan report creation untuk coverage. ([EclEmma](https://www.eclemma.org/jacoco/trunk/doc/maven.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Testcontainers membutuhkan Docker environment yang valid; GitHub-hosted runners umumnya menyediakan Docker environment, dan Testcontainers for Java memang ditujukan untuk menyediakan lightweight throwaway instances seperti database dalam JUnit tests. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model: Test Execution Layers

```text id="94cex8"
Developer Local
│
├── mvn test
│   ├── unit tests
│   ├── resource tests
│   └── fast component tests
│
├── mvn verify
│   ├── unit tests
│   ├── integration tests
│   ├── Testcontainers PostgreSQL
│   ├── external API stub tests
│   └── coverage checks
│
└── mvn -Pbenchmark verify
    ├── selected benchmarks
    └── performance regression evidence

CI / GitHub Actions
│
├── PR pipeline
│   ├── compile
│   ├── unit/resource tests
│   ├── selected integration tests
│   └── coverage/report artifact
│
├── main branch pipeline
│   ├── full integration tests
│   ├── Docker/Testcontainers tests
│   ├── workflow tests
│   └── quality gate
│
└── scheduled/nightly pipeline
    ├── full regression
    ├── benchmark/performance smoke
    ├── long-running integration tests
    └── dependency/security scans
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | Fokus implementasi | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| Test naming | `*Test`, `*IT`, `*Benchmark` | Memisahkan lifecycle test | Build predictable |
| Maven Surefire | Unit/resource test | Jalan di fase `test` | `mvn test` cepat |
| Maven Failsafe | Integration test | Jalan di `integration-test` + `verify` | `mvn verify` lengkap |
| Maven profiles | `unit`, `integration`, `benchmark`, `ci` | Control test scope | Developer/CI fleksibel |
| Tags | JUnit `@Tag` | Select/exclude test group | Pipeline granular |
| Testcontainers CI | Docker-backed tests | PostgreSQL/RabbitMQ/stub dependency | Integration test real |
| GitHub Actions Java setup | `actions/setup-java` | Java version + Maven cache | CI faster |
| GitHub Actions matrix | Java/OS/profile matrix | Cross-version validation | Compatibility |
| Coverage | JaCoCo report/check | Line/branch threshold | Quality gate |
| Test reports | Surefire/Failsafe reports | Debug failed test | CI artifact |
| Parallelism | Maven/JUnit parallel | Speed vs flakiness | Controlled execution |
| Benchmark profile | JMH separated | Tidak mengganggu PR | Performance evidence |
| Secrets/config | Test env isolation | No prod secrets | Safe CI |
| Quality gate | Required checks | PR cannot merge when broken | Engineering discipline |

---

# 4. Recommended Naming Convention

Gunakan convention sederhana dan konsisten.

```text id="4kg5df"
Unit/resource/component test:
- *Test.java
- executed by Maven Surefire
- command: mvn test

Integration test:
- *IT.java
- executed by Maven Failsafe
- command: mvn verify

Benchmark test:
- *Benchmark.java
- executed only with benchmark profile
- command: mvn -Pbenchmark verify
```

Contoh:

```text id="0iw21w"
CaseSubmissionServiceTest
CaseResourceTest
CaseMapperIT
CaseTransactionIT
PaymentGatewayClientIT
CaseApprovalProcessIT
JsonSerializationBenchmark
CaseMapperBenchmark
```

Prinsip:

```text id="t5ght5"
Nama file harus memberi tahu kapan test dijalankan.
Jangan pakai nama ambigu seperti CaseIntegrationTest jika Surefire masih mengambilnya.
```

---

# 5. Maven Surefire untuk Unit/Resource Test

Surefire bertugas menjalankan test di fase `test`.

```xml id="o4prvr"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
    <artifactId>maven-surefire-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${maven-surefire-plugin.version}</version>
    <configuration>
        <includes>
            <include>**/*Test.java</include>
        </includes>
        <excludes>
            <exclude>**/*IT.java</exclude>
            <exclude>**/*Benchmark.java</exclude>
        </excludes>
    </configuration>
</plugin>
```

Command:

```bash id="q373qf"
mvn test
```

Target:

```text id="z7gej4"
- cepat
- tidak butuh Docker
- tidak butuh external dependency
- cocok dijalankan sering oleh developer
```

Surefire mendukung JUnit Platform melalui provider/test engine di classpath, dan dokumentasi Maven Surefire menyediakan contoh penggunaan JUnit Platform. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-failsafe-plugin/usage.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 6. Maven Failsafe untuk Integration Test

Failsafe berjalan di lifecycle `integration-test` dan `verify`.

```xml id="fb8jc0"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
    <artifactId>maven-failsafe-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${maven-failsafe-plugin.version}</version>
    <configuration>
        <includes>
            <include>**/*IT.java</include>
        </includes>
        <excludes>
            <exclude>**/*Benchmark.java</exclude>
        </excludes>
    </configuration>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <goals>
                <goal>integration-test</goal>
                <goal>verify</goal>
            </goals>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

Command:

```bash id="ykbg7v"
mvn verify
```

Target:

```text id="ekbk4t"
- MyBatis + PostgreSQL Testcontainers
- external API stub server test
- Camunda workflow test
- transaction/concurrency test
- slower but realistic boundary tests
```

Dokumentasi Failsafe menyebut plugin ini dipanggil dengan `mvn verify` untuk integration tests. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/surefire/maven-failsafe-plugin/usage.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. Kenapa Failsafe, Bukan Surefire untuk Integration Test?

Failsafe didesain agar failure integration test tidak langsung menghentikan lifecycle sebelum cleanup/post-integration step selesai.

```text id="uzviwl"
Surefire:
- phase: test
- cocok untuk unit/resource test
- failure langsung fail build

Failsafe:
- phase: integration-test + verify
- cocok untuk test yang butuh app/container/resource lifecycle
- verify phase mengevaluasi hasil setelah integration-test phase
```

Practical implication:

```text id="g8deib"
Jika integration test start container/app,
Failsafe lebih aman karena lifecycle Maven memang memisahkan setup-run-verify.
```

---

# 8. Maven Profiles

Profiles membantu mengontrol test scope.

## Profile: integration

```xml id="cnfvtn"
<profiles>
    <profile>
        <id>integration</id>
        <build>
            <plugins>
                <plugin>
                    <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
                    <artifactId>maven-failsafe-plugin</artifactId>
                    <configuration>
                        <includes>
                            <include>**/*IT.java</include>
                        </includes>
                    </configuration>
                </plugin>
            </plugins>
        </build>
    </profile>
</profiles>
```

Command:

```bash id="zhurzq"
mvn -Pintegration verify
```

## Profile: skip integration

```bash id="5d1pij"
mvn test
```

atau:

```bash id="45ukxe"
mvn verify -DskipITs
```

Jika tim memakai property khusus:

```xml id="cmlywm"
<configuration>
    <skipITs>${skipITs}</skipITs>
</configuration>
```

---

# 9. Recommended Profile Matrix

| Profile | Command | Isi | Kapan dipakai |
|---|---|---|---|
| default | `mvn test` | unit + resource test | local fast feedback |
| integration | `mvn -Pintegration verify` | unit + integration | PR/main |
| ci | `mvn -Pci verify` | full checks + coverage | GitHub Actions |
| benchmark | `mvn -Pbenchmark verify` | JMH benchmark | manual/nightly |
| smoke | `mvn -Psmoke verify` | selected deployed smoke | release validation |

Prinsip:

```text id="5qjyxq"
Default local command harus cepat.
CI command harus lengkap.
Benchmark command harus eksplisit.
```

---

# 10. JUnit Tags

JUnit tags berguna untuk grouping.

```java id="e7xya1"
@Tag("unit")
class CaseSubmissionServiceTest {
}
```

```java id="gkkpmi"
@Tag("db")
class CaseMapperIT {
}
```

```java id="fcgzyp"
@Tag("external")
class PaymentGatewayClientIT {
}
```

```java id="17z5yw"
@Tag("workflow")
class CaseApprovalProcessIT {
}
```

Recommended tags:

```text id="x3gaqk"
unit
resource
db
external
workflow
slow
concurrency
benchmark
```

Surefire/Failsafe config can include/exclude groups:

```xml id="aojli4"
<configuration>
    <groups>${junit.groups}</groups>
    <excludedGroups>${junit.excludedGroups}</excludedGroups>
</configuration>
```

Command examples:

```bash id="kjmx3n"
mvn test -Djunit.groups=unit
mvn verify -Djunit.excludedGroups=slow
mvn verify -Djunit.groups=db,external
```

Practical rule:

```text id="41269d"
Use naming convention first.
Use tags for extra selectivity.
Do not rely only on tags if naming is inconsistent.
```

---

# 11. JaCoCo Coverage Setup

JaCoCo Maven Plugin provides runtime agent and report generation. ([EclEmma](https://www.eclemma.org/jacoco/trunk/doc/maven.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Basic config:

```xml id="dqoz7v"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.jacoco</groupId>
    <artifactId>jacoco-maven-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${jacoco-maven-plugin.version}</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <id>prepare-agent</id>
            <goals>
                <goal>prepare-agent</goal>
            </goals>
        </execution>

        <execution>
            <id>report</id>
            <phase>verify</phase>
            <goals>
                <goal>report</goal>
            </goals>
        </execution>

        <execution>
            <id>check</id>
            <phase>verify</phase>
            <goals>
                <goal>check</goal>
            </goals>
            <configuration>
                <rules>
                    <rule>
                        <element>BUNDLE</element>
                        <limits>
                            <limit>
                                <counter>LINE</counter>
                                <value>COVEREDRATIO</value>
                                <minimum>0.70</minimum>
                            </limit>
                            <limit>
                                <counter>BRANCH</counter>
                                <value>COVEREDRATIO</value>
                                <minimum>0.60</minimum>
                            </limit>
                        </limits>
                    </rule>
                </rules>
            </configuration>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

Prinsip praktis:

```text id="gog4ox"
Coverage adalah guardrail, bukan target absolut.
Coverage tinggi tidak membuktikan behavior benar.
Coverage rendah menunjukkan blind spot yang perlu dilihat.
```

---

# 12. Coverage Policy yang Lebih Masuk Akal

Jangan satu angka global terlalu tinggi untuk semua module.

| Module/area | Coverage expectation |
|---|---:|
| Domain policy/rule | Tinggi |
| Service orchestration | Sedang-tinggi |
| Mapper DTO/domain | Sedang |
| Resource boundary | Sedang |
| Generated code | Exclude |
| Config/bootstrap | Rendah/optional |
| POJO/record sederhana | Optional |
| Integration-heavy adapter | Jangan dipaksa unit coverage tinggi |

Practical recommendation:

```text id="wt64qn"
Gunakan coverage untuk mencegah regression besar,
bukan untuk memaksa test getter/setter atau generated code.
```

---

# 13. Exclude Generated/Noise Code from Coverage

```xml id="899jx3"
<configuration>
    <excludes>
        <exclude>**/generated/**</exclude>
        <exclude>**/*MapperImpl.class</exclude>
        <exclude>**/*Config.class</exclude>
        <exclude>**/*Application.class</exclude>
    </excludes>
</configuration>
```

Catatan:

```text id="8zw73y"
Jangan exclude business code hanya agar gate lolos.
Exclude hanya code yang memang tidak bernilai dites dengan coverage unit.
```

---

# 14. GitHub Actions: Basic Java Maven Pipeline

```yaml id="r8smzj"
name: ci

on:
  pull_request:
  push:
    branches:
      - main

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Java
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - name: Run unit and resource tests
        run: mvn -B test

      - name: Run integration tests
        run: mvn -B -Pintegration verify
```

`actions/setup-java` supports setting up the requested Java version and caching Maven dependencies. ([GitHub](https://github.com/actions/setup-java?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. Recommended GitHub Actions Pipeline Split

Lebih baik dipisah menjadi beberapa job agar failure jelas.

```yaml id="r6yh2b"
name: ci

on:
  pull_request:
  push:
    branches:
      - main

jobs:
  unit:
    name: Unit and resource tests
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - name: Run unit tests
        run: mvn -B test

  integration:
    name: Integration tests
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: unit

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - name: Run integration tests
        run: mvn -B -Pintegration verify
```

Why split?

```text id="8jzre2"
- unit feedback cepat
- integration failure terpisah
- easier rerun
- clearer required checks
```

---

# 16. GitHub Actions Matrix

Jika ingin test di beberapa Java version:

```yaml id="diur7a"
jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        java: ['17', '21']

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: ${{ matrix.java }}
          cache: maven

      - name: Run tests
        run: mvn -B verify
```

Untuk enterprise Java 17+:

```text id="r1nij9"
- Java 17 sebagai minimum runtime compatibility
- Java 21 jika mulai adoption LTS baru
- Jangan matrix terlalu besar jika tidak memberi value
```

---

# 17. Testcontainers di GitHub Actions

Testcontainers needs access to a valid Docker environment. Testcontainers for Java provides disposable containers like databases for JUnit tests, and GitHub Actions hosted runners commonly provide Docker support. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Basic job:

```yaml id="g3f2vn"
integration:
  runs-on: ubuntu-latest

  steps:
    - uses: actions/checkout@v4

    - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
      with:
        distribution: temurin
        java-version: '21'
        cache: maven

    - name: Verify Docker
      run: docker version

    - name: Run integration tests
      run: mvn -B -Pintegration verify
```

Practical notes:

```text id="t4jhrg"
- gunakan ubuntu-latest atau runner yang punya Docker
- jangan run Testcontainers inside restricted container tanpa Docker socket
- pastikan image pull tidak rate-limited parah
- hindari test yang start container terlalu banyak
```

---

# 18. Testcontainers Optimization

```text id="xq2i5g"
Good:
- static PostgreSQLContainer per test class
- reuse schema setup carefully
- truncate data between tests
- group DB tests by module
- avoid starting one container per test method unless needed

Risky:
- global reusable containers in CI without clear isolation
- parallel DB tests sharing same schema without unique data
- too many containers started at once
```

Testcontainers performs startup checks to ensure the environment is configured correctly before running containers. ([Testcontainers](https://java.testcontainers.org/features/configuration/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 19. Upload Test Reports as Artifacts

Useful for debugging CI failure.

```yaml id="dfpuiq"
- name: Upload test reports
  if: always()
  uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
  with:
    name: test-reports
    path: |
      **/target/surefire-reports/**
      **/target/failsafe-reports/**
      **/target/site/jacoco/**
```

Practical rule:

```text id="6z9e7r"
Use if: always()
So reports are uploaded even when tests fail.
```

---

# 20. GitHub Actions with Coverage Artifact

```yaml id="4g029q"
coverage:
  name: Coverage
  runs-on: ubuntu-latest

  steps:
    - uses: actions/checkout@v4

    - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
      with:
        distribution: temurin
        java-version: '21'
        cache: maven

    - name: Run verify with coverage
      run: mvn -B -Pci verify

    - name: Upload JaCoCo report
      if: always()
      uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
      with:
        name: jacoco-report
        path: target/site/jacoco
```

---

# 21. Required Checks Strategy

For PR merge protection, recommended checks:

```text id="a90vpg"
Required:
- build / compile
- unit and resource tests
- critical integration tests
- coverage check
- lint/static analysis if available

Optional or scheduled:
- full benchmark
- long-running E2E
- provider sandbox smoke
- full compatibility matrix
```

Practical rule:

```text id="v6k4mg"
Required checks should be stable.
Flaky checks destroy trust in CI.
```

---

# 22. Pipeline for Multi-Module Maven Project

For multi-module microservice:

```text id="ro82kd"
root
├── api
├── domain
├── application
├── persistence
├── workflow
├── external-clients
└── service-app
```

Recommended commands:

```bash id="yz42cw"
mvn -B test
mvn -B -Pintegration verify
```

Selective module command:

```bash id="ieqc2m"
mvn -pl persistence -am -Pintegration verify
```

Meaning:

```text id="32mlfz"
-pl persistence  → build selected module
-am              → also build required upstream modules
```

Use this for faster local feedback.

---

# 23. GitHub Actions Path-Based Optimization

For large repo, you can split workflows by changed paths.

Example concept:

```yaml id="4oyj05"
on:
  pull_request:
    paths:
      - 'service-a/**'
      - 'pom.xml'
      - '.github/workflows/service-a-ci.yml'
```

Caution:

```text id="82z2rc"
Path filtering is useful but risky.
Shared module changes must trigger dependent service tests.
```

For senior-level governance:

```text id="pssjyf"
Use path filtering only when module dependency graph is clear.
When in doubt, run broader tests on shared module changes.
```

---

# 24. Benchmark Profile

JMH benchmark should not run by default.

Example profile:

```xml id="yvfr9j"
<profile>
    <id>benchmark</id>
    <build>
        <plugins>
            <plugin>
                <groupId>org.openjdk.jmh</groupId>
                <artifactId>jmh-maven-plugin</artifactId>
                <version>${jmh.version}</version>
                <executions>
                    <execution>
                        <goals>
                            <goal>benchmark</goal>
                        </goals>
                    </execution>
                </executions>
            </plugin>
        </plugins>
    </build>
</profile>
```

Command:

```bash id="qb6lpt"
mvn -Pbenchmark clean verify
```

Practical policy:

```text id="lktl33"
PR:
- no full benchmark by default

Manual:
- benchmark when hot path changed

Nightly:
- selected benchmark regression
```

---

# 25. Nightly Workflow for Slow Tests

```yaml id="h0134i"
name: nightly-regression

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 19 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  full-regression:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - name: Run full regression
        run: mvn -B -Pci,slow,benchmark verify
```

Timezone note:

```text id="mszgja"
GitHub Actions cron uses UTC.
If team operates in Asia/Jakarta, 19:00 UTC = 02:00 WIB next day.
```

---

# 26. Environment Variables and Secrets

Do not hardcode secrets in tests.

```yaml id="sj8ehc"
env:
  APP_ENV: ci
  LOG_LEVEL: INFO
```

For external sandbox smoke only:

```yaml id="5gb58j"
env:
  PROVIDER_SANDBOX_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.PROVIDER_SANDBOX_TOKEN }}
```

Rules:

```text id="owhkjm"
- Unit/integration tests should not need production secrets.
- Stub server tests should use dummy credentials.
- Sandbox tests must be isolated and optional.
- Never print secrets in logs.
```

---

# 27. Docker Compose vs Testcontainers

| Approach | Use when | Pros | Cons |
|---|---|---|---|
| Testcontainers | Test controls dependencies | Per-test isolation, Java-native | Docker required |
| Docker Compose in CI | App stack integration/smoke | Mirrors deployed stack | Heavier, more moving parts |
| GitHub service containers | Simple DB service | Native Actions syntax | Less dynamic than Testcontainers |
| External shared env | Manual/system tests | Realistic | Flaky/stateful |

Recommended for this series:

```text id="jhnkyh"
Use Testcontainers for DB/integration tests.
Use Docker Compose only for app-level smoke if needed.
Avoid shared external environment for PR tests.
```

---

# 28. Parallel Test Execution

Parallelism can speed up but also reveal poor isolation.

## Maven/JUnit parallel risks

```text id="hvr631"
- shared static mutable state
- shared database schema/data
- shared ports
- Testcontainers resource pressure
- non-thread-safe fake/service
- order-dependent tests
```

Use carefully:

```xml id="tp9rqh"
<configuration>
    <properties>
        <configurationParameters>
            junit.jupiter.execution.parallel.enabled = true
        </configurationParameters>
    </properties>
</configuration>
```

Recommendation:

```text id="32t4y7"
Parallelize unit tests first.
Be careful parallelizing DB/concurrency/workflow tests.
```

---

# 29. Flaky Test Governance

Flaky tests are a process problem, not just a technical annoyance.

Policy:

```text id="vrjwpg"
If a test is flaky:
1. quarantine only if absolutely needed
2. create ticket with owner
3. keep visibility
4. fix root cause
5. do not silently ignore
```

Common flaky causes:

```text id="g5x8ei"
- Thread.sleep timing
- shared DB state
- test order dependency
- external network dependency
- random ports not handled
- real clock/timezone
- async process not awaited properly
- container resource pressure
```

---

# 30. Recommended CI Workflow by Test Type

| Test type | Local command | PR CI | Main CI | Nightly |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Unit | `mvn test` | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Resource/Jersey | `mvn test` | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| MyBatis/Postgres IT | `mvn -Pintegration verify` | Selected/Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Concurrency IT | `mvn -Pintegration verify` | Critical only | Yes | Yes |
| External stub IT | `mvn -Pintegration verify` | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Camunda workflow IT | `mvn -Pintegration verify` | Critical | Yes | Yes |
| Full E2E | custom | No/rare | Optional | Yes |
| Benchmark | `mvn -Pbenchmark verify` | No | Optional | Yes |

---

# 31. Example Full CI Workflow

```yaml id="dxhpcv"
name: java-ci

on:
  pull_request:
  push:
    branches:
      - main

jobs:
  unit:
    name: Unit and resource tests
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - name: Run unit/resource tests
        run: mvn -B test

      - name: Upload unit test reports
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: unit-test-reports
          path: '**/target/surefire-reports/**'

  integration:
    name: Integration tests
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: unit

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - name: Verify Docker
        run: docker version

      - name: Run integration tests
        run: mvn -B -Pintegration verify

      - name: Upload integration test reports
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: integration-test-reports
          path: |
            **/target/failsafe-reports/**
            **/target/site/jacoco/**
```

---

# 32. Example Faster PR Workflow

Kalau integration test sangat banyak:

```yaml id="ayqz8r"
name: pr-fast-check

on:
  pull_request:

jobs:
  fast-check:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - name: Unit/resource tests
        run: mvn -B test

      - name: Critical integration tests
        run: mvn -B -Pintegration verify -Djunit.groups=db,external
```

Then nightly full:

```yaml id="1yp2st"
name: nightly-full

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 19 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  full:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - run: mvn -B -Pci,slow verify
```

---

# 33. Build Reproducibility Notes

Practical controls:

```text id="9zjvw6"
- pin action versions by major at least, e.g. actions/setup-java@v4
- pin Maven plugin versions
- use Maven Wrapper if company policy allows
- use dependency lock/catalog if available internally
- do not use floating Docker image tags for critical tests if reproducibility matters
```

For PostgreSQL Testcontainers:

```text id="6ss4ip"
Better:
postgres:16.4-alpine

Riskier:
postgres:latest
```

---

# 34. Practical Maven Command Cheat Sheet

```bash id="a8fvtj"
# Fast local feedback
mvn test

# Run one test class
mvn -Dtest=CaseSubmissionServiceTest test

# Run one integration test
mvn -Dit.test=CaseMapperIT verify

# Run module and dependencies
mvn -pl persistence -am verify

# Skip tests
mvn -DskipTests package

# Skip integration tests if configured
mvn -DskipITs verify

# Run integration profile
mvn -Pintegration verify

# Run CI profile
mvn -Pci verify

# Run benchmark profile
mvn -Pbenchmark verify
```

---

# 35. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Semua test bernama `*Test` | Integration test masuk `mvn test` | Gunakan `*IT` |
| Semua test masuk PR required check | CI lambat/flaky | Split fast/full/nightly |
| Integration test pakai external real service | Flaky dan credential risk | Stub server/Testcontainers |
| Benchmark jalan di setiap PR | PR lambat dan noisy | Profile/manual/nightly |
| Coverage gate terlalu tinggi global | Engineer test noise | Target per module/area |
| Tidak upload test report | Failure susah dianalisis | Upload surefire/failsafe artifact |
| Testcontainers tanpa Docker check | Error membingungkan | `docker version` step |
| Parallelize DB tests tanpa isolation | Flaky | Unique schema/data atau disable parallel |
| Floating Docker image tags | Reproducibility buruk | Pin image version |
| Secrets dipakai di integration test biasa | Security risk | Dummy credentials/stub |

---

# 36. Practical Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini untuk setup Maven/CI:

```text id="yfow8e"
[ ] Unit/resource tests bernama *Test.
[ ] Integration tests bernama *IT.
[ ] Benchmark tests bernama *Benchmark.
[ ] Surefire hanya menjalankan *Test.
[ ] Failsafe hanya menjalankan *IT.
[ ] mvn test cepat dan tidak butuh Docker.
[ ] mvn verify menjalankan integration tests.
[ ] Maven profiles jelas: integration, ci, benchmark.
[ ] GitHub Actions memakai actions/setup-java dengan cache Maven.
[ ] Docker tersedia untuk Testcontainers job.
[ ] Test reports diupload dengan if: always().
[ ] JaCoCo report/check tersedia.
[ ] Required checks stabil dan tidak flaky.
[ ] Nightly workflow menjalankan slow/benchmark tests.
[ ] Tidak ada production secret di normal integration test.
[ ] Docker image/test dependency dipin cukup stabil.
```

---

# 37. Ringkasan Seri 11

Inti dari seri ini:

```text id="u0rbtc"
Testing strategy yang bagus harus didukung execution strategy yang rapi.
Kalau test layer sudah benar tapi Maven/CI kacau, developer tetap akan menghindari test.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text id="ul8gui"
Surefire untuk *Test.
Failsafe untuk *IT.
Benchmark harus explicit profile.
mvn test harus cepat.
mvn verify harus lengkap.
GitHub Actions harus split fast vs integration.
Testcontainers butuh Docker environment.
Coverage adalah guardrail, bukan tujuan utama.
Upload reports agar failure mudah dianalisis.
Required checks harus stabil.
```

## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:42:58 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 12/14**. Fokusnya bukan sekadar “coverage tinggi”, tetapi bagaimana membuat quality gate dan test suite yang tetap bernilai, readable, tidak rapuh, dan tidak menjadi beban maintenance.

# Seri 12/14 - JaCoCo Coverage, Quality Gates, and Test Maintainability  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada pertanyaan yang sering salah dipahami:

> “Apakah coverage tinggi berarti test suite bagus?”

Jawabannya: **belum tentu**.

Coverage membantu melihat bagian code yang disentuh test, tetapi tidak otomatis membuktikan assertion kuat, behavior penting diuji, atau failure mode tercakup. Dalam microservice Java berbasis **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL + Camunda**, test suite yang sehat harus menggabungkan:

```text id="4zuyvl"
coverage
quality gate
branch/error-path testing
mutation-thinking
test readability
fixture discipline
flaky test governance
test debt management
maintainability
```

JaCoCo Maven Plugin menyediakan agent dan report generation untuk coverage Maven project, termasuk contoh unit test dan integration test coverage report terpisah. ([EclEmma](https://www.eclemma.org/jacoco/trunk/doc/maven.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PIT/Pitest menyediakan mutation testing untuk Java; dokumentasi PIT Maven menyebut plugin Maven dapat menjalankan mutation testing, termasuk pada multi-module project. ([PIT Mutation Testing](https://pitest.org/quickstart/maven/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) JUnit 6 adalah generasi terbaru JUnit dan menyediakan fondasi modern untuk developer-side testing di JVM dengan requirement Java 17+. ([JUnit](https://junit.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model: Coverage Bukan Tujuan Akhir

```text id="fsoev8"
Bad mindset:
Coverage harus 90%, selesai.

Better mindset:
Coverage membantu menemukan blind spot.
Quality gate mencegah penurunan kualitas.
Mutation-thinking mengecek apakah assertion benar-benar membunuh bug.
Maintainability memastikan test tetap berguna 6 bulan lagi.
```

Coverage menjawab:

```text id="kfzlm5"
Baris/cabang mana yang dieksekusi test?
```

Coverage tidak menjawab:

```text id="l3n70f"
Apakah assertion benar?
Apakah error path penting diuji?
Apakah test meaningful?
Apakah race condition aman?
Apakah SQL benar?
Apakah API contract stabil?
Apakah workflow path benar?
```

---

# 3. Diagram

```text id="7z4zhe"
Test Quality Model
│
├── Coverage
│   ├── line coverage
│   ├── branch coverage
│   ├── method/class coverage
│   └── integration coverage
│
├── Quality Gate
│   ├── minimum threshold
│   ├── changed-code threshold
│   ├── module-specific threshold
│   └── exclude generated/noise code
│
├── Assertion Strength
│   ├── specific expected value
│   ├── negative/error path
│   ├── side effect assertion
│   ├── final DB state assertion
│   └── contract assertion
│
├── Mutation-Thinking
│   ├── would test fail if condition inverted?
│   ├── would test fail if return value changed?
│   ├── would test fail if exception removed?
│   └── would test fail if mapper field omitted?
│
├── Maintainability
│   ├── readable test names
│   ├── fixture builders
│   ├── no over-mocking
│   ├── stable test data
│   └── low flakiness
│
└── Governance
    ├── flaky test handling
    ├── test debt backlog
    ├── CI required checks
    ├── report artifacts
    └── review checklist
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | Fokus praktis | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| Line coverage | Mengetahui baris tersentuh test | Service/domain/resource | Blind spot terlihat |
| Branch coverage | Mengecek cabang `if/switch` | Error path, state transition, permission | Rule lebih aman |
| Integration coverage | Coverage dari `*IT` | MyBatis, resource, workflow | Boundary terukur |
| Quality gate | Minimum threshold | Build gagal jika coverage turun ekstrem | Discipline |
| Module-specific threshold | Threshold per module | Domain tinggi, config rendah | Gate lebih fair |
| Exclusions | Generated/config code | Hindari noise | Coverage meaningful |
| Assertion quality | Spesifik expected result | Hindari `notNull` only | Test lebih kuat |
| Mutation-thinking | Simulasi bug kecil | Apakah test benar-benar menangkap bug? | Test oracle lebih kuat |
| Pitest | Mutation testing Java | Optional untuk module kritikal | Confidence tambahan |
| Test readability | Naming + AAA | Test sebagai executable spec | Onboarding cepat |
| Fixture architecture | Builder/object mother | Test setup tidak noise | Maintainable |
| Flaky governance | Quarantine/fix policy | CI tetap dipercaya | Pipeline stabil |
| Test debt | Refactor test suite | Test tidak jadi liability | Sustainable |
| Review checklist | PR testing review | Test sesuai risiko | Better engineering culture |

---

# 5. Coverage Types

## 5.1 Line coverage

Menjawab:

```text id="9q8gq8"
Apakah baris ini dieksekusi oleh test?
```

Contoh:

```java id="k1w21d"
if (caseRecord.status() != CaseStatus.DRAFT) {
    throw new InvalidCaseStateException(caseId, caseRecord.status());
}
```

Kalau happy path saja dites, line throw ini tidak covered.

---

## 5.2 Branch coverage

Menjawab:

```text id="az6am7"
Apakah kedua sisi decision diuji?
```

Contoh:

```java id="i48ny6"
if (authorized) {
    approve();
} else {
    throw new ForbiddenCaseActionException();
}
```

Line coverage bisa terlihat cukup tinggi, tapi branch coverage rendah jika hanya `authorized=true` yang dites.

Untuk system lifecycle/regulatory/business process, **branch coverage sering lebih penting daripada line coverage**.

---

## 5.3 Method/class coverage

Berguna untuk menemukan class yang sama sekali tidak disentuh test, tapi jangan dijadikan target utama.

```text id="lgw8b6"
Good:
Detect class penting tidak punya test.

Bad:
Memaksa test semua getter/setter hanya demi method coverage.
```

---

## 5.4 Integration coverage

JaCoCo bisa menghasilkan coverage dari unit test dan integration test terpisah; dokumentasi JaCoCo Maven Plugin menyebut contoh report untuk unit tests dan integration tests, termasuk report unit di `target/site/jacoco/index.html` dan integration di `target/site/jacoco-it/index.html`. ([EclEmma](https://www.eclemma.org/jacoco/trunk/doc/maven.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Integration coverage membantu menjawab:

```text id="yb9d30"
Apakah boundary MyBatis/resource/workflow benar-benar dieksekusi integration test?
```

Namun tetap jangan salah baca:

```text id="9x3r05"
Integration coverage tinggi tidak berarti semua edge case diuji.
```

---

# 6. JaCoCo Maven Setup

## Basic setup

```xml id="cy684y"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.jacoco</groupId>
    <artifactId>jacoco-maven-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${jacoco-maven-plugin.version}</version>
    <executions>
        <execution>
            <id>prepare-agent</id>
            <goals>
                <goal>prepare-agent</goal>
            </goals>
        </execution>

        <execution>
            <id>report</id>
            <phase>verify</phase>
            <goals>
                <goal>report</goal>
            </goals>
        </execution>
    </executions>
</plugin>
```

Run:

```bash id="qjc7wy"
mvn verify
```

Output umum:

```text id="mj1ket"
target/site/jacoco/index.html
```

---

# 7. JaCoCo Quality Gate

Contoh gate sederhana:

```xml id="m4ur4b"
<execution>
    <id>jacoco-check</id>
    <phase>verify</phase>
    <goals>
        <goal>check</goal>
    </goals>
    <configuration>
        <rules>
            <rule>
                <element>BUNDLE</element>
                <limits>
                    <limit>
                        <counter>LINE</counter>
                        <value>COVEREDRATIO</value>
                        <minimum>0.70</minimum>
                    </limit>
                    <limit>
                        <counter>BRANCH</counter>
                        <value>COVEREDRATIO</value>
                        <minimum>0.60</minimum>
                    </limit>
                </limits>
            </rule>
        </rules>
    </configuration>
</execution>
```

Prinsip:

```text id="kfn3ls"
Mulai dengan threshold realistis.
Naikkan bertahap.
Jangan langsung paksa 90% global di codebase legacy.
```

---

# 8. Better Quality Gate Strategy

## Jangan satu threshold untuk semua module

| Module | Suggested gate |
|---|---:|
| `domain` | Tinggi |
| `application/service` | Sedang-tinggi |
| `api/resource` | Sedang |
| `persistence` | Jangan hanya unit coverage; butuh IT |
| `workflow` | Coverage + process path matrix |
| `external-client` | Unit + stub integration |
| `config/bootstrap` | Rendah/excluded sebagian |
| generated code | Excluded |

Contoh:

```text id="uu7o4n"
domain:
line >= 85%
branch >= 75%

application:
line >= 75%
branch >= 65%

resource:
line >= 60%
branch >= 50%

persistence:
unit coverage lower, integration tests mandatory
```

---

# 9. Exclusions yang Masuk Akal

```xml id="9x8bqs"
<configuration>
    <excludes>
        <exclude>**/generated/**</exclude>
        <exclude>**/*MapperImpl.class</exclude>
        <exclude>**/*Application.class</exclude>
        <exclude>**/*Config.class</exclude>
        <exclude>**/*Dto.class</exclude>
    </excludes>
</configuration>
```

Hati-hati dengan exclusion.

```text id="bqld51"
Good exclusion:
- generated code
- framework bootstrap
- pure config
- code yang divalidasi lewat integration/environment test

Bad exclusion:
- service logic sulit dites
- domain rule
- error path
- mapper manual yang rawan bug
```

---

# 10. Coverage Blind Spots

Coverage tinggi bisa tetap buruk jika assertion lemah.

## Contoh buruk

```java id="a3a1um"
@Test
void shouldSubmitCase() {
    SubmitCaseResult result = service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    assertThat(result).isNotNull();
}
```

Coverage mungkin tinggi, tapi test lemah.

## Contoh baik

```java id="dkg8e7"
@Test
void shouldSubmitCaseAndReturnSubmittedStatus() {
    SubmitCaseResult result = service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123");

    assertThat(result.caseId()).isEqualTo("CASE-001");
    assertThat(result.status()).isEqualTo(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED);
    assertThat(result.submittedBy()).isEqualTo("user-123");
    assertThat(result.submittedAt()).isEqualTo(FIXED_NOW);
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="cr9uja"
Coverage tells execution.
Assertions tell verification.
```

---

# 11. Assertion Strength Checklist

Untuk setiap test penting:

```text id="glgc1i"
[ ] Assert expected value spesifik.
[ ] Assert status/state final.
[ ] Assert error code/exception type.
[ ] Assert side effect penting.
[ ] Assert no side effect saat failure.
[ ] Assert DB final state untuk integration test.
[ ] Assert HTTP status + body + headers untuk resource test.
[ ] Assert workflow path/task/variable untuk Camunda test.
[ ] Hindari hanya isNotNull/isNotEmpty.
```

---

# 12. Branch Coverage Practical Example

Business rule:

```java id="44gku0"
public void submit(CaseRecord caseRecord) {
    if (caseRecord.status() != CaseStatus.DRAFT) {
        throw new InvalidCaseStateException(caseRecord.id());
    }

    if (!caseRecord.mandatoryDocumentsComplete()) {
        throw new CaseDocumentIncompleteException(caseRecord.id());
    }

    repository.markSubmitted(caseRecord.id());
}
```

Minimal tests:

```text id="2jyk3p"
1. DRAFT + documents complete → success
2. APPROVED + documents complete → InvalidCaseStateException
3. DRAFT + documents incomplete → CaseDocumentIncompleteException
```

Kalau hanya test success, coverage bisa melewati banyak baris, tapi branch risk masih tinggi.

---

# 13. Coverage untuk Error Path

Enterprise bug sering berada di error path:

```text id="shbh27"
not found
forbidden
invalid state
missing document
duplicate idempotency key
stale version
external timeout
validation failure
workflow incident
transaction rollback
```

Minimal rule:

```text id="a3rl75"
Setiap method kritikal harus punya:
- happy path
- one or more negative paths
- no side-effect assertion on failure
```

---

# 14. Mutation-Thinking

Mutation testing menyisipkan perubahan kecil ke code lalu melihat apakah test gagal.

Contoh mutation:

```java id="7669lg"
// original
if (amount.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) {
    throw new InvalidAmountException();
}

// mutant
if (amount.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) < 0) {
    throw new InvalidAmountException();
}
```

Jika test tidak gagal, berarti test tidak menangkap boundary `amount = 0`.

Pitest adalah tool mutation testing untuk Java, dan dokumentasi Maven-nya menjelaskan penggunaan Maven plugin untuk menjalankan mutation test. ([PIT Mutation Testing](https://pitest.org/quickstart/maven/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. Kapan Pakai Pitest?

Gunakan selektif, bukan selalu seluruh codebase.

Cocok untuk:

```text id="m9bul7"
domain policy
state transition
payment calculation
idempotency policy
retry classifier
permission policy
SLA/escalation calculation
validation logic
```

Kurang cocok untuk:

```text id="721d55"
generated code
config/bootstrap
DTO/record sederhana
framework adapter tipis
large integration-heavy module
```

---

# 16. Pitest Maven Example

```xml id="mhv0qd"
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.pitest</groupId>
    <artifactId>pitest-maven</artifactId>
    <version>${pitest.version}</version>
    <configuration>
        <targetClasses>
            <param>com.company.caseapp.domain.*</param>
            <param>com.company.caseapp.policy.*</param>
        </targetClasses>
        <targetTests>
            <param>com.company.caseapp.*Test</param>
        </targetTests>
        <mutationThreshold>70</mutationThreshold>
        <coverageThreshold>80</coverageThreshold>
    </configuration>
</plugin>
```

Command:

```bash id="ghbb99"
mvn org.pitest:pitest-maven:mutationCoverage
```

Practical policy:

```text id="qwsefy"
Run PIT:
- manually before major refactor
- nightly for critical modules
- not necessarily on every PR
```

---

# 17. Mutation-Thinking Tanpa Tool

Bahkan tanpa Pitest, biasakan review test dengan pertanyaan:

```text id="xwua17"
Kalau kondisi if dibalik, test gagal?
Kalau exception tidak dilempar, test gagal?
Kalau enum mapping salah, test gagal?
Kalau field response hilang, test gagal?
Kalau repository update tidak dipanggil, test gagal?
Kalau audit/workflow double-publish, test gagal?
Kalau retry jalan untuk 400, test gagal?
Kalau idempotency hash conflict diabaikan, test gagal?
```

Ini sering cukup untuk meningkatkan kualitas assertion.

---

# 18. Test Maintainability Principles

Test suite harus bisa dibaca seperti spesifikasi executable.

```text id="sxe2ex"
Good test:
- jelas behavior-nya
- setup minimal
- assertion spesifik
- tidak bergantung urutan
- tidak terlalu tahu implementation detail
- failure message membantu debugging
```

Bad test:

```text id="7z9ryb"
- 80 baris setup untuk satu assertion
- mock semua object
- verify semua internal call
- assert not null saja
- flaky tergantung waktu/thread
- nama test tidak menjelaskan behavior
```

---

# 19. AAA Pattern

Gunakan struktur:

```text id="muwcka"
Arrange
Act
Assert
```

Contoh:

```java id="y6ys77"
@Test
void shouldRejectSubmitWhenCaseIsAlreadyApproved() {
    // Arrange
    CaseRecord approvedCase = aCase()
            .withStatus(CaseStatus.APPROVED)
            .build();

    when(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001"))
            .thenReturn(Optional.of(approvedCase));

    // Act + Assert
    assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit("CASE-001", "user-123"))
            .isInstanceOf(InvalidCaseStateException.class);

    verifyNoInteractions(workflowGateway);
}
```

Untuk exception test, `Act` dan `Assert` sering digabung.

---

# 20. Test Naming

JUnit supports display names, nested tests, tags, and other modern test organization features in its user guide. ([JUnit](https://docs.junit.org/6.1.0/overview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Recommended method naming:

```java id="jx9qbx"
void shouldSubmitDraftCaseWhenDocumentsAreComplete()
void shouldRejectSubmitWhenCaseIsAlreadyApproved()
void shouldReturn409WhenServiceThrowsInvalidCaseState()
void shouldRollbackAuditWhenCaseUpdateFails()
void shouldNotClaimSameJobConcurrently()
```

Pattern:

```text id="57oiic"
should<ExpectedBehavior>When<Condition>
```

---

# 21. Nested Tests for Scenario Grouping

```java id="rgsr4w"
class CaseSubmissionServiceTest {

    @Nested
    class SubmitDraftCase {

        @Test
        void shouldMarkCaseSubmitted() {
        }

        @Test
        void shouldPublishAuditEvent() {
        }
    }

    @Nested
    class InvalidState {

        @Test
        void shouldRejectApprovedCase() {
        }

        @Test
        void shouldNotSignalWorkflow() {
        }
    }
}
```

Use when it improves readability. Jangan nested terlalu dalam.

---

# 22. Fixture Architecture

## Problem

```java id="mog7xq"
CaseRecord caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
        "CASE-001",
        CaseStatus.DRAFT,
        true,
        "user-123",
        Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
        null,
        BigDecimal.ZERO,
        List.of(),
        Map.of(),
        7
);
```

Setup seperti ini membuat test noise.

## Builder solution

```java id="4ww8ow"
public final class CaseRecordFixture {

    private String id = "CASE-001";
    private CaseStatus status = CaseStatus.DRAFT;
    private boolean mandatoryDocumentsComplete = true;
    private int version = 0;

    private CaseRecordFixture() {
    }

    public static CaseRecordFixture aCase() {
        return new CaseRecordFixture();
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withId(String id) {
        this.id = id;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withStatus(CaseStatus status) {
        this.status = status;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withMissingDocuments() {
        this.mandatoryDocumentsComplete = false;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecordFixture withVersion(int version) {
        this.version = version;
        return this;
    }

    public CaseRecord build() {
        return new CaseRecord(
                id,
                status,
                mandatoryDocumentsComplete,
                version
        );
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java id="kp7pmo"
CaseRecord approvedCase = aCase()
        .withStatus(CaseStatus.APPROVED)
        .build();
```

---

# 23. Fixture Rules

```text id="016euq"
Good fixture:
- default valid object
- only override fields relevant to test
- readable domain language
- no hidden surprising behavior
- close enough to actual domain

Bad fixture:
- one mega object mother with 100 methods
- hidden default status that matters
- random data making test nondeterministic
- shared mutable object across tests
```

---

# 24. Test Data Builders vs Object Mother

| Pattern | Cocok untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Test data builder | Object kompleks dengan variasi | Builder terlalu besar |
| Object mother | Common named scenario | Hidden assumptions |
| Inline data | Simple object | Noise if object grows |
| Fixture file | JSON/XML large payload | Harder to see intent |
| SQL fixture | DB integration test | Over-seeding |

Recommended mix:

```text id="j3e2rh"
Domain object:
- builder

Common named scenario:
- object mother method using builder

HTTP payload:
- inline for small JSON
- fixture file for large/contract payload

DB data:
- explicit seed helper
```

---

# 25. Avoid Over-Mocking

Over-mocking membuat coverage terlihat bagus tapi test rapuh.

Bad:

```java id="9e65jz"
CaseRecord caseRecord = mock(CaseRecord.class);
when(caseRecord.status()).thenReturn(CaseStatus.DRAFT);
when(caseRecord.id()).thenReturn("CASE-001");
```

Good:

```java id="tgfq2f"
CaseRecord caseRecord = aCase()
        .withStatus(CaseStatus.DRAFT)
        .build();
```

Rule:

```text id="93hmaf"
Mock dependency.
Do not mock data.
```

---

# 26. Test Smells

| Smell | Gejala | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Mystery guest | Data penting berasal dari fixture tidak jelas | Inline atau beri nama eksplisit |
| Over-specified test | Verify semua method call | Assert behavior |
| Weak assertion | `isNotNull()` saja | Assert field/state |
| Giant setup | Setup lebih panjang dari behavior | Builder/fake |
| Brittle mock | Pecah saat refactor internal | Reduce interaction verification |
| Shared mutable fixture | Test order dependent | Fresh object per test |
| Sleep-based test | Flaky/lambat | Latch/Awaitility/clock |
| Test logic duplicate production | Test bug sama dengan code bug | Expected value eksplisit |
| Random data | Failure sulit reproduce | Fixed deterministic data |
| Hidden clock | Timezone/current time flaky | Inject `Clock` |

---

# 27. Flaky Test Governance

Flaky test menghancurkan trust CI.

Common causes:

```text id="gcdunr"
real time clock
Thread.sleep
async processing without await condition
shared DB state
parallel test interference
external network
random ports/data
Docker resource pressure
timezone dependency
test order dependency
```

Policy:

```text id="dldu97"
1. Treat flaky test as production-quality issue.
2. Do not silently ignore.
3. Quarantine only with owner and ticket.
4. Fix root cause.
5. Re-enable quickly.
```

---

# 28. Stabilizing Async Tests

Bad:

```java id="7uhgyw"
Thread.sleep(1000);
assertThat(repository.findEvent("event-1")).isPresent();
```

Better:

```java id="luo3zm"
await()
        .atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
        .untilAsserted(() ->
                assertThat(repository.findEvent("event-1")).isPresent()
        );
```

If not using Awaitility, use polling helper with bounded timeout.

Rule:

```text id="vaqw9x"
Wait for condition, not time.
```

---

# 29. Stabilizing Time-Based Tests

Bad:

```java id="8gwxhi"
LocalDate dueDate = service.calculateDueDate();
assertThat(dueDate).isEqualTo(LocalDate.now().plusDays(5));
```

Good:

```java id="yeh9y2"
Clock fixedClock = Clock.fixed(
        Instant.parse("2026-07-15T00:00:00Z"),
        ZoneOffset.UTC
);

SlaCalculator calculator = new SlaCalculator(fixedClock);

assertThat(calculator.calculateDueDate(5))
        .isEqualTo(LocalDate.of(2026, 7, 20));
```

Rule:

```text id="12d49j"
Time is dependency.
Inject Clock.
```

---

# 30. Maintainable Integration Tests

Integration tests often rot because setup is unclear.

Good integration test should show:

```text id="019oww"
Given:
- exact DB rows
- exact external stub
- exact workflow variables

When:
- action executed

Then:
- final DB state
- emitted event
- HTTP response
- process state
```

Bad integration test:

```text id="hqc626"
Load huge SQL dump.
Call endpoint.
Assert status 200.
```

Better:

```java id="xvlj73"
fixture.draftCase("CASE-001")
        .withMandatoryDocumentsComplete();

Response response = submitCase("CASE-001", "user-123");

assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(202);
assertThat(caseRepository.findById("CASE-001").status())
        .isEqualTo(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED);
assertThat(auditRepository.findByCaseId("CASE-001"))
        .extracting(AuditRecord::action)
        .containsExactly("CASE_SUBMITTED");
```

---

# 31. Test Review Checklist for PR

Use this during code review:

```text id="83e7qg"
[ ] Does the test match the risk of the change?
[ ] Is there at least one negative/error-path test?
[ ] Are assertions specific enough?
[ ] Are mocks used only for dependencies?
[ ] Is time/randomness controlled?
[ ] Are fixtures readable?
[ ] Does resource test assert status/body/error code?
[ ] Does DB test assert final database state?
[ ] Does concurrency test assert exactly-one-success/final state?
[ ] Does workflow test assert process path/variable/task?
[ ] Is there any sleep/flaky assumption?
[ ] Are generated/config classes excluded only when justified?
```

---

# 32. Quality Gate by Change Type

| Change type | Required quality evidence |
|---|---|
| Business rule | Unit test + branch/error path |
| State transition | Matrix/parameterized test |
| Permission rule | Positive + forbidden test |
| API response | Resource test + JSON contract assertion |
| Exception mapper | Status + code + message/details |
| SQL/MyBatis | PostgreSQL integration test |
| Transaction | Commit/rollback/final DB state |
| Concurrency | Concurrent test + final invariant |
| External client | Stub server test |
| Camunda BPMN | Process path test |
| Performance hot path | Benchmark or perf smoke |

---

# 33. Test Debt Management

Test debt examples:

```text id="v5jkro"
ignored tests
flaky tests
tests with no assertion
tests only checking not null
large outdated fixtures
copy-pasted setup
mock-heavy brittle tests
unclear test naming
slow tests in wrong phase
```

Refactor test debt like production debt:

```text id="kmtggo"
- create backlog item
- assign owner
- measure impact
- fix gradually
- prevent recurrence in review
```

---

# 34. Practical Quality Dashboard

A good engineering dashboard can track:

```text id="jpd44x"
- unit test count
- integration test count
- average CI duration
- flaky test count
- coverage trend
- branch coverage trend
- failed required checks
- mutation score for critical modules
- test quarantine count
```

But do not optimize blindly for count.

```text id="igwotd"
More tests does not always mean better tests.
Better risk coverage means better tests.
```

---

# 35. What Not to Do

## Jangan mengejar 100% coverage buta

Efek buruk:

```text id="x9tuii"
- test getter/setter
- test implementation detail
- engineer menulis assertion lemah
- CI lambat
- coverage jadi vanity metric
```

## Jangan memakai coverage untuk menggantikan review

Coverage report tidak tahu domain risk.

## Jangan biarkan quality gate menghambat refactor sehat

Untuk legacy code, naikkan threshold bertahap.

## Jangan abaikan integration coverage

Untuk MyBatis/PostgreSQL/Camunda, unit coverage saja tidak cukup.

---

# 36. Recommended Baseline Policy

Untuk microservice enterprise baru/sehat:

```text id="r98jzd"
Domain/policy:
- high branch coverage
- parameterized tests for matrix rules
- optional mutation testing

Application service:
- happy path + failure path
- side effect/no side effect assertions

Resource/API:
- status/body/error code/header tests

Persistence:
- MyBatis/PostgreSQL integration tests
- constraint/transaction tests

Workflow:
- BPMN path matrix
- delegate/worker unit tests

External clients:
- stub server integration tests

CI:
- coverage gate reasonable
- reports uploaded
- flaky tests owned
```

---

# 37. Example Policy for JaCoCo Thresholds

```text id="nzowd9"
Initial baseline:
line >= 65%
branch >= 50%

After cleanup:
line >= 75%
branch >= 60%

Critical domain module:
line >= 85%
branch >= 75%

Generated/config excluded:
yes, with review
```

Practical rule:

```text id="jkjy3i"
Raise thresholds only after test suite quality improves.
Do not raise threshold just to create pressure without fixing fixture/test architecture.
```

---

# 38. Ringkasan Seri 12

Inti dari seri ini:

```text id="3gvzvx"
Coverage is a signal, not proof.
Quality gate is a guardrail, not a substitute for engineering judgment.
Maintainability determines whether test suite will still be trusted later.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text id="6jwgk4"
Prefer branch/error-path coverage for business rules.
Use module-specific thresholds.
Exclude only generated/noise code.
Write strong assertions.
Think like mutation testing even without PIT.
Use fixture builders to reduce noise.
Avoid over-mocking.
Control time/randomness.
Treat flaky tests as real engineering debt.
Review tests based on risk, not just count.
```

---

```text id="xmje60"
JaCoCo Coverage, Quality Gates, and Test Maintainability
```
