## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:47:53 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 13/14**. Fokusnya benchmark JVM yang benar: kapan perlu JMH, cara menghindari benchmark palsu, membaca throughput/latency/allocation, dan bagaimana menguji hot path microservice tanpa menyesatkan.

# Seri 13/14 - JMH Benchmark Testing for Java Microservices  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada **benchmark testing** untuk kode Java yang berada di hot path microservice.

Benchmark berbeda dari unit test dan integration test.

```text id="6q4r4e"
Unit test:
Apakah behavior benar?

Integration test:
Apakah boundary nyata benar?

Benchmark test:
Seberapa cepat/stabil/alokatif kode berjalan dalam kondisi terkontrol?
```

Untuk JVM, benchmark manual dengan `System.currentTimeMillis()` hampir selalu berisiko menyesatkan karena JVM punya JIT compilation, warmup, GC, dead-code elimination, escape analysis, dan banyak optimisasi runtime. **JMH - Java Microbenchmark Harness** adalah harness dari OpenJDK untuk membangun, menjalankan, dan menganalisis benchmark nano/micro/milli/macro di JVM. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openjdk/jmh/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Kapan Perlu Benchmark?

Benchmark tidak perlu untuk semua kode. Gunakan ketika ada alasan performance yang jelas.

## Cocok untuk JMH

```text id="9yk7dh"
- JSON serialization/deserialization hot path
- DTO/domain mapper yang dipanggil sangat sering
- validation/policy evaluation kompleks
- query result processing
- idempotency hash calculation
- signature/HMAC calculation
- file parsing kecil-menengah
- collection transformation besar
- custom caching strategy
- retry/backoff calculation
- payload compression/decompression
```

## Kurang cocok untuk JMH

```text id="9iwm41"
- endpoint full HTTP + DB + network
- Kubernetes/deployment performance
- browser/client latency
- real external API latency
- database query latency end-to-end
- workflow engine full runtime latency
```

Untuk full service performance, gunakan load/performance testing tools. Untuk **method-level hot path**, gunakan JMH.

---

# 3. Diagram

```text id="pegmz1"
Performance Testing Layers
│
├── JMH Microbenchmark
│   ├── mapper
│   ├── JSON parser
│   ├── validation rule
│   ├── crypto/signature
│   └── object allocation
│
├── Component Performance Test
│   ├── repository method with local DB
│   ├── HTTP client with stub server
│   └── workflow worker function
│
├── Service Load Test
│   ├── REST endpoint
│   ├── DB
│   ├── thread pool
│   ├── connection pool
│   └── downstream stub/real environment
│
└── Production Observability
    ├── latency percentiles
    ├── throughput
    ├── error rate
    ├── CPU/memory/GC
    └── saturation
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | Fokus praktis | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| Benchmark scope | Pilih hot path | Jangan benchmark semua hal | Benchmark meaningful |
| JMH setup | Maven/module `src/jmh/java` | Isolasi benchmark dari test biasa | Build rapi |
| Warmup | JIT stabilization | Hindari angka cold-start palsu | Result lebih valid |
| Fork | JVM process isolation | Kurangi polusi run sebelumnya | Reproducibility |
| Measurement | Iteration/time unit | Angka stabil | Comparable result |
| Benchmark mode | Throughput/average/sample/single-shot | Sesuai pertanyaan | Metric tepat |
| Blackhole | Prevent dead-code elimination | Output tidak dioptimasi hilang | Benchmark tidak palsu |
| State | `@State` object | Setup reusable input | Clean benchmark |
| Param | `@Param` variasi input | Ukur size/strategy berbeda | Matrix performance |
| Allocation | GC profiler | Lihat allocation pressure | Memory-aware |
| JSON benchmark | Jackson/JSON-B/FasterXML | Serialization hot path | Payload evidence |
| Mapper benchmark | Manual/MapStruct/custom | Object mapping cost | Mapper decision |
| Rule benchmark | Policy/state transition | Branch/rule performance | Domain hot path |
| Result reading | Score/error/unit | Hindari salah interpretasi | Evidence-based |
| CI profile | Benchmark explicit | Tidak memperlambat PR | Controlled execution |

---

# 5. JMH Maven Setup

Ada beberapa cara mengatur JMH. Praktis untuk microservice: pisahkan benchmark di profile khusus.

## Dependencies

```xml id="cm4n4r"
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.openjdk.jmh</groupId>
        <artifactId>jmh-core</artifactId>
        <version>${jmh.version}</version>
        <scope>test</scope>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.openjdk.jmh</groupId>
        <artifactId>jmh-generator-annprocess</artifactId>
        <version>${jmh.version}</version>
        <scope>test</scope>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

## Benchmark profile

```xml id="1n3gz1"
<profile>
    <id>benchmark</id>
    <build>
        <plugins>
            <plugin>
                <groupId>org.openjdk.jmh</groupId>
                <artifactId>jmh-maven-plugin</artifactId>
                <version>${jmh.version}</version>
                <executions>
                    <execution>
                        <goals>
                            <goal>benchmark</goal>
                        </goals>
                    </execution>
                </executions>
            </plugin>
        </plugins>
    </build>
</profile>
```

Command:

```bash id="intttl"
mvn -Pbenchmark clean verify
```

Prinsip:

```text id="3cagz1"
Benchmark tidak boleh jalan otomatis di mvn test.
Benchmark harus explicit profile.
```

---

# 6. Recommended Project Layout

```text id="bq3pba"
src/main/java
└── com/company/caseapp
    ├── mapper
    ├── policy
    ├── serialization
    └── service

src/test/java
└── unit/resource/integration tests

src/jmh/java
└── com/company/caseapp/benchmark
    ├── JsonSerializationBenchmark.java
    ├── CaseMapperBenchmark.java
    ├── CasePolicyBenchmark.java
    ├── HmacSignatureBenchmark.java
    └── IdempotencyHashBenchmark.java
```

Jika build belum mendukung `src/jmh/java`, alternatifnya:

```text id="r8mppu"
src/test/java/.../benchmark
```

Tetapi pastikan `*Benchmark` tidak dijalankan oleh Surefire/Failsafe biasa.

---

# 7. Basic JMH Benchmark

```java id="qea3xu"
package com.company.caseapp.benchmark;

import org.openjdk.jmh.annotations.Benchmark;

public class StringConcatBenchmark {

    @Benchmark
    public String concatWithPlus() {
        return "CASE-" + 123;
    }
}
```

Ini terlalu sederhana dan kurang bernilai untuk production, tetapi menunjukkan bentuk minimum.

---

# 8. JMH Annotations yang Paling Sering Dipakai

| Annotation | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `@Benchmark` | Method yang diukur |
| `@BenchmarkMode` | Mode measurement: throughput, average time, sample time, single shot |
| `@OutputTimeUnit` | Unit output, misalnya ms/us/ns |
| `@Warmup` | Iterasi warmup sebelum measurement |
| `@Measurement` | Iterasi measurement |
| `@Fork` | Jumlah JVM process terpisah |
| `@State` | Object state yang dipakai benchmark |
| `@Setup` | Setup input sebelum benchmark |
| `@TearDown` | Cleanup |
| `@Param` | Variasi parameter benchmark |
| `@OperationsPerInvocation` | Jumlah operasi per call benchmark |
| `Blackhole` | Consume output agar tidak dieliminasi |

JMH official repository menyebut JMH sebagai harness untuk membangun, menjalankan, dan menganalisis benchmark di JVM. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openjdk/jmh/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Oracle juga menjelaskan JMH membantu mengurangi jebakan benchmark JVM seperti optimisasi VM yang membuat hasil salah. ([Oracle](https://www.oracle.com/technical-resources/articles/java/architect-benchmarking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. Benchmark Mode

```java id="l0teyd"
@BenchmarkMode(Mode.Throughput)
```

| Mode | Pertanyaan yang dijawab |
|---|---|
| `Throughput` | Berapa operasi per waktu? |
| `AverageTime` | Rata-rata waktu per operasi? |
| `SampleTime` | Distribusi sample latency? |
| `SingleShotTime` | Waktu satu operasi cold-ish/one-shot? |
| `All` | Semua mode, biasanya terlalu banyak |

Praktis:

```text id="innpnk"
Mapper/JSON/rule hot path:
- Throughput atau AverageTime

Latency-sensitive operation:
- SampleTime

Cold-start-ish operation:
- SingleShotTime, hati-hati interpretasi
```

---

# 10. Warmup, Measurement, Fork

```java id="4llqa1"
@Warmup(iterations = 5, time = 1, timeUnit = TimeUnit.SECONDS)
@Measurement(iterations = 10, time = 1, timeUnit = TimeUnit.SECONDS)
@Fork(2)
```

Makna:

```text id="0bwecj"
Warmup:
- memberi waktu JVM/JIT mencapai kondisi lebih stabil

Measurement:
- iterasi yang dihitung sebagai hasil benchmark

Fork:
- menjalankan benchmark di JVM process terpisah
- mengurangi polusi dari run sebelumnya
```

JVM benchmark butuh warmup karena JIT compilation dan optimisasi runtime. Literatur terbaru juga menunjukkan warmup/steady-state tidak selalu trivial; sebagian benchmark tidak selalu mencapai steady state dan warmup estimate bisa salah. ([arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.15369?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 11. Blackhole dan Dead-Code Elimination

Buruk:

```java id="1a9i6i"
@Benchmark
public void mapCase() {
    mapper.toDto(caseRecord);
}
```

Jika hasil tidak dipakai, JVM bisa mengoptimasi pekerjaan tersebut.

Lebih baik:

```java id="0ooj37"
@Benchmark
public void mapCase(Blackhole blackhole) {
    CaseDto dto = mapper.toDto(caseRecord);
    blackhole.consume(dto);
}
```

Atau return value:

```java id="d7sy28"
@Benchmark
public CaseDto mapCase() {
    return mapper.toDto(caseRecord);
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="3g91q9"
Jangan benchmark pekerjaan yang hasilnya bisa dianggap tidak berguna oleh compiler.
```

---

# 12. State dan Setup

Gunakan `@State` agar setup tidak dihitung sebagai benchmark.

```java id="lh15gw"
@State(Scope.Thread)
public class CaseMapperBenchmark {

    private CaseMapper mapper;
    private CaseRecord caseRecord;

    @Setup(Level.Trial)
    public void setup() {
        mapper = new CaseMapper();

        caseRecord = new CaseRecord(
                "CASE-001",
                CaseStatus.SUBMITTED,
                true,
                "user-123",
                Instant.parse("2026-07-15T10:00:00Z"),
                7
        );
    }

    @Benchmark
    public CaseDto mapCase() {
        return mapper.toDto(caseRecord);
    }
}
```

Scope options:

| Scope | Kapan dipakai |
|---|---|
| `Scope.Thread` | Setiap benchmark thread punya state sendiri |
| `Scope.Benchmark` | State dishare semua thread |
| `Scope.Group` | Untuk benchmark group/concurrency |

Rule:

```text id="3r3y6l"
Default aman untuk microbenchmark murni: Scope.Thread.
Gunakan Scope.Benchmark jika ingin mengukur shared object/cache behavior.
```

---

# 13. Parameterized Benchmark

Gunakan `@Param` untuk variasi input.

```java id="m3ejm4"
@State(Scope.Thread)
public class JsonSerializationBenchmark {

    @Param({"small", "medium", "large"})
    private String payloadSize;

    private ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private CaseResponse response;

    @Setup(Level.Trial)
    public void setup() {
        objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();

        response = switch (payloadSize) {
            case "small" -> Fixtures.smallCaseResponse();
            case "medium" -> Fixtures.mediumCaseResponse();
            case "large" -> Fixtures.largeCaseResponse();
            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException(payloadSize);
        };
    }

    @Benchmark
    public String serializeCaseResponse() throws Exception {
        return objectMapper.writeValueAsString(response);
    }
}
```

Output akan memperlihatkan score per parameter.

Prinsip:

```text id="rz1s8p"
Benchmark satu ukuran payload saja sering misleading.
Test small/medium/large sesuai data production.
```

---

# 14. Benchmark JSON Serialization

Contoh hot path:

```text id="9r8wlp"
CaseResource returns list of cases.
Response serialized thousands of times per minute.
```

Benchmark:

```java id="f2q1oo"
@BenchmarkMode(Mode.Throughput)
@OutputTimeUnit(TimeUnit.SECONDS)
@Warmup(iterations = 5, time = 1)
@Measurement(iterations = 10, time = 1)
@Fork(2)
@State(Scope.Thread)
public class CaseJsonSerializationBenchmark {

    private ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private SearchCaseResponse response;

    @Param({"10", "100", "1000"})
    private int itemCount;

    @Setup(Level.Trial)
    public void setup() {
        objectMapper = JsonMapper.builder()
                .addModule(new JavaTimeModule())
                .disable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_AS_TIMESTAMPS)
                .build();

        response = SearchCaseResponseFixture.withItemCount(itemCount);
    }

    @Benchmark
    public String serializeSearchResponse() throws Exception {
        return objectMapper.writeValueAsString(response);
    }
}
```

Yang dilihat:

```text id="fx2sya"
- ops/sec turun seperti apa saat itemCount naik
- apakah allocation terlalu besar
- apakah konfigurasi ObjectMapper mahal
```

Important:

```text id="2r0x08"
ObjectMapper setup jangan dilakukan di method benchmark.
Di production ObjectMapper biasanya singleton/reused.
```

---

# 15. Benchmark JSON Deserialization

```java id="a8u0rr"
@BenchmarkMode(Mode.AverageTime)
@OutputTimeUnit(TimeUnit.MICROSECONDS)
@Warmup(iterations = 5, time = 1)
@Measurement(iterations = 10, time = 1)
@Fork(2)
@State(Scope.Thread)
public class SubmitCaseDeserializationBenchmark {

    private ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private String json;

    @Param({"small", "large"})
    private String payloadSize;

    @Setup(Level.Trial)
    public void setup() {
        objectMapper = JsonMapper.builder()
                .addModule(new JavaTimeModule())
                .build();

        json = switch (payloadSize) {
            case "small" -> """
                    {
                      "submittedBy": "user-123",
                      "comment": "Ready"
                    }
                    """;
            case "large" -> SubmitCasePayloads.largeJson();
            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException(payloadSize);
        };
    }

    @Benchmark
    public SubmitCaseRequest deserialize() throws Exception {
        return objectMapper.readValue(json, SubmitCaseRequest.class);
    }
}
```

---

# 16. Benchmark Mapper: Manual vs MapStruct vs Reflection

Misalnya ingin membandingkan mapper manual dan mapper framework.

```java id="cpcqhs"
@BenchmarkMode(Mode.Throughput)
@OutputTimeUnit(TimeUnit.SECONDS)
@Warmup(iterations = 5, time = 1)
@Measurement(iterations = 10, time = 1)
@Fork(2)
@State(Scope.Thread)
public class CaseMapperComparisonBenchmark {

    private ManualCaseMapper manualMapper;
    private GeneratedCaseMapper generatedMapper;
    private ReflectionCaseMapper reflectionMapper;
    private CaseRecord caseRecord;

    @Setup(Level.Trial)
    public void setup() {
        manualMapper = new ManualCaseMapper();
        generatedMapper = Mappers.getMapper(GeneratedCaseMapper.class);
        reflectionMapper = new ReflectionCaseMapper();

        caseRecord = CaseRecordFixture.submittedCase();
    }

    @Benchmark
    public CaseDto manualMapper() {
        return manualMapper.toDto(caseRecord);
    }

    @Benchmark
    public CaseDto generatedMapper() {
        return generatedMapper.toDto(caseRecord);
    }

    @Benchmark
    public CaseDto reflectionMapper() {
        return reflectionMapper.toDto(caseRecord);
    }
}
```

Caution:

```text id="22yx0q"
Jangan ambil keputusan arsitektur hanya dari microbenchmark.
Pertimbangkan maintainability, correctness, mapping complexity, dan readability.
```

---

# 17. Benchmark Business Rule / Policy

Contoh state transition policy:

```java id="wqprdr"
public class CaseTransitionPolicy {

    private static final Map<CaseStatus, Set<CaseStatus>> ALLOWED =
            Map.of(
                    CaseStatus.DRAFT, Set.of(CaseStatus.SUBMITTED, CaseStatus.CANCELLED),
                    CaseStatus.SUBMITTED, Set.of(CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW, CaseStatus.CANCELLED),
                    CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW, Set.of(CaseStatus.APPROVED, CaseStatus.REJECTED),
                    CaseStatus.APPROVED, Set.of(CaseStatus.CLOSED),
                    CaseStatus.REJECTED, Set.of(),
                    CaseStatus.CANCELLED, Set.of(),
                    CaseStatus.CLOSED, Set.of()
            );

    public boolean canTransition(CaseStatus from, CaseStatus to) {
        return ALLOWED.getOrDefault(from, Set.of()).contains(to);
    }
}
```

Benchmark:

```java id="umqe9g"
@BenchmarkMode(Mode.Throughput)
@OutputTimeUnit(TimeUnit.SECONDS)
@Warmup(iterations = 5, time = 1)
@Measurement(iterations = 10, time = 1)
@Fork(2)
@State(Scope.Thread)
public class CaseTransitionPolicyBenchmark {

    private CaseTransitionPolicy policy;

    @Param({"DRAFT", "SUBMITTED", "UNDER_REVIEW", "APPROVED"})
    private CaseStatus from;

    @Param({"SUBMITTED", "APPROVED", "REJECTED", "CLOSED"})
    private CaseStatus to;

    @Setup(Level.Trial)
    public void setup() {
        policy = new CaseTransitionPolicy();
    }

    @Benchmark
    public boolean canTransition() {
        return policy.canTransition(from, to);
    }
}
```

Caution:

```text id="hcux8e"
Jika rule murah dan tidak hot path, benchmark ini mungkin tidak perlu.
Benchmark policy hanya jika benar-benar dipanggil sangat sering atau ada regression concern.
```

---

# 18. Benchmark Idempotency Hash

```java id="qewdb9"
@BenchmarkMode(Mode.AverageTime)
@OutputTimeUnit(TimeUnit.MICROSECONDS)
@Warmup(iterations = 5, time = 1)
@Measurement(iterations = 10, time = 1)
@Fork(2)
@State(Scope.Thread)
public class IdempotencyHashBenchmark {

    private MessageDigest sha256;
    private byte[] payload;

    @Param({"256", "4096", "65536"})
    private int payloadBytes;

    @Setup(Level.Invocation)
    public void setup() throws Exception {
        sha256 = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
        payload = TestPayloads.bytes(payloadBytes);
    }

    @Benchmark
    public byte[] hashPayload() {
        return sha256.digest(payload);
    }
}
```

Note:

```text id="53sj5w"
Untuk object mutable seperti MessageDigest, setup level harus hati-hati.
Jika state-nya berubah per invocation, reset/instantiate sesuai kebutuhan.
```

---

# 19. Allocation Benchmark dengan GC Profiler

JMH bisa dijalankan dengan profiler, misalnya GC profiler.

Command:

```bash id="tsg0um"
java -jar target/benchmarks.jar CaseJsonSerializationBenchmark -prof gc
```

Yang dicari:

```text id="zc8h0r"
- allocation rate
- bytes allocated per operation
- GC count/time
```

Kenapa penting:

```text id="9d5qeo"
Dua implementasi bisa punya throughput mirip,
tetapi allocation berbeda jauh.
Allocation tinggi bisa meningkatkan GC pressure di production.
```

---

# 20. Membaca Output JMH

Contoh output:

```text id="b9s3po"
Benchmark                                      (itemCount)   Mode  Cnt      Score     Error  Units
CaseJsonSerializationBenchmark.serializeSearchResponse  10  thrpt   20  12000.123 ± 300.11  ops/s
CaseJsonSerializationBenchmark.serializeSearchResponse 100  thrpt   20   2400.456 ±  80.22  ops/s
CaseJsonSerializationBenchmark.serializeSearchResponse1000  thrpt   20    210.789 ±  10.01  ops/s
```

Interpretasi:

```text id="ae637g"
Mode:
- thrpt = throughput

Cnt:
- jumlah measurement samples

Score:
- estimasi utama

Error:
- interval/error estimate

Units:
- ops/s berarti operasi per detik
```

Jangan hanya lihat score. Lihat juga:

```text id="1g66db"
- error range
- stability antar fork
- allocation
- apakah input realistis
- apakah benchmark mengukur hal yang benar
```

---

# 21. Common JMH Pitfalls

| Pitfall | Contoh | Akibat |
|---|---|---|
| Dead-code elimination | Hasil method tidak dipakai | Kerja dioptimasi hilang |
| Setup masuk benchmark | Buat ObjectMapper tiap call | Mengukur setup, bukan operasi |
| Input tidak realistis | Payload kecil saja | Kesimpulan salah |
| Tidak ada warmup | Cold JVM | Angka tidak stabil |
| Fork terlalu sedikit | State JVM tercemar | Reproducibility rendah |
| Benchmark terlalu mikro | Method isolated tidak mirip production | Misleading |
| Constant folding | Input constant sederhana | JVM precompute |
| Logging aktif | I/O mendominasi | Bukan benchmark logic |
| DB/network asli | Noise tinggi | JMH tidak cocok |
| Compare beda semantics | Implementasi tidak setara | Keputusan salah |

---

# 22. Constant Folding Example

Buruk:

```java id="7lcgrh"
@Benchmark
public int calculate() {
    return 10 + 20;
}
```

JVM bisa precompute.

Lebih baik:

```java id="u1o2ds"
@State(Scope.Thread)
public class CalculationBenchmark {

    @Param({"10", "100", "1000"})
    private int value;

    @Benchmark
    public int calculate() {
        return value * 31;
    }
}
```

---

# 23. Benchmark dengan Realistic Input Distribution

Microbenchmark bisa misleading jika input branch probability tidak realistis. Riset terbaru tentang microbenchmark JVM menunjukkan bahwa profil yang tidak realistis dapat membuat JVM melakukan optimisasi yang tidak terjadi di aplikasi nyata; ini terutama terkait profile-guided/speculative optimizations seperti branch probabilities dan receiver types. ([arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23570?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical implication:

```text id="0f0d3y"
Jika production:
- 90% request valid
- 8% validation error
- 2% conflict

Jangan benchmark:
- 100% valid saja
```

Contoh benchmark branch mix:

```java id="e2yliy"
@Benchmark
public int validateMixedCases(Blackhole blackhole) {
    int valid = 0;

    for (CaseRecord record : records) {
        if (validator.isValid(record)) {
            valid++;
        }
    }

    return valid;
}
```

Setup `records` dengan distribusi realistis.

---

# 24. JMH vs Load Test

| Concern | JMH | Load test |
|---|---:|---:|
| Mapper speed | Yes | Maybe indirect |
| JSON serialization method | Yes | Indirect |
| HTTP endpoint latency | No | Yes |
| DB query latency under load | No | Yes |
| Connection pool saturation | No | Yes |
| Thread pool behavior | No | Yes |
| GC under service traffic | Partial | Yes |
| Kubernetes scaling | No | Yes |
| External system latency | No | Yes |
| Algorithm comparison | Yes | No/expensive |

Rule:

```text id="t8uwvq"
JMH tells you method-level performance.
Load test tells you system-level behavior.
Need both for performance-critical systems.
```

---

# 25. Benchmark CI Strategy

JMH benchmark can be noisy and slow. Do not run full benchmark on every PR by default.

Recommended:

```text id="91p81s"
Local/manual:
- developer runs benchmark before/after hot path change

PR:
- optional quick benchmark only for critical module

Main:
- selected performance smoke

Nightly:
- full benchmark suite
- compare trend
```

Command:

```bash id="2wu4pa"
mvn -Pbenchmark clean verify
```

Nightly GitHub Actions concept:

```yaml id="5m0rxu"
name: benchmark-nightly

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 19 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  benchmark:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - name: Run JMH benchmarks
        run: mvn -B -Pbenchmark clean verify

      - name: Upload benchmark reports
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: jmh-results
          path: |
            **/target/jmh-results.*
            **/target/benchmarks.*
```

---

# 26. Result Format JSON

JMH supports result output formats via command-line options. Common practice:

```bash id="omj2ig"
java -jar target/benchmarks.jar \
  -rf json \
  -rff target/jmh-result.json
```

Use JSON if you want trend tracking.

```text id="bgdnqp"
jmh-result.json can be archived as CI artifact.
Later, compare score trends per benchmark.
```

---

# 27. Performance Regression Policy

Avoid absolute hard gates unless environment is controlled.

Bad gate:

```text id="c944ek"
Fail PR if benchmark < 10000 ops/s on GitHub hosted runner.
```

Problem:

```text id="7a1xol"
CI machines vary.
Noisy neighbors.
Thermal/CPU scheduling.
Different container/runtime conditions.
```

Better policy:

```text id="fzjnrx"
- compare trend on same runner type
- use broad threshold
- review manually for large regressions
- run repeated benchmark for suspicious change
- require benchmark evidence for hot path PR
```

---

# 28. Practical Benchmark Review Checklist

Use this before trusting a benchmark:

```text id="mn7ui1"
[ ] Is the benchmark measuring a real hot path?
[ ] Is input size realistic?
[ ] Is input distribution realistic?
[ ] Is setup excluded from measured method?
[ ] Is result consumed/returned?
[ ] Are warmup and fork configured?
[ ] Are semantics of compared implementations identical?
[ ] Is allocation measured if relevant?
[ ] Is error range acceptable?
[ ] Are results repeatable enough?
[ ] Is this method-level question, not system-level?
[ ] Are conclusions limited to what was measured?
```

---

# 29. Practical Example: Before/After Optimization

Suppose old mapper:

```java id="xkgijh"
public CaseDto toDto(CaseRecord record) {
    return objectMapper.convertValue(record, CaseDto.class);
}
```

New mapper:

```java id="cyzfas"
public CaseDto toDto(CaseRecord record) {
    return new CaseDto(
            record.id(),
            record.status().name(),
            record.submittedAt()
    );
}
```

Benchmark both.

Potential result:

```text id="mxn582"
Manual mapper:
- faster
- lower allocation
- less reflection/object mapper overhead

ObjectMapper convertValue:
- simpler for dynamic structures
- slower
- more allocation
```

Decision should include:

```text id="9tjn8u"
- performance evidence
- maintainability
- mapping complexity
- correctness risk
- frequency in production
```

If mapper runs once per request, optimization may not matter. If mapper runs for 10,000 rows per request, it may matter.

---

# 30. Benchmarking PostgreSQL/MyBatis?

JMH is usually not the right tool for DB query benchmarking because network, IO, connection pool, disk cache, DB planner, and locks dominate.

Better:

```text id="5mlqzv"
For SQL:
- EXPLAIN ANALYZE
- integration/performance test with realistic data
- DB metrics
- query plan regression checks
- load test endpoint if query is user-facing
```

Use JMH only for pure Java part around DB result processing:

```text id="xjm7l7"
- mapping ResultSet-like object to DTO
- post-query aggregation
- in-memory filtering/sorting
```

---

# 31. Benchmarking Camunda Workflow?

JMH is also usually not right for full workflow runtime.

Use JMH for:

```text id="jo88j2"
- worker pure logic
- variable mapper
- payload transformation
- decision/policy function
```

Use workflow/performance tests for:

```text id="mhih26"
- process instance throughput
- job worker backpressure
- timer/message processing
- incident/retry behavior under load
```

---

# 32. Benchmarking External API Client?

JMH is not right for real network calls.

Use JMH for:

```text id="oklc9i"
- request signing
- JSON body creation
- response parsing
- retry policy classification
```

Use stub-server performance/load test for:

```text id="655aq3"
- HTTP client connection pool
- timeout behavior under concurrency
- retry storm behavior
```

---

# 33. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| `System.currentTimeMillis()` loop | JVM optimization misleading | Use JMH |
| Setup inside benchmark | Measures setup not operation | Use `@Setup` |
| No Blackhole/return | Dead-code elimination | Return/Blackhole |
| Benchmark with real DB | Noise dominates | Use DB perf tests |
| One payload size | Non-representative | Use `@Param` |
| No warmup | Cold JVM result | Configure warmup |
| No fork | Polluted JVM state | Use fork |
| Absolute CI gate | Flaky gate | Trend/manual review |
| Over-optimizing cold code | Wasted effort | Benchmark hot path only |
| Comparing unequal behavior | Invalid conclusion | Equal semantics first |

---

# 34. Minimal Benchmark Set for Java Microservice

Jika microservice punya performance concern, minimal meaningful benchmark:

```text id="g5gu8q"
1. JSON serialization for common response payload.
2. JSON deserialization for common command payload.
3. DTO/domain mapper for list response.
4. Idempotency hash/signature if used.
5. Business rule/policy if called at high volume.
6. File/parser hot path if service processes files.
7. Worker/delegate pure transformation if workflow-heavy.
```

Jangan lebih banyak dari yang bisa dipelihara.

---

# 35. Ringkasan Seri 13

Inti dari seri ini:

```text id="c2fm6x"
Benchmark JVM harus dilakukan dengan harness yang benar.
JMH membantu mengurangi jebakan JVM microbenchmark seperti warmup, JIT, dead-code elimination, dan fork isolation.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text id="wi3911"
Gunakan JMH untuk hot path Java.
Jangan gunakan stopwatch manual.
Pisahkan benchmark dari test biasa.
Gunakan warmup, measurement, dan fork.
Return result atau gunakan Blackhole.
Jangan ukur setup kecuali memang setup yang ingin diukur.
Gunakan input size dan distribution realistis.
Ukur allocation jika memory pressure relevan.
Jangan benchmark DB/network penuh dengan JMH.
Jangan buat absolute CI gate yang flaky.
Batasi kesimpulan sesuai scope benchmark.
```

## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:51:53 AM

# Seri 14/14 - Performance Regression Testing in CI/CD  
## Practical Testing Strategy for Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri terakhir ini fokus pada cara membawa performance testing ke pipeline secara sehat.

Targetnya bukan membuat CI menjadi load testing environment penuh. Targetnya adalah:

```text
- mendeteksi regression performa lebih awal
- menjaga hot path tidak memburuk diam-diam
- punya baseline dan trend
- punya threshold yang masuk akal
- punya artifact untuk debugging
- tidak membuat PR pipeline flaky
- tahu kapan perlu profiling, benchmark, load test, atau production observability
```

Untuk JVM, microbenchmark tetap sebaiknya memakai JMH karena JMH memang dibuat untuk membangun, menjalankan, dan menganalisis benchmark di JVM. ([GitHub](https://github.com/openjdk/jmh/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk load/performance smoke, tool seperti k6 bisa dipakai karena mendukung threshold sebagai pass/fail criteria untuk ekspektasi performa sistem. ([Grafana Labs](https://grafana.com/docs/k6/latest/using-k6/thresholds/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model

```text
Performance Regression Strategy
│
├── Micro-level
│   ├── JMH benchmark
│   ├── mapper/json/hash/policy benchmark
│   └── allocation regression
│
├── Component-level
│   ├── HTTP client with stub server
│   ├── repository with PostgreSQL Testcontainers
│   ├── workflow worker pure function
│   └── selected endpoint smoke
│
├── Service-level
│   ├── k6/Gatling/JMeter smoke
│   ├── latency threshold
│   ├── error rate threshold
│   ├── throughput expectation
│   └── resource observation
│
├── CI/CD policy
│   ├── PR fast checks
│   ├── main branch regression
│   ├── nightly benchmark/load smoke
│   └── release readiness check
│
└── Observability feedback
    ├── p50/p95/p99 latency
    ├── error rate
    ├── CPU/memory/GC
    ├── DB pool saturation
    ├── external dependency latency
    └── production SLO trend
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Practical skill | Fokus praktis | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| Baseline | Simpan hasil referensi | Bandingkan before/after | Regression terlihat |
| Threshold | Tentukan batas toleransi | Fail hanya untuk regression berarti | CI tidak noisy |
| Trend | Lacak historis | Performance drift terlihat | Evidence jangka panjang |
| JMH in CI | Benchmark hot path | Mapper/JSON/hash/allocation | Micro regression detected |
| k6/Gatling smoke | Endpoint smoke | Latency/error rate minimal | Service regression detected |
| Profiling artifact | JFR/GC log/thread dump | Debug regression | Root cause lebih cepat |
| Environment control | Runner/resource isolation | Kurangi noise | Result lebih stabil |
| PR policy | Fast + selected perf checks | Feedback cepat | Developer tidak terganggu |
| Nightly policy | Full benchmark/load smoke | Coverage performa luas | Regression dini |
| Release gate | Performance readiness | Sebelum deploy besar | Risk reduction |
| Triage | Classify regression | code/config/env/data | Action jelas |
| Observability loop | Prod metrics jadi input test | Test realistis | Continuous calibration |

---

# 4. Jenis Performance Test

| Jenis | Scope | Tool umum | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|---|
| Microbenchmark | Method/function | JMH | JSON, mapper, hash, policy |
| Allocation benchmark | Method/function | JMH `-prof gc` | GC pressure |
| Component perf smoke | Adapter/repository/client | JUnit + stub/Testcontainers | HTTP client, DB adapter |
| Endpoint smoke | Running service | k6/Gatling/JMeter | Latency/error rate basic |
| Load test | Service/system | k6/Gatling/JMeter | Throughput dan saturation |
| Soak test | Long running | k6/Gatling/custom | Memory leak/resource leak |
| Stress test | Beyond capacity | k6/Gatling | Breakpoint/capacity limit |
| Production observability | Real traffic | OTel/Prometheus/APM | SLO dan real regression |

---

# 5. Apa yang Masuk PR, Main, Nightly?

## PR pipeline

```text
Tujuan:
- cepat
- stabil
- hanya regression performa yang jelas
```

Cocok:

```text
- unit/resource/integration tests
- selected JMH quick benchmark untuk hot path yang disentuh
- lightweight endpoint smoke jika service bisa start cepat
- no full load test
```

## Main branch pipeline

```text
Tujuan:
- lebih lengkap setelah merge
```

Cocok:

```text
- full integration tests
- selected performance smoke
- benchmark artifact upload
- baseline update candidate
```

## Nightly pipeline

```text
Tujuan:
- mendeteksi drift dan regression luas
```

Cocok:

```text
- full JMH benchmark suite
- k6 endpoint smoke/load-lite
- allocation profiling
- JFR/GC artifact
- trend comparison
```

---

# 6. Baseline Strategy

Baseline adalah hasil referensi yang dipakai untuk membandingkan performa.

```text
Baseline options:
1. static file di repo
2. artifact dari main branch terakhir
3. historical data di dashboard
4. manually approved release baseline
```

Contoh baseline file:

```json
{
  "benchmarks": {
    "CaseJsonSerializationBenchmark.serializeSearchResponse[100]": {
      "score": 2400.0,
      "unit": "ops/s",
      "allowedRegressionPercent": 15
    },
    "CaseMapperBenchmark.manualMapper": {
      "score": 850000.0,
      "unit": "ops/s",
      "allowedRegressionPercent": 10
    }
  }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text
Jangan otomatis update baseline dari hasil buruk.
Baseline harus dipromosikan dari build yang dianggap sehat.
```

---

# 7. Threshold Strategy

Threshold tidak boleh terlalu ketat di environment noisy seperti shared CI runner.

## Bad threshold

```text
Fail jika throughput turun 1%.
```

Ini akan flaky.

## Better threshold

```text
Fail jika throughput turun > 15-25% untuk benchmark stabil.
Warn jika turun 5-15%.
Manual review untuk regression kecil tapi konsisten.
```

Untuk endpoint smoke:

```text
Fail jika:
- error_rate > 1%
- p95 latency > 500 ms
- p99 latency > 1000 ms
```

k6 mendukung threshold sebagai pass/fail criteria untuk metric performance. ([Grafana Labs](https://grafana.com/docs/k6/latest/using-k6/thresholds/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 8. Contoh k6 Smoke Test

```javascript
import http from 'k6/http';
import { check, sleep } from 'k6';

export const options = {
  vus: 5,
  duration: '30s',
  thresholds: {
    http_req_failed: ['rate<0.01'],
    http_req_duration: ['p(95)<500', 'p(99)<1000'],
  },
};

export default function () {
  const res = http.get(`${__ENV.BASE_URL}/health`);

  check(res, {
    'status is 200': (r) => r.status === 200,
  });

  sleep(1);
}
```

Run:

```bash
BASE_URL=http://localhost:8080 k6 run smoke.js
```

Ini bukan full load test. Ini smoke test untuk menangkap regression kasar.

---

# 9. Endpoint Performance Smoke untuk Jersey Service

Target endpoint:

```text
GET /health
GET /cases/{caseId}
POST /cases/{caseId}/submit
```

Minimal smoke:

```text
- service bisa start
- endpoint utama return sukses
- p95 masih di bawah threshold kasar
- error rate rendah
```

Jangan mulai dengan semua endpoint.

Mulai dari:

```text
1. health/readiness
2. one read-heavy endpoint
3. one write/command endpoint
4. one external dependency path dengan stub
```

---

# 10. GitHub Actions: Nightly Performance Smoke

GitHub Actions scheduled workflows memakai POSIX cron, dan scheduled workflows berjalan dalam UTC secara default. ([GitHub Docs](https://docs.github.com/actions/using-workflows/events-that-trigger-workflows?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```yaml
name: nightly-performance

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 19 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  performance-smoke:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - name: Build service
        run: mvn -B -DskipTests package

      - name: Start service
        run: |
          java -jar service-app/target/service-app.jar > app.log 2>&1 &
          echo $! > app.pid

      - name: Wait for service
        run: |
          for i in {1..30}; do
            curl -sf http://localhost:8080/health && exit 0
            sleep 2
          done
          echo "Service did not become healthy"
          cat app.log
          exit 1

      - name: Run k6 smoke
        uses: grafana/k6-action@v0.3.1
        with:
          filename: performance/smoke.js
        env:
          BASE_URL: http://localhost:8080

      - name: Upload logs
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: performance-logs
          path: |
            app.log
            **/target/surefire-reports/**
            **/target/failsafe-reports/**
```

Catatan WIB:

```text
19:00 UTC = 02:00 WIB hari berikutnya.
```

---

# 11. GitHub Actions: JMH Nightly

```yaml
name: nightly-jmh

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '30 19 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  jmh:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'
          cache: maven

      - name: Run JMH benchmarks
        run: |
          mvn -B -Pbenchmark clean verify \
            -Djmh.resultFormat=JSON \
            -Djmh.resultFile=target/jmh-result.json

      - name: Upload JMH results
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: jmh-results
          path: |
            **/target/jmh-result.json
            **/target/jmh-results.*
```

Prinsip:

```text
Benchmark artifact harus disimpan agar bisa dibandingkan.
Jangan hanya print ke log.
```

---

# 12. JMH Regression Comparison Script

Contoh sederhana Python untuk membandingkan JMH JSON.

```python
import json
import sys

baseline_file = sys.argv[1]
current_file = sys.argv[2]

with open(baseline_file) as f:
    baseline = json.load(f)

with open(current_file) as f:
    current = json.load(f)

baseline_by_name = {
    item["benchmark"]: item
    for item in baseline
}

failed = False

for item in current:
    name = item["benchmark"]
    if name not in baseline_by_name:
        print(f"WARN new benchmark without baseline: {name}")
        continue

    old_score = baseline_by_name[name]["primaryMetric"]["score"]
    new_score = item["primaryMetric"]["score"]
    unit = item["primaryMetric"]["scoreUnit"]

    if old_score <= 0:
        continue

    regression = (old_score - new_score) / old_score * 100

    print(f"{name}: old={old_score:.2f}, new={new_score:.2f}, regression={regression:.2f}% {unit}")

    if regression > 20:
        print(f"FAIL {name} regressed more than 20%")
        failed = True

if failed:
    sys.exit(1)
```

Catatan:

```text
Untuk throughput, score turun = regression.
Untuk latency/average time, score naik = regression.
Script production perlu tahu direction metric.
```

---

# 13. Direction-Aware Baseline

Format baseline yang lebih aman:

```json
{
  "CaseJsonSerializationBenchmark.serializeSearchResponse": {
    "metric": "throughput",
    "direction": "higher_is_better",
    "baseline": 2400.0,
    "warningRegressionPercent": 10,
    "failureRegressionPercent": 20
  },
  "SubmitCaseDeserializationBenchmark.deserialize": {
    "metric": "average_time",
    "direction": "lower_is_better",
    "baseline": 80.0,
    "warningRegressionPercent": 10,
    "failureRegressionPercent": 25
  }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text
Throughput: higher is better.
Latency/time/allocation: lower is better.
```

---

# 14. Allocation Regression

Throughput tidak cukup. Allocation bisa naik tanpa latency langsung terlihat.

Contoh risk:

```text
Sebelumnya:
- 2 KB/op

Sesudah:
- 30 KB/op

Throughput CI masih mirip,
tetapi production GC pressure naik.
```

Run JMH GC profiler:

```bash
java -jar target/benchmarks.jar \
  CaseJsonSerializationBenchmark \
  -prof gc \
  -rf json \
  -rff target/jmh-gc-result.json
```

Metric yang dicek:

```text
- alloc.rate
- alloc.rate.norm
- gc.count
- gc.time
```

Gunakan allocation threshold untuk hot path:

```text
Fail jika allocation per operation naik > 30% untuk hot path stabil.
Warn jika naik > 10%.
```

---

# 15. Java Flight Recorder Artifact

Untuk regression yang sulit dijelaskan, simpan JFR.

Run service dengan JFR:

```bash
java \
  -XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=recording.jfr,duration=60s,settings=profile \
  -jar service-app.jar
```

Artifact:

```yaml
- name: Upload JFR
  if: always()
  uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
  with:
    name: jfr-recording
    path: recording.jfr
```

Gunakan JFR saat:

```text
- latency naik tanpa jelas
- CPU naik
- allocation naik
- lock/thread contention dicurigai
- GC pause dicurigai
```

Jangan aktifkan JFR berat di setiap PR kecuali sudah terbukti cukup ringan untuk workflow Anda.

---

# 16. GC Log Artifact

Run dengan GC log:

```bash
java \
  -Xlog:gc*:file=gc.log:time,uptime,level,tags \
  -jar service-app.jar
```

Upload:

```yaml
- name: Upload GC log
  if: always()
  uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
  with:
    name: gc-log
    path: gc.log
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
- melihat GC frequency
- melihat pause trend
- mendeteksi allocation regression
- membandingkan sebelum/sesudah hot path change
```

---

# 17. Profiling Artifact Policy

| Artifact | Kapan dikumpulkan | Tujuan |
|---|---|---|
| JMH JSON | Nightly/manual | Trend microbenchmark |
| k6 summary | Nightly/main | Endpoint smoke trend |
| App log | Always on perf smoke | Debug failure |
| GC log | Nightly/perf job | Memory/GC regression |
| JFR | Nightly/manual on suspicion | Profiling CPU/allocation/locks |
| Thread dump | On timeout/failure | Deadlock/saturation |
| Heap dump | Rare/manual | Memory leak debugging |

---

# 18. CI Environment Noise

Shared runners bisa noisy. Jangan memperlakukan angka CI sebagai absolute truth.

Sources of noise:

```text
- shared CPU
- variable clock speed
- container scheduling
- Docker image pull time
- network variability
- JVM warmup differences
- background processes
```

Mitigation:

```text
- use broad thresholds
- compare repeated trends
- separate warning vs failure
- run benchmark multiple forks
- use dedicated runner for critical performance if needed
- do manual confirmation before major conclusion
```

Riset terbaru tentang JVM microbenchmark juga menunjukkan bahwa microbenchmark bisa misleading jika konteks dan profile runtime tidak realistis, meskipun sudah memakai harness seperti JMH. ([arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23570?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 19. Performance Regression Triage

Saat regression muncul, klasifikasikan dulu.

```text
Regression source:
1. Code logic
2. Algorithm/data structure
3. JSON serialization config
4. Mapper implementation
5. DB query/index/plan
6. Connection pool/thread pool
7. External dependency behavior
8. JVM/GC configuration
9. Container/CPU/memory limit
10. CI noise
11. Test data changed
```

Triage flow:

```text
1. Re-run failed benchmark/perf smoke.
2. Compare artifact: JMH JSON, k6 summary, logs.
3. Check recent code diff.
4. Check environment/config diff.
5. Reproduce locally or dedicated runner.
6. Profile if still unclear.
7. Decide: rollback, optimize, update baseline, or adjust threshold.
```

---

# 20. Performance Budget

Performance budget adalah batas yang disepakati.

Contoh API budget:

```text
GET /cases/{id}
- p95 < 150 ms
- p99 < 300 ms
- error rate < 0.1%

POST /cases/{id}/submit
- p95 < 300 ms
- p99 < 700 ms
- no duplicate workflow
- no DB connection pool saturation
```

Contoh hot path budget:

```text
CaseResponse serialization, 100 items:
- throughput tidak turun > 20% dari baseline
- allocation tidak naik > 30% dari baseline

Idempotency hash, 64 KB payload:
- average time tidak naik > 20%
```

Budget harus sesuai:

```text
- SLO/SLA
- traffic pattern
- user experience
- cost/resource constraints
- downstream dependency behavior
```

---

# 21. Observability Loop

Performance test harus dikalibrasi dari production evidence.

OpenTelemetry menyediakan framework observability open-source untuk menangkap traces, metrics, dan logs; Metrics API-nya memisahkan instrumentation dari SDK dan mendukung pengukuran mentah secara efisien. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Gunakan production metrics untuk menjawab:

```text
- endpoint mana yang paling sering dipakai?
- payload size nyata seperti apa?
- p95/p99 latency sekarang berapa?
- dependency mana yang paling lambat?
- query mana yang berat?
- GC pressure kapan naik?
- error rate meningkat di kondisi apa?
```

Lalu update test:

```text
- JMH payload size sesuai production
- k6 scenario sesuai traffic mix
- threshold sesuai SLO
- benchmark fokus ke hot path yang benar
```

---

# 22. Metrics yang Harus Dilihat

Untuk Java microservice:

```text
HTTP:
- request rate
- p50/p95/p99 latency
- error rate
- response size

JVM:
- heap used
- allocation rate
- GC pause/count
- thread count
- CPU usage

Database:
- query latency
- connection pool active/idle/pending
- lock wait
- deadlock count
- slow query count

External dependency:
- latency per dependency
- timeout rate
- retry count
- circuit breaker state

Workflow:
- process start rate
- job completion latency
- incident count
- retry count
- worker backlog
```

---

# 23. Prometheus Histogram / Percentile Notes

Percentile latency sebaiknya dipikirkan sebagai distribution, bukan average. Prometheus documentation menjelaskan histogram dan summary sebagai tipe metric yang berbeda untuk mengukur distribusi observasi. ([Prometheus](https://prometheus.io/docs/practices/histograms/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical rule:

```text
Average latency bisa terlihat baik sementara p99 buruk.
Gunakan p95/p99 untuk user-facing performance.
```

---

# 24. Performance Smoke dengan Stub Dependencies

Jangan membiarkan external dependency asli membuat perf smoke flaky.

Untuk CI:

```text
- PostgreSQL Testcontainer untuk DB path tertentu
- WireMock/MockWebServer untuk external API
- fake/stub workflow worker jika workflow engine bukan fokus
```

Untuk release/staging smoke:

```text
- boleh pakai environment lebih nyata
- tetap batasi scope
- gunakan data test isolated
```

---

# 25. Service Startup Time Regression

Untuk microservice containerized, startup time juga penting.

Measure:

```bash
START=$(date +%s)
java -jar service-app.jar > app.log 2>&1 &
PID=$!

for i in {1..60}; do
  if curl -sf http://localhost:8080/health; then
    END=$(date +%s)
    echo "startup_seconds=$((END-START))"
    kill $PID
    exit 0
  fi
  sleep 1
done

echo "startup timeout"
kill $PID
exit 1
```

Threshold:

```text
Fail if startup > 60s
Warn if startup > 30s
```

Useful for:

```text
- Docker/Kubernetes readiness
- autoscaling
- deployment rollback speed
- local developer experience
```

---

# 26. DB Query Regression

JMH bukan alat utama untuk query DB. Gunakan:

```text
- integration performance test with realistic data
- EXPLAIN ANALYZE
- slow query log
- query plan comparison
- endpoint smoke for query-heavy path
```

Minimal DB regression check:

```text
- seed realistic row count subset
- run critical query
- assert result correctness
- record execution time as warning signal
- inspect query plan manually if regression suspected
```

Jangan fail CI hanya karena query 5 ms lebih lambat di shared runner. Gunakan broad threshold atau dedicated runner.

---

# 27. Concurrency Performance Smoke

Untuk endpoint yang sensitive terhadap race:

```text
POST /cases/{id}/submit
POST /payments/{id}/capture
POST /orders/{id}/confirm
```

Smoke scenario:

```text
- 10 concurrent duplicate requests
- exactly one success or idempotent same response
- no duplicate DB rows
- no duplicate workflow/event
- latency remains bounded
```

Ini menggabungkan correctness + performance.

---

# 28. Retry Storm Regression

External dependency failure bisa menyebabkan retry storm.

Test with stub:

```text
Given external API returns 503
When 50 requests hit service
Then:
- retry attempts are bounded
- circuit breaker opens
- downstream request count does not explode
- API returns controlled error
```

Metrics to assert:

```text
- downstream request count
- service error rate
- response latency
- circuit breaker state
```

---

# 29. Example k6 Scenario: Duplicate Submit

```javascript
import http from 'k6/http';
import { check } from 'k6';

export const options = {
  vus: 10,
  iterations: 10,
  thresholds: {
    http_req_failed: ['rate<0.20'],
    http_req_duration: ['p(95)<1000'],
  },
};

export default function () {
  const payload = JSON.stringify({
    submittedBy: 'user-123',
    comment: 'duplicate submit smoke'
  });

  const params = {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Idempotency-Key': 'idem-duplicate-001',
      'X-Correlation-ID': `perf-${__VU}-${__ITER}`,
    },
  };

  const res = http.post(
    `${__ENV.BASE_URL}/cases/CASE-001/submit`,
    payload,
    params
  );

  check(res, {
    'status is 202 or 409 or idempotent 200': (r) =>
      [200, 202, 409].includes(r.status),
  });
}
```

Catatan:

```text
Untuk correctness final DB state, tetap perlu integration test/JUnit atau post-check API.
k6 lebih cocok untuk pressure/latency/error-rate smoke.
```

---

# 30. Release Readiness Performance Checklist

Sebelum release besar:

```text
[ ] Unit/resource/integration tests pass.
[ ] Critical JMH benchmark tidak regress signifikan.
[ ] Allocation hot path tidak naik signifikan.
[ ] Endpoint smoke pass.
[ ] Error rate threshold pass.
[ ] p95/p99 threshold pass.
[ ] Startup time acceptable.
[ ] DB slow query tidak muncul untuk path utama.
[ ] External dependency stub failure path controlled.
[ ] Retry/circuit breaker behavior aman.
[ ] JFR/GC artifact tersedia untuk build besar/perf-sensitive.
[ ] Baseline update disetujui jika perubahan performa expected.
[ ] Observability dashboard siap setelah deploy.
```

---

# 31. Performance Regression Decision Matrix

| Kondisi | Action |
|---|---|
| Regression kecil, CI noisy, tidak konsisten | Re-run, monitor trend |
| Regression 10-20%, hot path, konsisten | Investigate before merge |
| Regression >20%, hot path | Block/optimize/rollback |
| Throughput sama, allocation naik besar | Investigate GC risk |
| Endpoint p95 naik tapi JMH normal | Check DB/external/thread pool |
| JMH regress tapi endpoint normal | Decide berdasarkan hot path relevance |
| Regression expected karena feature baru | Update baseline with approval |
| Threshold terlalu ketat/flaky | Adjust threshold, keep visibility |
| Baseline usang | Refresh from known-good build |

---

# 32. What Not to Do

## Jangan jadikan GitHub hosted runner sebagai lab performance absolut

Gunakan untuk regression signal, bukan angka final kapasitas.

## Jangan load test production tanpa koordinasi

Risiko:

```text
- mengganggu user
- memicu autoscaling/cost
- menabrak rate limit external
- menghasilkan data kotor
```

## Jangan fail PR karena benchmark noise kecil

Gunakan threshold broad dan trend.

## Jangan update baseline otomatis setiap run

Baseline buruk akan menormalisasi regression.

## Jangan benchmark hal yang bukan bottleneck

Mulai dari observability dan profiling.

---

# 33. Practical Checklist

```text
[ ] Hot path performance sudah diidentifikasi dari metrics/profiling.
[ ] JMH benchmark hanya untuk kode Java hot path.
[ ] Endpoint smoke hanya untuk critical API.
[ ] Baseline disimpan dan direview.
[ ] Threshold punya warning dan failure level.
[ ] Throughput/latency direction dipahami.
[ ] Allocation regression dicek untuk hot path.
[ ] k6/Gatling smoke tidak bergantung external service asli.
[ ] CI artifact disimpan: JMH JSON, k6 summary, logs.
[ ] JFR/GC logs tersedia untuk job perf-sensitive.
[ ] Nightly workflow menjalankan full perf regression.
[ ] PR workflow tetap cepat dan stabil.
[ ] Regression triage flow jelas.
[ ] Baseline update butuh approval.
[ ] Production observability dipakai untuk mengkalibrasi test.
```

---

# 34. Ringkasan Seri 14

Inti dari seri ini:

```text
Performance regression testing di CI/CD harus menjadi early-warning system,
bukan pengganti observability production atau full load test environment.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text
Gunakan JMH untuk hot path Java.
Gunakan k6/Gatling/JMeter untuk endpoint/service smoke.
Pisahkan PR, main, nightly, dan release performance checks.
Simpan baseline dan artifact.
Gunakan threshold broad agar tidak flaky.
Cek latency, error rate, throughput, dan allocation.
Gunakan JFR/GC logs untuk debugging regression.
Jangan update baseline otomatis dari build buruk.
Kalibrasi test dari production observability.
```

---

# 35. Status Seri

```text
Seri 14/14 selesai:
Performance Regression Testing in CI/CD
```

Seluruh seri **Practical Testing Strategy in Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice** sudah selesai.

## Rekap 14 Seri

```text
1. Test Pyramid & Test Boundary Design
2. JUnit Jupiter Practical Unit Testing
3. Mockito, Fake, Stub, Spy
4. Testing Service Layer, Business Rules, and State Transitions
5. Jersey/JAX-RS Resource Testing
6. ExceptionMapper, Validation, Filter, and Serialization Testing
7. MyBatis + PostgreSQL Integration Test with Testcontainers
8. Transaction, Locking, Idempotency, and Concurrency Test
9. External API Integration Test with Stub Server
10. Camunda Workflow Integration Testing
11. Maven Surefire/Failsafe, GitHub Actions, and Test Profiles
12. JaCoCo Coverage, Quality Gates, and Test Maintainability
13. JMH Benchmark Testing for Java Microservices
14. Performance Regression Testing in CI/CD
```

## Rekomendasi Berikutnya

Setelah seri testing ini, rekomendasi paling natural adalah lanjut ke salah satu dari ini:

```text
A. Observability for Java Microservices
   - logging, metrics, tracing, OpenTelemetry, correlation ID, dashboard, alerting

B. Production Readiness Checklist
   - health check, readiness, graceful shutdown, timeout, retry, circuit breaker, config, security

C. Resilience Pattern
   - timeout, retry, bulkhead, circuit breaker, fallback, rate limiter, backpressure

D. PostgreSQL Performance & Query Optimization
   - indexing, EXPLAIN ANALYZE, lock, vacuum, connection pool, query plan regression
```

Rekomendasi saya: lanjut ke **A. Observability for Java Microservices**, karena testing dan performance regression akan jauh lebih kuat jika dikaitkan dengan metrics, logs, traces, dan dashboard production.
